=== FILE: .github/workflows/ci.yml ===
name: CI

on:
  push:
    branches: [ "main", "master" ]
  pull_request:
    branches: [ "main", "master" ]

jobs:
  test:
    name: Lint & Test (${{ matrix.os }}, Python ${{ matrix.python-version }})
    runs-on: ${{ matrix.os }}
    strategy:
      fail-fast: false
      matrix:
        os: [ubuntu-latest]
        python-version: ["3.11", "3.12"]

    steps:
      - name: Check out repository
        uses: actions/checkout@v4

      - name: Set up Python ${{ matrix.python-version }}
        uses: actions/setup-python@v5
        with:
          python-version: ${{ matrix.python-version }}
          cache: "pip"

      - name: Install dependencies
        run: |
          python -m pip install --upgrade pip
          pip install ruff black pytest pytest-cov
          pip install -e .

      - name: Run Ruff Lint
        run: ruff check .

      - name: Run Black Code Format Check
        run: black --check .

      - name: Run Pytest Test Suite
        run: pytest tests/ -v --cov


=== FILE: AGENTS.md ===
# Route.it Project Rules & System Specification

## Project Goal
Route.it is an adaptive quantum-inspired fleet routing system for the Smart India Hackathon 2026 (PS SIH26137: Quantum-Inspired Intelligent Traffic Route Optimization Using Metaheuristic Optimization).
It solves the capacitated vehicle routing problem with time windows (CVRPTW) with time-dependent travel times, and re-optimises when traffic events happen.
"Quantum-inspired" means a CLASSICAL algorithm: Quantum-behaved Particle Swarm Optimization (QPSO, Sun, Feng & Xu 2004). No quantum hardware or quantum libraries.

## Architecture
- QPSO decides the order of customer stops. Valhalla (road router) finds the actual roads and travel times.
- Pipeline: input -> cost oracle (travel-time matrix) -> QPSO over random-key giant tour -> Prins Split into feasible routes -> local search (2-opt, relocate, swap) -> optimised routes + ETAs.
- Dynamic layer: event -> impact check (re-plan only if delta F > theta, with a stability penalty) -> Tier 1 path repair -> Tier 2 local repair -> Tier 3 QPSO warm start.
- MVP scope: single depot, one vehicle type. Multi-depot, SUMO, CO2 objective come only after MVP is complete.

## Repo Layout
```
/solver       # QPSO, PSO baseline, Split, local search, constraints
/routing      # Valhalla client, cost oracle, cache, traffic adapters
/dynamic      # Event simulator, impact check, tiered repair
/api          # FastAPI service + WebSocket
/dashboard    # React + MapLibre UI
/benchmarks   # Runners, instances, results, plots
/docs         # Design notes, benchmark report, demo script
/tests        # Mirrors the source folders
```

## Tech Stack
Python 3.11+, NumPy, Numba, OR-Tools (baseline only), FastAPI, Pydantic, pytest, OSMnx, Valhalla (Docker), React, MapLibre GL.

## Coding Standards
- Type hints everywhere. Docstrings on public functions. Small pure functions where possible.
- Every module ships with pytest tests. Never mark a task done if tests fail.
- All randomness takes an explicit seed so results are reproducible.
- Config in YAML or Pydantic settings, no magic numbers inside algorithms.
- Keep the solver independent of the API and dashboard. The solver must run from a CLI.

## Integrity Rules
- NEVER fabricate, hardcode, estimate, or "illustrate" benchmark results or performance numbers. Every number in docs or UI must come from a real run saved in `/benchmarks/results`.
- If OR-Tools beats our solver on an instance, report it honestly.
- Do not claim real live traffic unless a real provider is wired in. Simulated or time-of-day traffic must be labelled as simulated in UI and docs.
- Do not silently change an algorithm to make a test pass. Explain any deviation.

## Working Style
- In Planning mode, always produce an implementation plan artifact and wait for approval before coding.
- Prefer the simplest approach that works, then optimise with profiling evidence.
- After each task, summarise: what changed, how to run it, what is untested.
- If requirements are ambiguous, ask one concise question instead of guessing.

=== FILE: api/geojson.py ===
"""GeoJSON FeatureCollection builder for Route.it API and MapLibre GL Dashboard.

Converts CVRPTW Problem and Solution into standard RFC 7946 GeoJSON
containing LineString routes with vehicle metrics and Point stops with ETAs.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from typing import Any
from routing.cost_oracle import CostOracle, ValhallaOracle
from solver.models import Customer, Depot, Problem, Route, Solution

# Curated palette for multi-vehicle visualization on dark / light MapLibre GL canvases
ROUTE_PALETTE = [
    "#3B82F6",  # Blue
    "#10B981",  # Emerald
    "#F59E0B",  # Amber
    "#EF4444",  # Rose
    "#8B5CF6",  # Violet
    "#EC4899",  # Pink
    "#06B6D4",  # Cyan
    "#84CC16",  # Lime
    "#F97316",  # Orange
    "#6366F1",  # Indigo
]


def solution_to_geojson(
    problem: Problem,
    solution: Solution,
    cost_oracle: CostOracle | None = None,
    current_time: float = 0.0,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """Generate a standard GeoJSON FeatureCollection for a routing solution.

    Args:
        problem: Problem instance with depot and customer coordinates.
        solution: Solution containing vehicle routes and arrival times.
        cost_oracle: Optional CostOracle for road-following geometries (Valhalla).
        current_time: Simulation timestamp to flag visited vs upcoming stops.

    Returns:
        GeoJSON FeatureCollection dict.
    """
    features: list[dict[str, Any]] = []

    # Map customers by ID
    cust_map: dict[str, Customer] = {c.id: c for c in problem.customers}

    # Stop service tracker: customer_id -> (vehicle_id, arrival_time)
    service_info: dict[str, tuple[str, float]] = {}

    # 1. Build Route LineString Features
    for r_idx, route in enumerate(solution.routes):
        if not route.stop_ids:
            continue

        color = ROUTE_PALETTE[r_idx % len(ROUTE_PALETTE)]

        # Collect sequence of waypoints: Depot -> stops... -> Depot
        waypoints: list[tuple[float, float]] = [(problem.depot.lat, problem.depot.lon)]
        for sid in route.stop_ids:
            c = cust_map.get(sid)
            if c is not None:
                waypoints.append((c.lat, c.lon))
        waypoints.append((problem.depot.lat, problem.depot.lon))

        coords: list[list[float]] = []

        # Attempt high-fidelity road polyline from Valhalla if available
        if isinstance(cost_oracle, ValhallaOracle):
            try:
                stops_seq = [problem.depot] + [cust_map[sid] for sid in route.stop_ids if sid in cust_map] + [problem.depot]
                res = cost_oracle.route(stops_seq)
                valhalla_coords = res.get("coordinates", [])
                coords = valhalla_coords if valhalla_coords else [[round(lon, 6), round(lat, 6)] for lat, lon in waypoints]
            except Exception:
                coords = [[round(lon, 6), round(lat, 6)] for lat, lon in waypoints]
        else:
            # Planar / Euclidean straight segment coordinates [lon, lat]
            coords = [[round(lon, 6), round(lat, 6)] for lat, lon in waypoints]

        features.append(
            {
                "type": "Feature",
                "id": f"route_{route.vehicle_id}",
                "geometry": {
                    "type": "LineString",
                    "coordinates": coords,
                },
                "properties": {
                    "feature_type": "route",
                    "vehicle_id": route.vehicle_id,
                    "route_index": r_idx,
                    "stop_ids": route.stop_ids,
                    "stop_count": len(route.stop_ids),
                    "arrival_times": route.arrival_times,
                    "load": route.load,
                    "distance_m": route.distance,
                    "duration_sec": route.duration,
                    "color": color,
                },
            }
        )

        for sid, arr in zip(route.stop_ids, route.arrival_times):
            service_info[sid] = (route.vehicle_id, arr)

    # 2. Build Depot Point Feature
    features.append(
        {
            "type": "Feature",
            "id": "depot",
            "geometry": {
                "type": "Point",
                "coordinates": [round(problem.depot.lon, 6), round(problem.depot.lat, 6)],
            },
            "properties": {
                "feature_type": "depot",
                "id": problem.depot.id,
                "name": problem.depot.name or "Central Depot",
                "ready_time": problem.depot.ready_time,
                "due_time": problem.depot.due_time,
                "service_time": problem.depot.service_time,
            },
        }
    )

    # 3. Build Customer Stop Point Features
    for cust in problem.customers:
        assigned_v, arr_time = service_info.get(cust.id, ("unassigned", 0.0))
        is_visited = current_time >= (arr_time + cust.service_time) if assigned_v != "unassigned" else False
        is_imminent = (arr_time - current_time) >= 0 and (arr_time - current_time) <= 300.0

        features.append(
            {
                "type": "Feature",
                "id": f"stop_{cust.id}",
                "geometry": {
                    "type": "Point",
                    "coordinates": [round(cust.lon, 6), round(cust.lat, 6)],
                },
                "properties": {
                    "feature_type": "customer",
                    "id": cust.id,
                    "name": cust.name or f"Customer {cust.id}",
                    "demand": cust.demand,
                    "ready_time": cust.ready_time,
                    "due_time": cust.due_time,
                    "service_time": cust.service_time,
                    "priority": cust.priority,
                    "vehicle_id": assigned_v,
                    "arrival_time": round(arr_time, 2) if assigned_v != "unassigned" else None,
                    "is_visited": is_visited,
                    "is_imminent": is_imminent,
                },
            }
        )

    return {
        "type": "FeatureCollection",
        "features": features,
    }


=== FILE: api/main.py ===
"""FastAPI main application entrypoint for Route.it.

Configures CORS for frontend dashboard development servers, registers
REST and WebSocket routers, and initializes telemetry lifecycle hooks.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import asyncio
from contextlib import asynccontextmanager
import logging
from typing import AsyncGenerator
from fastapi import FastAPI
from fastapi.middleware.cors import CORSMiddleware

from api import __version__
from api.routes.events import router as events_router
from api.routes.health import router as health_router
from api.routes.problems import router as problems_router
from api.routes.ws import router as ws_router
from api.websocket import manager

logging.basicConfig(level=logging.INFO)
logger = logging.getLogger("route_it_api")


@asynccontextmanager
async def lifespan(app: FastAPI) -> AsyncGenerator[None, None]:
    """Lifespan context manager for event loop capture and graceful shutdown."""
    loop = asyncio.get_running_loop()
    manager.set_event_loop(loop)
    logger.info(f"[API] Route.it Service v{__version__} initialized.")
    yield
    logger.info("[API] Route.it Service shutting down.")


def create_app() -> FastAPI:
    """Build and configure the FastAPI application instance."""
    app = FastAPI(
        title="Route.it Fleet Routing API",
        description=(
            "Adaptive Quantum-Inspired Fleet Routing Engine (CVRPTW) "
            "with Dynamic Incident Re-optimization for Smart India Hackathon 2026 (PS SIH26137)."
        ),
        version=__version__,
        docs_url="/docs",
        redoc_url="/redoc",
        openapi_url="/openapi.json",
        lifespan=lifespan,
    )

    # Enable CORS for React + MapLibre GL Dashboard development servers
    app.add_middleware(
        CORSMiddleware,
        allow_origins=[
            "http://localhost:3000",
            "http://127.0.0.1:3000",
            "http://localhost:5173",
            "http://127.0.0.1:5173",
            "*",  # Permissive for development convenience
        ],
        allow_credentials=True,
        allow_methods=["*"],
        allow_headers=["*"],
    )

    # Register API and WebSocket routers
    app.include_router(health_router)
    app.include_router(problems_router)
    app.include_router(events_router)
    app.include_router(ws_router)

    return app


app = create_app()


=== FILE: api/models.py ===
"""Pydantic request and response schemas for the Route.it API.

Reuses domain models from solver.models and dynamic.events while providing
clean HTTP schemas for problem management, async solve jobs, GeoJSON payloads,
and dynamic traffic event injection.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import time
from typing import Any, Literal
from pydantic import BaseModel, Field

from dynamic.events import EdgeCongestion, RoadBlockage, VehicleDelay
from solver.models import Customer, Depot, Problem, Route, Solution, Vehicle


class ProblemCreateRequest(BaseModel):
    """Payload for creating a new CVRPTW problem instance."""

    id: str | None = Field(None, description="Optional custom problem ID")
    depot: Depot = Field(..., description="Depot location, hours, and service turnaround")
    customers: list[Customer] = Field(..., min_length=1, description="List of customer delivery stops")
    vehicles: list[Vehicle] | None = Field(None, description="Optional vehicle fleet specifications")
    coordinate_system: Literal["geographic", "planar"] = Field(
        "geographic", description="Coordinate reference system ('geographic' or 'planar')"
    )


class ProblemResponse(BaseModel):
    """Metadata summary of a stored problem instance."""

    id: str
    depot: Depot
    num_customers: int
    num_vehicles: int
    bbox: dict[str, float] = Field(
        ...,
        description="Spatial bounding box {'min_lat': ..., 'min_lon': ..., 'max_lat': ..., 'max_lon': ...}",
    )
    created_at: float = Field(default_factory=time.time)


class SolveRequest(BaseModel):
    """Parameters for launching a classical QPSO solve job."""

    num_particles: int = Field(25, ge=5, le=200, description="Swarm size")
    max_iters: int = Field(60, ge=5, le=1000, description="Maximum iterations")
    cost_oracle_type: Literal["euclidean", "valhalla"] = Field(
        "euclidean", description="Routing oracle engine"
    )
    valhalla_url: str = Field("http://localhost:8002", description="Valhalla service URL")
    coordinate_system: Literal["geographic", "planar"] | None = Field(
        None, description="Coordinate system override ('geographic' or 'planar')"
    )
    seed: int = Field(42, description="Random seed for reproducibility")
    apply_tw_repair: bool = Field(True, description="Run time-window repair operator")
    enforce_priority_early_service: bool = Field(
        False, description="Enforce earlier service for priority customers"
    )


class SolveJob(BaseModel):
    """Internal and external state of an asynchronous optimization job."""

    job_id: str
    problem_id: str
    status: Literal["pending", "running", "completed", "failed"] = "pending"
    progress_iteration: int = 0
    max_iters: int = 0
    current_cost: float = 0.0
    error_message: str | None = None
    created_at: float = Field(default_factory=time.time)
    completed_at: float | None = None
    execution_time_sec: float = 0.0


class SolutionResponse(BaseModel):
    """Detailed routing solution with ETAs and MapLibre-ready GeoJSON features."""

    problem_id: str
    total_cost: float
    is_feasible: bool
    violations: dict[str, list[str]] = Field(default_factory=dict)
    num_routes: int
    routes: list[Route]
    geojson: dict[str, Any] = Field(
        ..., description="GeoJSON FeatureCollection containing Route LineStrings and Stop Points"
    )
    algorithm: str = "QPSO"
    execution_time_sec: float = 0.0


class DynamicEventCreate(BaseModel):
    """Payload for injecting a dynamic traffic incident into an active problem."""

    event_id: str | None = Field(None, description="Optional custom incident ID")
    event_type: Literal["edge_congestion", "road_blockage", "vehicle_delay"] = Field(
        ..., description="Type of dynamic event"
    )
    trigger_time: float = Field(0.0, ge=0.0, description="Simulation timestamp when event occurs")
    duration: float = Field(1800.0, gt=0.0, description="Incident duration in seconds")
    origin_id: str | None = Field(None, description="Corridor start stop ID")
    dest_id: str | None = Field(None, description="Corridor destination stop ID")
    edge_set: list[tuple[str, str]] | None = Field(
        None, description="Optional multi-edge blockage set"
    )
    multiplier: float = Field(2.0, ge=1.0, description="Slowdown multiplier for edge congestion")
    vehicle_id: str | None = Field(None, description="Target vehicle ID for vehicle delay")
    delay_seconds: float = Field(120.0, ge=0.0, description="Delay duration for vehicle delay")
    stop_id: str | None = Field(None, description="Stop location where vehicle delay occurs")
    description: str = Field("", description="Human-readable event description")

    def to_domain_event(self) -> EdgeCongestion | RoadBlockage | VehicleDelay:
        """Convert into a typed dynamic domain event."""
        evt_id = self.event_id or f"evt_{self.event_type}_{int(time.time() * 1000)}"

        if self.event_type == "edge_congestion":
            return EdgeCongestion(
                event_id=evt_id,
                trigger_time=self.trigger_time,
                duration=self.duration,
                origin_id=self.origin_id,
                dest_id=self.dest_id,
                edge_set=self.edge_set,
                multiplier=self.multiplier,
                description=self.description or f"Edge Congestion ({self.multiplier}x)",
            )
        elif self.event_type == "road_blockage":
            return RoadBlockage(
                event_id=evt_id,
                trigger_time=self.trigger_time,
                duration=self.duration,
                origin_id=self.origin_id,
                dest_id=self.dest_id,
                edge_set=self.edge_set,
                description=self.description or "Road Blockage",
            )
        elif self.event_type == "vehicle_delay":
            return VehicleDelay(
                event_id=evt_id,
                trigger_time=self.trigger_time,
                vehicle_id=self.vehicle_id or "V_1",
                delay_seconds=self.delay_seconds,
                stop_id=self.stop_id,
                description=self.description or f"Vehicle Delay ({self.delay_seconds}s)",
            )
        raise ValueError(f"Unsupported event_type: {self.event_type}")


class EventInjectResponse(BaseModel):
    """Outcome of tiered re-optimization triggered by an injected event."""

    event_id: str
    tier_selected: str
    wall_clock_time_ms: float
    cost_before: float
    cost_after: float
    delta_f: float
    stability_penalty: float
    stops_changed_count: int
    stops_changed: list[str] = Field(default_factory=list)
    is_feasible: bool
    message: str
    solution: SolutionResponse


class HealthResponse(BaseModel):
    """Operational health telemetry response."""

    status: str = "healthy"
    version: str
    uptime_seconds: float
    valhalla_connected: bool
    active_problems_count: int
    active_ws_connections: int


=== FILE: api/README.md ===
# Route.it Backend API & WebSocket Service

High-performance REST API and real-time WebSocket service bridging the classical Quantum-behaved Particle Swarm Optimization (QPSO) engine, the Valhalla road routing engine, the dynamic traffic event system, and the React + MapLibre GL frontend dashboard.

---

## 1. Interactive OpenAPI Documentation

When the API service is running locally, interactive API documentation is auto-generated at:
- **Swagger UI (Interactive API Explorer)**: [http://localhost:8000/docs](http://localhost:8000/docs)
- **ReDoc (API Specification Reference)**: [http://localhost:8000/redoc](http://localhost:8000/redoc)
- **OpenAPI Schema (JSON)**: [http://localhost:8000/openapi.json](http://localhost:8000/openapi.json)

---

## 2. Quickstart

### Starting the API Server
```bash
uvicorn api.main:app --host 0.0.0.0 --port 8000 --reload
```
The server will start on port `8000` with hot-reloading enabled.

> [!IMPORTANT]
> The real-time telemetry WebSocket endpoint (`/ws/problems/{id}`) requires `websockets` (or `uvicorn[standard]`), which is included in `pyproject.toml` dependencies.


---

## 3. API Endpoints Overview

| Method | Endpoint | Description |
| :--- | :--- | :--- |
| `GET` | `/health` | Service uptime, readiness, and connectivity health check. |
| `POST` | `/problems` | Create and store a new CVRPTW problem instance. |
| `GET` | `/problems` | List all stored problem instances with bounding boxes. |
| `GET` | `/problems/{id}` | Retrieve problem specifications and stop coordinates. |
| `DELETE`| `/problems/{id}` | Delete a problem and all associated solutions/jobs. |
| `POST` | `/problems/{id}/solve` | Launch asynchronous QPSO optimization job. |
| `GET` | `/problems/{id}/jobs/{job_id}` | Query status and progress of an optimization job. |
| `GET` | `/problems/{id}/solution` | Fetch active solution, ETAs, and MapLibre GeoJSON. |
| `POST` | `/problems/{id}/events` | Inject traffic incident and trigger tiered re-optimizer. |
| `GET` | `/problems/{id}/events` | Retrieve chronological audit log of all re-optimizations. |
| `WS` | `/ws/problems/{id}` | Real-time WebSocket stream for solve progress and re-optimization. |

---

## 4. End-to-End Workflow & cURL Examples

### 4.1 Health Check
```bash
curl -X GET "http://localhost:8000/health"
```

### 4.2 Create a Problem Instance
```bash
curl -X POST "http://localhost:8000/problems" \
  -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
  -d '{
    "id": "vadodara_demo",
    "depot": {
      "id": "depot",
      "lat": 22.3072,
      "lon": 73.1812,
      "ready_time": 0.0,
      "due_time": 36000.0,
      "service_time": 0.0
    },
    "customers": [
      {
        "id": "cust_1",
        "lat": 22.3120,
        "lon": 73.1750,
        "demand": 15.0,
        "ready_time": 600.0,
        "due_time": 3600.0,
        "service_time": 180.0,
        "priority": 2
      },
      {
        "id": "cust_2",
        "lat": 22.2980,
        "lon": 73.1920,
        "demand": 20.0,
        "ready_time": 1200.0,
        "due_time": 4800.0,
        "service_time": 240.0,
        "priority": 1
      }
    ]
  }'
```

### 4.3 Trigger Asynchronous Solving
```bash
curl -X POST "http://localhost:8000/problems/vadodara_demo/solve" \
  -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
  -d '{
    "num_particles": 25,
    "max_iters": 50,
    "cost_oracle_type": "euclidean",
    "apply_tw_repair": true,
    "seed": 42
  }'
```
Response:
```json
{
  "job_id": "job_a1b2c3d4",
  "problem_id": "vadodara_demo",
  "status": "pending",
  "progress_iteration": 0,
  "max_iters": 50,
  "current_cost": 0.0,
  "created_at": 1712180000.0
}
```

### 4.4 Retrieve Solution & MapLibre GeoJSON
```bash
curl -X GET "http://localhost:8000/problems/vadodara_demo/solution"
```
Response contains:
- `routes`: Vehicle routes, stop sequences, arrival times, durations.
- `geojson`: RFC 7946 GeoJSON `FeatureCollection` with styled LineStrings and Point markers ready for MapLibre GL `map.addSource('routes', ...)` rendering.

### 4.5 Inject Dynamic Traffic Event
```bash
curl -X POST "http://localhost:8000/problems/vadodara_demo/events" \
  -H "Content-Type: application/json" \
  -d '{
    "event_type": "edge_congestion",
    "trigger_time": 300.0,
    "duration": 1800.0,
    "origin_id": "depot",
    "dest_id": "cust_1",
    "multiplier": 2.5,
    "description": "Heavy traffic jam on RC Dutt Road"
  }'
```
Response:
```json
{
  "event_id": "evt_edge_congestion_1712180000",
  "tier_selected": "TIER_1_PATH_REPAIR",
  "wall_clock_time_ms": 1.84,
  "cost_before": 1240.5,
  "cost_after": 1310.2,
  "delta_f": 69.7,
  "stability_penalty": 0.0,
  "stops_changed_count": 0,
  "stops_changed": [],
  "is_feasible": true,
  "message": "Accepted Tier 1 Path Repair (preserved stop sequence)",
  "solution": { ... }
}
```

---

## 5. WebSocket Telemetry Protocol (`/ws/problems/{id}`)

Connect to `ws://localhost:8000/ws/problems/{id}` to receive real-time streams:

### 1. Solve Progress Broadcast
```json
{
  "type": "solve_progress",
  "problem_id": "vadodara_demo",
  "job_id": "job_a1b2c3d4",
  "iteration": 35,
  "max_iters": 50,
  "gbest_cost": 1240.5,
  "elapsed_ms": 182.4
}
```

### 2. Solve Completed Broadcast
```json
{
  "type": "solve_completed",
  "problem_id": "vadodara_demo",
  "job_id": "job_a1b2c3d4",
  "solution_cost": 1240.5,
  "is_feasible": true,
  "routes_count": 2,
  "execution_time_sec": 0.245
}
```

### 3. Dynamic Re-optimization Broadcast
```json
{
  "type": "reoptimization_update",
  "problem_id": "vadodara_demo",
  "event_id": "evt_road_block_01",
  "tier_selected": "TIER_2_LOCAL_REPAIR",
  "wall_clock_time_ms": 48.2,
  "cost_before": 1240.5,
  "cost_after": 1285.0,
  "delta_f": 44.5,
  "stability_penalty": 100.0,
  "stops_changed_count": 1,
  "stops_changed": ["cust_2"],
  "is_feasible": true,
  "message": "Accepted Tier 2 Local Repair (relocate/swap on affected routes)"
}
```

"""API route modules package."""


=== FILE: api/routes/events.py ===
"""Dynamic traffic incident injection and re-optimization endpoints."""

from __future__ import annotations

import logging
from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, HTTPException, status

from api.geojson import solution_to_geojson
from api.models import (
    DynamicEventCreate,
    EventInjectResponse,
    SolutionResponse,
)
from api.store import ProblemStore, get_store
from api.websocket import manager
from dynamic.reoptimizer import (
    DynamicReoptimizer,
    ReoptimizationRecord,
    ReoptimizerConfig,
)
from routing.cost_oracle import (
    EuclideanOracle,
    SimulatedEventAdapter,
    TimeOfDayProfileAdapter,
)

router = APIRouter(prefix="/problems", tags=["Dynamic Events & Re-optimization"])
logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


@router.post("/{problem_id}/events", response_model=EventInjectResponse, summary="Inject Traffic Event & Trigger Re-optimizer")
def inject_event(
    problem_id: str,
    event_req: DynamicEventCreate,
    store: ProblemStore = Depends(get_store),
) -> EventInjectResponse:
    """Inject a dynamic traffic incident (congestion, blockage, delay) into an active problem.

    Executes tiered dynamic re-optimization:
    - Tier 1: Path Repair (preserves stop sequence, re-computes timeline)
    - Tier 2: Local Repair (relocate/swap affected uncommitted stops)
    - Tier 3: QPSO Warm Start (swarm re-seeded around previous keys)
    Broadcasts real-time telemetry to all subscribed WebSocket clients.
    """
    problem = store.get_problem(problem_id)
    if problem is None:
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=status.HTTP_404_NOT_FOUND,
            detail=f"Problem '{problem_id}' not found",
        )

    current_solution = store.get_solution(problem_id)
    if current_solution is None:
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST,
            detail=f"Problem '{problem_id}' has not been solved yet. Please trigger /solve before injecting events.",
        )

    # 1. Convert to domain event
    domain_event = event_req.to_domain_event()

    # 2. Retrieve or initialize CostOracle with SimulatedEventAdapter
    oracle = store.get_oracle(problem_id)
    if oracle is None or not isinstance(oracle.traffic_adapter, SimulatedEventAdapter):
        traffic_adapter = SimulatedEventAdapter(
            base_adapter=TimeOfDayProfileAdapter({h: 1.0 for h in range(24)})
        )
        oracle = EuclideanOracle(base_speed_mps=8.333 if problem.coordinate_system == "geographic" else 1.0, coordinate_system=problem.coordinate_system, traffic_adapter=traffic_adapter)
        store.save_oracle(problem_id, oracle)

    # 3. Apply event incidents to traffic adapter
    if hasattr(domain_event, "to_simulated_incidents"):
        for inc in domain_event.to_simulated_incidents():
            oracle.traffic_adapter.add_incident(inc)

    # 4. Execute Tiered Dynamic Re-optimizer
    reopt_config = ReoptimizerConfig(
        theta=0.01,
        theta_absolute=2.0,
        commitment_horizon_seconds=60.0,
        seed=42,
    )
    reoptimizer = DynamicReoptimizer(problem, cost_oracle=oracle, config=reopt_config)

    reopt_solution, record = reoptimizer.reoptimize(
        current_solution=current_solution,
        current_time=event_req.trigger_time,
        event=domain_event,
    )

    # 5. Persist updated solution & record
    store.save_solution(problem_id, reopt_solution)
    store.add_event_record(problem_id, record)

    # 6. Broadcast reoptimization update to WebSocket subscribers
    manager.broadcast_sync(
        problem_id,
        {
            "type": "reoptimization_update",
            "problem_id": problem_id,
            "event_id": record.event_id,
            "tier_selected": record.tier_selected.value,
            "wall_clock_time_ms": record.wall_clock_time_ms,
            "cost_before": record.cost_before,
            "cost_after": record.cost_after,
            "delta_f": record.delta_f,
            "stability_penalty": record.stability_penalty,
            "stops_changed_count": record.stops_changed_count,
            "stops_changed": record.stops_changed,
            "is_feasible": record.is_feasible,
            "message": record.message,
        },
    )

    # 7. Generate updated GeoJSON payload
    geojson_data = solution_to_geojson(
        problem, reopt_solution, cost_oracle=oracle, current_time=event_req.trigger_time
    )
    solution_resp = SolutionResponse(
        problem_id=problem_id,
        total_cost=round(reopt_solution.total_cost, 2),
        is_feasible=reopt_solution.is_feasible,
        violations=reopt_solution.violations,
        num_routes=len(reopt_solution.routes),
        routes=reopt_solution.routes,
        geojson=geojson_data,
        algorithm=reopt_solution.algorithm,
        execution_time_sec=round(reopt_solution.execution_time_sec, 3),
    )

    return EventInjectResponse(
        event_id=record.event_id,
        tier_selected=record.tier_selected.value,
        wall_clock_time_ms=record.wall_clock_time_ms,
        cost_before=record.cost_before,
        cost_after=record.cost_after,
        delta_f=record.delta_f,
        stability_penalty=record.stability_penalty,
        stops_changed_count=record.stops_changed_count,
        stops_changed=record.stops_changed,
        is_feasible=record.is_feasible,
        message=record.message,
        solution=solution_resp,
    )


@router.get("/{problem_id}/events", response_model=list[dict], summary="Get Event Audit History")
def get_event_audit_history(
    problem_id: str,
    store: ProblemStore = Depends(get_store),
) -> list[dict]:
    """Retrieve the chronological audit history of all events and re-optimizations for a problem."""
    problem = store.get_problem(problem_id)
    if problem is None:
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=status.HTTP_404_NOT_FOUND,
            detail=f"Problem '{problem_id}' not found",
        )
    records = store.get_event_records(problem_id)
    return [r.model_dump() for r in records]


=== FILE: api/routes/health.py ===
"""Health check and service status endpoints."""

from __future__ import annotations

import time
import urllib.request
from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends

from api import __version__
from api.models import HealthResponse
from api.store import ProblemStore, get_store
from api.websocket import manager

router = APIRouter(tags=["Health"])

START_TIME = time.time()


def _check_valhalla_connection(url: str = "http://localhost:8002/status") -> bool:
    """Non-blocking check of Valhalla Docker container health."""
    try:
        req = urllib.request.Request(url, headers={"User-Agent": "RouteIt-HealthCheck"})
        with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=0.4) as resp:
            return resp.status == 200
    except Exception:
        return False


@router.get("/health", response_model=HealthResponse, summary="System Health & Readiness")
def get_health(store: ProblemStore = Depends(get_store)) -> HealthResponse:
    """Check Route.it API operational health, uptime, and connected services."""
    uptime = time.time() - START_TIME
    valhalla_ok = _check_valhalla_connection()
    problems_count = len(store.list_problems())
    ws_count = manager.get_total_connections_count()

    return HealthResponse(
        status="healthy",
        version=__version__,
        uptime_seconds=round(uptime, 2),
        valhalla_connected=valhalla_ok,
        active_problems_count=problems_count,
        active_ws_connections=ws_count,
    )


=== FILE: api/routes/problems.py ===
"""Problem management, async optimization jobs, and solution query endpoints."""

from __future__ import annotations

import asyncio
from concurrent.futures import ThreadPoolExecutor
import logging
import time
from uuid import uuid4
from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, HTTPException, Query, Response, status

from api.geojson import solution_to_geojson
from api.models import (
    ProblemCreateRequest,
    ProblemResponse,
    SolutionResponse,
    SolveJob,
    SolveRequest,
)
from api.store import ProblemStore, get_store
from api.websocket import manager
from routing.cost_oracle import (
    CostOracle,
    EuclideanOracle,
    SimulatedEventAdapter,
    TimeOfDayProfileAdapter,
    ValhallaOracle,
)
from solver.models import Customer, Depot, Problem, Solution, Vehicle
from solver.qpso import QPSOConfig, solve_qpso

router = APIRouter(prefix="/problems", tags=["Problems & Solver"])
logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

# Dedicated thread pool for non-blocking execution of CPU-bound QPSO solver runs
_solver_executor = ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=4, thread_name_prefix="qpso_worker")


def _compute_bbox(depot: Depot, customers: list[Customer]) -> dict[str, float]:
    """Compute spatial bounding box across depot and customer stops."""
    lats = [depot.lat] + [c.lat for c in customers]
    lons = [depot.lon] + [c.lon for c in customers]
    return {
        "min_lat": min(lats),
        "min_lon": min(lons),
        "max_lat": max(lats),
        "max_lon": max(lons),
    }


def _run_solve_worker(
    problem: Problem,
    job: SolveJob,
    req: SolveRequest,
    store: ProblemStore,
) -> None:
    """Worker function executed in thread pool for asynchronous solving."""
    job.status = "running"
    store.save_job(job)
    t_start = time.perf_counter()

    # Broadcast job start
    manager.broadcast_sync(
        problem.id,
        {
            "type": "solve_started",
            "problem_id": problem.id,
            "job_id": job.job_id,
            "max_iters": req.max_iters,
            "num_particles": req.num_particles,
        },
    )

    try:
        # 1. Initialize CostOracle & TrafficAdapter
        traffic_adapter = SimulatedEventAdapter(
            base_adapter=TimeOfDayProfileAdapter({h: 1.0 for h in range(24)})
        )
        oracle: CostOracle
        if req.cost_oracle_type == "valhalla":
            oracle = ValhallaOracle(
                base_url=req.valhalla_url,
                traffic_adapter=traffic_adapter,
            )
        else:
            coord_sys = req.coordinate_system or problem.coordinate_system
            oracle = EuclideanOracle(
                base_speed_mps=8.333 if coord_sys == "geographic" else 1.0,
                coordinate_system=coord_sys,
                traffic_adapter=traffic_adapter,
            )
        store.save_oracle(problem.id, oracle)

        # 2. Setup Solver Configuration
        qpso_cfg = QPSOConfig(
            num_particles=req.num_particles,
            max_iters=req.max_iters,
            seed=req.seed,
            apply_tw_repair=req.apply_tw_repair,
            enforce_priority_early_service=req.enforce_priority_early_service,
        )

        # 3. Progress callback streaming to WebSocket subscribers
        def on_step(iter_num: int, best_cost: float) -> None:
            job.progress_iteration = iter_num
            job.current_cost = round(best_cost, 2)
            store.save_job(job)

            manager.broadcast_sync(
                problem.id,
                {
                    "type": "solve_progress",
                    "problem_id": problem.id,
                    "job_id": job.job_id,
                    "iteration": iter_num,
                    "max_iters": req.max_iters,
                    "gbest_cost": round(best_cost, 2),
                    "elapsed_ms": round((time.perf_counter() - t_start) * 1000.0, 1),
                },
            )

        # 4. Execute Optimization
        solution, _ = solve_qpso(
            problem,
            config=qpso_cfg,
            cost_oracle=oracle,
            callback=on_step,
        )

        # 5. Save Solution & Update Job
        job.status = "completed"
        job.completed_at = time.time()
        job.execution_time_sec = round(time.perf_counter() - t_start, 3)
        job.current_cost = round(solution.total_cost, 2)
        store.save_job(job)
        store.save_solution(problem.id, solution)

        # 6. Broadcast completion telemetry
        manager.broadcast_sync(
            problem.id,
            {
                "type": "solve_completed",
                "problem_id": problem.id,
                "job_id": job.job_id,
                "solution_cost": round(solution.total_cost, 2),
                "is_feasible": solution.is_feasible,
                "routes_count": len(solution.routes),
                "execution_time_sec": job.execution_time_sec,
            },
        )

    except Exception as exc:
        logger.exception(f"[SOLVER] Job {job.job_id} on problem {problem.id} failed: {exc}")
        job.status = "failed"
        job.error_message = str(exc)
        job.completed_at = time.time()
        job.execution_time_sec = round(time.perf_counter() - t_start, 3)
        store.save_job(job)

        manager.broadcast_sync(
            problem.id,
            {
                "type": "solve_failed",
                "problem_id": problem.id,
                "job_id": job.job_id,
                "error": str(exc),
            },
        )


@router.post("", response_model=ProblemResponse, status_code=status.HTTP_201_CREATED, summary="Create Problem Instance")
def create_problem(
    req: ProblemCreateRequest,
    store: ProblemStore = Depends(get_store),
) -> ProblemResponse:
    """Register a new CVRPTW problem instance with depot and customer stops."""
    prob_id = req.id or f"prob_{uuid4().hex[:8]}"

    if store.get_problem(prob_id) is not None:
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=status.HTTP_409_CONFLICT,
            detail=f"Problem with ID '{prob_id}' already exists",
        )

    # Supply default vehicle fleet if none provided
    vehicles = req.vehicles
    if not vehicles:
        default_capacity = sum(c.demand for c in req.customers) * 0.5
        vehicles = [
            Vehicle(
                id=f"V_{i+1}",
                capacity=max(50.0, default_capacity),
                shift_start=req.depot.ready_time,
                shift_end=req.depot.due_time,
            )
            for i in range(max(3, len(req.customers) // 3))
        ]

    problem = Problem(
        id=prob_id,
        depot=req.depot,
        customers=req.customers,
        vehicles=vehicles,
        coordinate_system=req.coordinate_system,
    )
    problem.compute_euclidean_matrices(speed=8.333 if req.coordinate_system == "geographic" else 1.0)
    store.save_problem(problem)

    bbox = _compute_bbox(req.depot, req.customers)
    return ProblemResponse(
        id=prob_id,
        depot=req.depot,
        num_customers=problem.num_customers,
        num_vehicles=problem.num_vehicles,
        bbox=bbox,
    )


@router.get("", response_model=list[ProblemResponse], summary="List Problems")
def list_problems(store: ProblemStore = Depends(get_store)) -> list[ProblemResponse]:
    """Retrieve metadata list of all stored problem instances."""
    results: list[ProblemResponse] = []
    for p in store.list_problems():
        bbox = _compute_bbox(p.depot, p.customers)
        results.append(
            ProblemResponse(
                id=p.id,
                depot=p.depot,
                num_customers=p.num_customers,
                num_vehicles=p.num_vehicles,
                bbox=bbox,
            )
        )
    return results


@router.get("/{problem_id}", response_model=Problem, summary="Get Problem Specification")
def get_problem(problem_id: str, store: ProblemStore = Depends(get_store)) -> Problem:
    """Retrieve full CVRPTW problem definition by ID."""
    problem = store.get_problem(problem_id)
    if problem is None:
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=status.HTTP_404_NOT_FOUND,
            detail=f"Problem '{problem_id}' not found",
        )
    return problem


@router.delete("/{problem_id}", status_code=status.HTTP_204_NO_CONTENT, response_class=Response, summary="Delete Problem")
def delete_problem(problem_id: str, store: ProblemStore = Depends(get_store)) -> Response:
    """Delete a problem instance and all its associated solutions and jobs."""
    deleted = store.delete_problem(problem_id)
    if not deleted:
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=status.HTTP_404_NOT_FOUND,
            detail=f"Problem '{problem_id}' not found",
        )
    return Response(status_code=status.HTTP_204_NO_CONTENT)


@router.post("/{problem_id}/solve", response_model=SolveJob, status_code=status.HTTP_202_ACCEPTED, summary="Launch Optimization Job")
def solve_problem(
    problem_id: str,
    req: SolveRequest = SolveRequest(),
    store: ProblemStore = Depends(get_store),
) -> SolveJob:
    """Launch classical QPSO optimization asynchronously and stream progress to WebSockets."""
    problem = store.get_problem(problem_id)
    if problem is None:
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=status.HTTP_404_NOT_FOUND,
            detail=f"Problem '{problem_id}' not found",
        )

    job_id = f"job_{uuid4().hex[:8]}"
    job = SolveJob(
        job_id=job_id,
        problem_id=problem_id,
        status="pending",
        max_iters=req.max_iters,
    )
    store.save_job(job)

    # Launch non-blocking worker in solver thread pool
    _solver_executor.submit(_run_solve_worker, problem, job, req, store)

    return job


@router.get("/{problem_id}/jobs/{job_id}", response_model=SolveJob, summary="Get Job Status")
def get_job_status(
    problem_id: str,
    job_id: str,
    store: ProblemStore = Depends(get_store),
) -> SolveJob:
    """Query current status and iteration progress of an active or finished solve job."""
    job = store.get_job(job_id)
    if job is None or job.problem_id != problem_id:
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=status.HTTP_404_NOT_FOUND,
            detail=f"Job '{job_id}' for problem '{problem_id}' not found",
        )
    return job


@router.get("/{problem_id}/solution", response_model=SolutionResponse, summary="Get Active Fleet Solution")
def get_solution(
    problem_id: str,
    sim_time: float = Query(0.0, description="Simulation timestamp for visited stop flagging"),
    store: ProblemStore = Depends(get_store),
) -> SolutionResponse:
    """Retrieve active routing solution, visit ETAs, and GeoJSON features for MapLibre rendering."""
    problem = store.get_problem(problem_id)
    if problem is None:
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=status.HTTP_404_NOT_FOUND,
            detail=f"Problem '{problem_id}' not found",
        )

    solution = store.get_solution(problem_id)
    if solution is None:
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=status.HTTP_404_NOT_FOUND,
            detail=f"No solution available for problem '{problem_id}'. Please trigger /solve first.",
        )

    oracle = store.get_oracle(problem_id)
    geojson_data = solution_to_geojson(problem, solution, cost_oracle=oracle, current_time=sim_time)

    return SolutionResponse(
        problem_id=problem_id,
        total_cost=round(solution.total_cost, 2),
        is_feasible=solution.is_feasible,
        violations=solution.violations,
        num_routes=len(solution.routes),
        routes=solution.routes,
        geojson=geojson_data,
        algorithm=solution.algorithm,
        execution_time_sec=round(solution.execution_time_sec, 3),
    )


=== FILE: api/routes/ws.py ===
"""WebSocket endpoint for real-time telemetry and dynamic re-optimization streaming."""

from __future__ import annotations

import logging
from fastapi import APIRouter, WebSocket, WebSocketDisconnect

from api.websocket import manager

router = APIRouter(tags=["WebSocket Stream"])
logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


@router.websocket("/ws/problems/{problem_id}")
async def websocket_problem_stream(websocket: WebSocket, problem_id: str) -> None:
    """Stream live solver iteration progress and real-time re-optimization telemetry.

    Clients connect to `/ws/problems/{problem_id}` and receive:
    - `solve_progress`: iteration, gbest_cost, elapsed_ms
    - `solve_completed`: final cost, feasibility, routes count
    - `reoptimization_update`: tier used, wall-clock time, cost delta, changed stops
    """
    await manager.connect(problem_id, websocket)

    # Handshake welcome packet
    await websocket.send_json(
        {
            "type": "connection_established",
            "problem_id": problem_id,
            "message": "Connected to Route.it live telemetry stream",
        }
    )

    try:
        while True:
            # Keepalive / ping-pong handling from client
            data = await websocket.receive_text()
            if data.lower() == "ping":
                await websocket.send_json({"type": "pong"})
    except WebSocketDisconnect:
        manager.disconnect(problem_id, websocket)
        logger.debug(f"[WS] Disconnected: problem={problem_id}")
    except Exception as exc:
        manager.disconnect(problem_id, websocket)
        logger.warning(f"[WS] Connection closed with error: {exc}")


=== FILE: api/store.py ===
"""Storage layer interface and thread-safe in-memory implementation for Route.it API.

Enables full decoupling of API handlers from storage persistence.
Swappable to SQLite / persistent database without altering route logic.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from abc import ABC, abstractmethod
import threading
from typing import TYPE_CHECKING

from api.models import SolveJob
from solver.models import Problem, Solution

if TYPE_CHECKING:
    from dynamic.reoptimizer import ReoptimizationRecord
    from routing.cost_oracle import CostOracle


class ProblemStore(ABC):
    """Abstract interface defining required storage operations for Route.it."""

    @abstractmethod
    def save_problem(self, problem: Problem) -> None:
        """Store or update a problem instance."""
        ...

    @abstractmethod
    def get_problem(self, problem_id: str) -> Problem | None:
        """Retrieve a problem instance by ID."""
        ...

    @abstractmethod
    def list_problems(self) -> list[Problem]:
        """List all stored problem instances."""
        ...

    @abstractmethod
    def delete_problem(self, problem_id: str) -> bool:
        """Delete a problem instance and its associated solutions/jobs."""
        ...

    @abstractmethod
    def save_solution(self, problem_id: str, solution: Solution) -> None:
        """Store or update the active fleet solution for a problem."""
        ...

    @abstractmethod
    def get_solution(self, problem_id: str) -> Solution | None:
        """Retrieve the active fleet solution for a problem."""
        ...

    @abstractmethod
    def save_job(self, job: SolveJob) -> None:
        """Store or update optimization job status."""
        ...

    @abstractmethod
    def get_job(self, job_id: str) -> SolveJob | None:
        """Retrieve job by its unique identifier."""
        ...

    @abstractmethod
    def add_event_record(self, problem_id: str, record: ReoptimizationRecord) -> None:
        """Append an audit record from a re-optimization event."""
        ...

    @abstractmethod
    def get_event_records(self, problem_id: str) -> list[ReoptimizationRecord]:
        """Retrieve the chronological audit history for a problem."""
        ...

    @abstractmethod
    def save_oracle(self, problem_id: str, oracle: CostOracle) -> None:
        """Cache the active cost oracle instance associated with a problem."""
        ...

    @abstractmethod
    def get_oracle(self, problem_id: str) -> CostOracle | None:
        """Retrieve the cached cost oracle instance associated with a problem."""
        ...


class InMemoryProblemStore(ProblemStore):
    """Thread-safe in-memory implementation of ProblemStore."""

    def __init__(self) -> None:
        self._lock = threading.Lock()
        self._problems: dict[str, Problem] = {}
        self._solutions: dict[str, Solution] = {}
        self._jobs: dict[str, SolveJob] = {}
        self._event_records: dict[str, list[ReoptimizationRecord]] = {}
        self._oracles: dict[str, CostOracle] = {}

    def save_problem(self, problem: Problem) -> None:
        with self._lock:
            self._problems[problem.id] = problem

    def get_problem(self, problem_id: str) -> Problem | None:
        with self._lock:
            return self._problems.get(problem_id)

    def list_problems(self) -> list[Problem]:
        with self._lock:
            return list(self._problems.values())

    def delete_problem(self, problem_id: str) -> bool:
        with self._lock:
            if problem_id not in self._problems:
                return False
            del self._problems[problem_id]
            self._solutions.pop(problem_id, None)
            self._event_records.pop(problem_id, None)
            self._oracles.pop(problem_id, None)
            # Remove associated jobs
            job_ids_to_del = [j_id for j_id, job in self._jobs.items() if job.problem_id == problem_id]
            for j_id in job_ids_to_del:
                del self._jobs[j_id]
            return True

    def save_solution(self, problem_id: str, solution: Solution) -> None:
        with self._lock:
            self._solutions[problem_id] = solution

    def get_solution(self, problem_id: str) -> Solution | None:
        with self._lock:
            return self._solutions.get(problem_id)

    def save_job(self, job: SolveJob) -> None:
        with self._lock:
            self._jobs[job.job_id] = job

    def get_job(self, job_id: str) -> SolveJob | None:
        with self._lock:
            return self._jobs.get(job_id)

    def add_event_record(self, problem_id: str, record: ReoptimizationRecord) -> None:
        with self._lock:
            self._event_records.setdefault(problem_id, []).append(record)

    def get_event_records(self, problem_id: str) -> list[ReoptimizationRecord]:
        with self._lock:
            return list(self._event_records.get(problem_id, []))

    def save_oracle(self, problem_id: str, oracle: CostOracle) -> None:
        with self._lock:
            self._oracles[problem_id] = oracle

    def get_oracle(self, problem_id: str) -> CostOracle | None:
        with self._lock:
            return self._oracles.get(problem_id)

    def clear(self) -> None:
        """Clear all stored state (primarily for test resets)."""
        with self._lock:
            self._problems.clear()
            self._solutions.clear()
            self._jobs.clear()
            self._event_records.clear()
            self._oracles.clear()


# Global store instance for FastAPI dependency injection
_global_store: ProblemStore = InMemoryProblemStore()


def get_store() -> ProblemStore:
    """FastAPI dependency for accessing the active ProblemStore."""
    return _global_store


def set_store(store: ProblemStore) -> None:
    """Override active store (e.g. for testing or SQLite backend swap)."""
    global _global_store
    _global_store = store


=== FILE: api/websocket.py ===
"""Real-time WebSocket connection manager for Route.it API.

Maintains subscriber pools per problem_id and broadcasts live solver telemetry
(iterations, best cost) and dynamic re-optimization updates.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import asyncio
import logging
from typing import Any
from fastapi import WebSocket

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


class ConnectionManager:
    """Manages active WebSocket connections grouped by problem_id."""

    def __init__(self) -> None:
        self._connections: dict[str, list[WebSocket]] = {}
        self._loop: asyncio.AbstractEventLoop | None = None

    def set_event_loop(self, loop: asyncio.AbstractEventLoop) -> None:
        """Capture the running FastAPI event loop for sync-to-async scheduling."""
        self._loop = loop

    async def connect(self, problem_id: str, websocket: WebSocket) -> None:
        """Accept incoming connection and subscribe to problem updates."""
        if self._loop is None or not self._loop.is_running():
            self._loop = asyncio.get_running_loop()
        await websocket.accept()
        self._connections.setdefault(problem_id, []).append(websocket)
        logger.debug(f"[WS] Client connected to problem {problem_id} (active: {len(self._connections[problem_id])})")

    def disconnect(self, problem_id: str, websocket: WebSocket) -> None:
        """Unsubscribe connection from problem updates."""
        if problem_id in self._connections:
            if websocket in self._connections[problem_id]:
                self._connections[problem_id].remove(websocket)
            if not self._connections[problem_id]:
                del self._connections[problem_id]
        logger.debug(f"[WS] Client disconnected from problem {problem_id}")

    async def broadcast(self, problem_id: str, message: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
        """Broadcast JSON payload to all active clients subscribed to problem_id."""
        subscribers = list(self._connections.get(problem_id, []))
        if not subscribers:
            return

        dead_connections: list[WebSocket] = []
        for ws in subscribers:
            try:
                await ws.send_json(message)
            except Exception as e:
                logger.warning(f"[WS] Failed sending to client on problem {problem_id}: {e}")
                dead_connections.append(ws)

        for ws in dead_connections:
            self.disconnect(problem_id, ws)

    def broadcast_sync(self, problem_id: str, message: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
        """Thread-safe synchronization broadcast helper for background workers."""
        if self._loop is not None and self._loop.is_running():
            asyncio.run_coroutine_threadsafe(self.broadcast(problem_id, message), self._loop)
        else:
            try:
                loop = asyncio.get_running_loop()
                asyncio.run_coroutine_threadsafe(self.broadcast(problem_id, message), loop)
            except RuntimeError:
                pass

    def get_total_connections_count(self) -> int:
        """Return total active WebSocket clients across all problems."""
        return sum(len(conns) for conns in self._connections.values())


# Global manager instance
manager = ConnectionManager()

"""Route.it Benchmarking Suite.
