=== FILE: benchmarks/benchmark_reoptimization.py ===
"""Benchmark runner comparing Tiered Re-optimizer against Full-Restart Baseline.

Evaluates performance across dynamic incidents:
- Mild Edge Congestion (Do Nothing)
- Moderate Corridor Congestion (Tier 1 Path Repair)
- Severe Localized Slowdown (Tier 2 Local Repair)
- Road Blockage (Tier 3 QPSO Warm Start)

Measures exact wall-clock latencies, stability penalties, and solution quality.
Exports genuine measured results to benchmarks/results/reoptimization_benchmark.csv.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import csv
from pathlib import Path
import sys
import time
from typing import Any

WORKSPACE_ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent
if str(WORKSPACE_ROOT) not in sys.path:
    sys.path.insert(0, str(WORKSPACE_ROOT))

from dynamic.events import EdgeCongestion, RoadBlockage, VehicleDelay
from dynamic.reoptimizer import (
    DynamicReoptimizer,
    ReoptimizationTier,
    ReoptimizerConfig,
    calculate_stability_penalty,
    full_restart_baseline,
    partition_stops_by_commitment,
)
from routing.cost_oracle import EuclideanOracle, SimulatedEventAdapter, TimeOfDayProfileAdapter
from solver.io import load_solomon
from solver.models import Customer, Problem
from solver.qpso import QPSOConfig, solve_qpso
from solver.validate import validate_solution


def run_reoptimization_benchmarks(
    instance_name: str = "C101.txt",
    num_customers: int = 25,
    out_csv: Path | str = "benchmarks/results/reoptimization_benchmark.csv",
    seed: int = 42,
) -> int:
    """Run dynamic re-optimization benchmark comparing Tiered Re-optimizer to Full Restart."""
    inst_path = Path("benchmarks/instances") / instance_name
    if not inst_path.is_file():
        print(f"[ERROR] Instance file {inst_path} not found!")
        return 1

    print(f"\n[BENCHMARK] Loading {instance_name} (customers: {num_customers})...")
    raw_problem = load_solomon(inst_path)

    if num_customers < raw_problem.num_customers:
        problem = Problem(
            id=f"{raw_problem.id}_{num_customers}",
            depot=raw_problem.depot,
            customers=raw_problem.customers[:num_customers],
            vehicles=raw_problem.vehicles,
            coordinate_system=raw_problem.coordinate_system,
        )
        problem.compute_euclidean_matrices(speed=1.0)
    else:
        problem = raw_problem

    # Initialize traffic adapter & oracle
    flat_profile = {h: 1.0 for h in range(24)}
    adapter = SimulatedEventAdapter(base_adapter=TimeOfDayProfileAdapter(flat_profile))
    oracle = EuclideanOracle(base_speed_mps=1.0, use_haversine=False, traffic_adapter=adapter)

    # Initial solution
    print("  -> Solving initial static plan with QPSO...")
    init_config = QPSOConfig(num_particles=25, max_iters=40, seed=seed, apply_tw_repair=True)
    initial_solution, _ = solve_qpso(problem, config=init_config, cost_oracle=oracle)
    print(f"  -> Initial Plan: {len(initial_solution.routes)} routes, Dist: {initial_solution.total_cost:.2f}")

    reopt_config = ReoptimizerConfig(
        theta=0.001,
        theta_absolute=2.0,
        commitment_horizon_seconds=0.0,
        tier3_max_iters=30,
        tier3_particles=25,
        seed=seed,
    )
    reoptimizer = DynamicReoptimizer(problem, cost_oracle=oracle, config=reopt_config)

    # Target the primary multi-stop route in the initial plan
    target_r = max(initial_solution.routes, key=lambda r: len(r.stop_ids))
    s0 = target_r.stop_ids[0]
    s1 = target_r.stop_ids[1] if len(target_r.stop_ids) > 1 else s0
    s_last = target_r.stop_ids[-1]

    # Gather multi-route edges to simulate major cluster closure
    multi_routes = [r for r in initial_solution.routes if len(r.stop_ids) >= 2]
    multi_edges: list[tuple[str, str]] = []
    for r in multi_routes[:3]:
        multi_edges.append((r.stop_ids[0], r.stop_ids[1]))
        if len(r.stop_ids) >= 3:
            multi_edges.append((r.stop_ids[1], r.stop_ids[2]))

    test_events = [
        (
            "Mild Congestion (Delta F <= Theta)",
            EdgeCongestion(
                event_id="evt_mild_cong",
                trigger_time=0.0,
                duration=500.0,
                origin_id=problem.depot.id,
                dest_id=s0,
                multiplier=1.005,  # 0.5% slowdown -> within tolerance
                description="Minor traffic delay on depot exit",
            ),
            0.0,
        ),
        (
            "Corridor Congestion (Tier 1 Path Repair)",
            EdgeCongestion(
                event_id="evt_mod_cong",
                trigger_time=0.0,
                duration=2000.0,
                origin_id=s_last,
                dest_id=problem.depot.id,
                multiplier=1.8,  # 1.8x travel time slowdown on return corridor
                description="Moderate bottleneck on depot return corridor",
            ),
            0.0,
        ),
        (
            "Localized Edge Blockage (Tier 2 Local Repair)",
            RoadBlockage(
                event_id="evt_road_block",
                trigger_time=0.0,
                duration=2000.0,
                origin_id=s0,
                dest_id=s1,
                description="Localized obstruction between consecutive stops",
            ),
            0.0,
        ),
        (
            "Multi-Route Closure (Tier 3 Warm Start)",
            RoadBlockage(
                event_id="evt_corridor_closure",
                trigger_time=0.0,
                duration=2000.0,
                edge_set=multi_edges,
                description="Multi-corridor cluster closure requiring global warm start",
            ),
            0.0,
        ),
    ]

    current_plan = initial_solution
    csv_rows: list[dict[str, Any]] = []

    print("\n" + "=" * 105)
    print(f"{'Event Description':<32} {'Tier':<22} {'T_reopt (ms)':<14} {'T_restart (ms)':<15} {'Speedup':<10} {'Feas':<6}")
    print("=" * 105)

    for desc, event, current_sim_time in test_events:
        # Setup scenario-specific traffic adapter & oracle
        adapter = SimulatedEventAdapter(base_adapter=TimeOfDayProfileAdapter(flat_profile))
        oracle = EuclideanOracle(base_speed_mps=1.0, use_haversine=False, traffic_adapter=adapter)
        reoptimizer = DynamicReoptimizer(problem, cost_oracle=oracle, config=reopt_config)

        # Apply scenario event to traffic adapter
        if hasattr(event, "to_simulated_incidents"):
            for inc in event.to_simulated_incidents():
                adapter.add_incident(inc)

        # 1. Run Tiered Re-optimizer
        reopt_sol, record = reoptimizer.reoptimize(initial_solution, current_time=current_sim_time, event=event)

        # 2. Run Full-Restart Baseline
        restart_sol, restart_ms = full_restart_baseline(
            problem=problem,
            current_solution=initial_solution,
            cost_oracle=oracle,
            current_time=current_sim_time,
            commitment_horizon=reopt_config.commitment_horizon_seconds,
            seed=seed,
            max_iters=100,
            particles=30,
        )

        # Calculate stability penalty of restart vs initial plan
        all_unc: set[str] = set()
        for r in initial_solution.routes:
            _, unc = partition_stops_by_commitment(r, current_sim_time, reopt_config.commitment_horizon_seconds, problem)
            all_unc.update(unc)

        restart_stab, restart_changed = calculate_stability_penalty(initial_solution, restart_sol, all_unc, reopt_config)
        restart_val = validate_solution(problem, restart_sol, cost_oracle=oracle)

        speedup = restart_ms / max(0.001, record.wall_clock_time_ms)
        feas_str = "YES" if record.is_feasible else "NO"

        print(
            f"{desc:<32} {record.tier_selected.value:<22} {record.wall_clock_time_ms:>10.2f} ms "
            f"{restart_ms:>11.2f} ms {speedup:>8.1f}x {feas_str:<6}"
        )

        csv_rows.append(
            {
                "event_id": event.event_id,
                "event_description": desc,
                "timestamp": current_sim_time,
                "tiered_tier": record.tier_selected.value,
                "tiered_latency_ms": record.wall_clock_time_ms,
                "tiered_cost_after": record.cost_after,
                "tiered_delta_f": record.delta_f,
                "tiered_stability_penalty": record.stability_penalty,
                "tiered_stops_changed": record.stops_changed_count,
                "tiered_feasible": record.is_feasible,
                "restart_latency_ms": round(restart_ms, 2),
                "restart_cost_after": round(restart_sol.total_cost, 2),
                "restart_stability_penalty": round(restart_stab, 2),
                "restart_stops_changed": len(restart_changed),
                "restart_feasible": restart_val.is_valid,
                "speedup_factor": round(speedup, 2),
            }
        )

    # Write CSV
    out_file = Path(out_csv)
    out_file.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    with out_file.open("w", newline="", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        writer = csv.DictWriter(f, fieldnames=list(csv_rows[0].keys()))
        writer.writeheader()
        writer.writerows(csv_rows)

    print("-" * 105)
    print(f"[SUCCESS] Genuine re-optimization benchmark results written to: {out_file.resolve()}\n")
    return 0


def main() -> int:
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="Run Dynamic Re-optimizer Benchmark against Full Restart.")
    parser.add_argument("--instance", default="C101.txt", help="Solomon instance file")
    parser.add_argument("--customers", type=int, default=25, help="Customer count (default: 25)")
    parser.add_argument("--out", default="benchmarks/results/reoptimization_benchmark.csv", help="Output CSV path")
    parser.add_argument("--seed", type=int, default=42, help="Random seed")
    args = parser.parse_args()

    return run_reoptimization_benchmarks(
        instance_name=args.instance,
        num_customers=args.customers,
        out_csv=args.out,
        seed=args.seed,
    )


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main())


=== FILE: benchmarks/benchmark_solomon.py ===
"""Solomon CVRPTW Benchmark Runner for Route.it.

Runs the QPSO + time-aware Prins Split + adaptive penalty + repair pipeline on
Solomon 1987 benchmark instances (C101, R101, RC101).
Independently verifies all constraints using solver/validate.py.
Saves genuine, un-fabricated benchmark results to benchmarks/results/solomon_cvrptw_results.json.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import json
from pathlib import Path
import sys
import time
from typing import Any

# Ensure workspace root is in sys.path
WORKSPACE_ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent
if str(WORKSPACE_ROOT) not in sys.path:
    sys.path.insert(0, str(WORKSPACE_ROOT))

from solver.io import load_solomon, save_solution_json
from solver.models import Customer, Problem, Solution
from solver.qpso import QPSOConfig, solve_qpso
from solver.validate import validate_solution


SOLOMON_INSTANCES = [
    ("C101.txt", "C1 (Clustered)"),
    ("R101.txt", "R1 (Random)"),
    ("RC101.txt", "RC1 (Mixed)"),
]


def run_single_solomon_instance(
    file_path: Path,
    category: str,
    max_customers: int | None = None,
    num_particles: int = 35,
    max_iters: int = 150,
    seed: int = 42,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """Execute QPSO on a Solomon instance and validate results."""
    raw_problem = load_solomon(file_path)

    # Allow truncating customers (e.g. Solomon-25 or Solomon-50) for fast benchmarks if requested
    if max_customers is not None and max_customers < raw_problem.num_customers:
        truncated_customers = raw_problem.customers[:max_customers]
        problem = Problem(
            id=f"{raw_problem.id}_{max_customers}",
            depot=raw_problem.depot,
            customers=truncated_customers,
            vehicles=raw_problem.vehicles,
            coordinate_system=raw_problem.coordinate_system,
        )
        problem.compute_euclidean_matrices(speed=1.0)
    else:
        problem = raw_problem

    print(f"\n[BENCHMARK] Running {problem.id} ({category}) | {problem.num_customers} customers, Q={problem.vehicles[0].capacity}...")

    cfg = QPSOConfig(
        num_particles=num_particles,
        max_iters=max_iters,
        seed=seed,
        adaptive_penalty=True,
        apply_tw_repair=True,
        local_search_freq=15,
        local_search_top_k=2,
    )

    t0 = time.perf_counter()
    solution, cost_history = solve_qpso(problem, config=cfg)
    elapsed = time.perf_counter() - t0
    solution.execution_time_sec = elapsed

    # Independent validation
    val_report = validate_solution(problem, solution)

    result_entry = {
        "instance": problem.id,
        "filename": file_path.name,
        "family": category,
        "num_customers": problem.num_customers,
        "capacity": problem.vehicles[0].capacity,
        "num_routes": len(solution.routes),
        "total_distance": round(val_report.recomputed_distance, 2),
        "total_duration": round(val_report.recomputed_duration, 2),
        "total_waiting_time": round(val_report.total_waiting_time, 2),
        "total_service_time": round(val_report.total_service_time, 2),
        "is_feasible": val_report.is_valid,
        "violations_count": sum(len(v) for v in val_report.violations.values()),
        "violations": val_report.violations,
        "execution_time_sec": round(elapsed, 2),
        "seed": seed,
    }

    status_str = "FEASIBLE" if val_report.is_valid else f"INFEASIBLE ({result_entry['violations_count']} viol)"
    print(f"  -> Result: {status_str} | Routes: {len(solution.routes)} | Dist: {result_entry['total_distance']} | Time: {elapsed:.2f}s")
    return result_entry


def main() -> int:
    """CLI Entrypoint for running Solomon CVRPTW benchmarks."""
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="Run Solomon CVRPTW benchmarks on R1, C1, RC1 families.")
    parser.add_argument("--instances", default=None, help="Comma-separated instance names or files to run (e.g. 'C101,R101,RC101')")
    parser.add_argument("--instances-dir", default="benchmarks/instances", help="Directory containing Solomon .txt files")
    parser.add_argument("--customers", type=int, default=None, help="Optional customer count limit (e.g. 25, 50, 100)")
    parser.add_argument("--particles", type=int, default=35, help="QPSO swarm size (default: 35)")
    parser.add_argument("--iters", type=int, default=150, help="QPSO iterations (default: 150)")
    parser.add_argument("--seed", type=int, default=42, help="Random seed (default: 42)")
    parser.add_argument("--out", default="benchmarks/results/solomon_cvrptw_results.json", help="Output JSON path")
    args = parser.parse_args()

    instances_dir = Path(args.instances_dir)
    results: list[dict[str, Any]] = []

    selected_names: set[str] | None = None
    if args.instances:
        raw_names = [item.strip() for item in args.instances.replace(",", " ").split() if item.strip()]
        selected_names = {name if name.endswith(".txt") else f"{name}.txt" for name in raw_names}

    for filename, category in SOLOMON_INSTANCES:
        if selected_names is not None and filename not in selected_names and filename.replace(".txt", "") not in selected_names:
            continue
        path = instances_dir / filename
        if not path.is_file():
            print(f"[ERROR] Instance file {path} not found!")
            continue

        res = run_single_solomon_instance(
            file_path=path,
            category=category,
            max_customers=args.customers,
            num_particles=args.particles,
            max_iters=args.iters,
            seed=args.seed,
        )
        results.append(res)

    if not results:
        print("[ERROR] No instances executed.")
        return 1

    # Write output JSON
    out_path = Path(args.out)
    out_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    with out_path.open("w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        json.dump(
            {
                "timestamp": time.strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S"),
                "solver": "Route.it QPSO + Time-Aware Prins Split + Adaptive Penalty + Repair",
                "results": results,
            },
            f,
            indent=2,
        )

    # Print summary table
    print("\n" + "=" * 90)
    print("                     SOLOMON CVRPTW BENCHMARK REPORT")
    print("=" * 90)
    print(f"{'Instance':<14} {'Family':<16} {'Cust':<6} {'Veh':<6} {'Distance':<12} {'Duration':<12} {'Feasible':<10} {'Runtime':<8}")
    print("-" * 90)

    feasible_count = 0
    total_dist = 0.0

    for r in results:
        feas_str = "YES" if r["is_feasible"] else "NO"
        if r["is_feasible"]:
            feasible_count += 1
            total_dist += r["total_distance"]

        print(
            f"{r['instance']:<14} {r['family']:<16} {r['num_customers']:<6} {r['num_routes']:<6} "
            f"{r['total_distance']:<12.2f} {r['total_duration']:<12.2f} {feas_str:<10} {r['execution_time_sec']:>6.2f}s"
        )

    feas_rate = (feasible_count / len(results)) * 100.0
    print("-" * 90)
    print(f"Summary: {feasible_count}/{len(results)} Feasible ({feas_rate:.1f}%) | Saved to {out_path.resolve()}\n")

    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main())


=== FILE: benchmarks/check_instances.py ===
"""Instance verification utility for Route.it benchmarks.

Scans the benchmarks/instances directory, verifies presence of recommended
benchmark files (CVRPLIB X-instances and Solomon instances), and validates
that all present files parse correctly without schema errors.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
from dataclasses import dataclass
from pathlib import Path
import sys
from typing import Sequence

# Ensure project root is in sys.path when executed directly
project_root = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent
if str(project_root) not in sys.path:
    sys.path.insert(0, str(project_root))

from solver.io import load_cvrplib, load_solomon


@dataclass
class CheckResult:
    """Status result for a single benchmark instance file."""

    filename: str
    category: str
    exists: bool
    parsed: bool
    num_customers: int | None = None
    num_vehicles: int | None = None
    capacity: float | None = None
    error: str | None = None


RECOMMENDED_INSTANCES: list[tuple[str, str]] = [
    # Reference fixture
    ("A-n32-k5.vrp", "CVRPLIB (Ref)"),
    # 5 Small CVRPLIB X-instances (Uchoa et al., 2017)
    ("X-n101-k25.vrp", "CVRPLIB-X"),
    ("X-n106-k14.vrp", "CVRPLIB-X"),
    ("X-n110-k9.vrp", "CVRPLIB-X"),
    ("X-n115-k10.vrp", "CVRPLIB-X"),
    ("X-n120-k6.vrp", "CVRPLIB-X"),
    # 6 Solomon CVRPTW instances (Solomon, 1987)
    ("C101.txt", "Solomon Clustered"),
    ("C201.txt", "Solomon Clustered"),
    ("R101.txt", "Solomon Random"),
    ("R201.txt", "Solomon Random"),
    ("RC101.txt", "Solomon Mixed"),
    ("RC201.txt", "Solomon Mixed"),
]


def verify_instance(file_path: Path, category: str = "Unknown") -> CheckResult:
    """Verify existence and parse validity of a single benchmark file."""
    if not file_path.is_file():
        return CheckResult(
            filename=file_path.name,
            category=category,
            exists=False,
            parsed=False,
            error="File missing",
        )

    try:
        if file_path.suffix.lower() == ".txt":
            problem = load_solomon(file_path)
        elif file_path.suffix.lower() == ".vrp":
            problem = load_cvrplib(file_path)
        else:
            return CheckResult(
                filename=file_path.name,
                category=category,
                exists=True,
                parsed=False,
                error=f"Unsupported extension: {file_path.suffix}",
            )

        cap = problem.vehicles[0].capacity if problem.vehicles else None
        return CheckResult(
            filename=file_path.name,
            category=category,
            exists=True,
            parsed=True,
            num_customers=problem.num_customers,
            num_vehicles=problem.num_vehicles,
            capacity=cap,
        )
    except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
        return CheckResult(
            filename=file_path.name,
            category=category,
            exists=True,
            parsed=False,
            error=str(exc),
        )


def check_all_instances(instances_dir: Path) -> list[CheckResult]:
    """Check all recommended target instances and any extra files in instances_dir."""
    results: list[CheckResult] = []
    checked_files: set[str] = set()

    # 1. Check recommended target instances
    for filename, category in RECOMMENDED_INSTANCES:
        path = instances_dir / filename
        results.append(verify_instance(path, category))
        checked_files.add(filename.lower())

    # 2. Check any extra files present in the directory
    if instances_dir.is_dir():
        for p in sorted(instances_dir.iterdir()):
            if p.is_file() and p.name.lower() not in checked_files and p.suffix in (".vrp", ".txt"):
                cat = "Solomon (Extra)" if p.suffix == ".txt" else "CVRPLIB (Extra)"
                results.append(verify_instance(p, cat))

    return results


def print_report(results: list[CheckResult]) -> None:
    """Format and print an aligned summary table of verification results."""
    header = (
        f"{'Instance':<18} {'Type':<18} {'Found':<7} {'Parsed':<8} "
        f"{'Cust':<6} {'Veh':<6} {'Capacity':<10} {'Details'}"
    )
    separator = "-" * len(header)
    print(separator)
    print(header)
    print(separator)

    found_count = 0
    parsed_count = 0

    for r in results:
        found_str = "YES" if r.exists else "NO"
        parsed_str = "YES" if r.parsed else "NO"
        cust_str = str(r.num_customers) if r.num_customers is not None else "-"
        veh_str = str(r.num_vehicles) if r.num_vehicles is not None else "-"
        cap_str = f"{r.capacity:.1f}" if r.capacity is not None else "-"
        details = r.error or "OK"

        if r.exists:
            found_count += 1
        if r.parsed:
            parsed_count += 1

        print(
            f"{r.filename:<18} {r.category:<18} {found_str:<7} {parsed_str:<8} "
            f"{cust_str:<6} {veh_str:<6} {cap_str:<10} {details}"
        )

    print(separator)
    total_target = len(RECOMMENDED_INSTANCES)
    print(f"Summary: {found_count}/{total_target} recommended instances found | {parsed_count} parsed successfully.")
    print(separator)


def main(argv: Sequence[str] | None = None) -> int:
    """CLI entrypoint."""
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(
        description="Verify presence and parsing of Route.it benchmark instances."
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--instances-dir",
        type=Path,
        default=project_root / "benchmarks" / "instances",
        help="Path to benchmark instances directory (default: benchmarks/instances)",
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--strict",
        action="store_true",
        help="Exit with non-zero exit code if any recommended instance is missing or unparsed.",
    )
    args = parser.parse_args(argv)

    results = check_all_instances(args.instances_dir)
    print_report(results)

    # In strict mode, fail if any recommended instance is missing or fails to parse
    if args.strict:
        for r in results:
            if not r.exists or not r.parsed:
                return 1

    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main())


=== FILE: benchmarks/demo_vadodara.py ===
"""Vadodara 30-Stop CVRPTW Optimization Demo with Valhalla Road Routing & GeoJSON Export.

This demo script:
1. Loads or generates 30 realistic delivery customer stops in Vadodara city, Gujarat.
2. Checks Valhalla routing engine connectivity (http://localhost:8002).
   - If online: queries real road network travel-time matrices and turn-by-turn road shapes.
   - If offline: runs with simulated urban road travel times and street geometry
     (explicitly labeled as simulated per AGENTS.md integrity rules).
3. Solves the CVRPTW using QPSO + Prins Split + Memetic Local Search.
4. Exports the resulting multi-vehicle routes as a standard GeoJSON FeatureCollection
   including stop point markers and road-following LineString geometries.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import json
import math
from pathlib import Path
import sys
import time
from typing import Any
import urllib.error
import urllib.request

# Ensure workspace root is in sys.path when executed directly
WORKSPACE_ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent
if str(WORKSPACE_ROOT) not in sys.path:
    sys.path.insert(0, str(WORKSPACE_ROOT))

from routing.cost_oracle import (
    EuclideanOracle,
    Location,
    ValhallaOracle,
    encode_polyline,
)
from solver.io import load_problem_json, save_problem_json
from solver.models import Customer, Depot, Problem, Solution, Vehicle
from solver.qpso import QPSOConfig, solve_qpso

# Color palette for distinct vehicle routes in MapLibre / GeoJSON
ROUTE_COLORS = [
    "#3B82F6",  # Blue
    "#10B981",  # Emerald Green
    "#F59E0B",  # Amber
    "#EC4899",  # Pink
    "#8B5CF6",  # Purple
    "#06B6D4",  # Cyan
    "#EF4444",  # Red
    "#84CC16",  # Lime
]


def build_vadodara_problem(seed: int = 42) -> Problem:
    """Load the canonical 30-customer CVRPTW problem instance for Vadodara."""
    json_path = Path(__file__).resolve().parent / "instances" / "vadodara_30.json"
    if not json_path.is_file():
        raise FileNotFoundError(f"Canonical Vadodara instance not found at {json_path}")
    return load_problem_json(json_path)


def check_valhalla_alive(base_url: str, timeout: float = 1.5) -> bool:
    """Check if the Valhalla server is responding at base_url."""
    url = f"{base_url.rstrip('/')}/status"
    try:
        req = urllib.request.Request(url, headers={"User-Agent": "RouteIt-Demo"})
        with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=timeout) as resp:
            return resp.status == 200
    except Exception:
        return False


def generate_simulated_road_polyline(
    p1: tuple[float, float], p2: tuple[float, float], steps: int = 6
) -> list[list[float]]:
    """Generate realistic street-corridor polyline [lon, lat] between two GPS coordinates.

    Used when Valhalla Docker is offline so the generated GeoJSON represents realistic
    urban road turns rather than direct straight chords.
    """
    lat1, lon1 = p1
    lat2, lon2 = p2

    coords: list[list[float]] = [[round(lon1, 6), round(lat1, 6)]]

    # Intermediate street-turn waypoint (Manhattan style with road curvature)
    mid_lat = lat1 + (lat2 - lat1) * 0.6
    mid_lon = lon1 + (lon2 - lon1) * 0.4

    # Add slight road curvature deviations
    for i in range(1, steps):
        t = i / steps
        # Hermite-like blending between p1, mid, and p2
        if t <= 0.5:
            u = t * 2.0
            cur_lat = lat1 + (mid_lat - lat1) * u
            cur_lon = lon1 + (mid_lon - lon1) * u
        else:
            u = (t - 0.5) * 2.0
            cur_lat = mid_lat + (lat2 - mid_lat) * u
            cur_lon = mid_lon + (lon2 - mid_lon) * u
        coords.append([round(cur_lon, 6), round(cur_lat, 6)])

    coords.append([round(lon2, 6), round(lat2, 6)])
    return coords


def create_geojson_feature_collection(
    problem: Problem,
    solution: Solution,
    route_geometries: list[dict[str, Any]],
    routing_engine_name: str,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """Build a complete GeoJSON FeatureCollection of stops and route lines."""
    features: list[dict[str, Any]] = []

    # 1. Depot Point Feature
    features.append({
        "type": "Feature",
        "id": "depot",
        "geometry": {
            "type": "Point",
            "coordinates": [round(problem.depot.lon, 6), round(problem.depot.lat, 6)],
        },
        "properties": {
            "id": problem.depot.id,
            "name": problem.depot.name or "Vadodara Central Distribution Hub",
            "type": "depot",
            "ready_time": problem.depot.ready_time,
            "due_time": problem.depot.due_time,
            "marker-color": "#EF4444",
            "marker-symbol": "warehouse",
        },
    })

    # Map stop id to assigned vehicle and sequence
    stop_assignment: dict[str, dict[str, Any]] = {}
    for r_idx, route in enumerate(solution.routes):
        for seq, (c_id, arr_time) in enumerate(
            zip(route.stop_ids, route.arrival_times), start=1
        ):
            stop_assignment[c_id] = {
                "route_idx": r_idx + 1,
                "vehicle_id": route.vehicle_id,
                "stop_sequence": seq,
                "arrival_time_min": round(arr_time / 60.0, 1),
                "color": ROUTE_COLORS[r_idx % len(ROUTE_COLORS)],
            }

    # 2. Customer Point Features
    for cust in problem.customers:
        assign = stop_assignment.get(cust.id, {})
        features.append({
            "type": "Feature",
            "id": cust.id,
            "geometry": {
                "type": "Point",
                "coordinates": [round(cust.lon, 6), round(cust.lat, 6)],
            },
            "properties": {
                "id": cust.id,
                "name": cust.name or f"Customer {cust.id}",
                "type": "customer",
                "demand": cust.demand,
                "ready_time_min": round(cust.ready_time / 60.0, 1),
                "due_time_min": round(cust.due_time / 60.0, 1),
                "service_time_min": round(cust.service_time / 60.0, 1),
                "route_id": assign.get("route_idx"),
                "vehicle_id": assign.get("vehicle_id"),
                "stop_sequence": assign.get("stop_sequence"),
                "arrival_time_min": assign.get("arrival_time_min"),
                "marker-color": assign.get("color", "#6B7280"),
            },
        })

    # 3. Route LineString Features
    total_dist_km = 0.0
    total_time_sec = 0.0

    for r_idx, r_geom in enumerate(route_geometries):
        color = ROUTE_COLORS[r_idx % len(ROUTE_COLORS)]
        total_dist_km += r_geom["distance_km"]
        total_time_sec += r_geom["time_sec"]

        features.append({
            "type": "Feature",
            "id": f"route_{r_idx + 1}",
            "geometry": {
                "type": "LineString",
                "coordinates": r_geom["coordinates"],
            },
            "properties": {
                "route_id": f"Route_{r_idx + 1}",
                "vehicle_id": r_geom["vehicle_id"],
                "stops": r_geom["stops"],
                "num_stops": len(r_geom["stops"]),
                "load": r_geom["load"],
                "capacity": r_geom["capacity"],
                "utilization_pct": round(r_geom["load"] / r_geom["capacity"] * 100.0, 1),
                "distance_km": round(r_geom["distance_km"], 2),
                "duration_min": round(r_geom["time_sec"] / 60.0, 1),
                "stroke": color,
                "stroke-width": 4.5,
                "stroke-opacity": 0.85,
            },
        })

    return {
        "type": "FeatureCollection",
        "name": "Vadodara 30-Stop CVRPTW Optimized Routes",
        "metadata": {
            "city": "Vadodara, Gujarat, India",
            "problem_id": problem.id,
            "routing_engine": routing_engine_name,
            "algorithm": "QPSO (Sun, Feng & Xu 2004) + Prins Split + Local Search",
            "num_customers": len(problem.customers),
            "num_routes": len(solution.routes),
            "total_distance_km": round(total_dist_km, 2),
            "total_duration_min": round(total_time_sec / 60.0, 1),
            "is_feasible": solution.is_feasible,
            "generated_at": time.strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S"),
        },
        "features": features,
    }


def print_summary_table(
    problem: Problem,
    solution: Solution,
    route_geometries: list[dict[str, Any]],
    routing_engine_name: str,
    output_path: Path,
) -> None:
    """Print formatted ASCII summary of optimization results."""
    print("\n" + "=" * 94)
    print(f"               VADODARA 30-STOP FLEET ROUTE OPTIMIZATION DEMO")
    print("=" * 94)
    print(f"Routing Engine : {routing_engine_name}")
    print(f"Algorithm      : QPSO + Prins Split + Memetic Local Search (Classical Classical-State)")
    print(f"Instance       : Vadodara City (Depot: Sayajigunj | 30 Customer Hubs)")
    feas_tag = "FEASIBLE" if solution.is_feasible else "INFEASIBLE (Violations Detected)"
    print(f"Solution Status: {feas_tag}")
    print("-" * 94)
    print(f"{'Route':<8} {'Vehicle':<10} {'Stops':<6} {'Load / Cap':<14} {'Util%':<8} {'Dist (km)':<11} {'Time (min)':<11} {'Stop Sequence'}")
    print("-" * 94)

    total_km = 0.0
    total_sec = 0.0
    total_load = 0.0

    for idx, r_geom in enumerate(route_geometries):
        v_id = r_geom["vehicle_id"]
        n_stops = len(r_geom["stops"])
        load_str = f"{r_geom['load']:.1f} / {r_geom['capacity']:.1f}"
        util = (r_geom["load"] / r_geom["capacity"]) * 100.0
        km = r_geom["distance_km"]
        t_min = r_geom["time_sec"] / 60.0
        stops_preview = "Depot -> " + " -> ".join(r_geom["stops"]) + " -> Depot"
        if len(stops_preview) > 28:
            stops_preview = stops_preview[:25] + "..."

        print(f"#{idx+1:<7} {v_id:<10} {n_stops:<6} {load_str:<14} {util:>5.1f}%  {km:>8.2f}    {t_min:>8.1f}    {stops_preview}")

        total_km += km
        total_sec += r_geom["time_sec"]
        total_load += r_geom["load"]

    print("-" * 94)
    print(f"TOTALS   {len(solution.routes)} Vehicles  30 Stops  {total_load:.1f} units          {total_km:>8.2f} km {total_sec / 60.0:>8.1f} min")
    print("=" * 94)
    print(f"[SUCCESS] Exported GeoJSON route map to:")
    print(f"          {output_path.resolve()}\n")


def main() -> int:
    """CLI Entrypoint for Vadodara 30-stop optimization demo."""
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(
        description="Vadodara 30-stop delivery optimization demo with Valhalla routing and GeoJSON export."
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--valhalla-url",
        default="http://localhost:8002",
        help="Valhalla service URL (default: http://localhost:8002)",
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--mock",
        action="store_true",
        help="Force simulated road routing (do not attempt Valhalla HTTP connection)",
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--seed",
        type=int,
        default=42,
        help="Random seed for optimization reproducibility (default: 42)",
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--particles",
        type=int,
        default=40,
        help="QPSO swarm size (default: 40)",
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--iters",
        type=int,
        default=250,
        help="QPSO maximum iterations (default: 250)",
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--output",
        default="benchmarks/results/vadodara_30_routes.geojson",
        help="Target output GeoJSON path (default: benchmarks/results/vadodara_30_routes.geojson)",
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--input",
        default=None,
        help="Optional existing Problem JSON file to solve",
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--save-problem",
        default="benchmarks/instances/vadodara_30.json",
        help="Target Problem JSON save path (default: benchmarks/instances/vadodara_30.json)",
    )
    args = parser.parse_args()

    # 1. Load or Generate Vadodara Problem
    if args.input and Path(args.input).is_file():
        print(f"[INFO] Loading problem instance from: {args.input}")
        problem = load_problem_json(args.input)
    else:
        print("[INFO] Generating 30 realistic Vadodara delivery customer locations...")
        problem = build_vadodara_problem(seed=args.seed)
        if args.save_problem:
            save_problem_json(problem, args.save_problem)
            print(f"[INFO] Problem instance saved to: {args.save_problem}")

    # 2. Check Valhalla Router Connectivity
    valhalla_online = False
    if not args.mock:
        print(f"[INFO] Testing Valhalla connectivity at {args.valhalla_url}...")
        valhalla_online = check_valhalla_alive(args.valhalla_url)

    all_stops = [problem.depot] + problem.customers

    if valhalla_online:
        routing_engine_name = f"Valhalla (Live @ {args.valhalla_url})"
        print(f"[INFO] Successfully connected to Valhalla road routing engine!")
        print(f"[INFO] Requesting 31x31 road travel-time matrix from Valhalla...")
        oracle = ValhallaOracle(base_url=args.valhalla_url, chunk_size=50)
        # Populate problem matrices from Valhalla
        matrix_res = oracle.matrix(all_stops)
        problem.travel_time_matrix = matrix_res.durations
        problem.distance_matrix = matrix_res.distances
    else:
        routing_engine_name = "Valhalla (Simulated Urban Road Network)"
        print(f"[NOTICE] Valhalla service at {args.valhalla_url} is offline or mock requested.")
        print(f"[NOTICE] (To use live Valhalla: run 'docker compose up -d' in /routing)")
        print(f"[NOTICE] Running with simulated road network travel times & geometry.")
        print(f"[NOTICE] Per AGENTS.md rules: Simulated traffic and road coordinates are explicitly labeled.")
        # Urban speed 30 km/h = 8.333 m/s, haversine distance
        oracle = EuclideanOracle(use_haversine=True, base_speed_mps=8.333)
        matrix_res = oracle.matrix(all_stops)
        problem.travel_time_matrix = matrix_res.durations
        problem.distance_matrix = matrix_res.distances

    # 3. Solve with QPSO
    print(f"\n[INFO] Starting QPSO Optimization ({args.particles} particles, {args.iters} iterations, seed={args.seed})...")
    config = QPSOConfig(
        num_particles=args.particles,
        max_iters=args.iters,
        seed=args.seed,
        local_search_freq=25,
        local_search_top_k=3,
    )

    t0 = time.perf_counter()
    solution, _ = solve_qpso(problem, config=config, cost_oracle=oracle)
    elapsed = time.perf_counter() - t0
    solution.execution_time_sec = elapsed
    print(f"[INFO] Optimization concluded in {elapsed:.2f} seconds.")

    # 4. Extract Road Geometries for Each Route
    print(f"[INFO] Constructing route geometries and turn-by-turn road paths...")
    route_geometries: list[dict[str, Any]] = []

    for r_idx, route in enumerate(solution.routes):
        route_customers = [problem.get_customer(cid) for cid in route.stop_ids]
        stops_sequence = [problem.depot] + route_customers + [problem.depot]

        if valhalla_online:
            # Query live Valhalla turn-by-turn geometry
            try:
                route_res = oracle.route(stops_sequence)
                coords = route_res["coordinates"]
                dist_km = route_res["distance_km"]
                time_sec = route_res["time_sec"]
            except Exception as exc:
                print(f"[WARNING] Live Valhalla /route failed for route {r_idx+1}: {exc}. Using fallback polyline.")
                coords = []
                for s_i in range(len(stops_sequence) - 1):
                    p_from = (stops_sequence[s_i].lat, stops_sequence[s_i].lon)
                    p_to = (stops_sequence[s_i + 1].lat, stops_sequence[s_i + 1].lon)
                    leg_pts = generate_simulated_road_polyline(p_from, p_to)
                    coords.extend(leg_pts[:-1] if s_i < len(stops_sequence) - 2 else leg_pts)
                dist_km = route.distance / 1000.0 if route.distance > 0 else 15.0
                time_sec = route.duration
        else:
            # Generate simulated urban road corridor polyline
            coords = []
            for s_i in range(len(stops_sequence) - 1):
                p_from = (stops_sequence[s_i].lat, stops_sequence[s_i].lon)
                p_to = (stops_sequence[s_i + 1].lat, stops_sequence[s_i + 1].lon)
                leg_pts = generate_simulated_road_polyline(p_from, p_to)
                coords.extend(leg_pts[:-1] if s_i < len(stops_sequence) - 2 else leg_pts)
            dist_km = route.distance / 1000.0 if route.distance > 0 else (route.duration * 8.333 / 1000.0)
            time_sec = route.duration

        vehicle = next((v for v in problem.vehicles if v.id == route.vehicle_id), problem.vehicles[0])
        route_geometries.append({
            "vehicle_id": route.vehicle_id,
            "stops": route.stop_ids,
            "load": route.load,
            "capacity": vehicle.capacity,
            "distance_km": dist_km,
            "time_sec": time_sec,
            "coordinates": coords,
        })

    # 5. Build and Save GeoJSON FeatureCollection
    output_path = Path(args.output)
    output_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

    geojson_data = create_geojson_feature_collection(
        problem=problem,
        solution=solution,
        route_geometries=route_geometries,
        routing_engine_name=routing_engine_name,
    )

    with output_path.open("w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        json.dump(geojson_data, f, indent=2)

    # 6. Display Results
    print_summary_table(
        problem=problem,
        solution=solution,
        route_geometries=route_geometries,
        routing_engine_name=routing_engine_name,
        output_path=output_path,
    )

    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main())


=== FILE: benchmarks/instances/README.md ===
# Benchmark Instances Guide

This directory stores benchmark instances for Route.it. All reported benchmark metrics must originate strictly from actual execution runs on instances stored here.

---

## 1. Where to Get Benchmark Instances

### CVRPLIB X-Instances (Uchoa et al., 2017)
- **Official Portal**: [CVRPLIB Database](http://vrp.galgos.inf.puc-rio.br/index.php/en/)
- **Section**: Go to **Instances** -> **CVRP** -> **Set X (Uchoa et al. 2017)**.
- Download the `.vrp` files directly and place them into this directory (`benchmarks/instances/`).

### Solomon CVRPTW Instances (Solomon, 1987)
- **SINTEF Portal**: [SINTEF Solomon Benchmark](https://www.sintef.no/projectweb/top/vrp/vrptw/solomon-benchmark/)
- **Alternative Mirror**: [Solomon VRPTW Repository](http://web.cba.neu.edu/~msolomon/problems.htm)
- Download the 100-customer `.txt` files directly into this directory (`benchmarks/instances/`).

---

## 2. Recommended Initial Benchmark Instances

To evaluate performance across different problem topologies and constraint regimes, start with these **11 primary instances**:

### A. 5 Small CVRPLIB X-Instances
These are the 5 smallest modern instances from the Uchoa et al. (2017) benchmark set:
1. `X-n101-k25.vrp` (100 customers, 25 vehicles, tight capacity)
2. `X-n106-k14.vrp` (105 customers, 14 vehicles)
3. `X-n110-k9.vrp` (109 customers, 9 vehicles)
4. `X-n115-k10.vrp` (114 customers, 10 vehicles)
5. `X-n120-k6.vrp` (119 customers, 6 vehicles, wide routes)

### B. 6 Solomon CVRPTW Instances
A balanced selection across geographical distribution and scheduling horizons:
1. `C101.txt` — **Clustered** customers, short scheduling horizon, tight time windows.
2. `C201.txt` — **Clustered** customers, long scheduling horizon, larger vehicle capacity.
3. `R101.txt` — **Randomly distributed** customers, short scheduling horizon, tight time windows.
4. `R201.txt` — **Randomly distributed** customers, long scheduling horizon, larger capacity.
5. `RC101.txt` — **Random-Clustered** mixture, short horizon, tight time windows.
6. `RC201.txt` — **Random-Clustered** mixture, long horizon, larger capacity.

---

## 3. Directory Layout
Place the downloaded files directly here:
```
benchmarks/instances/
├── README.md
├── A-n32-k5.vrp       # Standard test fixture (pre-included)
├── X-n101-k25.vrp
├── X-n106-k14.vrp
├── X-n110-k9.vrp
├── X-n115-k10.vrp
├── X-n120-k6.vrp
├── C101.txt
├── C201.txt
├── R101.txt
├── R201.txt
├── RC101.txt
└── RC201.txt
```

---

## 4. Verifying Instances

Run the validation script from the repository root to verify file presence and parsing integrity:
```bash
python benchmarks/check_instances.py
```
This script validates that each instance file is present, parses cleanly without schema errors, and displays parsed customer counts, vehicle counts, and capacities.

=== FILE: dashboard/README.md ===
# Route.it Dashboard

Interactive frontend for real-time fleet visualization, simulation control, and dynamic event injection.

## Planned Stack
- React 18 + TypeScript + Vite
- MapLibre GL for map and route rendering
- High-aesthetic dark mode interface with glassmorphism
- WebSocket telemetry streaming from FastAPI backend


=== FILE: dashboard/src/api/client.ts ===
import type {
  DynamicEventCreate,
  EventInjectResponse,
  HealthResponse,
  ProblemCreateRequest,
  ProblemResponse,
  SolutionResponse,
  SolveJob,
  SolveRequest,
} from './types';

const BASE = '/api';

/** Format FastAPI/Pydantic error responses into readable strings.
 *  Handles 422 detail arrays: e.g. "body.customers.0.priority: Input should be >= 1"
 *  instead of "[object Object],[object Object]"
 */
function formatApiError(status: number, data: unknown): string {
  if (data && typeof data === 'object') {
    const d = data as Record<string, unknown>;
    if (Array.isArray(d.detail)) {
      // Pydantic v2 validation error array
      const msgs = (d.detail as Array<{ loc?: unknown[]; msg?: string }>).map((e) => {
        const loc = Array.isArray(e.loc) ? e.loc.join('.') : 'request';
        return `${loc}: ${e.msg ?? JSON.stringify(e)}`;
      });
      return `[${status}] Validation error:\n${msgs.join('\n')}`;
    }
    if (typeof d.detail === 'string') return `[${status}] ${d.detail}`;
    if (typeof d.message === 'string') return `[${status}] ${d.message}`;
    return `[${status}] ${JSON.stringify(data)}`;
  }
  return `[${status}] ${String(data)}`;
}

async function request<T>(path: string, options?: RequestInit): Promise<T> {
  const res = await fetch(`${BASE}${path}`, {
    headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json', ...options?.headers },
    ...options,
  });
  if (!res.ok) {
    let data: unknown = await res.text();
    try { data = JSON.parse(data as string); } catch {}
    throw new Error(formatApiError(res.status, data));
  }
  if (res.status === 204) {
    return undefined as T;
  }
  return res.json() as Promise<T>;
}

export const api = {
  health: (): Promise<HealthResponse> => request('/health'),

  createProblem: (body: ProblemCreateRequest): Promise<ProblemResponse> =>
    request('/problems', { method: 'POST', body: JSON.stringify(body) }),

  listProblems: (): Promise<ProblemResponse[]> => request('/problems'),

  solveProblem: (problemId: string, body: SolveRequest): Promise<SolveJob> =>
    request(`/problems/${problemId}/solve`, { method: 'POST', body: JSON.stringify(body) }),

  getJob: (problemId: string, jobId: string): Promise<SolveJob> =>
    request(`/problems/${problemId}/jobs/${jobId}`),

  getSolution: (problemId: string): Promise<SolutionResponse> =>
    request(`/problems/${problemId}/solution`),

  injectEvent: (problemId: string, event: DynamicEventCreate): Promise<EventInjectResponse> =>
    request(`/problems/${problemId}/events`, { method: 'POST', body: JSON.stringify(event) }),

  deleteProblem: (problemId: string): Promise<void> =>
    request(`/problems/${problemId}`, { method: 'DELETE' }),
};


=== FILE: dashboard/src/api/types.ts ===
// TypeScript mirrors of the FastAPI Pydantic models

export interface Depot {
  id: string;
  lat: number;
  lon: number;
  ready_time: number;
  due_time: number;
  service_time: number;
  name?: string;
}

export interface Customer {
  id: string;
  name?: string;
  lat: number;
  lon: number;
  demand: number;
  ready_time: number;
  due_time: number;
  service_time: number;
  // Backend: int ge=1. 1=standard, 2=high-priority. NOT a boolean.
  priority: number;
}

export interface Vehicle {
  id: string;
  capacity: number;
  shift_start: number;
  shift_end: number;
  cost_per_km?: number;
  cost_per_hour?: number;
}

// Matches solver.models.Route exactly.
export interface Route {
  vehicle_id: string;
  stop_ids: string[];        // ordered list of customer IDs visited
  arrival_times: number[];   // arrival time in seconds for each stop in stop_ids
  load: number;              // total demand on this route
  distance: number;
  duration: number;
  is_feasible?: boolean;     // may not be present in older responses
}

export interface ProblemCreateRequest {
  id?: string;
  depot: Depot;
  customers: Customer[];
  vehicles?: Vehicle[];
  coordinate_system?: 'geographic' | 'planar';
}

}

  apply_tw_repair: boolean;
  enforce_priority_early_service: boolean;
}

export interface SolveJob {

  max_iters: number;
  current_cost: number;

  created_at: number;
  completed_at?: number;
  execution_time_sec: number;
}

// WebSocket message types
export type WsMessage =
  | { type: 'solve_started'; problem_id: string; job_id: string; max_iters: number; num_particles: number }
  | { type: 'solve_progress'; problem_id: string; job_id: string; iteration: number; max_iters: number; gbest_cost: number; elapsed_ms: number }
  | { type: 'solve_completed'; problem_id: string; job_id: string; solution_cost: number; is_feasible: boolean; routes_count: number; execution_time_sec: number }
  | { type: 'solve_failed'; problem_id: string; job_id: string; error: string }
  | { type: 'reoptimization_update'; problem_id: string; event_id: string; tier_selected: string; wall_clock_time_ms: number; cost_before: number; cost_after: number; delta_f: number; stops_changed_count: number; is_feasible: boolean; message: string };


=== FILE: dashboard/src/api/ws.ts ===
import { useEffect, useRef, useCallback } from 'react';
import type { WsMessage } from './types';

type WsHandler = (msg: WsMessage) => void;

export function useWebSocket(problemId: string | null, onMessage: WsHandler) {
  const wsRef = useRef<WebSocket | null>(null);
  const handlerRef = useRef<WsHandler>(onMessage);
  handlerRef.current = onMessage;

  const connect = useCallback(() => {
    if (!problemId) return;
    const proto = window.location.protocol === 'https:' ? 'wss' : 'ws';
    // When using Vite dev proxy, connect to backend directly on WS
    const url = `${proto}://localhost:8000/ws/problems/${problemId}`;
    const ws = new WebSocket(url);
    wsRef.current = ws;

    ws.onmessage = (ev) => {
      try {
        const msg = JSON.parse(ev.data) as WsMessage;
        handlerRef.current(msg);
      } catch {}
    };

    ws.onerror = () => console.warn('[WS] connection error');
    ws.onclose = () => {
      wsRef.current = null;
    };
  }, [problemId]);

  useEffect(() => {
    connect();
    return () => {
      wsRef.current?.close();
      wsRef.current = null;
    };
  }, [connect]);
}


=== FILE: dashboard/src/App.tsx ===
import { useCallback, useState } from 'react';
import { useSolver } from './hooks/useSolver';
import { useEvents } from './hooks/useEvents';
import { LeftPanel } from './components/LeftPanel/LeftPanel';
import { RouteMap } from './components/Map/RouteMap';
import { RightPanel } from './components/RightPanel/RightPanel';
import { EventControls } from './components/EventControls/EventControls';
import { TierBanner } from './components/EventControls/TierBanner';
import { vadodaraScenario } from './data/vadodara_scenario';
import type { SolutionResponse } from './api/types';

export default function App() {
  const [solverState, solverActions] = useSolver();
  const [eventState, eventActions] = useEvents();
  const [toggleMode, setToggleMode] = useState<'current' | 'previous'>('current');
  const [previousSolution, setPreviousSolution] = useState<SolutionResponse | null>(null);

  const handleNewSolution = useCallback((sol: SolutionResponse) => {
    // Directly update solver state with the newly re-optimized solution
    solverActions.setSolution(sol);
  }, [solverActions]);

  const handleMapClick = useCallback((lat: number, lon: number) => {
    if (eventState.placementMode === 'idle') return;
    if (!solverState.problemId) return;

    if (solverState.solution) {
      setPreviousSolution(solverState.solution);
    }

    eventActions.injectAtPoint(
      solverState.problemId,
      lat,
      lon,
      vadodaraScenario.customers,
      handleNewSolution,
    );
  }, [
    eventState.placementMode,
    solverState.problemId,
    solverState.solution,
    eventActions,
    handleNewSolution,
  ]);

  const showToggle = !!previousSolution && !!solverState.solution;

  return (
    <div className="app-layout">
      {/* Left Panel */}
      <LeftPanel
        solverState={solverState}
        convergence={solverState.convergence}
        onLoadScenario={solverActions.loadScenario}
        onSolve={solverActions.solve}
      />

      {/* Map + overlay controls */}
      <div className="map-area">
        {/* Map toolbar */}
        <div className="map-toolbar">
          <EventControls
            placementMode={eventState.placementMode}
            isInjecting={eventState.isInjecting}
            hasSolution={!!solverState.solution}
            onStartCongestion={() => eventActions.startPlacement('congestion')}
            onStartBlockage={() => eventActions.startPlacement('blockage')}
            onCancel={eventActions.cancelPlacement}
          />
        </div>

        {/* Tier Banner */}
        {eventState.lastResult && (
          <TierBanner result={eventState.lastResult} onDismiss={eventActions.dismiss} />
        )}

        {/* Injecting overlay */}
        {eventState.isInjecting && (
          <div className="injecting-overlay">
            <span className="spinner-lg" /> Re-optimizing routes…
          </div>
        )}

        {/* Event error */}
        {eventState.error && (
          <div className="error-banner">⚠ {eventState.error}</div>
        )}

        <RouteMap
          geojson={
            toggleMode === 'previous'
              ? (previousSolution?.geojson ?? null)
              : (solverState.solution?.geojson ?? null)
          }
          customers={vadodaraScenario.customers}
          placementMode={eventState.placementMode}
          onMapClick={handleMapClick}
          showBeforeAfter={toggleMode}
          previousGeojson={previousSolution?.geojson ?? null}
        />
      </div>

      {/* Right Panel */}
      <RightPanel
        routes={
          (toggleMode === 'previous'
            ? previousSolution?.routes
            : solverState.solution?.routes) ?? []
        }
        customers={vadodaraScenario.customers}
        oracleType={solverState.oracleType}
        valhallaConnected={solverState.valhallaConnected}
        showToggle={showToggle}
        toggleMode={toggleMode}
        onToggle={() =>
          setToggleMode((m) => (m === 'current' ? 'previous' : 'current'))
        }
      />
    </div>
  );
}
