=== FILE: dynamic/events.py ===
"""Dynamic Traffic and Fleet Event Models for Route.it.

Defines event types (EdgeCongestion, RoadBlockage, VehicleDelay, NewOrder),
JSON scenario serialization/deserialization, conversion to SimulatedIncident
for CostOracle TrafficAdapters, and deterministic seeded scenario generation.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import json
from pathlib import Path
import random
from typing import Annotated, Any, Literal, Union
from pydantic import BaseModel, Field, model_validator

from routing.cost_oracle import SimulatedIncident
from solver.models import Customer, Problem


class DynamicEvent(BaseModel):
    """Base class for all dynamic events occurring in a route execution scenario."""

    event_id: str = Field(..., description="Unique event identifier")
    event_type: str = Field(..., description="Discriminator identifying the event type")
    trigger_time: float = Field(..., ge=0.0, description="Timestamp in seconds when the event occurs")
    duration: float = Field(0.0, ge=0.0, description="Duration in seconds the event remains active")
    description: str = Field("", description="Human-readable description of the incident")

    @property
    def end_time(self) -> float:
        """Timestamp in seconds when the event concludes."""
        return self.trigger_time + self.duration


class EdgeCongestion(DynamicEvent):
    """Congestion on a road edge, corridor, or geographic zone slowing traffic."""

    event_type: Literal["edge_congestion"] = "edge_congestion"
    multiplier: float = Field(
        2.0,
        gt=1.0,
        description="Factor increasing travel time (e.g. 2.0 = travel time doubles, speed halves)",
    )
    origin_id: str | None = Field(None, description="Starting stop ID of affected edge")
    dest_id: str | None = Field(None, description="Ending stop ID of affected edge")
    edge_set: list[tuple[str, str]] | None = Field(
        None,
        description="Optional list of (origin_id, dest_id) pairs affected",
    )
    center_lat: float | None = Field(None, description="Center latitude of congestion zone")
    center_lon: float | None = Field(None, description="Center longitude of congestion zone")
    radius_meters: float | None = Field(None, ge=0.0, description="Radius in metres of affected zone")

    @model_validator(mode="after")
    def validate_spatial_scope(self) -> EdgeCongestion:
        """Ensure at least one spatial scoping parameter is provided."""
        has_od = self.origin_id is not None or self.dest_id is not None
        has_edges = bool(self.edge_set)
        has_geo = (
            self.center_lat is not None
            and self.center_lon is not None
            and self.radius_meters is not None
        )
        if not (has_od or has_edges or has_geo):
            raise ValueError(
                f"EdgeCongestion {self.event_id} must specify origin_id/dest_id, edge_set, or geographic radius"
            )
        return self

    def to_simulated_incidents(self) -> list[SimulatedIncident]:
        """Convert into SimulatedIncident objects for SimulatedEventAdapter."""
        speed_mult = 1.0 / self.multiplier
        incidents: list[SimulatedIncident] = []

        if self.edge_set:
            for idx, (orig, dest) in enumerate(self.edge_set):
                incidents.append(
                    SimulatedIncident(
                        event_id=f"{self.event_id}_edge_{idx}",
                        speed_multiplier=speed_mult,
                        start_time=self.trigger_time,
                        end_time=self.end_time,
                        origin_id=orig,
                        dest_id=dest,
                        description=f"{self.description} [Edge {orig}->{dest}]",
                    )
                )

        if self.origin_id is not None or self.dest_id is not None or self.radius_meters is not None:
            incidents.append(
                SimulatedIncident(
                    event_id=self.event_id,
                    speed_multiplier=speed_mult,
                    start_time=self.trigger_time,
                    end_time=self.end_time,
                    origin_id=self.origin_id,
                    dest_id=self.dest_id,
                    center_lat=self.center_lat,
                    center_lon=self.center_lon,
                    radius_meters=self.radius_meters,
                    description=self.description,
                )
            )

        return incidents


class RoadBlockage(DynamicEvent):
    """Complete obstruction or closure of a road segment or corridor."""

    event_type: Literal["road_blockage"] = "road_blockage"
    origin_id: str | None = Field(None, description="Starting stop ID of blocked corridor")
    dest_id: str | None = Field(None, description="Ending stop ID of blocked corridor")
    edge_set: list[tuple[str, str]] | None = Field(
        None,
        description="Optional list of (origin_id, dest_id) pairs blocked",
    )
    center_lat: float | None = Field(None, description="Center latitude of closed zone")
    center_lon: float | None = Field(None, description="Center longitude of closed zone")
    radius_meters: float | None = Field(None, ge=0.0, description="Radius in metres of closed zone")

    @model_validator(mode="after")
    def validate_spatial_scope(self) -> RoadBlockage:
        """Ensure at least one spatial scoping parameter is provided."""
        has_od = self.origin_id is not None or self.dest_id is not None
        has_edges = bool(self.edge_set)
        has_geo = (
            self.center_lat is not None
            and self.center_lon is not None
            and self.radius_meters is not None
        )
        if not (has_od or has_edges or has_geo):
            raise ValueError(
                f"RoadBlockage {self.event_id} must specify origin_id/dest_id, edge_set, or geographic radius"
            )
        return self

    def to_simulated_incidents(self) -> list[SimulatedIncident]:
        """Convert into SimulatedIncident objects with speed_multiplier=0.0."""
        incidents: list[SimulatedIncident] = []

        if self.edge_set:
            for idx, (orig, dest) in enumerate(self.edge_set):
                incidents.append(
                    SimulatedIncident(
                        event_id=f"{self.event_id}_edge_{idx}",
                        speed_multiplier=0.0,
                        start_time=self.trigger_time,
                        end_time=self.end_time,
                        origin_id=orig,
                        dest_id=dest,
                        description=f"{self.description} [Blocked {orig}->{dest}]",
                    )
                )

        if self.origin_id is not None or self.dest_id is not None or self.radius_meters is not None:
            incidents.append(
                SimulatedIncident(
                    event_id=self.event_id,
                    speed_multiplier=0.0,
                    start_time=self.trigger_time,
                    end_time=self.end_time,
                    origin_id=self.origin_id,
                    dest_id=self.dest_id,
                    center_lat=self.center_lat,
                    center_lon=self.center_lon,
                    radius_meters=self.radius_meters,
                    description=self.description,
                )
            )

        return incidents


class VehicleDelay(DynamicEvent):
    """Sudden delay experienced by a specific vehicle (breakdown, inspection, dwell)."""

    event_type: Literal["vehicle_delay"] = "vehicle_delay"
    vehicle_id: str = Field(..., description="ID of the affected vehicle")
    delay_seconds: float = Field(..., gt=0.0, description="Additional delay incurred in seconds")
    stop_id: str | None = Field(None, description="Optional stop ID where delay occurred")

    def __init__(self, **data: Any) -> None:
        super().__init__(**data)
        # For VehicleDelay, duration defaults to delay_seconds
        if self.duration == 0.0:
            self.duration = self.delay_seconds


class NewOrder(DynamicEvent):
    """Dynamic on-demand customer order appearing during execution."""

    event_type: Literal["new_order"] = "new_order"
    customer: Customer = Field(..., description="New customer stop specification")


# Discriminated union of all supported dynamic event types
AnyDynamicEvent = Annotated[
    Union[EdgeCongestion, RoadBlockage, VehicleDelay, NewOrder],
    Field(discriminator="event_type"),
]


class Scenario(BaseModel):
    """Complete dynamic scenario containing scheduled events over a time horizon."""

    id: str = Field(..., description="Unique scenario identifier")
    name: str = Field("Route.it Scenario", description="Scenario display name")
    start_time: float = Field(0.0, ge=0.0, description="Simulation start time in seconds")
    duration: float = Field(86400.0, gt=0.0, description="Scenario horizon duration in seconds")
    events: list[AnyDynamicEvent] = Field(default_factory=list, description="Scheduled events")

    @property
    def end_time(self) -> float:
        """Scenario conclusion timestamp."""
        return self.start_time + self.duration

    def to_json(self, indent: int = 2) -> str:
        """Serialize scenario to formatted JSON string."""
        return self.model_dump_json(indent=indent)

    def save(self, path: Path | str) -> None:
        """Write scenario to JSON file."""
        target = Path(path)
        target.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        target.write_text(self.to_json(), encoding="utf-8")

    @classmethod
    def from_json(cls, json_str: str) -> Scenario:
        """Load scenario from JSON string."""
        return cls.model_validate_json(json_str)

    @classmethod
    def load(cls, path: Path | str) -> Scenario:
        """Read and parse scenario from JSON file."""
        content = Path(path).read_text(encoding="utf-8")
        return cls.from_json(content)


def generate_random_scenario(
    problem: Problem,
    num_events: int = 5,
    event_types: list[str] | None = None,
    seed: int = 42,
    start_time: float = 0.0,
    time_horizon: float = 3600.0,
) -> Scenario:
    """Deterministically generate a repeatable dynamic scenario for a problem.

    Uses an explicit seed to guarantee reproducibility.

    Args:
        problem: Problem instance providing customers, depot, and vehicles.
        num_events: Total number of dynamic events to generate.
        event_types: Subset of ["edge_congestion", "road_blockage", "vehicle_delay", "new_order"].
        seed: Random seed for reproducibility.
        start_time: Simulation start time in seconds.
        time_horizon: Time window across which events are scheduled.

    Returns:
        Fully populated, deterministic Scenario.
    """
    if num_events <= 0:
        return Scenario(
            id=f"scenario_{problem.id}_empty",
            name=f"Empty Scenario for {problem.id}",
            start_time=start_time,
            duration=time_horizon,
            events=[],
        )

    allowed_types = event_types or [
        "edge_congestion",
        "road_blockage",
        "vehicle_delay",
        "new_order",
    ]
    rng = random.Random(seed)

    all_node_ids = [problem.depot.id] + [c.id for c in problem.customers]
    events: list[AnyDynamicEvent] = []

    for i in range(num_events):
        event_id = f"evt_{i + 1:03d}"
        chosen_type = rng.choice(allowed_types)
        # Schedule event between start_time + 10% horizon and 85% horizon
        trigger_time = round(start_time + rng.uniform(0.10, 0.85) * time_horizon, 1)
        duration = round(rng.uniform(300.0, min(1800.0, time_horizon * 0.4)), 1)

        if chosen_type == "edge_congestion":
            orig = rng.choice(all_node_ids)
            dest_candidates = [nid for nid in all_node_ids if nid != orig]
            dest = rng.choice(dest_candidates)
            multiplier = round(rng.uniform(1.5, 3.5), 2)
            events.append(
                EdgeCongestion(
                    event_id=event_id,
                    trigger_time=trigger_time,
                    duration=duration,
                    origin_id=orig,
                    dest_id=dest,
                    multiplier=multiplier,
                    description=f"Traffic slowdown {multiplier}x between {orig} and {dest}",
                )
            )

        elif chosen_type == "road_blockage":
            orig = rng.choice(all_node_ids)
            dest_candidates = [nid for nid in all_node_ids if nid != orig]
            dest = rng.choice(dest_candidates)
            events.append(
                RoadBlockage(
                    event_id=event_id,
                    trigger_time=trigger_time,
                    duration=duration,
                    origin_id=orig,
                    dest_id=dest,
                    description=f"Road blockage between {orig} and {dest}",
                )
            )

        elif chosen_type == "vehicle_delay":
            veh = rng.choice(problem.vehicles)
            delay_sec = round(rng.uniform(120.0, 600.0), 1)
            target_stop = rng.choice([c.id for c in problem.customers])
            events.append(
                VehicleDelay(
                    event_id=event_id,
                    trigger_time=trigger_time,
                    vehicle_id=veh.id,
                    delay_seconds=delay_sec,
                    stop_id=target_stop,
                    description=f"Vehicle {veh.id} delayed by {delay_sec}s at stop {target_stop}",
                )
            )

        elif chosen_type == "new_order":
            # Generate a new customer nearby the existing customers
            ref_cust = rng.choice(problem.customers)
            # Jitter coordinates slightly
            lat_jitter = rng.uniform(-0.015, 0.015)
            lon_jitter = rng.uniform(-0.015, 0.015)
            avg_demand = sum(c.demand for c in problem.customers) / len(problem.customers)
            new_cust = Customer(
                id=f"cust_dynamic_{i + 1}",
                lat=round(ref_cust.lat + lat_jitter, 6),
                lon=round(ref_cust.lon + lon_jitter, 6),
                demand=round(rng.uniform(0.5 * avg_demand, 1.2 * avg_demand), 1),
                ready_time=trigger_time + rng.uniform(60.0, 300.0),
                due_time=trigger_time + rng.uniform(1200.0, 2400.0),
                service_time=ref_cust.service_time,
                priority=rng.choice([1, 1, 2, 3]),
            )
            events.append(
                NewOrder(
                    event_id=event_id,
                    trigger_time=trigger_time,
                    duration=0.0,
                    customer=new_cust,
                    description=f"New urgent on-demand order {new_cust.id} (demand: {new_cust.demand})",
                )
            )

    # Sort events by trigger_time
    events.sort(key=lambda e: e.trigger_time)

    return Scenario(
        id=f"scenario_{problem.id}_seed{seed}",
        name=f"Deterministic Scenario for {problem.id} (seed={seed})",
        start_time=start_time,
        duration=time_horizon,
        events=events,
    )


=== FILE: dynamic/reoptimizer.py ===
"""Dynamic Route Re-optimizer with Tiered Escalation for Route.it.

Features:
- Impact Check: Evaluates cost delta (delta F) and critical road blockages.
- Stability Penalty: Freezes visited and committed stops, penalizing route flip-flopping.
- Tier 1 Path Repair: Re-evaluates timeline maintaining stop sequences.
- Tier 2 Local Repair: Targeted relocate and swap operators on affected uncommitted stops.
- Tier 3 QPSO Warm Start: Swarm seeded around previous keys with reduced iterations and elevated alpha.
- Escalation Logic: Ascends tiers by severity; accepts result only if feasible and superior to do-nothing.
- Audit Logging: Accurate wall-clock timing, cost delta, and stop mutation tracking.
- Full-Restart Baseline: Standard cold-start benchmark comparator.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import copy
from enum import Enum
import logging
import math
import time
from typing import Any, Sequence
import numpy as np
from pydantic import BaseModel, Field

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

from dynamic.events import AnyDynamicEvent
from routing.cost_oracle import CostOracle, Location, to_location
from solver.local_search import project_tour_to_keys
from solver.models import Customer, Depot, Problem, Route, Solution, Vehicle
from solver.qpso import QPSOConfig, solve_qpso
from solver.validate import ValidationResult, validate_solution


class ReoptimizationTier(str, Enum):
    """Categorization of re-optimization tiers."""

    DO_NOTHING = "DO_NOTHING"
    TIER_1_PATH_REPAIR = "TIER_1_PATH_REPAIR"
    TIER_2_LOCAL_REPAIR = "TIER_2_LOCAL_REPAIR"
    TIER_3_QPSO_WARM_START = "TIER_3_QPSO_WARM_START"
    FALLBACK = "FALLBACK"


class ReoptimizerConfig(BaseModel):
    """Configuration settings for dynamic route re-optimization."""

    theta: float = Field(0.05, ge=0.0, description="Relative cost delta threshold to trigger re-planning (e.g. 5%)")
    theta_absolute: float = Field(15.0, ge=0.0, description="Absolute cost delta threshold in seconds/distance")
    commitment_horizon_seconds: float = Field(300.0, ge=0.0, description="Horizon within which imminent stops are frozen")
    lambda_veh: float = Field(50.0, ge=0.0, description="Stability penalty per customer moved to a different vehicle")
    lambda_order: float = Field(10.0, ge=0.0, description="Stability penalty per customer order inversion on same vehicle")

    # Tier 3 QPSO Warm Start settings
    tier3_max_iters: int = Field(40, ge=5, description="Reduced iteration budget for warm-start swarm")
    tier3_particles: int = Field(30, ge=5, description="Swarm size for warm-start swarm")
    tier3_alpha_max: float = Field(1.2, gt=0.0, description="Elevated initial alpha for exploratory jumping")
    tier3_alpha_min: float = Field(0.4, gt=0.0, description="Final alpha for convergence")
    tier3_seed_fraction: float = Field(0.6, ge=0.1, le=1.0, description="Fraction of swarm seeded around previous keys")
    seed: int = Field(42, description="Random seed for reproducible re-optimization")


class ReoptimizationRecord(BaseModel):
    """Audit log entry capturing performance and outcome of a dynamic re-plan."""

    event_id: str = Field(..., description="Triggering event identifier or label")
    timestamp: float = Field(..., description="Simulation timestamp in seconds")
    tier_selected: ReoptimizationTier = Field(..., description="Executed re-optimization tier")
    wall_clock_time_ms: float = Field(..., description="Measured execution duration in milliseconds")
    cost_before: float = Field(..., description="Plan cost under new conditions prior to re-optimization")
    cost_after: float = Field(..., description="Final plan cost after re-optimization")
    delta_f: float = Field(..., description="Raw cost delta: cost_after - cost_before")
    stability_penalty: float = Field(0.0, description="Calculated penalty for stop reassignment and reordering")
    stops_changed_count: int = Field(0, description="Number of customer stops whose vehicle or order changed")
    stops_changed: list[str] = Field(default_factory=list, description="IDs of customer stops changed")
    is_feasible: bool = Field(..., description="Whether final solution satisfies all operational constraints")
    message: str = Field("", description="Summary description of re-optimization decision")


def partition_stops_by_commitment(
    route: Route,
    current_time: float,
    commitment_horizon: float,
    problem: Problem,
) -> tuple[list[str], list[str]]:
    """Partition a route's stops into frozen prefix stops and uncommitted suffix stops.

    - Visited stops (arrival + service <= current_time) are FROZEN.
    - Imminent stops (arrival - current_time <= commitment_horizon) are COMMITTED / FROZEN.
    - Remaining stops are UNCOMMITTED (eligible for re-planning).

    Args:
        route: Assigned vehicle route.
        current_time: Current simulation timestamp.
        commitment_horizon: Window in seconds defining driver commitment.
        problem: Problem instance.

    Returns:
        (frozen_stops, uncommitted_stops)
    """
    frozen: list[str] = []
    uncommitted: list[str] = []

    for idx, sid in enumerate(route.stop_ids):
        arr = route.arrival_times[idx] if idx < len(route.arrival_times) else 0.0
        cust = problem.get_customer(sid)

        # Stop is completed
        if arr + cust.service_time <= current_time:
            frozen.append(sid)
        # Stop is currently active or within commitment horizon
        elif arr - current_time <= commitment_horizon:
            frozen.append(sid)
        else:
            uncommitted.append(sid)

    return frozen, uncommitted


def calculate_stability_penalty(
    old_solution: Solution,
    new_solution: Solution,
    uncommitted_stops: set[str],
    config: ReoptimizerConfig,
) -> tuple[float, list[str]]:
    """Calculate the stability penalty for modifying uncommitted stops versus the previous plan.

    Penalizes:
    - Moving an uncommitted stop to a different vehicle (lambda_veh)
    - Inverting the relative order between two uncommitted stops on the same vehicle (lambda_order)

    Args:
        old_solution: Baseline solution prior to re-planning.
        new_solution: Proposed re-optimized solution.
        uncommitted_stops: Set of customer stop IDs that were eligible for re-planning.
        config: Reoptimizer configuration.

    Returns:
        (total_penalty, list_of_changed_stop_ids)
    """
    if not uncommitted_stops:
        return 0.0, []

    # Map customer ID to (vehicle_id, position) in old solution
    old_map: dict[str, tuple[str, int]] = {}
    for r in old_solution.routes:
        for pos, sid in enumerate(r.stop_ids):
            if sid in uncommitted_stops:
                old_map[sid] = (r.vehicle_id, pos)

    # Map customer ID to (vehicle_id, position) in new solution
    new_map: dict[str, tuple[str, int]] = {}
    for r in new_solution.routes:
        for pos, sid in enumerate(r.stop_ids):
            if sid in uncommitted_stops:
                new_map[sid] = (r.vehicle_id, pos)

    changed_stops: list[str] = []
    reassigned_count = 0
    inversion_count = 0

    # 1. Check vehicle reassignment
    for sid in uncommitted_stops:
        old_v = old_map.get(sid, (None, 0))[0]
        new_v = new_map.get(sid, (None, 0))[0]
        if old_v != new_v:
            reassigned_count += 1
            changed_stops.append(sid)

    # 2. Check relative order inversions on the same vehicle
    for r in new_solution.routes:
        v_stops = [sid for sid in r.stop_ids if sid in uncommitted_stops and old_map.get(sid, (None, 0))[0] == r.vehicle_id]
        n_v = len(v_stops)
        for i in range(n_v):
            for j in range(i + 1, n_v):
                s_i, s_j = v_stops[i], v_stops[j]
                old_pos_i = old_map[s_i][1]
                old_pos_j = old_map[s_j][1]
                # If order flipped
                if old_pos_i > old_pos_j:
                    inversion_count += 1
                    if s_i not in changed_stops:
                        changed_stops.append(s_i)
                    if s_j not in changed_stops:
                        changed_stops.append(s_j)

    penalty = config.lambda_veh * reassigned_count + config.lambda_order * inversion_count
    return penalty, changed_stops


def evaluate_solution_under_oracle(
    solution: Solution,
    problem: Problem,
    cost_oracle: CostOracle,
    current_time: float = 0.0,
) -> tuple[float, bool, bool]:
    """Evaluate a solution under the given CostOracle.

    Returns:
        (total_cost, has_blockage, has_tw_violation)
    """
    total_cost = 0.0
    has_blockage = False
    has_tw_viol = False

    depot_loc = to_location(problem.depot)

    for r in solution.routes:
        if not r.stop_ids:
            continue

        veh = next((v for v in problem.vehicles if v.id == r.vehicle_id), None)
        if veh is None:
            logger.warning(
                "Route vehicle_id '%s' not found in problem.vehicles; falling back to unit costs (c_dist=1.0, c_time=1.0)",
                r.vehicle_id,
            )
            c_dist = 1.0
            c_time = 1.0
        else:
            c_dist = veh.cost_per_distance
            c_time = veh.cost_per_time

        curr_time = max(current_time, problem.depot.ready_time)
        prev_loc = depot_loc
        route_dist = 0.0

        for sid in r.stop_ids:
            cust = problem.get_customer(sid)
            c_loc = to_location(cust)

            if not math.isfinite(curr_time):
                tt = float("inf")
                dist = 0.0
            else:
                tt = cost_oracle.travel_time(prev_loc, c_loc, depart_time=curr_time)
                dist = cost_oracle.distance(prev_loc, c_loc)

            if not math.isfinite(tt):
                has_blockage = True
                tt = 1e9  # Large finite number for cost accounting

            arr_time = curr_time + tt
            route_dist += dist

            if arr_time > cust.due_time:
                has_tw_viol = True
                total_cost += (arr_time - cust.due_time) * 100.0

            curr_time = (max(arr_time, cust.ready_time) + cust.service_time) if math.isfinite(arr_time) else float("inf")
            prev_loc = c_loc

        # Return to depot
        if math.isfinite(curr_time):
            tt_back = cost_oracle.travel_time(prev_loc, depot_loc, depart_time=curr_time)
            dist_back = cost_oracle.distance(prev_loc, depot_loc)
            route_dist += dist_back
            if not math.isfinite(tt_back):
                has_blockage = True
                route_dur = 1e7
            else:
                route_dur = (curr_time + tt_back) - max(current_time, problem.depot.ready_time)
        else:
            has_blockage = True
            route_dur = 1e7

        total_cost += route_dist * c_dist + route_dur * c_time

    if has_blockage:
        total_cost += 1e7

    return total_cost, has_blockage, has_tw_viol


class DynamicReoptimizer:
    """Tiered dynamic re-optimizer for Route.it fleet routing."""

    def __init__(
        self,
        problem: Problem,
        cost_oracle: CostOracle | None = None,
        config: ReoptimizerConfig | None = None,
    ) -> None:
        """Initialize re-optimizer.

        Args:
            problem: Global CVRPTW problem instance.
            cost_oracle: CostOracle representing active road and traffic network.
            config: Configuration parameters.
        """
        from routing.cost_oracle import EuclideanOracle

        self.problem = problem
        self.cost_oracle = cost_oracle or EuclideanOracle(
            coordinate_system=problem.coordinate_system,
            base_speed_mps=8.333 if problem.coordinate_system == "geographic" else 1.0,
        )
        self.config = config or ReoptimizerConfig()
        self.audit_log: list[ReoptimizationRecord] = []

    def _calc_route_cost(self, r: Route) -> float:
        """Calculate route cost with explicit warning if vehicle ID is not in problem.vehicles."""
        veh = next((v for v in self.problem.vehicles if v.id == r.vehicle_id), None) if self.problem.vehicles else None
        if veh is None:
            logger.warning(
                "Route vehicle_id '%s' not found in problem.vehicles; falling back to unit costs (c_dist=1.0, c_time=1.0)",
                r.vehicle_id,
            )
            c_dist = 1.0
            c_time = 1.0
        else:
            c_dist = veh.cost_per_distance
            c_time = veh.cost_per_time
        return r.distance * c_dist + r.duration * c_time

    def check_impact(
        self,
        current_solution: Solution,
        current_time: float = 0.0,
    ) -> tuple[bool, str, float, bool]:
        """Check whether the new traffic conditions require re-planning.

        Re-plans only if:
        1. A critical edge on a remaining planned route is blocked (travel_time == inf), OR
        2. A time-window violation occurs, OR
        3. Delta F > theta * cost_before and Delta F > theta_absolute.

        Returns:
            (should_replan, reason, delta_f, is_blocked)
        """
        cost_under_new, is_blocked, has_tw_viol = evaluate_solution_under_oracle(
            current_solution, self.problem, self.cost_oracle, current_time
        )
        cost_before = sum(self._calc_route_cost(r) for r in current_solution.routes)
        if cost_before <= 0.0:
            cost_before = current_solution.total_cost
        delta_f = cost_under_new - cost_before

        if is_blocked:
            return True, "Critical planned corridor is blocked", delta_f, True

        if has_tw_viol:
            return True, f"Time-window breach under new traffic (delta_F={delta_f:.1f})", delta_f, False

        rel_delta = delta_f / max(1.0, cost_before)
        if delta_f > self.config.theta_absolute and rel_delta > self.config.theta:
            return (
                True,
                f"Significant traffic delay: delta_F={delta_f:.1f} ({rel_delta * 100:.1f}% > {self.config.theta * 100:.1f}%)",
                delta_f,
                False,
            )

        return (
            False,
            f"Traffic impact within tolerance: delta_F={delta_f:.1f} ({rel_delta * 100:.1f}% <= {self.config.theta * 100:.1f}%)",
            delta_f,
            False,
        )

    def reoptimize(
        self,
        current_solution: Solution,
        current_time: float = 0.0,
        event: AnyDynamicEvent | None = None,
    ) -> tuple[Solution, ReoptimizationRecord]:
        """Execute tiered re-optimization triggered by a dynamic traffic event.

        Ascends through:
        - Tier 1: Path Repair (re-route segment, keep stop sequence)
        - Tier 2: Local Repair (relocate/swap only affected stops/routes)
        - Tier 3: QPSO Warm Start (swarm seeded around previous solution keys)
        - Fallback: Best feasible solution or do-nothing plan.

        Args:
            current_solution: Current active fleet solution.
            current_time: Simulation timestamp.
            event: Optional triggering event.

        Returns:
            (reoptimized_solution, audit_record)
        """
        start_wall_clock = time.perf_counter()
        event_id = event.event_id if event is not None else f"evt_t{current_time:.0f}"

        # 1. Impact Assessment
        should_replan, reason, delta_f, is_blocked = self.check_impact(
            current_solution, current_time
        )

        cost_before = current_solution.total_cost

        # Determine uncommitted stops across all routes
        all_uncommitted: set[str] = set()
        frozen_by_vehicle: dict[str, list[str]] = {}
        uncommitted_by_vehicle: dict[str, list[str]] = {}

        for r in current_solution.routes:
            frz, unc = partition_stops_by_commitment(
                r, current_time, self.config.commitment_horizon_seconds, self.problem
            )
            frozen_by_vehicle[r.vehicle_id] = frz
            uncommitted_by_vehicle[r.vehicle_id] = unc
            all_uncommitted.update(unc)

        if not should_replan or not all_uncommitted:
            # Do Nothing: Keep current plan
            elapsed_ms = (time.perf_counter() - start_wall_clock) * 1000.0
            rec = ReoptimizationRecord(
                event_id=event_id,
                timestamp=current_time,
                tier_selected=ReoptimizationTier.DO_NOTHING,
                wall_clock_time_ms=round(elapsed_ms, 3),
                cost_before=round(cost_before, 2),
                cost_after=round(cost_before, 2),
                delta_f=round(delta_f, 2),
                stability_penalty=0.0,
                stops_changed_count=0,
                stops_changed=[],
                is_feasible=current_solution.is_feasible,
                message=reason,
            )
            self.audit_log.append(rec)
            return current_solution, rec

        # Evaluate do-nothing baseline under new conditions
        cost_do_nothing, dn_blocked, dn_tw_viol = evaluate_solution_under_oracle(
            current_solution, self.problem, self.cost_oracle, current_time
        )
        cost_do_nothing = round(cost_do_nothing, 2)
        best_objective = cost_do_nothing if not dn_blocked and not dn_tw_viol else float("inf")
        best_solution = current_solution
        chosen_tier = ReoptimizationTier.FALLBACK
        stops_changed_best: list[str] = []
        stability_pen_best = 0.0

        # -------------------------------------------------------------------
        # TIER 1: Path Repair (Keep stop sequence, update timeline via router)
        # -------------------------------------------------------------------
        t1_sol = self._tier_1_path_repair(current_solution, current_time)
        t1_report = validate_solution(self.problem, t1_sol, cost_oracle=self.cost_oracle)

        if t1_report.is_valid:
            t1_stab, t1_changed = calculate_stability_penalty(
                current_solution, t1_sol, all_uncommitted, self.config
            )
            t1_total_obj = t1_sol.total_cost + t1_stab
            if t1_total_obj <= best_objective + 0.05:
                best_objective = t1_total_obj
                best_solution = t1_sol
                chosen_tier = ReoptimizationTier.TIER_1_PATH_REPAIR
                stops_changed_best = t1_changed
                stability_pen_best = t1_stab

        # If Tier 1 resolved the issue satisfactorily and did not encounter blockages, accept Tier 1
        if chosen_tier == ReoptimizationTier.TIER_1_PATH_REPAIR and not is_blocked:
            elapsed_ms = (time.perf_counter() - start_wall_clock) * 1000.0
            rec = ReoptimizationRecord(
                event_id=event_id,
                timestamp=current_time,
                tier_selected=chosen_tier,
                wall_clock_time_ms=round(elapsed_ms, 3),
                cost_before=round(cost_before, 2),
                cost_after=round(best_solution.total_cost, 2),
                delta_f=round(best_solution.total_cost - cost_before, 2),
                stability_penalty=round(stability_pen_best, 2),
                stops_changed_count=len(stops_changed_best),
                stops_changed=stops_changed_best,
                is_feasible=True,
                message="Accepted Tier 1 Path Repair (preserved stop sequence)",
            )
            self.audit_log.append(rec)
            return best_solution, rec

        # -------------------------------------------------------------------
        # TIER 2: Local Repair (Relocate/swap on affected uncommitted stops)
        # -------------------------------------------------------------------
        t2_sol = self._tier_2_local_repair(
            current_solution, frozen_by_vehicle, uncommitted_by_vehicle, current_time
        )
        t2_report = validate_solution(self.problem, t2_sol, cost_oracle=self.cost_oracle)

        if t2_report.is_valid:
            t2_stab, t2_changed = calculate_stability_penalty(
                current_solution, t2_sol, all_uncommitted, self.config
            )
            t2_total_obj = t2_sol.total_cost + t2_stab
            if t2_total_obj < best_objective:
                best_objective = t2_total_obj
                best_solution = t2_sol
                chosen_tier = ReoptimizationTier.TIER_2_LOCAL_REPAIR
                stops_changed_best = t2_changed
                stability_pen_best = t2_stab

        # If Tier 2 resolved the issue, accept Tier 2
        if chosen_tier == ReoptimizationTier.TIER_2_LOCAL_REPAIR:
            elapsed_ms = (time.perf_counter() - start_wall_clock) * 1000.0
            rec = ReoptimizationRecord(
                event_id=event_id,
                timestamp=current_time,
                tier_selected=chosen_tier,
                wall_clock_time_ms=round(elapsed_ms, 3),
                cost_before=round(cost_before, 2),
                cost_after=round(best_solution.total_cost, 2),
                delta_f=round(best_solution.total_cost - cost_before, 2),
                stability_penalty=round(stability_pen_best, 2),
                stops_changed_count=len(stops_changed_best),
                stops_changed=stops_changed_best,
                is_feasible=True,
                message="Accepted Tier 2 Local Repair (relocate/swap on affected routes)",
            )
            self.audit_log.append(rec)
            return best_solution, rec

        # -------------------------------------------------------------------
        # TIER 3: QPSO Warm Start (Swarm seeded around previous keys)
        # -------------------------------------------------------------------
        t3_sol = self._tier_3_qpso_warm_start(
            current_solution, frozen_by_vehicle, uncommitted_by_vehicle, current_time
        )
        t3_report = validate_solution(self.problem, t3_sol, cost_oracle=self.cost_oracle)

        if t3_report.is_valid:
            t3_stab, t3_changed = calculate_stability_penalty(
                current_solution, t3_sol, all_uncommitted, self.config
            )
            t3_total_obj = t3_sol.total_cost + t3_stab
            if t3_total_obj < best_objective:
                best_objective = t3_total_obj
                best_solution = t3_sol
                chosen_tier = ReoptimizationTier.TIER_3_QPSO_WARM_START
                stops_changed_best = t3_changed
                stability_pen_best = t3_stab

        # -------------------------------------------------------------------
        # Fallback / Final Evaluation
        # -------------------------------------------------------------------
        elapsed_ms = (time.perf_counter() - start_wall_clock) * 1000.0
        final_val = validate_solution(self.problem, best_solution, cost_oracle=self.cost_oracle)

        rec = ReoptimizationRecord(
            event_id=event_id,
            timestamp=current_time,
            tier_selected=chosen_tier,
            wall_clock_time_ms=round(elapsed_ms, 3),
            cost_before=round(cost_before, 2),
            cost_after=round(best_solution.total_cost, 2),
            delta_f=round(best_solution.total_cost - cost_before, 2),
            stability_penalty=round(stability_pen_best, 2),
            stops_changed_count=len(stops_changed_best),
            stops_changed=stops_changed_best,
            is_feasible=final_val.is_valid,
            message=(
                f"Accepted {chosen_tier.value} with cost {best_solution.total_cost:.2f}"
                if final_val.is_valid
                else "No feasible plan found across all tiers; fallback plan returned"
            ),
        )
        self.audit_log.append(rec)
        return best_solution, rec

    def _tier_1_path_repair(self, solution: Solution, current_time: float) -> Solution:
        """Tier 1: Retain stop sequences and update timeline using router/oracle."""
        rebuilt_routes: list[Route] = []
        depot_loc = to_location(self.problem.depot)

        for r in solution.routes:
            if not r.stop_ids:
                rebuilt_routes.append(r.model_copy())
                continue

            curr_time = max(current_time, self.problem.depot.ready_time)
            prev_loc = depot_loc
            arrival_times: list[float] = []
            route_dist = 0.0

            for sid in r.stop_ids:
                cust = self.problem.get_customer(sid)
                c_loc = to_location(cust)
                if not math.isfinite(curr_time):
                    tt = float("inf")
                    dist = 0.0
                else:
                    tt = self.cost_oracle.travel_time(prev_loc, c_loc, depart_time=curr_time)
                    dist = self.cost_oracle.distance(prev_loc, c_loc)

                route_dist += dist
                arr = curr_time + tt
                arrival_times.append(arr)
                curr_time = (max(arr, cust.ready_time) + cust.service_time) if math.isfinite(arr) else float("inf")
                prev_loc = c_loc

            # Return to depot
            if math.isfinite(curr_time):
                route_dist += self.cost_oracle.distance(prev_loc, depot_loc)
                tt_depot = self.cost_oracle.travel_time(prev_loc, depot_loc, depart_time=curr_time)
                total_duration = (curr_time + tt_depot) - max(current_time, self.problem.depot.ready_time)
            else:
                total_duration = float("inf")

            rebuilt_routes.append(
                Route(
                    vehicle_id=r.vehicle_id,
                    stop_ids=list(r.stop_ids),
                    arrival_times=arrival_times,
                    load=r.load,
                    distance=round(route_dist, 2),
                    duration=round(total_duration, 2),
                )
            )

        tot_cost = sum(self._calc_route_cost(r) for r in rebuilt_routes)
        return Solution(
            routes=rebuilt_routes,
            total_cost=round(tot_cost, 2),
            is_feasible=True,
            violations={},
            algorithm="Tier1_PathRepair",
            execution_time_sec=0.0,
            seed=self.config.seed,
        )

    def _tier_2_local_repair(
        self,
        solution: Solution,
        frozen: dict[str, list[str]],
        uncommitted: dict[str, list[str]],
        current_time: float,
    ) -> Solution:
        """Tier 2: Relocate and swap operations focused strictly on uncommitted stops."""
        routes_dict: dict[str, list[str]] = {
            r.vehicle_id: list(r.stop_ids) for r in solution.routes
        }

        # Build flat list of uncommitted stops
        uncommitted_stops_list: list[str] = []
        for stops in uncommitted.values():
            uncommitted_stops_list.extend(stops)

        # Try relocating each uncommitted stop into alternative positions / routes
        best_sol = solution
        best_cost = evaluate_solution_under_oracle(solution, self.problem, self.cost_oracle, current_time)[0]

        for sid in uncommitted_stops_list:
            # Locate current vehicle holding sid
            cur_v = next((v for v, r in routes_dict.items() if sid in r), None)
            if cur_v is None:
                continue

            cur_route = routes_dict[cur_v]
            cur_frozen_len = len(frozen.get(cur_v, []))
            if sid not in cur_route:
                continue
            cur_idx = cur_route.index(sid)
            if cur_idx < cur_frozen_len:
                continue  # Committed stop, cannot move

            best_move_dict: dict[str, list[str]] | None = None
            best_move_sol: Solution | None = None
            best_move_cost = best_cost

            # 1. Try Intra-route relocate
            for target_pos in range(cur_frozen_len, len(cur_route)):
                if target_pos == cur_idx:
                    continue
                cand_route = list(cur_route)
                cand_route.remove(sid)
                cand_route.insert(target_pos, sid)

                cand_routes_dict = dict(routes_dict)
                cand_routes_dict[cur_v] = cand_route

                cand_sol = self._build_solution_from_dict(cand_routes_dict, current_time)
                rep = validate_solution(self.problem, cand_sol, cost_oracle=self.cost_oracle)
                if rep.is_valid and cand_sol.total_cost < best_move_cost:
                    best_move_cost = cand_sol.total_cost
                    best_move_sol = cand_sol
                    best_move_dict = cand_routes_dict

            # 2. Try Inter-route relocate to another vehicle
            for tgt_v, tgt_route in routes_dict.items():
                if tgt_v == cur_v:
                    continue
                tgt_frozen_len = len(frozen.get(tgt_v, []))

                for target_pos in range(tgt_frozen_len, len(tgt_route) + 1):
                    cand_src = list(cur_route)
                    cand_src.remove(sid)
                    cand_tgt = list(tgt_route)
                    cand_tgt.insert(target_pos, sid)

                    cand_routes_dict = dict(routes_dict)
                    cand_routes_dict[cur_v] = cand_src
                    cand_routes_dict[tgt_v] = cand_tgt

                    cand_sol = self._build_solution_from_dict(cand_routes_dict, current_time)
                    rep = validate_solution(self.problem, cand_sol, cost_oracle=self.cost_oracle)
                    if rep.is_valid and cand_sol.total_cost < best_move_cost:
                        best_move_cost = cand_sol.total_cost
                        best_move_sol = cand_sol
                        best_move_dict = cand_routes_dict

            if best_move_dict is not None and best_move_sol is not None:
                routes_dict = best_move_dict
                best_cost = best_move_cost
                best_sol = best_move_sol

        return best_sol

    def _tier_3_qpso_warm_start(
        self,
        solution: Solution,
        frozen: dict[str, list[str]],
        uncommitted: dict[str, list[str]],
        current_time: float,
    ) -> Solution:
        """Tier 3: Swarm metaheuristic seeded around previous solution keys with reduced iterations."""
        all_uncommitted_stops: list[str] = []
        for unc in uncommitted.values():
            all_uncommitted_stops.extend(unc)

        if not all_uncommitted_stops:
            return solution

        # Construct sub-problem for uncommitted stops
        uncommitted_customers = [self.problem.get_customer(sid) for sid in all_uncommitted_stops]
        sub_problem = Problem(
            id=f"{self.problem.id}_uncommitted",
            depot=self.problem.depot,
            customers=uncommitted_customers,
            vehicles=self.problem.vehicles,
            coordinate_system=self.problem.coordinate_system,
        )

        n_unc = len(uncommitted_customers)
        # Project previous order to continuous keys
        prev_tour = list(range(1, n_unc + 1))
        old_keys = np.linspace(0.1, 0.9, n_unc)
        seed_keys = project_tour_to_keys(old_keys, prev_tour)

        qpso_config = QPSOConfig(
            num_particles=self.config.tier3_particles,
            max_iters=self.config.tier3_max_iters,
            alpha_max=self.config.tier3_alpha_max,
            alpha_min=self.config.tier3_alpha_min,
            seed=self.config.seed,
            apply_tw_repair=True,
        )

        sub_sol, _ = solve_qpso(
            problem=sub_problem,
            config=qpso_config,
            cost_oracle=self.cost_oracle,
            seed_keys=seed_keys,
            seed_fraction=self.config.tier3_seed_fraction,
        )

        # Re-attach frozen prefix stops to the newly re-routed uncommitted stops
        combined_routes_dict: dict[str, list[str]] = {v_id: list(frz) for v_id, frz in frozen.items()}

        for r in sub_sol.routes:
            if r.vehicle_id in combined_routes_dict:
                combined_routes_dict[r.vehicle_id].extend(r.stop_ids)
            else:
                # Assign to vehicle with smallest load
                min_v = min(combined_routes_dict.keys(), key=lambda v: len(combined_routes_dict[v]))
                combined_routes_dict[min_v].extend(r.stop_ids)

        return self._build_solution_from_dict(combined_routes_dict, current_time)

    def _build_solution_from_dict(
        self,
        routes_dict: dict[str, list[str]],
        current_time: float,
    ) -> Solution:
        """Helper to construct a Solution and compute timelines from a vehicle -> stop_ids mapping."""
        depot_loc = to_location(self.problem.depot)
        routes: list[Route] = []

        for v_id, stop_ids in routes_dict.items():
            if not stop_ids:
                routes.append(
                    Route(
                        vehicle_id=v_id,
                        stop_ids=[],
                        arrival_times=[],
                        load=0.0,
                        distance=0.0,
                        duration=0.0,
                    )
                )
                continue

            curr_time = max(current_time, self.problem.depot.ready_time)
            prev_loc = depot_loc
            arrival_times: list[float] = []
            route_dist = 0.0
            route_load = 0.0

            for sid in stop_ids:
                cust = self.problem.get_customer(sid)
                route_load += cust.demand
                c_loc = to_location(cust)

                if not math.isfinite(curr_time):
                    tt = float("inf")
                    dist = 0.0
                else:
                    tt = self.cost_oracle.travel_time(prev_loc, c_loc, depart_time=curr_time)
                    dist = self.cost_oracle.distance(prev_loc, c_loc)

                route_dist += dist
                arr = curr_time + tt
                arrival_times.append(arr)
                curr_time = (max(arr, cust.ready_time) + cust.service_time) if math.isfinite(arr) else float("inf")
                prev_loc = c_loc

            if math.isfinite(curr_time):
                route_dist += self.cost_oracle.distance(prev_loc, depot_loc)
                tt_depot = self.cost_oracle.travel_time(prev_loc, depot_loc, depart_time=curr_time)
                total_duration = (curr_time + tt_depot) - max(current_time, self.problem.depot.ready_time)
            else:
                total_duration = float("inf")

            routes.append(
                Route(
                    vehicle_id=v_id,
                    stop_ids=list(stop_ids),
                    arrival_times=arrival_times,
                    load=route_load,
                    distance=round(route_dist, 2),
                    duration=round(total_duration, 2),
                )
            )

        total_cost = sum(self._calc_route_cost(r) for r in routes)
        return Solution(
            routes=routes,
            total_cost=round(total_cost, 2),
            is_feasible=True,
            violations={},
            algorithm="DynamicReoptimizer",
            execution_time_sec=0.0,
            seed=self.config.seed,
        )


def full_restart_baseline(
    problem: Problem,
    current_solution: Solution,
    cost_oracle: CostOracle,
    current_time: float = 0.0,
    commitment_horizon: float = 300.0,
    seed: int = 42,
    max_iters: int = 150,
    particles: int = 35,
) -> tuple[Solution, float]:
    """Execute a full-restart baseline re-plan for uncommitted stops from cold scratch.

    Used as the scientific benchmark comparator against the tiered re-optimizer.

    Args:
        problem: Complete problem instance.
        current_solution: Current active solution.
        cost_oracle: CostOracle representing new conditions.
        current_time: Current simulation timestamp.
        commitment_horizon: Commitment window for freezing.
        seed: Random seed.
        max_iters: Iteration budget for cold start (standard 150).
        particles: Particle swarm size (standard 35).

    Returns:
        (restarted_solution, wall_clock_time_ms)
    """
    start = time.perf_counter()

    frozen: dict[str, list[str]] = {}
    uncommitted: list[str] = []

    for r in current_solution.routes:
        frz, unc = partition_stops_by_commitment(r, current_time, commitment_horizon, problem)
        frozen[r.vehicle_id] = frz
        uncommitted.extend(unc)

    if not uncommitted:
        elapsed = (time.perf_counter() - start) * 1000.0
        return current_solution, elapsed

    uncommitted_customers = [problem.get_customer(sid) for sid in uncommitted]
    sub_problem = Problem(
        id=f"{problem.id}_restart",
        depot=problem.depot,
        customers=uncommitted_customers,
        vehicles=problem.vehicles,
        coordinate_system=problem.coordinate_system,
    )

    cold_config = QPSOConfig(
        num_particles=particles,
        max_iters=max_iters,
        seed=seed,
        alpha_max=1.0,
        alpha_min=0.5,
        apply_tw_repair=True,
    )

    sub_sol, _ = solve_qpso(
        problem=sub_problem,
        config=cold_config,
        cost_oracle=cost_oracle,
    )

    # Stitch frozen prefixes with cold-started suffixes
    routes_dict: dict[str, list[str]] = {v_id: list(frz) for v_id, frz in frozen.items()}
    for r in sub_sol.routes:
        if r.vehicle_id in routes_dict:
            routes_dict[r.vehicle_id].extend(r.stop_ids)
        else:
            min_v = min(routes_dict.keys(), key=lambda v: len(routes_dict[v]))
            routes_dict[min_v].extend(r.stop_ids)

    reoptimizer = DynamicReoptimizer(problem, cost_oracle)
    final_sol = reoptimizer._build_solution_from_dict(routes_dict, current_time)
    elapsed = (time.perf_counter() - start) * 1000.0
    return final_sol, elapsed


=== FILE: dynamic/simulator.py ===
"""Discrete Dynamic Fleet Simulation Engine for Route.it.

Simulates fleet movement along planned CVRPTW routes over time, applying
dynamic incidents (traffic congestion, road blockages, vehicle delays, new orders),
modulating the CostOracle via TrafficAdapter, and emitting time-stamped state snapshots.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from enum import Enum
import math
from typing import Any
from pydantic import BaseModel, Field

from dynamic.events import (
    AnyDynamicEvent,
    EdgeCongestion,
    NewOrder,
    RoadBlockage,
    Scenario,
    VehicleDelay,
)
from routing.cost_oracle import (
    CostOracle,
    EuclideanOracle,
    Location,
    SimulatedEventAdapter,
    TimeOfDayProfileAdapter,
    to_location,
)
from solver.models import Customer, Depot, Problem, Route, Solution


class VehicleStatus(str, Enum):
    """Operational status of a vehicle during route execution."""

    IDLE = "IDLE"              # Waiting at depot before departure
    EN_ROUTE = "EN_ROUTE"      # Traveling along a road leg between stops
    WAITING = "WAITING"        # Arrived at customer before ready_time
    SERVICING = "SERVICING"    # Actively unloading / servicing customer
    COMPLETED = "COMPLETED"    # Completed all stops and returned to depot


class VehicleState(BaseModel):
    """Real-time operational snapshot of a single vehicle in the fleet."""

    vehicle_id: str
    route_index: int
    status: VehicleStatus
    current_coords: tuple[float, float] = Field(..., description="(lat, lon) current position")
    current_leg_origin_id: str
    current_leg_dest_id: str
    current_stop_index: int = Field(
        ...,
        description="Index of current stop in route.stop_ids (-1 before first customer, len after last)",
    )
    leg_departure_time: float
    leg_arrival_time: float
    service_start_time: float | None = None
    service_end_time: float | None = None
    current_load: float = 0.0
    visited_stops: list[str] = Field(default_factory=list)
    remaining_stops: list[str] = Field(default_factory=list)
    delay_seconds: float = 0.0


class SimulationSnapshot(BaseModel):
    """Global system snapshot emitted at a simulation clock tick."""

    timestamp: float = Field(..., description="Current simulation time in seconds")
    step: int = Field(..., description="Simulation step counter")
    vehicle_states: dict[str, VehicleState] = Field(
        ...,
        description="Current state of each vehicle keyed by vehicle_id",
    )
    active_events: list[str] = Field(
        default_factory=list,
        description="List of event IDs currently active in the traffic network",
    )
    pending_orders: list[Customer] = Field(
        default_factory=list,
        description="Unrouted on-demand dynamic customer orders",
    )
    all_completed: bool = Field(
        ...,
        description="Whether all active vehicles have finished and returned to depot",
    )


class FleetSimulator:
    """Discrete-time dynamic fleet simulator for CVRPTW executions."""

    def __init__(
        self,
        problem: Problem,
        solution: Solution,
        cost_oracle: CostOracle | None = None,
        traffic_adapter: SimulatedEventAdapter | None = None,
        scenario: Scenario | None = None,
        start_time: float = 0.0,
    ) -> None:
        """Initialize simulation environment.

        Args:
            problem: CVRPTW problem instance.
            solution: Planned multi-vehicle routing solution.
            cost_oracle: CostOracle for travel-time evaluation. Defaults to EuclideanOracle.
            traffic_adapter: SimulatedEventAdapter to apply dynamic traffic events to.
            scenario: Dynamic scenario containing scheduled events.
            start_time: Simulation starting clock in seconds (default: 0.0).
        """
        self.problem = problem
        self.solution = solution
        self.clock = float(start_time)
        self.step_count = 0
        self.scenario = scenario

        # Configure TrafficAdapter & CostOracle
        if traffic_adapter is not None:
            self.traffic_adapter = traffic_adapter
        elif cost_oracle is not None and isinstance(cost_oracle.traffic_adapter, SimulatedEventAdapter):
            self.traffic_adapter = cost_oracle.traffic_adapter
        else:
            self.traffic_adapter = SimulatedEventAdapter(
                base_adapter=TimeOfDayProfileAdapter({h: 1.0 for h in range(24)})
            )

        if cost_oracle is not None:
            self.cost_oracle = cost_oracle
            if self.cost_oracle.traffic_adapter is None:
                self.cost_oracle.traffic_adapter = self.traffic_adapter
        else:
            self.cost_oracle = EuclideanOracle(traffic_adapter=self.traffic_adapter)

        # Internal tracking
        self.active_events: dict[str, AnyDynamicEvent] = {}
        self.completed_events: set[str] = set()
        self.pending_orders: list[Customer] = []
        self.vehicle_routes: dict[str, Route] = {}
        self.vehicles: dict[str, VehicleState] = {}

        self._nodes: dict[str, Location] = {
            self.problem.depot.id: to_location(self.problem.depot)
        }
        for cust in self.problem.customers:
            self._nodes[cust.id] = to_location(cust)

        self._init_fleet()

    def _get_node(self, node_id: str) -> Location:
        """Fetch location for a given node ID."""
        if node_id in self._nodes:
            return self._nodes[node_id]
        if node_id == self.problem.depot.id:
            return to_location(self.problem.depot)
        cust = self.problem.get_customer(node_id)
        loc = to_location(cust)
        self._nodes[node_id] = loc
        return loc

    def _init_fleet(self) -> None:
        """Initialize vehicle states from planned solution routes."""
        depot_loc = (self.problem.depot.lat, self.problem.depot.lon)

        for route_idx, route in enumerate(self.solution.routes):
            v_id = route.vehicle_id
            self.vehicle_routes[v_id] = route

            if not route.stop_ids:
                # Empty route
                self.vehicles[v_id] = VehicleState(
                    vehicle_id=v_id,
                    route_index=route_idx,
                    status=VehicleStatus.COMPLETED,
                    current_coords=depot_loc,
                    current_leg_origin_id=self.problem.depot.id,
                    current_leg_dest_id=self.problem.depot.id,
                    current_stop_index=0,
                    leg_departure_time=self.clock,
                    leg_arrival_time=self.clock,
                    current_load=0.0,
                    visited_stops=[],
                    remaining_stops=[],
                )
                continue

            # First leg: Depot -> route.stop_ids[0]
            first_dest_id = route.stop_ids[0]
            first_cust = self.problem.get_customer(first_dest_id)

            # Earliest possible vehicle departure from depot
            veh_model = next((v for v in self.problem.vehicles if v.id == v_id), None)
            shift_start = veh_model.shift_start if veh_model else 0.0
            planned_depot_departure = max(self.clock, self.problem.depot.ready_time, shift_start)

            t_travel = self.cost_oracle.travel_time(
                self.problem.depot, first_cust, planned_depot_departure
            )
            planned_arrival = planned_depot_departure + t_travel

            is_idle = self.clock < planned_depot_departure
            status = VehicleStatus.IDLE if is_idle else VehicleStatus.EN_ROUTE

            self.vehicles[v_id] = VehicleState(
                vehicle_id=v_id,
                route_index=route_idx,
                status=status,
                current_coords=depot_loc,
                current_leg_origin_id=self.problem.depot.id,
                current_leg_dest_id=first_dest_id,
                current_stop_index=0,
                leg_departure_time=planned_depot_departure,
                leg_arrival_time=planned_arrival,
                current_load=0.0,
                visited_stops=[],
                remaining_stops=list(route.stop_ids),
                delay_seconds=0.0,
            )

    def apply_event(self, event: AnyDynamicEvent) -> None:
        """Dynamically apply an event to the simulation environment."""
        if event.event_id in self.active_events or event.event_id in self.completed_events:
            return

        if isinstance(event, (EdgeCongestion, RoadBlockage)):
            incidents = event.to_simulated_incidents()
            for inc in incidents:
                self.traffic_adapter.add_incident(inc)
            # If cost_oracle has cache, clear to reflect immediate traffic changes
            if getattr(self.cost_oracle, "cache", None) is not None:
                self.cost_oracle.cache.clear()
            self.active_events[event.event_id] = event

        elif isinstance(event, VehicleDelay):
            if event.vehicle_id in self.vehicles:
                v_state = self.vehicles[event.vehicle_id]
                v_state.delay_seconds += event.delay_seconds
                if v_state.status == VehicleStatus.EN_ROUTE:
                    v_state.leg_arrival_time += event.delay_seconds
                elif v_state.status == VehicleStatus.SERVICING and v_state.service_end_time is not None:
                    v_state.service_end_time += event.delay_seconds
                elif v_state.status == VehicleStatus.WAITING:
                    # Delay waiting/service start
                    v_state.leg_arrival_time += event.delay_seconds
                elif v_state.status == VehicleStatus.IDLE:
                    v_state.leg_departure_time += event.delay_seconds
                    v_state.leg_arrival_time += event.delay_seconds
            self.active_events[event.event_id] = event

        elif isinstance(event, NewOrder):
            self.pending_orders.append(event.customer)
            self._nodes[event.customer.id] = to_location(event.customer)
            self.active_events[event.event_id] = event

    def remove_event(self, event_id: str) -> None:
        """Deactivate and clean up an active event."""
        event = self.active_events.pop(event_id, None)
        if event is None:
            return

        self.completed_events.add(event_id)

        if isinstance(event, (EdgeCongestion, RoadBlockage)):
            incidents = event.to_simulated_incidents()
            for inc in incidents:
                self.traffic_adapter.remove_incident(inc.event_id)
            if getattr(self.cost_oracle, "cache", None) is not None:
                self.cost_oracle.cache.clear()

    def step(self, delta_seconds: float = 60.0) -> SimulationSnapshot:
        """Advance the simulation clock by delta_seconds and update all states.

        Args:
            delta_seconds: Amount of time in seconds to step forward.

        Returns:
            SimulationSnapshot capturing the state at the new timestamp.
        """
        if delta_seconds <= 0:
            raise ValueError("delta_seconds must be strictly positive")

        target_time = round(self.clock + delta_seconds, 6)
        self.clock = target_time
        self.step_count += 1

        # 1. Process Scenario Events
        if self.scenario is not None:
            for event in self.scenario.events:
                if event.trigger_time <= self.clock and event.event_id not in self.active_events and event.event_id not in self.completed_events:
                    self.apply_event(event)

        # Deactivate expired events
        expired_ids = [
            e_id for e_id, e in self.active_events.items()
            if e.end_time <= self.clock
        ]
        for e_id in expired_ids:
            self.remove_event(e_id)

        # 2. Advance Each Vehicle
        for v_id, v_state in self.vehicles.items():
            self._advance_vehicle(v_state)

        # 3. Build & Return Snapshot
        all_completed = all(v.status == VehicleStatus.COMPLETED for v in self.vehicles.values())
        return SimulationSnapshot(
            timestamp=self.clock,
            step=self.step_count,
            vehicle_states={k: v.model_copy() for k, v in self.vehicles.items()},
            active_events=list(self.active_events.keys()),
            pending_orders=list(self.pending_orders),
            all_completed=all_completed,
        )

    def _advance_vehicle(self, v_state: VehicleState) -> None:
        """Advance state and coordinates for a single vehicle at current clock."""
        if v_state.status == VehicleStatus.COMPLETED:
            return

        route = self.vehicle_routes[v_state.vehicle_id]

        # State: IDLE
        if v_state.status == VehicleStatus.IDLE:
            if self.clock >= v_state.leg_departure_time:
                v_state.status = VehicleStatus.EN_ROUTE

        # State: EN_ROUTE
        if v_state.status == VehicleStatus.EN_ROUTE:
            orig_loc = self._get_node(v_state.current_leg_origin_id)
            dest_loc = self._get_node(v_state.current_leg_dest_id)

            if self.clock < v_state.leg_arrival_time:
                # Interpolate coordinate position
                total_duration = max(0.001, v_state.leg_arrival_time - v_state.leg_departure_time)
                elapsed = max(0.0, self.clock - v_state.leg_departure_time)
                frac = min(1.0, elapsed / total_duration)

                cur_lat = orig_loc.lat + frac * (dest_loc.lat - orig_loc.lat)
                cur_lon = orig_loc.lon + frac * (dest_loc.lon - orig_loc.lon)
                v_state.current_coords = (round(cur_lat, 6), round(cur_lon, 6))
                return
            else:
                # Vehicle has arrived at destination stop
                v_state.current_coords = (dest_loc.lat, dest_loc.lon)

                # Check if arrived at final return to depot
                if v_state.current_leg_dest_id == self.problem.depot.id and v_state.current_stop_index >= len(route.stop_ids):
                    v_state.status = VehicleStatus.COMPLETED
                    return

                # Arrived at customer stop
                cust = self.problem.get_customer(v_state.current_leg_dest_id)
                arr_time = v_state.leg_arrival_time

                if self.clock < cust.ready_time:
                    v_state.status = VehicleStatus.WAITING
                    v_state.service_start_time = cust.ready_time
                    v_state.service_end_time = cust.ready_time + cust.service_time
                    return
                else:
                    v_state.status = VehicleStatus.SERVICING
                    srv_start = max(arr_time, cust.ready_time)
                    v_state.service_start_time = srv_start
                    v_state.service_end_time = srv_start + cust.service_time

        # State: WAITING
        if v_state.status == VehicleStatus.WAITING:
            cust = self.problem.get_customer(v_state.current_leg_dest_id)
            if self.clock >= cust.ready_time:
                v_state.status = VehicleStatus.SERVICING
                v_state.service_start_time = cust.ready_time
                v_state.service_end_time = cust.ready_time + cust.service_time

        # State: SERVICING
        if v_state.status == VehicleStatus.SERVICING:
            if v_state.service_end_time is not None and self.clock >= v_state.service_end_time:
                # Service complete! Record stop and transition
                completed_stop = v_state.current_leg_dest_id
                v_state.visited_stops.append(completed_stop)
                if completed_stop in v_state.remaining_stops:
                    v_state.remaining_stops.remove(completed_stop)

                cust = self.problem.get_customer(completed_stop)
                v_state.current_load += cust.demand

                # Plan next leg
                v_state.current_stop_index += 1
                departure_now = v_state.service_end_time

                if v_state.current_stop_index < len(route.stop_ids):
                    next_stop_id = route.stop_ids[v_state.current_stop_index]
                    next_loc = self._get_node(next_stop_id)
                else:
                    # All customer stops done -> return to depot
                    next_stop_id = self.problem.depot.id
                    next_loc = self._get_node(next_stop_id)

                cur_node = self._get_node(completed_stop)
                leg_t = self.cost_oracle.travel_time(cur_node, next_loc, departure_now)

                v_state.current_leg_origin_id = completed_stop
                v_state.current_leg_dest_id = next_stop_id
                v_state.leg_departure_time = departure_now
                v_state.leg_arrival_time = departure_now + leg_t
                v_state.service_start_time = None
                v_state.service_end_time = None

                if self.clock < v_state.leg_arrival_time:
                    v_state.status = VehicleStatus.EN_ROUTE
                    total_dur = max(0.001, v_state.leg_arrival_time - departure_now)
                    frac = min(1.0, max(0.0, self.clock - departure_now) / total_dur)
                    cur_lat = cur_node.lat + frac * (next_loc.lat - cur_node.lat)
                    cur_lon = cur_node.lon + frac * (next_loc.lon - cur_node.lon)
                    v_state.current_coords = (round(cur_lat, 6), round(cur_lon, 6))
                else:
                    # Fast step caught arrival directly
                    if next_stop_id == self.problem.depot.id:
                        v_state.status = VehicleStatus.COMPLETED
                        v_state.current_coords = (next_loc.lat, next_loc.lon)
                    else:
                        v_state.status = VehicleStatus.EN_ROUTE
                        self._advance_vehicle(v_state)

    def run_until(self, target_time: float, step_size: float = 60.0) -> list[SimulationSnapshot]:
        """Run simulation stepping forward until clock reaches target_time."""
        snapshots: list[SimulationSnapshot] = []
        while self.clock < target_time:
            delta = min(step_size, target_time - self.clock)
            snapshots.append(self.step(delta))
        return snapshots

    def run_to_completion(
        self, step_size: float = 60.0, max_steps: int = 10000
    ) -> list[SimulationSnapshot]:
        """Run simulation until all vehicles complete routes or max_steps reached."""
        snapshots: list[SimulationSnapshot] = []
        for _ in range(max_steps):
            snap = self.step(step_size)
            snapshots.append(snap)
            if snap.all_completed:
                break
        return snapshots

=== FILE: README.md ===
# Route.it 🚀

**Adaptive Quantum-Inspired Fleet Routing System**
*Smart India Hackathon 2026 (PS SIH26137: Quantum-Inspired Intelligent Traffic Route Optimization Using Metaheuristic Optimization)*

---

## 📌 Project Overview
**Route.it** solves the **Capacitated Vehicle Routing Problem with Time Windows (CVRPTW)** with time-dependent travel times, featuring real-time adaptive re-optimization under dynamic road events (traffic congestion, accidents, roadblocks).

> [!NOTE]
> **What does "Quantum-Inspired" mean?**
> It refers to a **CLASSICAL** metaheuristic algorithm executed on standard CPU infrastructure: **Quantum-behaved Particle Swarm Optimization (QPSO)** (Sun, Feng & Xu, 2004). No quantum hardware or external quantum simulation libraries are used.

---

## 🏛️ System Architecture

```mermaid
graph LR
    Input[Problem Input] --> Oracle[Cost Oracle / Valhalla]
    Oracle --> QPSO[QPSO Giant Tour]
    QPSO --> Split[Prins Split Algorithm]
    Split --> LocalSearch[Local Search 2-Opt/Relocate/Swap]
    LocalSearch --> OptimisedRoutes[Optimised Routes + ETAs]
    OptimisedRoutes --> Dynamic[Traffic Event & Impact Check]
    Dynamic --> TieredRepair[3-Tiered Repair System]
```

1. **Cost Oracle**: Valhalla routing client + local distance/time matrix calculator with disk and LRU cache.
2. **QPSO Permutation Engine**: Continuous random-key representation mapped to customer giant tours using quantum delta-potential well dynamics.
3. **Prins Split Algorithm**: Polynomial-time optimal partitioning of giant tours into feasible vehicle routes adhering strictly to vehicle capacities and customer time windows.
4. **Local Search Engine**: 2-Opt (intra-route), Relocate (inter-route), and Swap (inter-route) moves for local refinement.
5. **Dynamic Layer**:
   - Evaluates disruption impact ($\Delta F > \theta$).
   - **Tier 1**: Path repair (reroute edge via Valhalla alternative paths).
   - **Tier 2**: Local repair (local relocate/insertion).
   - **Tier 3**: QPSO warm start (particle swarm initialized around best tour with stability penalty).

---

```bash
# Clone repository
git clone <repo-url>
cd "SIH 2026 Antigravity"

# Using Makefile
make install

# Or manually using pip (includes uvicorn[standard] and websockets for real-time streaming)
pip install -e ".[dev]"
```

> [!NOTE]
> The real-time telemetry streaming endpoint (`/ws/problems/{id}`) requires `websockets` (provided automatically via `uvicorn[standard]` and `websockets` in `pyproject.toml`).

Verify the installation using `pytest`:
```bash

```

```bash

```

```bash
pre-commit install
```

## ⚖️ Integrity Rules
1. **Zero Fabrication**: Benchmark numbers and performance metrics are never simulated, estimated, or fabricated. Every reported result originates from experimental runs logged in `/benchmarks/results`.
2. **Honest Baselines**: If OR-Tools or any baseline outperforms our algorithm on a given instance, it is explicitly and honestly reported.
3. **Traffic Transparency**: Simulated traffic is explicitly designated as simulated; real live traffic claims require active provider integration.
4. **Reproducibility**: All metaheuristics accept explicit seeds to guarantee reproducible trajectories.
