=== FILE: routing/cost_oracle.py ===
"""Route.it Cost Oracle and Traffic Engine.

INTEGRITY / SIMULATION NOTICE:
------------------------------
Unless an authentic commercial or enterprise traffic provider adapter
(such as TomTom Routing API, Google Maps Platform, or HERE Traffic) is explicitly
configured, ALL traffic dynamics, speed profiles, and event slowdowns in this module
are SIMULATED (based on the time-of-day profile models of Ichoua et al. 2003
or synthetic runtime incident injections).
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from abc import ABC, abstractmethod
from collections import OrderedDict
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
import json
import logging
import math
import sqlite3
from typing import Any, Callable, Literal, Sequence
import urllib.error
import urllib.request

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Location & Domain Data Structures
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Location:
    """Geographic or planar location representation."""

    id: str
    lat: float
    lon: float


def to_location(loc: Any) -> Location:
    """Coerce various location-like inputs into a canonical Location.

    Supports:
    - Location instances
    - Objects with .id, .lat, .lon attributes (e.g., solver.models.Depot, Customer)
    - Dicts: {"id": str, "lat": float, "lon": float}
    - Tuples: (id, lat, lon)
    """
    if isinstance(loc, Location):
        return loc
    if hasattr(loc, "id") and hasattr(loc, "lat") and hasattr(loc, "lon"):
        return Location(id=str(loc.id), lat=float(loc.lat), lon=float(loc.lon))
    if isinstance(loc, dict):
        return Location(
            id=str(loc.get("id", f"loc_{loc['lat']}_{loc['lon']}")),
            lat=float(loc["lat"]),
            lon=float(loc["lon"]),
        )
    if isinstance(loc, (tuple, list)) and len(loc) == 3:
        return Location(id=str(loc[0]), lat=float(loc[1]), lon=float(loc[2]))
    raise TypeError(f"Cannot coerce {type(loc)} to Location: {loc!r}")


@dataclass
class MatrixResult:
    """Distance and duration matrices for a set of points."""

    durations: list[list[float]]  # [N][N] in seconds
    distances: list[list[float]]  # [N][N] in metres

    def __iter__(self):
        """Allow tuple unpacking: durations, distances = result."""
        return iter((self.durations, self.distances))


@dataclass
class StopTimeline:
    """Timeline metrics for a single visited stop in a route."""

    stop_id: str
    arrival_time: float
    service_start_time: float
    departure_time: float
    wait_time: float
    travel_time_from_prev: float
    distance_from_prev: float
    lateness: float


@dataclass
class RouteTimeline:
    """Complete time-dependent evaluation of a multi-stop vehicle route."""

    stops: list[StopTimeline]
    total_travel_time: float
    total_distance: float
    total_duration: float
    total_wait_time: float
    total_lateness: float


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Caching Subsystem
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

@dataclass
class CacheStats:
    """Hit and miss statistics for cost caches."""

    hits: int = 0
    misses: int = 0

    @property
    def total_lookups(self) -> int:
        return self.hits + self.misses

    @property
    def hit_ratio(self) -> float:
        if self.total_lookups == 0:
            return 0.0
        return self.hits / self.total_lookups


class CostCache(ABC):
    """Abstract interface for travel-time and distance caching."""

    def __init__(self, bucket_size_seconds: float = 900.0) -> None:
        """Initialize cache.

        Args:
            bucket_size_seconds: Duration of time bucket in seconds (default: 15 min = 900s).
        """
        if bucket_size_seconds <= 0:
            raise ValueError("bucket_size_seconds must be positive")
        self.bucket_size_seconds = float(bucket_size_seconds)
        self.stats = CacheStats()

    def get_time_bucket(self, depart_time: float) -> int:
        """Map continuous departure timestamp to an integer bucket index."""
        return int(math.floor(depart_time / self.bucket_size_seconds))

    @abstractmethod
    def get(
        self, origin_id: str, dest_id: str, time_bucket: int
    ) -> tuple[float, float] | None:
        """Retrieve (duration_seconds, distance_metres) from cache."""
        ...

    @abstractmethod
    def set(
        self,
        origin_id: str,
        dest_id: str,
        time_bucket: int,
        duration: float,
        distance: float,
    ) -> None:
        """Store (duration_seconds, distance_metres) in cache."""
        ...

    @abstractmethod
    def clear(self) -> None:
        """Clear all cached entries and reset statistics."""
        ...


class InMemoryLRUCache(CostCache):
    """In-memory Least Recently Used (LRU) cost cache with hit/miss tracking."""

    def __init__(
        self,
        max_size: int = 50_000,
        bucket_size_seconds: float = 900.0,
    ) -> None:
        super().__init__(bucket_size_seconds=bucket_size_seconds)
        self.max_size = max_size
        self._store: OrderedDict[tuple[str, str, int], tuple[float, float]] = OrderedDict()

    def get(
        self, origin_id: str, dest_id: str, time_bucket: int
    ) -> tuple[float, float] | None:
        key = (origin_id, dest_id, time_bucket)
        if key in self._store:
            self._store.move_to_end(key)
            self.stats.hits += 1
            return self._store[key]
        self.stats.misses += 1
        return None

    def set(
        self,
        origin_id: str,
        dest_id: str,
        time_bucket: int,
        duration: float,
        distance: float,
    ) -> None:
        key = (origin_id, dest_id, time_bucket)
        if key in self._store:
            self._store.move_to_end(key)
        self._store[key] = (float(duration), float(distance))
        if len(self._store) > self.max_size:
            self._store.popitem(last=False)

    def clear(self) -> None:
        self._store.clear()
        self.stats.hits = 0
        self.stats.misses = 0


class SQLiteCache(CostCache):
    """Persistent or in-memory SQLite-backed cost cache."""

    def __init__(
        self,
        db_path: str = ":memory:",
        bucket_size_seconds: float = 900.0,
    ) -> None:
        super().__init__(bucket_size_seconds=bucket_size_seconds)
        self.db_path = db_path
        self._conn = sqlite3.connect(self.db_path)
        self._create_table()

    def _create_table(self) -> None:
        with self._conn:
            self._conn.execute(
                """
                CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS cost_cache (
                    origin_id TEXT NOT NULL,
                    dest_id TEXT NOT NULL,
                    time_bucket INTEGER NOT NULL,
                    duration REAL NOT NULL,
                    distance REAL NOT NULL,
                    PRIMARY KEY (origin_id, dest_id, time_bucket)
                )
                """
            )

    def get(
        self, origin_id: str, dest_id: str, time_bucket: int
    ) -> tuple[float, float] | None:
        cursor = self._conn.cursor()
        cursor.execute(
            """
            SELECT duration, distance FROM cost_cache
            WHERE origin_id = ? AND dest_id = ? AND time_bucket = ?
            """,
            (origin_id, dest_id, time_bucket),
        )
        row = cursor.fetchone()
        if row is not None:
            self.stats.hits += 1
            return (float(row[0]), float(row[1]))
        self.stats.misses += 1
        return None

    def set(
        self,
        origin_id: str,
        dest_id: str,
        time_bucket: int,
        duration: float,
        distance: float,
    ) -> None:
        with self._conn:
            self._conn.execute(
                """
                INSERT OR REPLACE INTO cost_cache
                (origin_id, dest_id, time_bucket, duration, distance)
                VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
                """,
                (origin_id, dest_id, time_bucket, float(duration), float(distance)),
            )

    def clear(self) -> None:
        with self._conn:
            self._conn.execute("DELETE FROM cost_cache")
        self.stats.hits = 0
        self.stats.misses = 0


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Traffic Adapters (Simulated & Provider Stubs)
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

class TrafficAdapter(ABC):
    """Abstract interface for time-dependent traffic conditions.

    NOTICE: All sub-classes except authenticated ProviderAdapters generate
    SIMULATED traffic conditions.
    """

    @abstractmethod
    def get_speed_multiplier(
        self, origin: Location, dest: Location, depart_time: float
    ) -> float:
        """Return speed factor for a trip starting at depart_time.

        Multiplier semantics:
        - 1.0: Free-flow baseline speed.
        - 0.5: Half speed (travel time doubles).
        - 1.2: 20% faster than baseline (e.g. night).
        - 0.0: Full road blockage (travel time is infinite).

        Actual Travel Time = Baseline Travel Time / Speed Multiplier
        """
        ...


class TimeOfDayProfileAdapter(TrafficAdapter):
    """Simulated time-of-day speed multiplier profile.

    Based on the standard time-dependent VRP formulation of
    Ichoua, Gendreau & Potvin (2003): "A parallel tabu search heuristic for
    the dynamic and time-dependent vehicle routing problem with time windows".

    Default profile (seconds mapped to 24h day):
    - 00:00 - 06:00: 1.15 (light night traffic)
    - 06:00 - 08:00: 1.00 (standard morning flow)
    - 08:00 - 10:00: 0.65 (morning rush peak congestion)
    - 10:00 - 16:00: 0.90 (steady midday business flow)
    - 16:00 - 19:30: 0.60 (evening rush hour severe congestion)
    - 19:30 - 22:00: 0.85 (evening recovery)
    - 22:00 - 24:00: 1.10 (night flow)
    """

    DEFAULT_HOURLY_PROFILE: dict[int, float] = {
        0: 1.15, 1: 1.15, 2: 1.15, 3: 1.15, 4: 1.15, 5: 1.15,
        6: 1.00, 7: 1.00,
        8: 0.65, 9: 0.65,
        10: 0.90, 11: 0.90, 12: 0.90, 13: 0.90, 14: 0.90, 15: 0.90,
        16: 0.60, 17: 0.60, 18: 0.60, 19: 0.70,
        20: 0.85, 21: 0.85,
        22: 1.10, 23: 1.10,
    }

    def __init__(self, hourly_profile: dict[int, float] | None = None) -> None:
        """Initialize profile adapter.

        Args:
            hourly_profile: Optional dict mapping hour (0..23) to positive speed multiplier.
        """
        self.profile = (
            dict(hourly_profile) if hourly_profile is not None else dict(self.DEFAULT_HOURLY_PROFILE)
        )
        for h, v in self.profile.items():
            if not (0 <= h <= 23):
                raise ValueError(f"Invalid hour {h}; must be 0..23")
            if v <= 0:
                raise ValueError(f"Speed multiplier for hour {h} must be positive, got {v}")

    def get_speed_multiplier(
        self, origin: Location, dest: Location, depart_time: float
    ) -> float:
        """Retrieve simulated speed multiplier according to departure hour."""
        if not math.isfinite(depart_time):
            return 1.0
        day_seconds = depart_time % 86400.0
        hour = int(day_seconds // 3600)
        return self.profile.get(hour, 1.0)


@dataclass
class SimulatedIncident:
    """Dynamic traffic incident description."""

    event_id: str
    speed_multiplier: float  # e.g., 0.2 for 80% slowdown, 0.0 for blockage
    start_time: float
    end_time: float
    origin_id: str | None = None
    dest_id: str | None = None
    center_lat: float | None = None
    center_lon: float | None = None
    radius_meters: float | None = None
    description: str = ""


class SimulatedEventAdapter(TrafficAdapter):
    """Simulated runtime traffic event injector.

    Allows runtime injection of accidents, roadworks, and full corridor blockages
    for testing dynamic route re-planning.
    """

    def __init__(self, base_adapter: TrafficAdapter | None = None) -> None:
        """Initialize event adapter.

        Args:
            base_adapter: Underlying traffic adapter to modulate (default: TimeOfDayProfileAdapter).
        """
        self.base_adapter = base_adapter or TimeOfDayProfileAdapter()
        self._incidents: dict[str, SimulatedIncident] = {}

    def add_incident(self, incident: SimulatedIncident) -> None:
        """Register a runtime traffic incident."""
        if incident.speed_multiplier < 0.0:
            raise ValueError("speed_multiplier cannot be negative")
        if incident.start_time > incident.end_time:
            raise ValueError("incident start_time cannot exceed end_time")
        self._incidents[incident.event_id] = incident

    def remove_incident(self, event_id: str) -> None:
        """Remove an incident by ID."""
        self._incidents.pop(event_id, None)

    def clear_incidents(self) -> None:
        """Clear all active incidents."""
        self._incidents.clear()

    @property
    def active_incidents(self) -> list[SimulatedIncident]:
        return list(self._incidents.values())

    def _haversine_distance(self, lat1: float, lon1: float, lat2: float, lon2: float) -> float:
        """Planar or haversine distance in metres."""
        r = 6371000.0  # Earth radius in metres
        phi1, phi2 = math.radians(lat1), math.radians(lat2)
        dphi = math.radians(lat2 - lat1)
        dlam = math.radians(lon2 - lon1)
        a = math.sin(dphi / 2.0) ** 2 + math.cos(phi1) * math.cos(phi2) * math.sin(dlam / 2.0) ** 2
        return 2.0 * r * math.atan2(math.sqrt(a), math.sqrt(1.0 - a))

    def get_speed_multiplier(
        self, origin: Location, dest: Location, depart_time: float
    ) -> float:
        """Calculate combined speed multiplier including all active incidents."""
        base_multiplier = self.base_adapter.get_speed_multiplier(origin, dest, depart_time)
        effective_multiplier = base_multiplier

        for inc in self._incidents.values():
            if not (inc.start_time <= depart_time <= inc.end_time):
                continue

            applies = False
            # Check Origin-Destination match
            if inc.origin_id is not None or inc.dest_id is not None:
                matches_orig = inc.origin_id is None or inc.origin_id == origin.id
                matches_dest = inc.dest_id is None or inc.dest_id == dest.id
                if matches_orig and matches_dest:
                    applies = True

            # Check Geographic Radius match
            if inc.center_lat is not None and inc.center_lon is not None and inc.radius_meters is not None:
                dist_orig = self._haversine_distance(origin.lat, origin.lon, inc.center_lat, inc.center_lon)
                dist_dest = self._haversine_distance(dest.lat, dest.lon, inc.center_lat, inc.center_lon)
                if dist_orig <= inc.radius_meters or dist_dest <= inc.radius_meters:
                    applies = True

            if applies:
                if inc.speed_multiplier == 0.0:
                    return 0.0  # Total blockage
                effective_multiplier = min(effective_multiplier, inc.speed_multiplier)

        return effective_multiplier


class ProviderAdapter(TrafficAdapter):
    """Stub documentation adapter for authentic external traffic APIs.

    INTEGRITY NOTICE:
    This adapter serves as a defined hook for external commercial traffic APIs
    (e.g., TomTom Flow & Incidents, Google Distance Matrix with departure_time=now,
    HERE Real-Time Traffic).

    Without a valid commercial provider subscription, network credentials, and
    polling ingestion pipeline, Route.it operates in SIMULATED traffic mode.
    """

    def __init__(self, provider_name: str = "generic_provider", api_key: str | None = None) -> None:
        self.provider_name = provider_name
        self.api_key = api_key
        if not api_key:
            logger.warning(
                "ProviderAdapter [%s] initialized without API key. "
                "Operating under simulated baseline multiplier (1.0).",
                provider_name,
            )

    def get_speed_multiplier(
        self, origin: Location, dest: Location, depart_time: float
    ) -> float:
        """Hook for querying live traffic provider.

        When unconfigured, logs a notice and returns 1.0.
        """
        if not self.api_key:
            return 1.0

        raise NotImplementedError(
            f"ProviderAdapter [{self.provider_name}] requires customer-supplied "
            f"API integration code and authenticated endpoint access."
        )


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Cost Oracle Base Class
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

class CostOracle(ABC):
    """Abstract base class for distance and travel-time estimation."""

    def __init__(
        self,
        cache: CostCache | None = None,
        traffic_adapter: TrafficAdapter | None = None,
    ) -> None:
        self.cache = cache
        self.traffic_adapter = traffic_adapter

    @abstractmethod
    def _query_base_cost(self, origin: Location, dest: Location) -> tuple[float, float]:
        """Compute base (travel_time_sec, distance_m) without traffic adjustments."""
        ...

    def distance(self, origin: Any, dest: Any) -> float:
        """Return distance in metres between origin and dest."""
        orig_loc = to_location(origin)
        dest_loc = to_location(dest)
        if orig_loc.id == dest_loc.id:
            return 0.0

        if self.cache is not None:
            cached = self.cache.get(orig_loc.id, dest_loc.id, time_bucket=0)
            if cached is not None:
                return cached[1]

        _, dist = self._query_base_cost(orig_loc, dest_loc)
        return dist

    def travel_time(self, origin: Any, dest: Any, depart_time: float = 0.0) -> float:
        """Return estimated travel time in seconds between origin and dest departing at depart_time.

        Accounts for time-dependent traffic conditions and cache lookups.
        """
        orig_loc = to_location(origin)
        dest_loc = to_location(dest)
        if orig_loc.id == dest_loc.id:
            return 0.0

        time_bucket = self.cache.get_time_bucket(depart_time) if self.cache is not None else 0

        # Check Cache
        if self.cache is not None:
            cached = self.cache.get(orig_loc.id, dest_loc.id, time_bucket)
            if cached is not None:
                return cached[0]

        # Base Cost
        base_duration, base_distance = self._query_base_cost(orig_loc, dest_loc)

        # Traffic Factor
        if self.traffic_adapter is not None:
            speed_factor = self.traffic_adapter.get_speed_multiplier(orig_loc, dest_loc, depart_time)
            if speed_factor <= 0.0:
                adjusted_duration = float("inf")
            else:
                adjusted_duration = base_duration / speed_factor
        else:
            adjusted_duration = base_duration

        # Populate Cache
        if self.cache is not None:
            self.cache.set(orig_loc.id, dest_loc.id, time_bucket, adjusted_duration, base_distance)

        return adjusted_duration

    @abstractmethod
    def matrix(
        self, points: Sequence[Any], depart_time: float = 0.0
    ) -> MatrixResult:
        """Compute duration (seconds) and distance (metres) matrices for points."""
        ...

    def evaluate_route(
        self,
        stops: Sequence[Any],
        start_time: float = 0.0,
        service_times: Sequence[float] | None = None,
        ready_times: Sequence[float] | None = None,
        due_times: Sequence[float] | None = None,
    ) -> RouteTimeline:
        """Perform sequential time-dependent evaluation of a vehicle route.

        CRITICAL EVALUATION RULE:
        Arrival time at stop k is strictly computed from the actual departure time
        of the previous stop k-1:
            arrival_k = departure_{k-1} + travel_time(stop_{k-1}, stop_k, depart_time=departure_{k-1})
        """
        norm_stops = [to_location(s) for s in stops]
        n = len(norm_stops)
        if n == 0:
            return RouteTimeline(
                stops=[],
                total_travel_time=0.0,
                total_distance=0.0,
                total_duration=0.0,
                total_wait_time=0.0,
                total_lateness=0.0,
            )

        s_times = list(service_times) if service_times is not None else [0.0] * n
        r_times = list(ready_times) if ready_times is not None else [0.0] * n
        d_times = list(due_times) if due_times is not None else [float("inf")] * n

        stop_timelines: list[StopTimeline] = []
        total_travel_time = 0.0
        total_distance = 0.0
        total_wait_time = 0.0
        total_lateness = 0.0

        # Initial stop (Depot or route origin)
        cur_arr = start_time
        cur_start = max(cur_arr, r_times[0])
        cur_wait = cur_start - cur_arr
        cur_late = max(0.0, cur_arr - d_times[0])
        cur_dep = cur_start + s_times[0]

        total_wait_time += cur_wait
        total_lateness += cur_late

        stop_timelines.append(
            StopTimeline(
                stop_id=norm_stops[0].id,
                arrival_time=cur_arr,
                service_start_time=cur_start,
                departure_time=cur_dep,
                wait_time=cur_wait,
                travel_time_from_prev=0.0,
                distance_from_prev=0.0,
                lateness=cur_late,
            )
        )

        prev_dep = cur_dep

        # Subsequent stops
        for i in range(1, n):
            leg_dist = self.distance(norm_stops[i - 1], norm_stops[i])
            leg_time = self.travel_time(norm_stops[i - 1], norm_stops[i], depart_time=prev_dep)

            cur_arr = prev_dep + leg_time
            cur_start = max(cur_arr, r_times[i])
            cur_wait = cur_start - cur_arr
            cur_late = max(0.0, cur_arr - d_times[i])
            cur_dep = cur_start + s_times[i]

            total_travel_time += leg_time
            total_distance += leg_dist
            total_wait_time += cur_wait
            total_lateness += cur_late

            stop_timelines.append(
                StopTimeline(
                    stop_id=norm_stops[i].id,
                    arrival_time=cur_arr,
                    service_start_time=cur_start,
                    departure_time=cur_dep,
                    wait_time=cur_wait,
                    travel_time_from_prev=leg_time,
                    distance_from_prev=leg_dist,
                    lateness=cur_late,
                )
            )

            prev_dep = cur_dep

        total_duration = prev_dep - start_time

        return RouteTimeline(
            stops=stop_timelines,
            total_travel_time=total_travel_time,
            total_distance=total_distance,
            total_duration=total_duration,
            total_wait_time=total_wait_time,
            total_lateness=total_lateness,
        )


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Euclidean Oracle (Benchmarking & Unit Testing)
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def decode_polyline(encoded: str, precision: float = 1e6) -> list[tuple[float, float]]:
    """Decode a Google / Valhalla encoded polyline into a list of (lat, lon) coordinates."""
    coordinates: list[tuple[float, float]] = []
    idx = 0
    length = len(encoded)
    lat = 0
    lon = 0

    while idx < length:
        shift = 0
        result = 0
        while True:
            if idx >= length:
                break
            b = ord(encoded[idx]) - 63
            idx += 1
            result |= (b & 0x1F) << shift
            shift += 5
            if b < 0x20:
                break
        dlat = ~(result >> 1) if (result & 1) else (result >> 1)
        lat += dlat

        shift = 0
        result = 0
        while True:
            if idx >= length:
                break
            b = ord(encoded[idx]) - 63
            idx += 1
            result |= (b & 0x1F) << shift
            shift += 5
            if b < 0x20:
                break
        dlon = ~(result >> 1) if (result & 1) else (result >> 1)
        lon += dlon

        coordinates.append((lat / precision, lon / precision))

    return coordinates


def encode_polyline(
    coordinates: Sequence[tuple[float, float]], precision: float = 1e6
) -> str:
    """Encode a sequence of (lat, lon) coordinates into a Google / Valhalla polyline string."""
    output: list[str] = []
    prev_lat = 0
    prev_lon = 0

    for lat, lon in coordinates:
        curr_lat = int(round(lat * precision))
        curr_lon = int(round(lon * precision))
        dlat = curr_lat - prev_lat
        dlon = curr_lon - prev_lon
        prev_lat = curr_lat
        prev_lon = curr_lon

        for val in (dlat, dlon):
            val = ~(val << 1) if val < 0 else (val << 1)
            while val >= 0x20:
                output.append(chr((0x20 | (val & 0x1F)) + 63))
                val >>= 5
            output.append(chr(val + 63))

    return "".join(output)


class EuclideanOracle(CostOracle):
    """Euclidean & planar/haversine oracle for fast tests and benchmarks."""

    def __init__(
        self,
        base_speed_mps: float | None = None,
        cache: CostCache | None = None,
        traffic_adapter: TrafficAdapter | None = None,
        use_haversine: bool | None = None,
        planar_scale: float = 1.0,
        coordinate_system: Literal["geographic", "planar"] | None = None,
    ) -> None:
        """Initialize Euclidean oracle.

        Args:
            base_speed_mps: Base vehicle speed (default 8.333 m/s for geographic, 1.0 for planar).
            cache: Optional cost cache.
            traffic_adapter: Optional traffic adapter.
            use_haversine: Deprecated alias for coordinate_system == 'geographic'.
            planar_scale: Scaling factor for planar Euclidean distance (default: 1.0).
            coordinate_system: 'geographic' (Haversine distance in metres) or 'planar' (Euclidean hypot).
        """
        super().__init__(cache=cache, traffic_adapter=traffic_adapter)
        if coordinate_system is not None:
            self.coordinate_system: Literal["geographic", "planar"] = coordinate_system
        elif use_haversine is not None:
            self.coordinate_system = "geographic" if use_haversine else "planar"
        else:
            self.coordinate_system = "geographic"

        self.planar_scale = float(planar_scale)
        if base_speed_mps is None:
            self.base_speed_mps = 8.333 if self.coordinate_system == "geographic" else 1.0
        else:
            if base_speed_mps <= 0.0:
                raise ValueError("base_speed_mps must be positive")
            self.base_speed_mps = float(base_speed_mps)

    @property
    def use_haversine(self) -> bool:
        return self.coordinate_system == "geographic"

    def _query_base_cost(self, origin: Location, dest: Location) -> tuple[float, float]:
        if origin.id == dest.id:
            return 0.0, 0.0

        if self.coordinate_system == "geographic":
            # Haversine distance in metres
            r = 6371000.0
            phi1, phi2 = math.radians(origin.lat), math.radians(dest.lat)
            dphi = math.radians(dest.lat - origin.lat)
            dlam = math.radians(dest.lon - origin.lon)
            a = (
                math.sin(dphi / 2.0) ** 2
                + math.cos(phi1) * math.cos(phi2) * math.sin(dlam / 2.0) ** 2
            )
            dist_m = 2.0 * r * math.atan2(math.sqrt(a), math.sqrt(1.0 - a))
            speed = self.base_speed_mps if self.base_speed_mps > 1.0 else 8.333
            duration_sec = dist_m / speed
            return duration_sec, dist_m
        else:
            # Planar Euclidean distance
            dist = math.hypot(origin.lat - dest.lat, origin.lon - dest.lon) * self.planar_scale
            duration_sec = dist / self.base_speed_mps
            return duration_sec, dist

    def matrix(
        self, points: Sequence[Any], depart_time: float = 0.0
    ) -> MatrixResult:
        """Compute all-pairs duration and distance matrices."""
        norm_points = [to_location(p) for p in points]
        n = len(norm_points)
        durations = [[0.0] * n for _ in range(n)]
        distances = [[0.0] * n for _ in range(n)]

        for i in range(n):
            for j in range(n):
                if i == j:
                    continue
                d = self.distance(norm_points[i], norm_points[j])
                t = self.travel_time(norm_points[i], norm_points[j], depart_time=depart_time)
                distances[i][j] = d
                durations[i][j] = t

        return MatrixResult(durations=durations, distances=distances)


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Valhalla Oracle (Dockerized Matrix Engine with Chunking)
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

class ValhallaOracle(CostOracle):
    """Road network oracle backed by Dockerized Valhalla routing service."""

    def __init__(
        self,
        base_url: str = "http://localhost:8002",
        costing: str = "auto",
        chunk_size: int = 50,
        timeout: float = 10.0,
        cache: CostCache | None = None,
        traffic_adapter: TrafficAdapter | None = None,
    ) -> None:
        """Initialize Valhalla cost oracle.

        Args:
            base_url: Valhalla HTTP endpoint URL (default: http://localhost:8002).
            costing: Routing costing model (default: "auto").
            chunk_size: Maximum sources/targets per subquery to avoid server limit exhaustion.
            timeout: HTTP socket timeout in seconds.
            cache: Optional CostCache backend.
            traffic_adapter: Optional traffic adapter.
        """
        super().__init__(cache=cache, traffic_adapter=traffic_adapter)
        self.base_url = base_url.rstrip("/")
        self.costing = costing
        self.chunk_size = max(1, chunk_size)
        self.timeout = timeout

    def _query_sources_to_targets(
        self, sources: list[Location], targets: list[Location]
    ) -> list[list[dict[str, Any]]]:
        """Execute a single /sources_to_targets POST query against Valhalla."""
        url = f"{self.base_url}/sources_to_targets"
        payload = {
            "sources": [{"lat": s.lat, "lon": s.lon} for s in sources],
            "targets": [{"lat": t.lat, "lon": t.lon} for t in targets],
            "costing": self.costing,
            "units": "kilometers",
        }
        data_bytes = json.dumps(payload).encode("utf-8")
        req = urllib.request.Request(
            url,
            data=data_bytes,
            headers={"Content-Type": "application/json", "User-Agent": "RouteIt-Oracle"},
            method="POST",
        )

        try:
            with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=self.timeout) as resp:
                if resp.status != 200:
                    raise RuntimeError(f"Valhalla returned HTTP status {resp.status}")
                body = resp.read().decode("utf-8")
                res = json.loads(body)
                return res.get("sources_to_targets", [])
        except urllib.error.URLError as exc:
            raise ConnectionError(
                f"Failed to query Valhalla matrix endpoint at {url}: {exc}"
            ) from exc

    def _query_base_cost(self, origin: Location, dest: Location) -> tuple[float, float]:
        """Fetch base time and distance for a single OD pair."""
        res = self._query_sources_to_targets([origin], [dest])
        if not res or not res[0]:
            raise RuntimeError(f"Valhalla returned empty matrix for {origin.id} -> {dest.id}")
        entry = res[0][0]
        # Valhalla returns time in seconds, distance in km (when units="kilometers")
        dur_sec = float(entry.get("time", 0.0))
        dist_m = float(entry.get("distance", 0.0)) * 1000.0
        return dur_sec, dist_m

    def matrix(
        self, points: Sequence[Any], depart_time: float = 0.0
    ) -> MatrixResult:
        """Compute full N x N matrix in chunks respecting Valhalla limits."""
        norm_points = [to_location(p) for p in points]
        n = len(norm_points)
        durations = [[0.0] * n for _ in range(n)]
        distances = [[0.0] * n for _ in range(n)]

        if n <= 1:
            return MatrixResult(durations=durations, distances=distances)

        time_bucket = self.cache.get_time_bucket(depart_time) if self.cache is not None else 0

        # Query in chunks of size (chunk_size x chunk_size)
        for i in range(0, n, self.chunk_size):
            src_chunk = norm_points[i : i + self.chunk_size]
            for j in range(0, n, self.chunk_size):
                tgt_chunk = norm_points[j : j + self.chunk_size]

                # Check if all pairs in this chunk are already cached
                all_cached = True
                chunk_durs: list[list[float]] = []
                chunk_dists: list[list[float]] = []

                if self.cache is not None:
                    for r_idx, s in enumerate(src_chunk):
                        row_durs: list[float] = []
                        row_dists: list[float] = []
                        for c_idx, t in enumerate(tgt_chunk):
                            if s.id == t.id:
                                row_durs.append(0.0)
                                row_dists.append(0.0)
                                continue
                            cached = self.cache.get(s.id, t.id, time_bucket)
                            if cached is not None:
                                row_durs.append(cached[0])
                                row_dists.append(cached[1])
                            else:
                                all_cached = False
                                break
                        if not all_cached:
                            break
                        chunk_durs.append(row_durs)
                        chunk_dists.append(row_dists)

                if all_cached and self.cache is not None:
                    for r_idx in range(len(src_chunk)):
                        for c_idx in range(len(tgt_chunk)):
                            durations[i + r_idx][j + c_idx] = chunk_durs[r_idx][c_idx]
                            distances[i + r_idx][j + c_idx] = chunk_dists[r_idx][c_idx]
                    continue

                # Query Valhalla for this submatrix
                raw_matrix = self._query_sources_to_targets(src_chunk, tgt_chunk)

                for r_idx, row in enumerate(raw_matrix):
                    s_loc = src_chunk[r_idx]
                    for c_idx, col in enumerate(row):
                        t_loc = tgt_chunk[c_idx]
                        if s_loc.id == t_loc.id:
                            durations[i + r_idx][j + c_idx] = 0.0
                            distances[i + r_idx][j + c_idx] = 0.0
                            continue

                        base_time = float(col.get("time", 0.0))
                        base_dist = float(col.get("distance", 0.0)) * 1000.0

                        # Apply Traffic Multiplier
                        if self.traffic_adapter is not None:
                            speed_factor = self.traffic_adapter.get_speed_multiplier(
                                s_loc, t_loc, depart_time
                            )
                            adj_time = base_time / speed_factor if speed_factor > 0.0 else float("inf")
                        else:
                            adj_time = base_time

                        durations[i + r_idx][j + c_idx] = adj_time
                        distances[i + r_idx][j + c_idx] = base_dist

                        # Store in cache
                        if self.cache is not None:
                            self.cache.set(s_loc.id, t_loc.id, time_bucket, adj_time, base_dist)

        return MatrixResult(durations=durations, distances=distances)

    def route(
        self,
        locations: Sequence[Any],
        costing: str | None = None,
        directions_options: dict[str, Any] | None = None,
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
        """Compute full turn-by-turn route with decoded geometry for a sequence of stops.

        Args:
            locations: Sequence of stops to visit in order.
            costing: Optional costing model override (default: self.costing).
            directions_options: Optional Valhalla directions options (default: units=kilometers).

        Returns:
            Dict containing:
            - "trip": Trip dictionary from Valhalla.
            - "coordinates": Decoded list of [lon, lat] coordinates (GeoJSON format).
            - "distance_km": Total route distance in km.
            - "time_sec": Total duration in seconds.
        """
        norm_locs = [to_location(loc) for loc in locations]
        url = f"{self.base_url}/route"
        payload = {
            "locations": [{"lat": loc.lat, "lon": loc.lon, "type": "break"} for loc in norm_locs],
            "costing": costing or self.costing,
            "directions_options": directions_options or {"units": "kilometers"},
        }
        data_bytes = json.dumps(payload).encode("utf-8")
        req = urllib.request.Request(
            url,
            data=data_bytes,
            headers={"Content-Type": "application/json", "User-Agent": "RouteIt-Oracle"},
            method="POST",
        )

        try:
            with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=self.timeout) as resp:
                if resp.status != 200:
                    raise RuntimeError(f"Valhalla /route returned HTTP {resp.status}")
                body = resp.read().decode("utf-8")
                res = json.loads(body)
        except urllib.error.URLError as exc:
            raise ConnectionError(f"Failed to query Valhalla /route endpoint at {url}: {exc}") from exc

        trip = res.get("trip", {})
        summary = trip.get("summary", {})
        legs = trip.get("legs", [])

        # Decode all leg polylines into [lon, lat] points for GeoJSON
        geojson_coords: list[list[float]] = []
        for leg in legs:
            shape = leg.get("shape", "")
            if shape:
                decoded_lat_lon = decode_polyline(shape, precision=1e6)
                for lat, lon in decoded_lat_lon:
                    # In GeoJSON: [longitude, latitude]
                    geojson_coords.append([round(lon, 6), round(lat, 6)])

        return {
            "trip": trip,
            "coordinates": geojson_coords,
            "distance_km": float(summary.get("length", 0.0)),
            "time_sec": float(summary.get("time", 0.0)),
        }



=== FILE: routing/docker-compose.yml ===
services:
  valhalla:
    image: ghcr.io/valhalla/valhalla-scripted:latest
    container_name: route_it_valhalla
    restart: unless-stopped
    ports:
      - "8002:8002"
    volumes:
      # Map custom_files directory containing vadodara.osm.pbf and built valhalla tiles
      - ./custom_files:/custom_files
    environment:
      # Disable elevation to keep build lightweight (<30s, avoids downloading SRTM tiles)
      - build_elevation=False
      # Build admin database for country boundaries & left-hand driving rules (India)
      - build_admins=True
      # Build timezone database for time-dependent routing (critical for CVRPTW)
      - build_time_zones=True
      # Consolidate graph tiles into valhalla_tiles.tar for fast container restarts
      - build_tar=True
      # Only rebuild tiles if the .osm.pbf hash changes
      - force_rebuild=False
      # Start valhalla_service HTTP daemon after processing
      - serve_tiles=True
      # Limit concurrency to 2 threads to prevent CPU/memory exhaustion on build/serve
      - server_threads=2



=== FILE: routing/healthcheck.py ===
"""Health check utility for local Valhalla Docker instance.

Verifies:
1. HTTP connectivity and status check via GET /status
2. Live route calculation via POST /route in Vadodara
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import json
import sys
import time
from typing import Any, Sequence
import urllib.error
import urllib.request


def check_valhalla_health(base_url: str = "http://localhost:8002", timeout: float = 5.0) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """Test connectivity, status, and route query against a Valhalla endpoint.

    Args:
        base_url: Base HTTP URL of the Valhalla service.
        timeout: Socket timeout in seconds.

    Returns:
        Dictionary containing health status, version, and latency metrics.
    """
    clean_url = base_url.rstrip("/")
    status_url = f"{clean_url}/status"
    route_url = f"{clean_url}/route"

    report: dict[str, Any] = {
        "url": clean_url,
        "healthy": False,
        "status_code": None,
        "version": None,
        "tileset_last_modified": None,
        "status_latency_ms": None,
        "route_latency_ms": None,
        "sample_route_km": None,
        "sample_route_sec": None,
        "error": None,
    }

    # 1. Check GET /status
    try:
        t0 = time.perf_counter()
        req = urllib.request.Request(status_url, headers={"User-Agent": "RouteIt-Healthcheck"})
        with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=timeout) as response:
            status_latency = (time.perf_counter() - t0) * 1000.0
            report["status_code"] = response.status
            report["status_latency_ms"] = round(status_latency, 2)
            if response.status == 200:
                data = json.loads(response.read().decode("utf-8"))
                report["version"] = data.get("version")
                report["tileset_last_modified"] = data.get("tileset_last_modified")
            else:
                report["error"] = f"GET /status returned HTTP {response.status}"
                return report
    except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
        report["error"] = f"Failed to connect to {status_url}: {exc}"
        return report

    # 2. Check POST /route with Vadodara coordinates (Junction to Sayaji Baug)
    route_payload = {
        "locations": [
            {"lat": 22.3108, "lon": 73.1811, "type": "break"},
            {"lat": 22.3129, "lon": 73.1906, "type": "break"},
        ],
        "costing": "auto",
        "directions_options": {"units": "kilometers"},
    }

    try:
        t0 = time.perf_counter()
        payload_bytes = json.dumps(route_payload).encode("utf-8")
        req = urllib.request.Request(
            route_url,
            data=payload_bytes,
            headers={"Content-Type": "application/json", "User-Agent": "RouteIt-Healthcheck"},
            method="POST",
        )
        with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=timeout) as response:
            route_latency = (time.perf_counter() - t0) * 1000.0
            report["route_latency_ms"] = round(route_latency, 2)
            if response.status == 200:
                data = json.loads(response.read().decode("utf-8"))
                trip = data.get("trip", {})
                summary = trip.get("summary", {})
                report["sample_route_km"] = summary.get("length")
                report["sample_route_sec"] = summary.get("time")
                report["healthy"] = True
            else:
                report["error"] = f"POST /route returned HTTP {response.status}"
    except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
        report["error"] = f"Failed to query {route_url}: {exc}"

    return report


def main(argv: Sequence[str] | None = None) -> int:
    """CLI entrypoint."""
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(
        description="Verify health and response latency of local Valhalla Docker instance."
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--url",
        "-u",
        type=str,
        default="http://localhost:8002",
        help="Valhalla service URL (default: http://localhost:8002)",
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--timeout",
        "-t",
        type=float,
        default=5.0,
        help="Request timeout in seconds (default: 5.0)",
    )
    args = parser.parse_args(argv)

    print(f"Checking Valhalla health at {args.url} (timeout: {args.timeout}s)...")
    res = check_valhalla_health(args.url, timeout=args.timeout)

    print("-" * 50)
    if res["healthy"]:
        print("Status:           HEALTHY (ONLINE)")
        print(f"Version:          {res['version']}")
        print(f"Status Latency:   {res['status_latency_ms']} ms")
        print(f"Route Latency:    {res['route_latency_ms']} ms")
        print(f"Sample Route:     {res['sample_route_km']} km in {res['sample_route_sec']} s")
        print("-" * 50)
        return 0
    else:
        print("Status:           UNHEALTHY (OFFLINE / ERROR)")
        print(f"Error:            {res['error']}")
        print("-" * 50)
        print("Hint: Ensure the Valhalla container is started via 'docker compose up -d' in /routing")
        print("      and vadodara.osm.pbf is placed in routing/custom_files/.")
        return 1


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main())


=== FILE: routing/README.md ===
# Valhalla Routing Engine Setup (Vadodara / Gujarat)

This directory manages the local Dockerized **Valhalla** road routing engine for Route.it. Valhalla powers our cost oracle by calculating real road network travel times, turn-by-turn routes, and distance matrices.

---

## 1. Verified Docker Image & Architecture

- **Official Image**: `ghcr.io/valhalla/valhalla-scripted:latest`  
  *(Public image on GitHub Container Registry, maintained directly upstream by the Valhalla core team).*
- **Legacy Image**: `ghcr.io/gis-ops/docker-valhalla/valhalla:latest`  
  *(Archived by GIS-Ops; upstream `valhalla-scripted` inherits all entrypoint scripts and options).*
- **Port**: `8002` (standard Valhalla HTTP API port).
- **Directory Mount**: `./custom_files:/custom_files` (stores OSM PBF inputs, built tile archives, and cached SQLite DBs).

---

## 2. Download the OSM Extract (Vadodara or Gujarat)

Geofabrik only provides the full country extract (`india-latest.osm.pbf`, ~1.5 GB), which is unnecessarily heavy for local development. We recommend obtaining a small extract for **Vadodara** (~5–12 MB) or **Gujarat** (~100–140 MB).

### Option A: Vadodara City Extract via BBBike (Recommended: < 12 MB)
1. Navigate to the **[BBBike Custom Extract Service](https://extract.bbbike.org/)**.
2. Enter the bounding box coordinates for **Vadodara**:
   - **South (min lat)**: `22.18`
   - **West (min lon)**: `73.08`
   - **North (max lat)**: `22.42`
   - **East (max lon)**: `73.32`
3. Under **Format**, select: **`Protocolbuffer (PBF)`**.
4. Set the name to: `vadodara` and click **Extract**.
5. Once generated (~1–2 minutes), download the `.osm.pbf` file and save it as:
   ```
   routing/custom_files/vadodara.osm.pbf
   ```

### Option B: Entire Gujarat State Extract via BBBike (~120 MB)
If you require routing across all of Gujarat (Ahmedabad, Surat, Rajkot, Vadodara):
1. On **[BBBike Extract](https://extract.bbbike.org/)**, set bounding box coordinates:
   - **South (min lat)**: `20.10`
   - **West (min lon)**: `68.10`
   - **North (max lat)**: `24.70`
   - **East (max lon)**: `74.50`
2. Select format **`Protocolbuffer (PBF)`**, name it `gujarat`, and download to:
   ```
   routing/custom_files/gujarat.osm.pbf
   ```

### Option C: Crop from Geofabrik India (via `osmium`)
If you already possess `india-latest.osm.pbf` and `osmium-tool`:
```bash
# Extract Vadodara bounding box
osmium extract --bbox 73.08,22.18,73.32,22.42 india-latest.osm.pbf -o routing/custom_files/vadodara.osm.pbf
```

---

## 3. Starting the Valhalla Container

Once your `.osm.pbf` file is placed inside `routing/custom_files/`:

```bash
# Navigate to the routing directory
cd routing

# Launch the Valhalla container in detached mode
docker compose up -d
```

### Inspect Build & Initialization Logs
On the first run, the scripted container detects any `*.pbf` files in `/custom_files`, builds the routing graph tiles, packages them into `valhalla_tiles.tar`, and starts `valhalla_service`:
```bash
docker compose logs -f valhalla
```
Look for the startup confirmation:
```
INFO: Found config file. Starting valhalla service!
```

### Stop the Service
```bash
docker compose down
```

### Force a Full Tile Rebuild
If you update the `.osm.pbf` file or change routing options, rebuild tiles by setting `force_rebuild=True`:
```bash
docker compose run --rm -e force_rebuild=True valhalla
```

## 6. Upstream Verification & Limitations Report

### What Was Verified
1. **Docker Image**: Verified that `ghcr.io/valhalla/valhalla-scripted:latest` is the official, actively maintained image from `valhalla/valhalla`, and that it is publicly accessible without registry authentication.
2. **Configuration Variables**: Verified against `docker/scripts/docker-entrypoint.sh` and `docker/scripts/configure_valhalla.sh`:
   - `build_elevation=False` avoids downloading SRTM elevation tiles.
   - `build_admins=True` invokes `valhalla_build_admins` on input `.pbf` to configure country borders and left-hand driving rules for India.
   - `build_time_zones=True` creates `timezones.sqlite` enabling time-dependent routing.
   - `build_tar=True` packages graph tiles into `valhalla_tiles.tar` for fast container restarts.
   - `server_threads=2` restricts concurrency to prevent CPU/memory exhaustion.
   - `serve_tiles=True` launches `valhalla_service`.
3. **Volume Mount**: Verified that any `*.pbf` placed in `/custom_files` is automatically detected by the container's glob loop `$(ls $CUSTOM_FILES/*.pbf)`.
4. **API Formats**: Verified `/route` and `/sources_to_targets` request and response JSON schemas against official Valhalla API documentation.

### What Could Not Be Verified
1. **Live Container Execution**: Docker is not currently installed or running on this Windows host (`docker` command not recognized in PowerShell). Live tile generation, container startup logs, and real network roundtrips could not be executed on this machine.
2. **Empirical Tile Build Memory/Time**: The exact build time and RAM consumption of the Vadodara PBF extract inside the container could not be measured locally without a functioning Docker daemon.

---

## 7. Cost Oracle & Traffic Engine (`routing/cost_oracle.py`)

The cost oracle subsystem abstracts road network routing and travel-time estimation away from the metaheuristic optimization solver.

> [!IMPORTANT]
> **INTEGRITY NOTICE: TRAFFIC IS SIMULATED**  
> Unless an authentic external commercial provider adapter (such as TomTom Routing API, Google Maps Platform, or HERE Traffic) is explicitly configured and authenticated, **ALL traffic conditions, hourly congestion curves, and dynamic incidents are SIMULATED** (using the time-dependent speed profile formulation of Ichoua et al. 2003 or synthetic incident injection).

### 7.1 Architecture & Oracles
- **`CostOracle` (Abstract Base)**:
  - `travel_time(origin, dest, depart_time=0.0) -> float`: Duration in seconds.
  - `distance(origin, dest) -> float`: Distance in metres.
  - `matrix(points, depart_time=0.0) -> MatrixResult`: $N \times N$ duration (seconds) and distance (metres) matrices (supports tuple unpacking: `durations, distances = oracle.matrix(...)`).
  - `evaluate_route(stops, start_time, ...)`: Sequential time-dependent timeline evaluation.
- **`ValhallaOracle`**:
  - Connects to the local/remote Valhalla container `/sources_to_targets` endpoint.
  - **Chunked Matrix Querying**: Splits large matrix requests into configurable blocks (`chunk_size`, default 50) to prevent Valhalla max matrix limits exhaustion.
  - Converts kilometers to metres and times to seconds.
- **`EuclideanOracle`**:
  - Fast, deterministic planar or haversine distance oracle for Solomon / CVRPLIB benchmarks and offline unit testing.

### 7.2 Caching Subsystem (`CostCache`)
- **Key Strategy**: `(origin_id, dest_id, time_bucket)` where `time_bucket = int(depart_time // bucket_size_seconds)` (default 15 minutes = 900s).
- **Implementations**:
  - `InMemoryLRUCache`: In-memory `OrderedDict` bounded by `max_size` (default 50,000).
  - `SQLiteCache`: Persistent SQLite database (`cost_cache.sqlite` or `:memory:`).
- **Telemetry**: Records `hits`, `misses`, and `hit_ratio` for cache efficiency monitoring.

### 7.3 Traffic Adapters (`TrafficAdapter`)
- **`TimeOfDayProfileAdapter`**:
  - Hourly speed multipliers based on Ichoua, Gendreau & Potvin (2003).
  - Morning rush (08:00–10:00: 0.65x speed, +54% travel time).
  - Evening rush (16:00–19:30: 0.60x speed, +67% travel time).
  - Night (22:00–06:00: 1.15x speed, -13% travel time).
- **`SimulatedEventAdapter`**:
  - Runtime injection of dynamic incidents: road closures (`speed_multiplier=0.0` -> infinite travel time), lane blockages, or severe bottlenecks.
  - Targets specific OD pairs or geographic radius zones `(center_lat, center_lon, radius_m)`.
- **`ProviderAdapter` (Stub)**:
  - Integration template for real commercial providers (TomTom, Google, HERE).
  - In default mode without credentials, logs a notice and defaults to baseline free-flow.

### 7.4 Time-Dependent Route Evaluation
Arrival time at stop $k$ is strictly computed from the actual departure time of the previous stop $k-1$:
$$\text{arrival}_k = \text{departure}_{k-1} + \text{travel\_time}(\text{stop}_{k-1}, \text{stop}_k, \text{departure}_{k-1})$$
$$\text{departure}_k = \max(\text{arrival}_k, \text{ready\_time}_k) + \text{service\_time}_k$$

### 7.5 Running the Unit Tests
```bash
python -m pytest tests/routing/test_cost_oracle.py -v
```
To run the live integration test when Valhalla is running:
```bash
python -m pytest tests/routing/test_cost_oracle.py -v -m integration
```




=== FILE: solver/__init__.py ===
"""Route.it Solver Package.

Implements Quantum-behaved Particle Swarm Optimization (QPSO),
classical PSO baseline, Prins Split algorithm for CVRPTW, and local search operators.
"""

__version__ = "0.1.0"


=== FILE: solver/io.py ===
"""Benchmark and data loader/saver utilities for Route.it.

Supports:
- Solomon (1987) CVRPTW .txt benchmark instances
- CVRPLIB TSPLIB-style .vrp benchmark instances
- JSON serialization/deserialization for Problem and Solution
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import math
from pathlib import Path
import re

from solver.models import Customer, Depot, Problem, Route, Solution, Vehicle


def load_solomon(file_path: str | Path) -> Problem:
    """Parse a standard Solomon (1987) CVRPTW benchmark text file.

    Args:
        file_path: Path to the Solomon .txt instance file.

    Returns:
        Fully initialized Problem instance with computed Euclidean matrices.
    """
    path = Path(file_path)
    if not path.is_file():
        raise FileNotFoundError(f"Solomon instance not found at {path}")

    with path.open("r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        lines = [line.strip() for line in f if line.strip()]

    instance_name = lines[0] if lines else path.stem
    num_vehicles: int | None = None
    capacity: float | None = None

    idx = 0
    while idx < len(lines):
        line = lines[idx]
        if line.startswith("VEHICLE"):
            idx += 1
            # Next line usually has headers (NUMBER CAPACITY) or the values
            parts = lines[idx].split()
            if parts and parts[0].isdigit():
                num_vehicles = int(parts[0])
                capacity = float(parts[1])
            else:
                idx += 1
                parts = lines[idx].split()
                num_vehicles = int(parts[0])
                capacity = float(parts[1])
            break
        idx += 1

    if num_vehicles is None or capacity is None:
        raise ValueError(f"Could not parse VEHICLE section in {path}")

    # Find CUSTOMER section
    while idx < len(lines) and not lines[idx].startswith("CUSTOMER"):
        idx += 1

    # Skip header line (CUST NO. XCOORD ...)
    idx += 1
    while idx < len(lines):
        first_token = lines[idx].split()[0]
        if first_token.isdigit():
            break
        idx += 1

    depot: Depot | None = None
    customers: list[Customer] = []

    for line in lines[idx:]:
        parts = line.split()
        if len(parts) < 7:
            continue
        cust_id = parts[0]
        x = float(parts[1])
        y = float(parts[2])
        demand = float(parts[3])
        ready_time = float(parts[4])
        due_time = float(parts[5])
        service_time = float(parts[6])

        if cust_id == "0":
            depot = Depot(
                id="depot",
                lat=y,
                lon=x,
                ready_time=ready_time,
                due_time=due_time,
                service_time=service_time,
            )
        else:
            customers.append(
                Customer(
                    id=cust_id,
                    lat=y,
                    lon=x,
                    demand=demand,
                    ready_time=ready_time,
                    due_time=due_time,
                    service_time=service_time,
                )
            )

    if depot is None:
        raise ValueError(f"Depot (customer 0) was not found in {path}")

    vehicles = [
        Vehicle(
            id=f"V_{i+1}",
            capacity=capacity,
            shift_start=depot.ready_time,
            shift_end=depot.due_time,
        )
        for i in range(num_vehicles)
    ]

    problem = Problem(
        id=instance_name,
        depot=depot,
        customers=customers,
        vehicles=vehicles,
        coordinate_system="planar",
    )
    problem.compute_euclidean_matrices(speed=1.0)
    return problem


def load_cvrplib(file_path: str | Path) -> Problem:
    """Parse a standard CVRPLIB / TSPLIB .vrp file.

    Extracts coordinates, demands, capacity, depot, and vehicle count.
    Default time windows and service times are applied since CVRP has no windows.

    Args:
        file_path: Path to the .vrp file.

    Returns:
        Problem instance with computed Euclidean matrices.
    """
    path = Path(file_path)
    if not path.is_file():
        raise FileNotFoundError(f"CVRPLIB instance not found at {path}")

    with path.open("r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        lines = [line.strip() for line in f if line.strip()]

    name = path.stem
    comment = ""
    capacity: float | None = None
    dimension: int | None = None
    node_coords: dict[int, tuple[float, float]] = {}
    demands: dict[int, float] = {}
    depot_node_id: int = 1

    section = ""
    for line in lines:
        if line.startswith("NAME"):
            name = line.split(":", 1)[1].strip() if ":" in line else line.split()[1]
        elif line.startswith("COMMENT"):
            comment = line.split(":", 1)[1].strip() if ":" in line else line
        elif line.startswith("CAPACITY"):
            capacity = float(line.split(":", 1)[1].strip() if ":" in line else line.split()[1])
        elif line.startswith("DIMENSION"):
            dimension = int(line.split(":", 1)[1].strip() if ":" in line else line.split()[1])
        elif line.startswith("NODE_COORD_SECTION"):
            section = "NODE_COORD_SECTION"
            continue
        elif line.startswith("DEMAND_SECTION"):
            section = "DEMAND_SECTION"
            continue
        elif line.startswith("DEPOT_SECTION"):
            section = "DEPOT_SECTION"
            continue
        elif line.startswith("EOF"):
            break
        elif section == "NODE_COORD_SECTION":
            parts = line.split()
            if len(parts) >= 3:
                node_coords[int(parts[0])] = (float(parts[1]), float(parts[2]))
        elif section == "DEMAND_SECTION":
            parts = line.split()
            if len(parts) >= 2:
                demands[int(parts[0])] = float(parts[1])
        elif section == "DEPOT_SECTION":
            parts = line.split()
            if parts and parts[0] != "-1":
                depot_node_id = int(parts[0])

    if capacity is None:
        raise ValueError(f"CAPACITY header missing in {path}")
    if not node_coords:
        raise ValueError(f"No coordinates found in {path}")

    # Determine number of vehicles from name or comment (e.g. A-n32-k5 -> 5 vehicles)
    num_vehicles: int | None = None
    k_match = re.search(r"-k(\d+)", name, re.IGNORECASE)
    if k_match:
        num_vehicles = int(k_match.group(1))
    else:
        truck_match = re.search(r"trucks?\s*:\s*(\d+)", comment, re.IGNORECASE)
        if truck_match:
            num_vehicles = int(truck_match.group(1))

    total_demand = sum(demands.values())
    if num_vehicles is None:
        num_vehicles = max(1, math.ceil(total_demand / capacity))

    depot_x, depot_y = node_coords[depot_node_id]
    depot = Depot(
        id="depot",
        lat=depot_y,
        lon=depot_x,
        ready_time=0.0,
        due_time=1e7,
        service_time=0.0,
    )

    customers: list[Customer] = []
    for node_id, (x, y) in sorted(node_coords.items()):
        if node_id == depot_node_id:
            continue
        customers.append(
            Customer(
                id=str(node_id),
                lat=y,
                lon=x,
                demand=demands.get(node_id, 0.0),
                ready_time=0.0,
                due_time=1e7,
                service_time=0.0,
            )
        )

    vehicles = [
        Vehicle(
            id=f"V_{i+1}",
            capacity=capacity,
            shift_start=0.0,
            shift_end=1e7,
        )
        for i in range(num_vehicles)
    ]

    problem = Problem(
        id=name,
        depot=depot,
        customers=customers,
        vehicles=vehicles,
        coordinate_system="planar",
    )
    problem.compute_euclidean_matrices(speed=1.0)
    return problem


def save_problem_json(problem: Problem, file_path: str | Path) -> None:
    """Serialize a Problem instance to a JSON file."""
    path = Path(file_path)
    path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    with path.open("w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        f.write(problem.model_dump_json(indent=2))


def load_problem_json(file_path: str | Path) -> Problem:
    """Deserialize a Problem instance from a JSON file."""
    path = Path(file_path)
    if not path.is_file():
        raise FileNotFoundError(f"Problem file not found at {path}")
    with path.open("r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        return Problem.model_validate_json(f.read())


def save_solution_json(solution: Solution, file_path: str | Path) -> None:
    """Serialize a Solution instance to a JSON file."""
    path = Path(file_path)
    path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    with path.open("w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        f.write(solution.model_dump_json(indent=2))


def load_solution_json(file_path: str | Path) -> Solution:
    """Deserialize a Solution instance from a JSON file."""
    path = Path(file_path)
    if not path.is_file():
        raise FileNotFoundError(f"Solution file not found at {path}")
    with path.open("r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        return Solution.model_validate_json(f.read())


=== FILE: solver/local_search.py ===
"""Local Search Engine for CVRPTW.

Implements:
- Intra-route 2-Opt
- Inter-route Relocate (Insertion)
- Inter-route Swap
- First-improvement search strategy
- Neighbour-list pruning for rapid neighborhood exploration
- Continuous random-key write-back for memetic swarm integration
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from typing import Sequence
import numpy as np


def build_neighbour_list(dist_matrix: np.ndarray, k_nearest: int = 15) -> np.ndarray:
    """Precompute the k nearest customer neighbours for each node.

    Args:
        dist_matrix: (n+1) x (n+1) distance matrix where 0 is depot.
        k_nearest: Number of nearest neighbours to store per customer.

    Returns:
        (n+1) x k array of nearest customer indices (1-based).
    """
    n = dist_matrix.shape[0] - 1
    k = min(k_nearest, max(1, n - 1))
    neighbours = np.zeros((n + 1, k), dtype=np.int64)

    for i in range(1, n + 1):
        # Exclude depot (0) and self (i)
        dists = dist_matrix[i, 1:].copy()
        dists[i - 1] = 1e12  # mask self
        # argsort + 1 gives 1-based customer indices
        closest = np.argsort(dists)[:k] + 1
        neighbours[i] = closest

    return neighbours


def route_cost(route: Sequence[int], dist_matrix: np.ndarray) -> float:
    """Compute total distance of a route starting and ending at depot (0)."""
    if not route:
        return 0.0
    cost = dist_matrix[0, route[0]]
    for idx in range(len(route) - 1):
        cost += dist_matrix[route[idx], route[idx + 1]]
    cost += dist_matrix[route[-1], 0]
    return float(cost)


def total_routes_cost(routes: list[list[int]], dist_matrix: np.ndarray) -> float:
    """Compute total distance across all vehicle routes."""
    return sum(route_cost(r, dist_matrix) for r in routes)


def intra_route_2opt(route: list[int], dist_matrix: np.ndarray) -> bool:
    """Apply intra-route 2-opt using first-improvement.

    Returns True if an improving 2-opt inversion was executed.
    """
    p = len(route)
    if p < 3:
        return False

    for i in range(p - 1):
        c_prev = 0 if i == 0 else route[i - 1]
        c_i = route[i]

        for j in range(i + 1, p):
            c_j = route[j]
            c_next = 0 if j == p - 1 else route[j + 1]

            # In symmetric TSP/VRP, delta is:
            # - dist(c_prev, c_i) - dist(c_j, c_next) + dist(c_prev, c_j) + dist(c_i, c_next)
            delta = (
                dist_matrix[c_prev, c_j]
                + dist_matrix[c_i, c_next]
                - dist_matrix[c_prev, c_i]
                - dist_matrix[c_j, c_next]
            )

            if delta < -1e-7:
                route[i : j + 1] = route[i : j + 1][::-1]
                return True

    return False


def inter_route_relocate(
    routes: list[list[int]],
    loads: list[float],
    dist_matrix: np.ndarray,
    demands: np.ndarray,
    capacity: float,
    neighbour_set: dict[int, set[int]] | None = None,
) -> bool:
    """Apply inter-route relocate (single customer insertion) using first-improvement.

    Returns True if an improving customer relocation was executed.
    """
    num_routes = len(routes)
    if num_routes < 2:
        return False

    for r1 in range(num_routes):
        route1 = routes[r1]
        p1 = len(route1)
        if p1 <= 1:
            continue

        for i in range(p1):
            u = route1[i]
            d_u = demands[u]

            u_prev = 0 if i == 0 else route1[i - 1]
            u_next = 0 if i == p1 - 1 else route1[i + 1]

            # Cost change for removing u from route 1
            delta_remove = (
                dist_matrix[u_prev, u_next]
                - dist_matrix[u_prev, u]
                - dist_matrix[u, u_next]
            )

            for r2 in range(num_routes):
                if r1 == r2:
                    continue

                # Capacity check
                if loads[r2] + d_u > capacity:
                    continue

                route2 = routes[r2]
                p2 = len(route2)

                # Prune with neighbour set if provided
                if neighbour_set is not None:
                    u_nb = neighbour_set.get(u)
                    if u_nb is not None and not any(node in u_nb for node in route2):
                        continue

                # Try inserting u before first, between, or after last
                for j in range(-1, p2):
                    v_prev = 0 if j == -1 else route2[j]
                    v_next = 0 if j == p2 - 1 else route2[j + 1]

                    delta_insert = (
                        dist_matrix[v_prev, u]
                        + dist_matrix[u, v_next]
                        - dist_matrix[v_prev, v_next]
                    )

                    total_delta = delta_remove + delta_insert
                    if total_delta < -1e-7:
                        # Execute relocation
                        route1.pop(i)
                        route2.insert(j + 1, u)
                        loads[r1] -= d_u
                        loads[r2] += d_u
                        return True

    return False


def inter_route_swap(
    routes: list[list[int]],
    loads: list[float],
    dist_matrix: np.ndarray,
    demands: np.ndarray,
    capacity: float,
    neighbour_set: dict[int, set[int]] | None = None,
) -> bool:
    """Apply inter-route swap (exchange two customers) using first-improvement.

    Returns True if an improving customer swap was executed.
    """
    num_routes = len(routes)
    if num_routes < 2:
        return False

    for r1 in range(num_routes):
        route1 = routes[r1]
        p1 = len(route1)
        if p1 == 0:
            continue

        for i in range(p1):
            u = route1[i]
            d_u = demands[u]

            u_prev = 0 if i == 0 else route1[i - 1]
            u_next = 0 if i == p1 - 1 else route1[i + 1]

            for r2 in range(r1 + 1, num_routes):
                route2 = routes[r2]
                p2 = len(route2)
                if p2 == 0:
                    continue

                for j in range(p2):
                    v = route2[j]
                    d_v = demands[v]

                    # Joint capacity feasibility check
                    if loads[r1] - d_u + d_v > capacity:
                        continue
                    if loads[r2] - d_v + d_u > capacity:
                        continue

                    # Prune with neighbour list if provided
                    if neighbour_set is not None:
                        u_nb = neighbour_set.get(u)
                        if u_nb is not None and v not in u_nb:
                            continue

                    v_prev = 0 if j == 0 else route2[j - 1]
                    v_next = 0 if j == p2 - 1 else route2[j + 1]

                    delta1 = (
                        dist_matrix[u_prev, v]
                        + dist_matrix[v, u_next]
                        - dist_matrix[u_prev, u]
                        - dist_matrix[u, u_next]
                    )
                    delta2 = (
                        dist_matrix[v_prev, u]
                        + dist_matrix[u, v_next]
                        - dist_matrix[v_prev, v]
                        - dist_matrix[v, v_next]
                    )

                    total_delta = delta1 + delta2
                    if total_delta < -1e-7:
                        # Execute swap
                        route1[i] = v
                        route2[j] = u
                        loads[r1] = loads[r1] - d_u + d_v
                        loads[r2] = loads[r2] - d_v + d_u
                        return True

    return False


def run_local_search(
    routes: list[list[int]],
    dist_matrix: np.ndarray,
    demands: np.ndarray,
    capacity: float,
    neighbour_matrix: np.ndarray | None = None,
    max_passes: int = 50,
) -> tuple[list[list[int]], float]:
    """Execute composite local search (2-opt, relocate, swap) until local minimum.

    Args:
        routes: List of routes, each a list of 1-based customer indices.
        dist_matrix: (n+1) x (n+1) distance matrix.
        demands: 1D array of customer demands.
        capacity: Vehicle capacity limit.
        neighbour_matrix: Optional precomputed nearest neighbors.
        max_passes: Maximum iterations through all neighborhood operators.

    Returns:
        (improved_routes, total_cost)
    """
    # Deep copy routes so original caller is not mutated unexpectedly
    routes_copy = [list(r) for r in routes if r]
    loads = [float(sum(demands[c] for c in r)) for r in routes_copy]

    neighbour_set: dict[int, set[int]] | None = None
    if neighbour_matrix is not None:
        neighbour_set = {
            i: set(neighbour_matrix[i]) for i in range(1, neighbour_matrix.shape[0])
        }

    for _ in range(max_passes):
        improved = False

        # 1. Intra-route 2-Opt on each route
        for r in routes_copy:
            if intra_route_2opt(r, dist_matrix):
                improved = True
                break

        if improved:
            continue

        # 2. Inter-route Relocate
        if inter_route_relocate(
            routes_copy, loads, dist_matrix, demands, capacity, neighbour_set
        ):
            # Clean up empty routes
            routes_copy = [r for r in routes_copy if r]
            loads = [float(sum(demands[c] for c in r)) for r in routes_copy]
            improved = True
            continue

        # 3. Inter-route Swap
        if inter_route_swap(
            routes_copy, loads, dist_matrix, demands, capacity, neighbour_set
        ):
            improved = True
            continue

        # If no operator found an improvement, local optimum reached
        if not improved:
            break

    # Clean empty routes
    routes_copy = [r for r in routes_copy if r]
    total_cost = total_routes_cost(routes_copy, dist_matrix)
    return routes_copy, total_cost


def flatten_routes(routes: list[list[int]]) -> list[int]:
    """Concatenate customer routes into a giant tour permutation (1-based indices)."""
    flat: list[int] = []
    for r in routes:
        flat.extend(r)
    return flat


def project_tour_to_keys(old_keys: np.ndarray, improved_tour: Sequence[int]) -> np.ndarray:
    """Write back an improved customer permutation into continuous random keys.

    Preserves the exact values and distribution of old_keys while ensuring
    that np.argsort(new_keys) + 1 yields improved_tour precisely.

    Args:
        old_keys: 1D float array of particle keys in [0, 1].
        improved_tour: Sequence of 1-based customer indices in visiting order.

    Returns:
        1D float array of updated keys.
    """
    sorted_keys = np.sort(old_keys)
    new_keys = np.empty_like(old_keys)

    for rank, cust_id in enumerate(improved_tour):
        # cust_id is 1-based, index in keys is cust_id - 1
        new_keys[cust_id - 1] = sorted_keys[rank]

    return new_keys


=== FILE: solver/models.py ===
"""Domain models for Route.it CVRPTW solver.

Defines Pydantic v2 data models for Problem components (Depot, Customer, Vehicle, Problem)
and Solution components (Route, Solution).
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import math
from typing import Any, Literal
from pydantic import BaseModel, Field, field_validator, model_validator


class Depot(BaseModel):
    """Depot location and operational window."""

    id: str = "depot"
    lat: float = Field(..., description="Latitude or Y-coordinate")
    lon: float = Field(..., description="Longitude or X-coordinate")
    ready_time: float = Field(0.0, ge=0.0, description="Depot opening time in seconds")
    due_time: float = Field(float("inf"), description="Depot closing time in seconds")
    service_time: float = Field(0.0, ge=0.0, description="Depot handling / turnaround time in seconds")
    name: str | None = Field(None, description="Human-readable location label")

    @field_validator("due_time", mode="before")
    @classmethod
    def validate_due_time(cls, v: Any) -> float:
        """Handle null/None from JSON and ensure due time is strictly non-negative."""
        if v is None:
            return float("inf")
        val = float(v)
        if val < 0:
            raise ValueError("due_time must be non-negative")
        return val


class Customer(BaseModel):
    """Customer stop representing delivery demand and time window."""

    id: str = Field(..., description="Unique customer identifier")
    lat: float = Field(..., description="Latitude or Y-coordinate")
    lon: float = Field(..., description="Longitude or X-coordinate")
    demand: float = Field(..., ge=0.0, description="Units of capacity required")
    ready_time: float = Field(..., ge=0.0, description="Earliest service start time in seconds (e_i)")
    due_time: float = Field(..., description="Latest service start time in seconds (l_i)")
    service_time: float = Field(..., ge=0.0, description="Duration to service stop in seconds (s_i)")
    priority: int = Field(1, ge=1, description="Customer priority score (higher = higher priority)")
    name: str | None = Field(None, description="Human-readable customer label")

    @field_validator("due_time", mode="before")
    @classmethod
    def validate_customer_due_time(cls, v: Any) -> float:
        """Handle null/None from JSON as unconstrained infinity."""
        if v is None:
            return float("inf")
        return float(v)

    @model_validator(mode="after")
    def validate_time_window(self) -> Customer:
        """Validate that ready_time does not exceed due_time."""
        if self.ready_time > self.due_time:
            raise ValueError(
                f"Customer {self.id}: ready_time ({self.ready_time}) cannot exceed due_time ({self.due_time})"
            )
        return self


class Vehicle(BaseModel):
    """Vehicle profile and shift specifications."""

    id: str = Field(..., description="Unique vehicle identifier")
    capacity: float = Field(..., gt=0.0, description="Max load capacity (Q)")
    shift_start: float = Field(0.0, ge=0.0, description="Shift start time in seconds")
    shift_end: float = Field(float("inf"), description="Shift conclusion time in seconds")
    fixed_cost: float = Field(0.0, ge=0.0, description="Fixed mobilization cost")
    cost_per_time: float = Field(1.0, ge=0.0, description="Operational cost per unit time (seconds)")
    cost_per_distance: float = Field(1.0, ge=0.0, description="Operational cost per unit distance (metres)")

    @field_validator("shift_end", mode="before")
    @classmethod
    def validate_shift_end(cls, v: Any) -> float:
        """Handle null/None from JSON as unconstrained infinity."""
        if v is None:
            return float("inf")
        return float(v)

    @model_validator(mode="after")
    def validate_shift(self) -> Vehicle:
        """Validate shift interval."""
        if self.shift_start > self.shift_end:
            raise ValueError(
                f"Vehicle {self.id}: shift_start ({self.shift_start}) cannot exceed shift_end ({self.shift_end})"
            )
        return self


class Problem(BaseModel):
    """Complete problem instance specification for CVRPTW."""

    id: str = Field(..., description="Problem or instance identifier")
    depot: Depot = Field(..., description="Central distribution depot")
    customers: list[Customer] = Field(..., description="List of customers to service")
    vehicles: list[Vehicle] = Field(..., description="Available vehicle fleet")
    coordinate_system: Literal["geographic", "planar"] = Field(
        "geographic",
        description="Coordinate reference system: 'geographic' (WGS84 lat/lon in degrees) or 'planar' (Cartesian x/y)",
    )
    travel_time_matrix: list[list[float]] | None = Field(
        None,
        description="NxN travel time matrix index 0=depot, 1..N=customers",
    )
    distance_matrix: list[list[float]] | None = Field(
        None,
        description="NxN distance matrix index 0=depot, 1..N=customers",
    )

    @field_validator("customers")
    @classmethod
    def validate_unique_customer_ids(cls, customers: list[Customer]) -> list[Customer]:
        """Ensure all customer IDs are distinct."""
        ids = [c.id for c in customers]
        if len(ids) != len(set(ids)):
            raise ValueError("Customer IDs must be unique within a Problem")
        return customers

    @field_validator("vehicles")
    @classmethod
    def validate_unique_vehicle_ids(cls, vehicles: list[Vehicle]) -> list[Vehicle]:
        """Ensure all vehicle IDs are distinct."""
        ids = [v.id for v in vehicles]
        if len(ids) != len(set(ids)):
