            raise ValueError("Vehicle IDs must be unique within a Problem")
        return vehicles

    @property
    def num_customers(self) -> int:
        """Return total number of customers."""
        return len(self.customers)

    @property
    def num_vehicles(self) -> int:
        """Return total number of vehicles."""
        return len(self.vehicles)

    def get_customer(self, customer_id: str) -> Customer:
        """Fetch customer by ID."""
        for c in self.customers:
            if c.id == customer_id:
                return c
        raise KeyError(f"Customer {customer_id} not found in problem")

    def get_customer_index(self, customer_id: str) -> int:
        """Fetch 1-based index (0 is depot) of customer."""
        for idx, c in enumerate(self.customers, start=1):
            if c.id == customer_id:
                return idx
        raise KeyError(f"Customer {customer_id} not found in problem")

    def compute_euclidean_matrices(self, speed: float = 1.0) -> None:
        """Compute Euclidean distance and travel-time matrices based on coordinates.

        Node 0 is the depot, followed by customers 1..N.

        Args:
            speed: Uniform travel speed (distance units per time unit). Defaults to 1.0.
        """
        if speed <= 0:
            raise ValueError("Speed must be positive")

        nodes = [(self.depot.lat, self.depot.lon)] + [(c.lat, c.lon) for c in self.customers]
        n = len(nodes)

        dist_mat: list[list[float]] = [[0.0] * n for _ in range(n)]
        time_mat: list[list[float]] = [[0.0] * n for _ in range(n)]

        for i in range(n):
            lat_i, lon_i = nodes[i]
            for j in range(i + 1, n):
                lat_j, lon_j = nodes[j]
                if self.coordinate_system == "geographic":
                    # Haversine distance in metres
                    r = 6371000.0
                    phi1, phi2 = math.radians(lat_i), math.radians(lat_j)
                    dphi = math.radians(lat_j - lat_i)
                    dlam = math.radians(lon_j - lon_i)
                    a = math.sin(dphi / 2.0) ** 2 + math.cos(phi1) * math.cos(phi2) * math.sin(dlam / 2.0) ** 2
                    d = 2.0 * r * math.atan2(math.sqrt(a), math.sqrt(1.0 - a))
                else:
                    d = math.hypot(lat_i - lat_j, lon_i - lon_j)
                dist_mat[i][j] = dist_mat[j][i] = d
                t = d / speed
                time_mat[i][j] = time_mat[j][i] = t

        self.distance_matrix = dist_mat
        self.travel_time_matrix = time_mat


class Route(BaseModel):
    """Assigned vehicle route representing stop sequence and timeline."""

    vehicle_id: str = Field(..., description="ID of assigned vehicle")
    stop_ids: list[str] = Field(..., description="Sequence of customer stop IDs visited")
    arrival_times: list[float] = Field(
        ...,
        description="Arrival times in seconds at each customer stop in stop_ids",
    )
    load: float = Field(..., ge=0.0, description="Total demand serviced on this route")
    distance: float = Field(0.0, ge=0.0, description="Total distance traveled in metres")
    duration: float = Field(0.0, ge=0.0, description="Total route duration including service in seconds")


class Solution(BaseModel):
    """Complete multi-route solution with feasibility and performance metrics."""

    routes: list[Route] = Field(..., description="Collection of vehicle routes")
    total_cost: float = Field(..., description="Objective function value (distance / duration / penalties)")
    is_feasible: bool = Field(..., description="Whether solution satisfies all constraints")
    violations: dict[str, list[str]] = Field(
        default_factory=dict,
        description="Constraint violations categorized by type (e.g. capacity, time_window)",
    )
    algorithm: str = Field("QPSO", description="Algorithm that produced this solution")
    execution_time_sec: float = Field(0.0, ge=0.0, description="Execution runtime in seconds")
    seed: int | None = Field(None, description="Random seed used for reproduction")


=== FILE: solver/ortools_baseline.py ===
"""Google OR-Tools routing baseline for CVRP and CVRPTW.

Serves as an exact and metaheuristic baseline for benchmarking against Route.it's QPSO solver.
Uses the identical distance matrix and demand specifications to guarantee rigorous comparability.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import time
from typing import Sequence
import numpy as np
from ortools.constraint_solver import pywrapcp, routing_enums_pb2
from pydantic import BaseModel, Field

from solver.models import Problem, Route, Solution


class ORToolsConfig(BaseModel):
    """Configuration parameters for the OR-Tools baseline solver."""

    time_limit_sec: float = Field(10.0, gt=0.0, description="Solver runtime time limit in seconds")
    first_solution_strategy: str = Field(
        "PATH_CHEAPEST_ARC",
        description="Initial heuristic strategy (PATH_CHEAPEST_ARC, AUTOMATIC, etc.)",
    )
    metaheuristic: str = Field(
        "GUIDED_LOCAL_SEARCH",
        description="Local search metaheuristic (GUIDED_LOCAL_SEARCH, TABU_SEARCH, etc.)",
    )
    scale_factor: int = Field(
        10000,
        ge=1,
        description="Scaling factor to convert floating-point distances to integers for OR-Tools",
    )


def solve_ortools(
    problem: Problem,
    config: ORToolsConfig | None = None,
) -> Solution:
    """Solve the problem instance using Google OR-Tools Routing engine.

    Args:
        problem: Fully initialized Problem instance with precomputed matrices.
        config: ORToolsConfig options. If None, default settings are used.

    Returns:
        Solution model containing routes, costs, and feasibility metrics.
    """
    if config is None:
        config = ORToolsConfig()

    start_time = time.perf_counter()
    n = problem.num_customers
    num_vehicles = problem.num_vehicles

    if n == 0:
        return Solution(
            routes=[],
            total_cost=0.0,
            is_feasible=True,
            violations={},
            algorithm="OR-Tools",
            execution_time_sec=0.0,
            seed=None,
        )

    if problem.distance_matrix is None:
        problem.compute_euclidean_matrices()

    dist_mat = np.asarray(problem.distance_matrix, dtype=np.float64)
    scale = config.scale_factor

    # Scale distance matrix to integers for OR-Tools internal graph
    num_nodes = n + 1
    int_dist_mat = np.round(dist_mat * scale).astype(np.int64)

    # Depot is node 0
    manager = pywrapcp.RoutingIndexManager(num_nodes, num_vehicles, 0)
    routing = pywrapcp.RoutingModel(manager)

    # 1. Distance transit callback
    def distance_callback(from_index: int, to_index: int) -> int:
        from_node = manager.IndexToNode(from_index)
        to_node = manager.IndexToNode(to_index)
        return int(int_dist_mat[from_node][to_node])

    transit_callback_index = routing.RegisterTransitCallback(distance_callback)
    routing.SetArcCostEvaluatorOfAllVehicles(transit_callback_index)

    # 2. Demand and Capacity dimension
    # Node 0 is depot (demand 0), nodes 1..n are customers
    demands = [0] + [int(round(c.demand * 100)) for c in problem.customers]
    vehicle_capacities = [int(round(v.capacity * 100)) for v in problem.vehicles]

    def demand_callback(from_index: int) -> int:
        from_node = manager.IndexToNode(from_index)
        return demands[from_node]

    demand_callback_index = routing.RegisterUnaryTransitCallback(demand_callback)
    routing.AddDimensionWithVehicleCapacity(
        demand_callback_index,
        0,  # null capacity slack
        vehicle_capacities,
        True,  # start cumul to zero
        "Capacity",
    )

    # 3. Search parameters
    search_parameters = pywrapcp.DefaultRoutingSearchParameters()

    # Initial heuristic
    strategy = getattr(
        routing_enums_pb2.FirstSolutionStrategy,
        config.first_solution_strategy,
        routing_enums_pb2.FirstSolutionStrategy.PATH_CHEAPEST_ARC,
    )
    search_parameters.first_solution_strategy = strategy

    # Metaheuristic
    meta = getattr(
        routing_enums_pb2.LocalSearchMetaheuristic,
        config.metaheuristic,
        routing_enums_pb2.LocalSearchMetaheuristic.GUIDED_LOCAL_SEARCH,
    )
    search_parameters.local_search_metaheuristic = meta

    # Time limit
    sec = int(config.time_limit_sec)
    nanos = int((config.time_limit_sec - sec) * 1e9)
    search_parameters.time_limit.seconds = sec
    search_parameters.time_limit.nanos = nanos

    # 4. Solve
    solution_proto = routing.SolveWithParameters(search_parameters)
    elapsed_time = time.perf_counter() - start_time

    if not solution_proto:
        return Solution(
            routes=[],
            total_cost=float("inf"),
            is_feasible=False,
            violations={"ortools": ["No solution found within time limit"]},
            algorithm="OR-Tools",
            execution_time_sec=elapsed_time,
            seed=None,
        )

    # 5. Extract routes using our exact float distance matrix
    routes: list[Route] = []
    total_cost = 0.0

    for vehicle_idx in range(num_vehicles):
        index = routing.Start(vehicle_idx)
        stop_ids: list[str] = []
        route_load = 0.0
        route_dist = 0.0
        prev_node = 0

        # Traverse vehicle path
        while not routing.IsEnd(index):
            node = manager.IndexToNode(index)
            if node != 0:
                cust = problem.customers[node - 1]
                stop_ids.append(cust.id)
                route_load += cust.demand
                route_dist += dist_mat[prev_node, node]
                prev_node = node
            index = solution_proto.Value(routing.NextVar(index))

        # Return to depot if route served any customer
        if stop_ids:
            route_dist += dist_mat[prev_node, 0]
            veh_id = problem.vehicles[vehicle_idx].id
            routes.append(
                Route(
                    vehicle_id=veh_id,
                    stop_ids=stop_ids,
                    arrival_times=[0.0] * len(stop_ids),
                    load=route_load,
                    distance=route_dist,
                    duration=route_dist,
                )
            )
            total_cost += route_dist

    # Verify feasibility
    is_feasible = True
    violations: dict[str, list[str]] = {}

    visited_count = sum(len(r.stop_ids) for r in routes)
    if visited_count != n:
        is_feasible = False
        violations.setdefault("unassigned", []).append(
            f"Only {visited_count} of {n} customers served"
        )

    for r in routes:
        if r.load > problem.vehicles[0].capacity:
            is_feasible = False
            violations.setdefault("capacity", []).append(
                f"Vehicle {r.vehicle_id} load {r.load:.1f} exceeds capacity"
            )

    return Solution(
        routes=routes,
        total_cost=total_cost,
        is_feasible=is_feasible,
        violations=violations,
        algorithm="OR-Tools",
        execution_time_sec=elapsed_time,
        seed=None,
    )


=== FILE: solver/pso.py ===
"""Classical Particle Swarm Optimization (PSO) baseline for CVRPTW.

Serves as an empirical baseline to compare against the quantum-inspired QPSO solver.
Uses the identical continuous random-key encoding in [0, 1]^n and time-aware Prins Split evaluation.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import math
import time
from typing import Any, Callable
import numpy as np
from pydantic import BaseModel, Field

from solver.models import Problem, Solution
from solver.repair import repair_solution_time_windows
from solver.split import _split_cost_numba, prins_split
from solver.tensor import CostTensor, precompute_cost_tensor
from solver.validate import validate_solution


class PSOConfig(BaseModel):
    """Configuration parameters for the classical PSO baseline solver."""

    num_particles: int = Field(40, ge=2, description="Swarm size (M)")
    max_iters: int = Field(500, ge=1, description="Maximum iterations (T)")
    inertia_weight: float = Field(0.7298, description="Inertia weight (w)")
    c1: float = Field(1.49618, description="Cognitive acceleration coefficient (c1)")
    c2: float = Field(1.49618, description="Social acceleration coefficient (c2)")
    v_max: float = Field(0.2, gt=0.0, description="Maximum velocity boundary")
    seed: int | None = Field(None, description="Random seed for reproducibility")
    max_vehicles: int | None = Field(None, description="Optional cap on vehicle count")
    allow_extra_vehicles: bool = Field(False, description="Allow generating more routes than available fleet")

    # Driver duty limit and time window constraints
    max_duty_duration: float | None = Field(None, description="Max allowed shift duration per route")
    hard_time_windows: bool = Field(False, description="Strictly forbid time window violations during search")
    hard_duty_limit: bool = Field(False, description="Strictly forbid duty limit violations")

    # Priority customer configuration
    enforce_priority_early_service: bool = Field(
        False, description="Enforce early service window for priority customers"
    )
    priority_early_ratio: float = Field(0.5, description="Fraction of window for priority early service")

    # Adaptive penalty mechanism
    adaptive_penalty: bool = Field(True, description="Dynamically tune constraint penalty weights")
    penalty_alpha_init: float = Field(100.0, description="Initial capacity penalty weight")
    penalty_beta_init: float = Field(100.0, description="Initial time-window penalty weight")
    penalty_gamma_init: float = Field(100.0, description="Initial duty-limit penalty weight")
    adapt_freq: int = Field(5, description="Frequency (iterations) for adaptive penalty update")

    # Post-processing repair
    apply_tw_repair: bool = Field(True, description="Apply time-window repair operator on best solution")


def solve_pso(
    problem: Problem,
    config: PSOConfig | None = None,
    cost_oracle: Any | None = None,
    callback: Callable[[int, float], None] | None = None,
) -> tuple[Solution, list[float]]:
    """Execute classical PSO to optimize vehicle routes.

    Args:
        problem: Fully initialized Problem instance.
        config: PSOConfig options. If None, default settings are used.
        cost_oracle: Optional CostOracle for road network or traffic evaluation.
        callback: Optional callback(iteration, gbest_cost) invoked at each step.

    Returns:
        (Solution, cost_history) where cost_history records gbest cost per iteration.
    """
    if config is None:
        config = PSOConfig()

    start_time = time.perf_counter()
    rng = np.random.default_rng(config.seed)

    n = problem.num_customers
    if n == 0:
        return Solution(
            routes=[],
            total_cost=0.0,
            is_feasible=True,
            violations={},
            algorithm="PSO",
            execution_time_sec=0.0,
            seed=config.seed,
        ), [0.0]

    # Precompute time-bucketed CostTensor from CostOracle or problem
    cost_tensor = precompute_cost_tensor(problem, cost_oracle=cost_oracle)
    cost_data = cost_tensor.travel_time_tensor
    dist_mat = cost_tensor.distance_matrix

    num_nodes = n + 1
    demands = np.zeros(num_nodes, dtype=np.float64)
    services = np.zeros(num_nodes, dtype=np.float64)
    ready_times = np.zeros(num_nodes, dtype=np.float64)
    due_times = np.full(num_nodes, 1e12, dtype=np.float64)
    priority_factors = np.ones(num_nodes, dtype=np.float64)

    services[0] = problem.depot.service_time
    ready_times[0] = problem.depot.ready_time
    due_times[0] = problem.depot.due_time

    for idx, c in enumerate(problem.customers, start=1):
        demands[idx] = c.demand
        services[idx] = c.service_time
        ready_times[idx] = c.ready_time
        due_times[idx] = c.due_time
        priority_factors[idx] = 1.0 + (c.priority - 1) * 2.0

        if config.enforce_priority_early_service and c.priority > 1:
            early_due = c.ready_time + config.priority_early_ratio * (c.due_time - c.ready_time)
            due_times[idx] = early_due

    capacity = problem.vehicles[0].capacity if problem.vehicles else 100.0
    limit_v = config.max_vehicles if config.max_vehicles is not None else (len(problem.vehicles) if (problem.vehicles and not config.allow_extra_vehicles) else -1)

    max_duty = -1.0
    if config.max_duty_duration is not None:
        max_duty = config.max_duty_duration
    elif problem.vehicles:
        v0 = problem.vehicles[0]
        if math.isfinite(v0.shift_end) and math.isfinite(v0.shift_start):
            diff = v0.shift_end - v0.shift_start
            if diff > 0:
                max_duty = diff

    cur_alpha = config.penalty_alpha_init
    cur_beta = config.penalty_beta_init
    cur_gamma = config.penalty_gamma_init

    M = config.num_particles
    T = config.max_iters
    w = config.inertia_weight
    c1 = config.c1
    c2 = config.c2
    v_max = config.v_max

    # 1. Initialize positions in [0, 1]^n and velocities in [-v_max, v_max]^n
    X = rng.uniform(0.0, 1.0, size=(M, n))
    V = rng.uniform(-v_max, v_max, size=(M, n))

    P = np.copy(X)
    pbest_costs = np.full(M, np.inf, dtype=np.float64)

    gbest_x = np.copy(X[0])
    gbest_cost = np.inf

    def evaluate_particle_cost(keys: np.ndarray) -> float:
        tour = np.argsort(keys) + 1
        return _split_cost_numba(
            tour,
            cost_data,
            demands,
            capacity,
            limit_v,
            t_start=cost_tensor.t_start,
            bucket_size=cost_tensor.bucket_size,
            service_times=services,
            ready_times=ready_times,
            due_times=due_times,
            depot_due_time=problem.depot.due_time,
            priority_factors=priority_factors,
            max_duty=max_duty,
            alpha=cur_alpha,
            beta=cur_beta,
            gamma=cur_gamma,
            hard_time_windows=config.hard_time_windows,
            hard_duty_limit=config.hard_duty_limit,
        )

    # Initial evaluation
    for i in range(M):
        cost = evaluate_particle_cost(X[i])
        pbest_costs[i] = cost
        if cost < gbest_cost:
            gbest_cost = cost
            gbest_x = np.copy(X[i])

    cost_history: list[float] = [float(gbest_cost)]

    # 2. Main PSO iteration loop
    for t in range(T):
        for i in range(M):
            r1 = rng.uniform(0.0, 1.0, size=n)
            r2 = rng.uniform(0.0, 1.0, size=n)

            # Classical velocity update
            V[i] = w * V[i] + c1 * r1 * (P[i] - X[i]) + c2 * r2 * (gbest_x - X[i])
            V[i] = np.clip(V[i], -v_max, v_max)

            # Position update
            X[i] = np.clip(X[i] + V[i], 0.0, 1.0)

            cost = evaluate_particle_cost(X[i])

            # Update personal best
            if cost < pbest_costs[i]:
                pbest_costs[i] = cost
                P[i] = np.copy(X[i])

                # Update global best
                if cost < gbest_cost:
                    gbest_cost = cost
                    gbest_x = np.copy(X[i])

        # Adaptive Penalty Update based on Swarm Feasibility Ratio
        if config.adaptive_penalty and (t + 1) % config.adapt_freq == 0:
            sample_size = min(15, M)
            feas_count = 0
            for s_idx in range(sample_size):
                p_tour = np.argsort(X[s_idx]) + 1
                _, _, is_f, _ = prins_split(
                    problem,
                    p_tour,
                    max_vehicles=config.max_vehicles,
                    cost_oracle=cost_oracle,
                    cost_tensor=cost_tensor,
                    max_duty_duration=config.max_duty_duration,
                    hard_time_windows=config.hard_time_windows,
                    hard_duty_limit=config.hard_duty_limit,
                    enforce_priority_early_service=config.enforce_priority_early_service,
                    priority_early_ratio=config.priority_early_ratio,
                    alpha=cur_alpha,
                    beta=cur_beta,
                    gamma=cur_gamma,
                )
                if is_f:
                    feas_count += 1

            feas_ratio = feas_count / sample_size
            if feas_ratio < 0.20:
                cur_alpha = min(10000.0, cur_alpha * 1.25)
                cur_beta = min(10000.0, cur_beta * 1.25)
                cur_gamma = min(10000.0, cur_gamma * 1.25)
            elif feas_ratio > 0.45:
                cur_alpha = max(1.0, cur_alpha / 1.25)
                cur_beta = max(1.0, cur_beta / 1.25)
                cur_gamma = max(1.0, cur_gamma / 1.25)

        cost_history.append(float(gbest_cost))
        if callback is not None:
            callback(t + 1, float(gbest_cost))

    elapsed_time = time.perf_counter() - start_time

    # 3. Final route assembly using full prins_split
    best_tour = np.argsort(gbest_x) + 1
    total_cost, routes, is_feasible, violations = prins_split(
        problem,
        best_tour,
        max_vehicles=config.max_vehicles,
        allow_extra_vehicles=config.allow_extra_vehicles,
        cost_oracle=cost_oracle,
        cost_tensor=cost_tensor,
        max_duty_duration=config.max_duty_duration,
        hard_time_windows=config.hard_time_windows,
        hard_duty_limit=config.hard_duty_limit,
        enforce_priority_early_service=config.enforce_priority_early_service,
        priority_early_ratio=config.priority_early_ratio,
        alpha=cur_alpha,
        beta=cur_beta,
        gamma=cur_gamma,
    )

    solution = Solution(
        routes=routes,
        total_cost=total_cost,
        is_feasible=is_feasible,
        violations=violations,
        algorithm="PSO",
        execution_time_sec=elapsed_time,
        seed=config.seed,
    )

    # 4. Apply Time-Window Repair Operator if solution has residual time-window violations
    if config.apply_tw_repair and (not is_feasible or "time_window" in violations):
        solution = repair_solution_time_windows(
            problem, solution, cost_oracle=cost_oracle, max_passes=50
        )

    # 5. Independent validation stamp
    val_report = validate_solution(
        problem,
        solution,
        cost_oracle=cost_oracle,
        max_vehicles=config.max_vehicles,
        max_duty_duration=config.max_duty_duration,
        hard_duty_limit=config.hard_duty_limit,
        enforce_priority_early_service=config.enforce_priority_early_service,
        priority_early_ratio=config.priority_early_ratio,
    )
    solution.is_feasible = val_report.is_valid
    solution.violations = val_report.violations
    if solution.is_feasible:
        solution.total_cost = val_report.recomputed_cost

    return solution, cost_history


=== FILE: solver/qpso.py ===
"""Quantum-behaved Particle Swarm Optimization (QPSO) for CVRPTW.

Implements classical QPSO (Sun, Feng & Xu, 2004) over a continuous random-key space.
Each particle is a vector in [0, 1]^n, decoded into a giant tour via argsort,
and evaluated using the time-aware Prins Split algorithm.
Supports adaptive penalties, memetic local search, driver duty limits,
priority customer window scaling, and time-window repair operators.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import math
import time
from typing import Any, Callable
import numpy as np
from pydantic import BaseModel, Field

from solver.local_search import (
    build_neighbour_list,
    flatten_routes,
    project_tour_to_keys,
    run_local_search,
)
from solver.models import Problem, Solution
from solver.repair import repair_solution_time_windows
from solver.split import _split_cost_numba, prins_split
from solver.tensor import CostTensor, precompute_cost_tensor
from solver.validate import validate_solution


class QPSOConfig(BaseModel):
    """Configuration parameters for the QPSO solver."""

    num_particles: int = Field(40, ge=2, description="Swarm size (M)")
    max_iters: int = Field(500, ge=1, description="Maximum iterations (T)")
    alpha_max: float = Field(1.0, gt=0.0, description="Initial contraction-expansion coefficient")
    alpha_min: float = Field(0.5, gt=0.0, description="Final contraction-expansion coefficient")
    seed: int | None = Field(None, description="Random seed for reproducible execution")
    max_vehicles: int | None = Field(None, description="Optional cap on vehicle count")
    allow_extra_vehicles: bool = Field(False, description="Allow generating more routes than available fleet")

    # Driver duty limit and time window constraints
    max_duty_duration: float | None = Field(None, description="Max allowed shift duration per route")
    hard_time_windows: bool = Field(False, description="Strictly forbid time window violations during search")
    hard_duty_limit: bool = Field(False, description="Strictly forbid duty limit violations")

    # Priority customer configuration
    enforce_priority_early_service: bool = Field(
        False, description="Enforce early service window for priority customers"
    )
    priority_early_ratio: float = Field(0.5, description="Fraction of window for priority early service")

    # Adaptive penalty mechanism
    adaptive_penalty: bool = Field(True, description="Dynamically tune constraint penalty weights")
    penalty_alpha_init: float = Field(100.0, description="Initial capacity penalty weight")
    penalty_beta_init: float = Field(100.0, description="Initial time-window penalty weight")
    penalty_gamma_init: float = Field(100.0, description="Initial duty-limit penalty weight")
    adapt_freq: int = Field(5, description="Frequency (iterations) for adaptive penalty update")

    # Post-processing repair
    apply_tw_repair: bool = Field(True, description="Apply time-window repair operator on best solution")

    # Memetic local search settings
    local_search_freq: int = Field(
        20,
        description="Apply local search every m iterations (<=0 to disable)",
    )
    local_search_top_k: int = Field(
        3,
        ge=1,
        description="Number of top particles to refine with local search",
    )
    use_neighbour_list: bool = Field(
        True,
        description="Whether to use nearest-neighbour list pruning in local search",
    )
    k_nearest: int = Field(15, ge=2, description="Number of nearest neighbours per customer")


def solve_qpso(
    problem: Problem,
    config: QPSOConfig | None = None,
    cost_oracle: Any | None = None,
    callback: Callable[[int, float], None] | None = None,
    seed_keys: np.ndarray | None = None,
    seed_fraction: float = 0.5,
) -> tuple[Solution, list[float]]:
    """Execute QPSO to optimize vehicle routes for a given CVRPTW problem instance.

    Args:
        problem: Fully initialized Problem instance.
        config: QPSOConfig options. If None, default settings are used.
        cost_oracle: Optional CostOracle for road network or traffic evaluation.
        callback: Optional callback(iteration, gbest_cost) invoked at each step.
        seed_keys: Optional initial continuous keys array of size n to warm-start the swarm.
        seed_fraction: Fraction of swarm particles initialized around seed_keys (default: 0.5).

    Returns:
        (Solution, cost_history) where cost_history records gbest cost per iteration.
    """
    if config is None:
        config = QPSOConfig()

    start_time = time.perf_counter()
    rng = np.random.default_rng(config.seed)

    n = problem.num_customers
    if n == 0:
        return Solution(
            routes=[],
            total_cost=0.0,
            is_feasible=True,
            violations={},
            algorithm="QPSO",
            execution_time_sec=0.0,
            seed=config.seed,
        ), [0.0]

    # Precompute time-bucketed CostTensor from CostOracle or problem
    cost_tensor = precompute_cost_tensor(problem, cost_oracle=cost_oracle)
    cost_data = cost_tensor.travel_time_tensor
    dist_mat = cost_tensor.distance_matrix

    num_nodes = n + 1
    demands = np.zeros(num_nodes, dtype=np.float64)
    services = np.zeros(num_nodes, dtype=np.float64)
    ready_times = np.zeros(num_nodes, dtype=np.float64)
    due_times = np.full(num_nodes, 1e12, dtype=np.float64)
    priority_factors = np.ones(num_nodes, dtype=np.float64)

    services[0] = problem.depot.service_time
    ready_times[0] = problem.depot.ready_time
    due_times[0] = problem.depot.due_time

    for idx, c in enumerate(problem.customers, start=1):
        demands[idx] = c.demand
        services[idx] = c.service_time
        ready_times[idx] = c.ready_time
        due_times[idx] = c.due_time
        priority_factors[idx] = 1.0 + (c.priority - 1) * 2.0

        if config.enforce_priority_early_service and c.priority > 1:
            early_due = c.ready_time + config.priority_early_ratio * (c.due_time - c.ready_time)
            due_times[idx] = early_due

    capacity = problem.vehicles[0].capacity if problem.vehicles else 100.0
    limit_v = config.max_vehicles if config.max_vehicles is not None else (len(problem.vehicles) if (problem.vehicles and not config.allow_extra_vehicles) else -1)

    max_duty = -1.0
    if config.max_duty_duration is not None:
        max_duty = config.max_duty_duration
    elif problem.vehicles:
        v0 = problem.vehicles[0]
        if math.isfinite(v0.shift_end) and math.isfinite(v0.shift_start):
            diff = v0.shift_end - v0.shift_start
            if diff > 0:
                max_duty = diff

    # Adaptive penalty parameters
    cur_alpha = config.penalty_alpha_init
    cur_beta = config.penalty_beta_init
    cur_gamma = config.penalty_gamma_init

    M = config.num_particles
    T = config.max_iters

    # Precompute neighbour list if local search is enabled
    neighbour_mat = (
        build_neighbour_list(dist_mat, config.k_nearest)
        if (config.local_search_freq > 0 and config.use_neighbour_list)
        else None
    )

    # 1. Initialize particle positions uniformly in [0, 1]^n
    X = rng.uniform(0.0, 1.0, size=(M, n))
    if seed_keys is not None and len(seed_keys) == n:
        num_seeded = max(1, min(M, int(M * seed_fraction)))
        X[0] = np.clip(seed_keys, 0.0, 1.0)
        for i in range(1, num_seeded):
            noise = rng.normal(0.0, 0.05, size=n)
            X[i] = np.clip(seed_keys + noise, 0.0, 1.0)

    # Personal best positions and costs
    P = np.copy(X)
    pbest_costs = np.full(M, np.inf, dtype=np.float64)

    gbest_x = np.copy(X[0])
    gbest_cost = np.inf

    def evaluate_particle_cost(keys: np.ndarray) -> float:
        tour = np.argsort(keys) + 1
        return _split_cost_numba(
            tour,
            cost_data,
            demands,
            capacity,
            limit_v,
            t_start=cost_tensor.t_start,
            bucket_size=cost_tensor.bucket_size,
            service_times=services,
            ready_times=ready_times,
            due_times=due_times,
            depot_due_time=problem.depot.due_time,
            priority_factors=priority_factors,
            max_duty=max_duty,
            alpha=cur_alpha,
            beta=cur_beta,
            gamma=cur_gamma,
            hard_time_windows=config.hard_time_windows,
            hard_duty_limit=config.hard_duty_limit,
        )

    # Initial evaluation
    for i in range(M):
        cost = evaluate_particle_cost(X[i])
        pbest_costs[i] = cost
        if cost < gbest_cost:
            gbest_cost = cost
            gbest_x = np.copy(X[i])

    cost_history: list[float] = [float(gbest_cost)]

    # 2. Main QPSO iteration loop
    for t in range(T):
        # Linearly anneal alpha coefficient from alpha_max down to alpha_min
        ce_alpha = config.alpha_max - (t / T) * (config.alpha_max - config.alpha_min)

        # Center of gravity: mean best position across all particles
        mbest = np.mean(P, axis=0)

        for i in range(M):
            # Stochastic local attractor
            phi = rng.uniform(0.0, 1.0, size=n)
            p = phi * P[i] + (1.0 - phi) * gbest_x

            # Quantum delta-potential well wave function update
            u = rng.uniform(1e-10, 1.0, size=n)
            sign = np.where(rng.random(size=n) < 0.5, 1.0, -1.0)
            step = ce_alpha * np.abs(mbest - X[i]) * np.log(1.0 / u)
            X[i] = p + sign * step

            # Clamp keys to [0, 1]
            X[i] = np.clip(X[i], 0.0, 1.0)

            cost = evaluate_particle_cost(X[i])

            if cost < pbest_costs[i]:
                pbest_costs[i] = cost
                P[i] = np.copy(X[i])

                if cost < gbest_cost:
                    gbest_cost = cost
                    gbest_x = np.copy(X[i])

        # Adaptive Penalty Update based on Swarm Feasibility Ratio
        if config.adaptive_penalty and (t + 1) % config.adapt_freq == 0:
            # Sample swarm particles for feasibility
            sample_size = min(15, M)
            feas_count = 0
            for s_idx in range(sample_size):
                p_tour = np.argsort(X[s_idx]) + 1
                _, _, is_f, _ = prins_split(
                    problem,
                    p_tour,
                    max_vehicles=config.max_vehicles,
                    cost_oracle=cost_oracle,
                    cost_tensor=cost_tensor,
                    max_duty_duration=config.max_duty_duration,
                    hard_time_windows=config.hard_time_windows,
                    hard_duty_limit=config.hard_duty_limit,
                    enforce_priority_early_service=config.enforce_priority_early_service,
                    priority_early_ratio=config.priority_early_ratio,
                    alpha=cur_alpha,
                    beta=cur_beta,
                    gamma=cur_gamma,
                )
                if is_f:
                    feas_count += 1

            feas_ratio = feas_count / sample_size
            if feas_ratio < 0.20:
                # Mostly infeasible: drive harder towards feasibility
                cur_alpha = min(10000.0, cur_alpha * 1.25)
                cur_beta = min(10000.0, cur_beta * 1.25)
                cur_gamma = min(10000.0, cur_gamma * 1.25)
            elif feas_ratio > 0.45:
                # Mostly feasible: allow relaxed exploration
                cur_alpha = max(1.0, cur_alpha / 1.25)
                cur_beta = max(1.0, cur_beta / 1.25)
                cur_gamma = max(1.0, cur_gamma / 1.25)

        # 3. Memetic Step: Local Search on top k particles every m iterations
        if config.local_search_freq > 0 and (t + 1) % config.local_search_freq == 0:
            top_k_indices = np.argsort(pbest_costs)[: config.local_search_top_k]

            for idx in top_k_indices:
                p_tour = np.argsort(P[idx]) + 1
                _, current_routes, is_feas, _ = prins_split(
                    problem,
                    p_tour,
                    max_vehicles=config.max_vehicles,
                    cost_oracle=cost_oracle,
                    cost_tensor=cost_tensor,
                    max_duty_duration=config.max_duty_duration,
                    hard_time_windows=config.hard_time_windows,
                    hard_duty_limit=config.hard_duty_limit,
                    enforce_priority_early_service=config.enforce_priority_early_service,
                    priority_early_ratio=config.priority_early_ratio,
                    alpha=cur_alpha,
                    beta=cur_beta,
                    gamma=cur_gamma,
                )
                if not is_feas:
                    continue

                route_indices = [
                    [problem.get_customer_index(sid) for sid in r.stop_ids]
                    for r in current_routes
                ]

                improved_routes, ls_cost = run_local_search(
                    route_indices, dist_mat, demands, capacity, neighbour_mat
                )

                if ls_cost < pbest_costs[idx] - 1e-7:
                    improved_tour = flatten_routes(improved_routes)
                    new_keys = project_tour_to_keys(P[idx], improved_tour)
                    P[idx] = new_keys
                    X[idx] = np.copy(new_keys)
                    pbest_costs[idx] = ls_cost

                    if ls_cost < gbest_cost:
                        gbest_cost = ls_cost
                        gbest_x = np.copy(new_keys)

        cost_history.append(float(gbest_cost))
        if callback is not None:
            callback(t + 1, float(gbest_cost))

    # 4. Final Solution Construction from Global Best
    best_tour = np.argsort(gbest_x) + 1
    total_cost, routes, is_feasible, violations = prins_split(
        problem,
        best_tour,
        max_vehicles=config.max_vehicles,
        allow_extra_vehicles=config.allow_extra_vehicles,
        cost_oracle=cost_oracle,
        cost_tensor=cost_tensor,
        max_duty_duration=config.max_duty_duration,
        hard_time_windows=config.hard_time_windows,
        hard_duty_limit=config.hard_duty_limit,
        enforce_priority_early_service=config.enforce_priority_early_service,
        priority_early_ratio=config.priority_early_ratio,
        alpha=cur_alpha,
        beta=cur_beta,
        gamma=cur_gamma,
    )

    # 5. Local Search refinement if feasible
    if config.local_search_freq > 0 and is_feasible:
        route_indices = [
            [problem.get_customer_index(sid) for sid in r.stop_ids]
            for r in routes
        ]
        improved_routes, final_ls_cost = run_local_search(
            route_indices, dist_mat, demands, capacity, neighbour_mat
        )
        if final_ls_cost < total_cost - 1e-7:
            improved_tour = flatten_routes(improved_routes)
            total_cost, routes, is_feasible, violations = prins_split(
                problem,
                improved_tour,
                max_vehicles=config.max_vehicles,
                allow_extra_vehicles=config.allow_extra_vehicles,
                cost_oracle=cost_oracle,
                cost_tensor=cost_tensor,
                max_duty_duration=config.max_duty_duration,
                hard_time_windows=config.hard_time_windows,
                hard_duty_limit=config.hard_duty_limit,
                enforce_priority_early_service=config.enforce_priority_early_service,
                priority_early_ratio=config.priority_early_ratio,
                alpha=cur_alpha,
                beta=cur_beta,
                gamma=cur_gamma,
            )

    elapsed_time = time.perf_counter() - start_time

    solution = Solution(
        routes=routes,
        total_cost=total_cost,
        is_feasible=is_feasible,
        violations=violations,
        algorithm="QPSO+LS" if config.local_search_freq > 0 else "QPSO",
        execution_time_sec=elapsed_time,
        seed=config.seed,
    )

    # 6. Apply Time-Window Repair Operator if solution has residual time-window violations
    if config.apply_tw_repair and (not is_feasible or "time_window" in violations):
        solution = repair_solution_time_windows(
            problem, solution, cost_oracle=cost_oracle, max_passes=50
        )

    # 7. Independent validation stamp
    val_report = validate_solution(
        problem,
        solution,
        cost_oracle=cost_oracle,
        max_vehicles=config.max_vehicles,
        max_duty_duration=config.max_duty_duration,
        hard_duty_limit=config.hard_duty_limit,
        enforce_priority_early_service=config.enforce_priority_early_service,
        priority_early_ratio=config.priority_early_ratio,
    )
    solution.is_feasible = val_report.is_valid
    solution.violations = val_report.violations
    if solution.is_feasible:
        solution.total_cost = val_report.recomputed_cost

    return solution, cost_history


=== FILE: solver/repair.py ===
"""Time-window repair operator for Route.it CVRPTW solver.

Performs targeted local search repair to eliminate or minimize time-window violations
in multi-route solutions using intra/inter-route relocate and swap operators.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import copy
import math
from typing import Any
from solver.models import Customer, Problem, Route, Solution
from solver.validate import validate_solution


def _simulate_route_timeline(
    problem: Problem,
    stop_ids: list[str],
    customer_map: dict[str, Customer],
    customer_idx_map: dict[str, int],
    cost_oracle: Any | None,
    loc_depot: Any | None,
    loc_custs: dict[str, Any],
) -> tuple[float, float, float, list[float]]:
    """Simulate route timeline returning (total_distance, total_duration, lateness_violation, arrival_times)."""
    if not stop_ids:
        return 0.0, 0.0, 0.0, []

    curr_time = problem.depot.ready_time
    prev_idx = 0
    prev_loc = loc_depot
    prev_pt = (problem.depot.lat, problem.depot.lon)
    route_dist = 0.0
    tw_viol = 0.0
    arrival_times: list[float] = []

    matrix = problem.travel_time_matrix
    dist_matrix = problem.distance_matrix

    for cid in stop_ids:
        cust = customer_map[cid]
        c_idx = customer_idx_map[cid]
        c_pt = (cust.lat, cust.lon)
        c_loc = loc_custs.get(cid)

        if cost_oracle is not None and prev_loc is not None and c_loc is not None:
            tt = float(cost_oracle.travel_time(prev_loc, c_loc, depart_time=curr_time))
            d = float(cost_oracle.distance(prev_loc, c_loc))
        elif matrix is not None:
            tt = float(matrix[prev_idx][c_idx])
            d = float(dist_matrix[prev_idx][c_idx]) if dist_matrix is not None else tt
        else:
            d = math.hypot(prev_pt[0] - c_pt[0], prev_pt[1] - c_pt[1])
            tt = d

        route_dist += d
        arr = curr_time + tt
        arrival_times.append(arr)

        if arr > cust.due_time:
            lateness = arr - cust.due_time
            p_fac = 1.0 + (cust.priority - 1) * 2.0
            tw_viol += lateness * p_fac

        start_serv = max(arr, cust.ready_time)
        curr_time = start_serv + cust.service_time
        prev_idx = c_idx
        prev_loc = c_loc
        prev_pt = c_pt

    # Return to depot
    depot_pt = (problem.depot.lat, problem.depot.lon)
    if cost_oracle is not None and prev_loc is not None and loc_depot is not None:
        tt = float(cost_oracle.travel_time(prev_loc, loc_depot, depart_time=curr_time))
        d = float(cost_oracle.distance(prev_loc, loc_depot))
    elif matrix is not None:
        tt = float(matrix[prev_idx][0])
        d = float(dist_matrix[prev_idx][0]) if dist_matrix is not None else tt
    else:
        d = math.hypot(prev_pt[0] - depot_pt[0], prev_pt[1] - depot_pt[1])
        tt = d

    route_dist += d
    depot_arr = curr_time + tt
    if depot_arr > problem.depot.due_time:
        tw_viol += (depot_arr - problem.depot.due_time)

    duration = depot_arr - problem.depot.ready_time
    return route_dist, duration, tw_viol, arrival_times


def repair_solution_time_windows(
    problem: Problem,
    solution: Solution,
    cost_oracle: Any | None = None,
    max_passes: int = 50,
) -> Solution:
    """Repair time-window violations in a Solution using relocate, swap, and reordering.

    Args:
        problem: Problem instance.
        solution: Starting solution (which may be infeasible due to time windows).
        cost_oracle: Optional CostOracle for dynamic or road network routing.
        max_passes: Maximum optimization passes over late customers.

    Returns:
        Repaired Solution (with updated routes, cost, and feasibility status).
    """
    if solution.is_feasible and not solution.violations:
        return solution

    customer_map: dict[str, Customer] = {c.id: c for c in problem.customers}
    customer_idx_map: dict[str, int] = {c.id: idx for idx, c in enumerate(problem.customers, start=1)}

    loc_depot = None
    loc_custs: dict[str, Any] = {}
    if cost_oracle is not None:
        from routing.cost_oracle import Location
        loc_depot = Location(id=problem.depot.id, lat=problem.depot.lat, lon=problem.depot.lon)
        for c in problem.customers:
            loc_custs[c.id] = Location(id=c.id, lat=c.lat, lon=c.lon)

    # Work on a mutable copy of stop sequences per route
    route_stops: list[list[str]] = [list(r.stop_ids) for r in solution.routes]
    vehicle_ids: list[str] = [r.vehicle_id for r in solution.routes]
    capacities: list[float] = [
        next((v.capacity for v in problem.vehicles if v.id == vid), problem.vehicles[0].capacity if problem.vehicles else 100.0)
        for vid in vehicle_ids
    ]

    def eval_route(s_ids: list[str]) -> tuple[float, float, float, list[float]]:
        return _simulate_route_timeline(
            problem, s_ids, customer_map, customer_idx_map, cost_oracle, loc_depot, loc_custs
        )

    # Initial state
    current_metrics = [eval_route(s) for s in route_stops]
    total_tw_viol = sum(m[2] for m in current_metrics)

    if total_tw_viol <= 1e-4:
        return solution

    improved = True
    passes = 0

    while improved and passes < max_passes and total_tw_viol > 1e-4:
        improved = False
        passes += 1

        # Scan each route
        for r_idx in range(len(route_stops)):
            stops = route_stops[r_idx]
            if not stops:
                continue

            r_dist, r_dur, r_viol, arr_times = current_metrics[r_idx]
            if r_viol <= 1e-4:
                continue

            # Identify late customers in this route
            for pos in range(len(stops)):
                cid = stops[pos]
                cust = customer_map[cid]
                if arr_times[pos] <= cust.due_time:
                    continue  # not late

                # Strategy 1: Intra-route Relocate (Move late customer earlier in same route)
                for new_pos in range(pos):
                    cand_stops = list(stops)
                    cand_stops.pop(pos)
                    cand_stops.insert(new_pos, cid)

                    c_dist, c_dur, c_viol, c_arrs = eval_route(cand_stops)
                    if c_viol < r_viol - 1e-4:
                        route_stops[r_idx] = cand_stops
                        current_metrics[r_idx] = (c_dist, c_dur, c_viol, c_arrs)
                        total_tw_viol = sum(m[2] for m in current_metrics)
                        improved = True
                        break

                if improved:
                    break

                # Strategy 2: Inter-route Relocate (Move late customer to another route)
                demand = cust.demand
                for other_idx in range(len(route_stops)):
                    if other_idx == r_idx:
                        continue

                    # Check capacity of target route
                    other_load = sum(customer_map[c].demand for c in route_stops[other_idx])
                    if other_load + demand > capacities[other_idx]:
                        continue

                    # Try inserting cid at each position in other route
                    best_cand_other = None
                    best_other_viol = current_metrics[other_idx][2]
                    best_pos = -1

                    for ins_pos in range(len(route_stops[other_idx]) + 1):
                        cand_other = list(route_stops[other_idx])
                        cand_other.insert(ins_pos, cid)
                        o_dist, o_dur, o_viol, o_arrs = eval_route(cand_other)

                        # We evaluate net change in both routes
                        cand_src = list(stops)
                        cand_src.pop(pos)
                        s_dist, s_dur, s_viol, s_arrs = eval_route(cand_src)

                        net_change = (s_viol + o_viol) - (r_viol + current_metrics[other_idx][2])
                        if net_change < -1e-4:
                            # Accepted move!
                            route_stops[r_idx] = cand_src
                            route_stops[other_idx] = cand_other
                            current_metrics[r_idx] = (s_dist, s_dur, s_viol, s_arrs)
                            current_metrics[other_idx] = (o_dist, o_dur, o_viol, o_arrs)
                            total_tw_viol = sum(m[2] for m in current_metrics)
                            improved = True
                            break

                    if improved:
                        break

                if improved:
                    break

                # Strategy 3: Intra-route Swap (Swap with an earlier stop)
                for swap_pos in range(pos):
                    cand_stops = list(stops)
                    cand_stops[pos], cand_stops[swap_pos] = cand_stops[swap_pos], cand_stops[pos]
                    c_dist, c_dur, c_viol, c_arrs = eval_route(cand_stops)
                    if c_viol < r_viol - 1e-4:
                        route_stops[r_idx] = cand_stops
                        current_metrics[r_idx] = (c_dist, c_dur, c_viol, c_arrs)
                        total_tw_viol = sum(m[2] for m in current_metrics)
                        improved = True
                        break

                if improved:
                    break

            if improved:
                break

    # Build final Route objects from repaired sequences
    repaired_routes: list[Route] = []
    total_cost = 0.0

    for r_idx, stops in enumerate(route_stops):
        if not stops:
            continue
        dist, dur, viol, arrs = eval_route(stops)
        load = sum(customer_map[c].demand for c in stops)
        total_cost += dist
        repaired_routes.append(
            Route(
                vehicle_id=vehicle_ids[r_idx],
                stop_ids=stops,
                arrival_times=arrs,
                load=load,
                distance=dist,
                duration=dur,
            )
        )

    # Re-validate with independent validator
    tentative_sol = Solution(
        routes=repaired_routes,
        total_cost=total_cost,
        is_feasible=(total_tw_viol <= 1e-4),
        algorithm=solution.algorithm + " + TW-Repair",
        execution_time_sec=solution.execution_time_sec,
        seed=solution.seed,
    )

    val = validate_solution(problem, tentative_sol, cost_oracle=cost_oracle)
    tentative_sol.is_feasible = val.is_valid
    tentative_sol.violations = val.violations
    tentative_sol.total_cost = val.recomputed_cost

    return tentative_sol


=== FILE: solver/run.py ===
"""Command line interface runner for Route.it solvers (QPSO and baseline PSO).

Usage example:
    python -m solver.run --instance benchmarks/instances/A-n32-k5.vrp --seed 1 --particles 40 --iters 500 --out result.json
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
from pathlib import Path
import sys
from typing import Sequence

# Ensure project root is in sys.path when executed directly
project_root = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent
if str(project_root) not in sys.path:
    sys.path.insert(0, str(project_root))

from solver.io import load_cvrplib, load_solomon, save_solution_json
from solver.models import Problem, Solution
from solver.ortools_baseline import ORToolsConfig, solve_ortools
from solver.pso import PSOConfig, solve_pso
from solver.qpso import QPSOConfig, solve_qpso


def load_instance(path_str: str | Path) -> Problem:
    """Load a problem instance based on file extension (.vrp or .txt)."""
    p = Path(path_str)
    if not p.is_file():
        raise FileNotFoundError(f"Instance file not found: {p}")

    suffix = p.suffix.lower()
    if suffix == ".vrp":
        return load_cvrplib(p)
    elif suffix == ".txt":
        return load_solomon(p)
    else:
        raise ValueError(f"Unsupported file format '{suffix}'. Expected .vrp or .txt")


def run_solver(
    instance_path: str | Path,
    solver_name: str = "qpso",
    seed: int = 1,
    particles: int = 40,
    iters: int = 500,
    alpha_max: float = 1.0,
    alpha_min: float = 0.5,
    max_vehicles: int | None = None,
    ls_freq: int = 20,
    ls_top_k: int = 3,
    no_ls: bool = False,
    time_limit_sec: float = 10.0,
    out_path: str | Path | None = None,
    verbose: bool = True,
) -> Solution:
    """Load instance, execute selected solver, and optionally persist solution."""
    if verbose:
        print(f"Loading instance: {instance_path}")

    problem = load_instance(instance_path)

    if verbose:
        print(
            f"Loaded '{problem.id}' with {problem.num_customers} customers, "
            f"{problem.num_vehicles} vehicles, capacity {problem.vehicles[0].capacity:.1f}"
        )

    def progress_callback(iteration: int, gbest_cost: float) -> None:
        if verbose and (iteration % 50 == 0 or iteration == iters or iteration == 1):
            print(f"  [Iter {iteration:4d}/{iters:4d}] Current Best Cost: {gbest_cost:10.2f}")

    if solver_name.lower() == "qpso":
        actual_ls_freq = 0 if no_ls else ls_freq
        if verbose:
            mode_str = "Memetic QPSO+LS" if actual_ls_freq > 0 else "Pure QPSO"
            print(
                f"Running {mode_str} (particles={particles}, iters={iters}, seed={seed}, "
                f"alpha=[{alpha_max}, {alpha_min}], ls_freq={actual_ls_freq}, ls_top_k={ls_top_k})..."
            )
        cfg = QPSOConfig(
            num_particles=particles,
            max_iters=iters,
            alpha_max=alpha_max,
            alpha_min=alpha_min,
            seed=seed,
            max_vehicles=max_vehicles,
            local_search_freq=actual_ls_freq,
            local_search_top_k=ls_top_k,
        )
        solution, _ = solve_qpso(problem, cfg, callback=progress_callback)
    elif solver_name.lower() == "pso":
        if verbose:
            print(f"Running Baseline PSO (particles={particles}, iters={iters}, seed={seed})...")
        pso_cfg = PSOConfig(
            num_particles=particles,
            max_iters=iters,
            seed=seed,
            max_vehicles=max_vehicles,
        )
        solution, _ = solve_pso(problem, pso_cfg, callback=progress_callback)
    elif solver_name.lower() == "ortools":
        if verbose:
            print(f"Running Google OR-Tools Baseline (time_limit={time_limit_sec:.1f}s)...")
        ortools_cfg = ORToolsConfig(time_limit_sec=time_limit_sec)
        solution = solve_ortools(problem, ortools_cfg)
    else:
        raise ValueError(f"Unknown solver '{solver_name}'. Choose 'qpso', 'pso', or 'ortools'.")

    if verbose:
        print("\n" + "=" * 50)
        print(f"Algorithm:       {solution.algorithm}")
        print(f"Total Cost:      {solution.total_cost:.2f}")
        print(f"Feasible:        {solution.is_feasible}")
        print(f"Routes:          {len(solution.routes)}")
        print(f"Execution Time:  {solution.execution_time_sec:.3f} s")
        if solution.violations:
            print(f"Violations:      {solution.violations}")
        print("=" * 50)

    if out_path:
        save_solution_json(solution, out_path)
        if verbose:
            print(f"Solution saved to {out_path}")

    return solution


def main(argv: Sequence[str] | None = None) -> int:
    """CLI entrypoint."""
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(
        description="Route.it Solver CLI Runner (QPSO & Baseline PSO)."
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--instance",
        "-i",
        type=str,
        required=True,
        help="Path to problem instance (.vrp or .txt)",
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--solver",
        "-s",
        type=str,
        default="qpso",
        choices=["qpso", "pso", "ortools"],
        help="Optimization algorithm to run (default: qpso)",
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--seed",
        type=int,
        default=1,
        help="Random seed for reproducible results (default: 1)",
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--particles",
        "-p",
        type=int,
        default=40,
        help="Swarm size / number of particles (default: 40)",
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--iters",
        type=int,
        default=500,
        help="Maximum optimization iterations (default: 500)",
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--time-limit",
        "-t",
        type=float,
        default=10.0,
        help="Time limit in seconds (primarily for OR-Tools, default: 10.0)",
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--alpha-max",
        type=float,
        default=1.0,
        help="QPSO initial alpha coefficient (default: 1.0)",
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--alpha-min",
        type=float,
        default=0.5,
        help="QPSO final alpha coefficient (default: 0.5)",
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--max-vehicles",
        type=int,
        default=None,
        help="Optional maximum number of vehicle routes allowed",
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--ls-freq",
        type=int,
        default=20,
        help="Apply local search every m iterations (default: 20, 0 to disable)",
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--ls-top-k",
        type=int,
        default=3,
        help="Number of top particles refined with local search (default: 3)",
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--no-ls",
        action="store_true",
        help="Disable memetic local search entirely",
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--out",
        "-o",
        type=str,
        default=None,
        help="Path to output JSON file to save the final solution",
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--quiet",
        "-q",
        action="store_true",
        help="Suppress console progress output",
    )

    args = parser.parse_args(argv)

    try:
        run_solver(
            instance_path=args.instance,
            solver_name=args.solver,
            seed=args.seed,
            particles=args.particles,
            iters=args.iters,
            alpha_max=args.alpha_max,
            alpha_min=args.alpha_min,
            max_vehicles=args.max_vehicles,
            ls_freq=args.ls_freq,
            ls_top_k=args.ls_top_k,
            no_ls=args.no_ls,
            time_limit_sec=args.time_limit,
            out_path=args.out,
            verbose=not args.quiet,
        )
        return 0
    except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
        print(f"Error running solver: {exc}", file=sys.stderr)
        return 1


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main())


=== FILE: solver/split.py ===
"""Prins Split algorithm for CVRPTW.

Partitions a continuous random-key giant tour into optimal feasible vehicle
routes using a shortest-path-on-DAG formulation (Prins, 2004).
Includes time windows with waiting times, service times, driver duty limits,
priority customer scaling, and adaptive constraint penalties.
Supports Numba JIT acceleration with pure NumPy fallback.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import math
from typing import Any, Sequence
import numpy as np

# Optional Numba JIT acceleration
try:
    from numba import njit  # type: ignore
    HAS_NUMBA = True
except ImportError:
    HAS_NUMBA = False

    def njit(*args: Any, **kwargs: Any) -> Any:  # type: ignore
        """Fallback no-op decorator when numba is not installed."""
        def decorator(func: Any) -> Any:
            return func
        return decorator

from solver.models import Customer, Problem, Route
from solver.tensor import CostTensor, precompute_cost_tensor


@njit(fastmath=True)
def _split_cost_numba(
    tour: np.ndarray,
    cost_data: np.ndarray,
    demands: np.ndarray,
    capacity: float,
    max_vehicles: int = -1,
    overflow_penalty: float = 10000.0,
    t_start: float = 0.0,
    bucket_size: float = 900.0,
    service_times: np.ndarray | None = None,
    ready_times: np.ndarray | None = None,
    due_times: np.ndarray | None = None,
    depot_due_time: float = 1e12,
    priority_factors: np.ndarray | None = None,
    max_duty: float = -1.0,
    alpha: float = 100.0,
    beta: float = 100.0,
    gamma: float = 100.0,
    hard_time_windows: bool = False,
    hard_duty_limit: bool = False,
) -> float:
    """Numba-accelerated evaluation of the optimal split cost for a giant tour.

    Supports pure CVRP (fast O(1) stationary loop) as well as CVRPTW with
    waiting times, service times, driver duty limits, and priority lateness penalties.
    """
    n = len(tour)
    if n == 0:
        return 0.0

    is_stationary = (cost_data.ndim == 2) or (cost_data.shape[0] == 1)
    has_services = service_times is not None
    has_priorities = priority_factors is not None

    # Check if time windows are active (any customer due_time is constrained)
    has_tw = False
    if due_times is not None:
        for idx in range(len(due_times)):
            if due_times[idx] < 1e11:
                has_tw = True
                break

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # 1. Stationary Path (2D Matrix or B=1 Tensor)
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    if is_stationary:
        dist_matrix = cost_data if cost_data.ndim == 2 else cost_data[0]

        # Case A: Pure CVRP without time windows & duty limits (O(1) incremental update)
        if not has_tw and max_duty <= 0.0:
            if max_vehicles <= 0:
                V = np.full(n + 1, 1e12, dtype=np.float64)
                V[0] = 0.0

                for i in range(n):
                    if V[i] >= 1e12:
                        continue
                    load = 0.0
                    cost = 0.0

                    for j in range(i + 1, n + 1):
                        cust = tour[j - 1]
                        load += demands[cust]
                        if load > capacity:
                            break

                        if j == i + 1:
                            cost = dist_matrix[0, cust] + dist_matrix[cust, 0]
                        else:
                            prev = tour[j - 2]
                            cost = cost - dist_matrix[prev, 0] + dist_matrix[prev, cust] + dist_matrix[cust, 0]

                        cand = V[i] + cost
                        if cand < V[j]:
                            V[j] = cand

                return V[n]

            # Constrained vehicle count: 2D DP table V[j, v]
            K = max_vehicles
            V_mat = np.full((n + 1, K + 1), 1e12, dtype=np.float64)
            V_mat[0, 0] = 0.0

            for v in range(1, K + 1):
                for i in range(n):
                    if V_mat[i, v - 1] >= 1e12:
                        continue
                    load = 0.0
                    cost = 0.0

                    for j in range(i + 1, n + 1):
                        cust = tour[j - 1]
                        load += demands[cust]
                        if load > capacity:
                            break

                        if j == i + 1:
                            cost = dist_matrix[0, cust] + dist_matrix[cust, 0]
                        else:
                            prev = tour[j - 2]
                            cost = cost - dist_matrix[prev, 0] + dist_matrix[prev, cust] + dist_matrix[cust, 0]

                        cand = V_mat[i, v - 1] + cost
                        if cand < V_mat[j, v]:
                            V_mat[j, v] = cand

            min_cost = 1e12
            for v in range(1, K + 1):
                if V_mat[n, v] < min_cost:
                    min_cost = V_mat[n, v]

            if min_cost < 1e12:
                return min_cost

            unc_cost = _split_cost_numba(
                tour, cost_data, demands, capacity, max_vehicles=-1, overflow_penalty=overflow_penalty
            )
            return unc_cost + overflow_penalty

        # Case B: Stationary CVRPTW (time windows / duty limits active)
        if max_vehicles <= 0:
            V = np.full(n + 1, 1e12, dtype=np.float64)
            V[0] = 0.0

            for i in range(n):
                if V[i] >= 1e12:
                    continue
                load = 0.0
                t = t_start
                prev = 0
                route_dist = 0.0
                tw_viol = 0.0

                for j in range(i + 1, n + 1):
                    cust = tour[j - 1]
                    load += demands[cust]
                    if load > capacity:
                        break

                    travel = dist_matrix[prev, cust]
                    route_dist += travel
                    arr = t + travel

                    if has_tw and ready_times is not None and due_times is not None:
                        if arr < ready_times[cust]:
                            start_serv = ready_times[cust]
                        else:
                            start_serv = arr
                            if arr > due_times[cust]:
                                lateness = arr - due_times[cust]
                                if hard_time_windows:
                                    break
                                p_fac = priority_factors[cust] if has_priorities else 1.0
                                tw_viol += lateness * p_fac
                    else:
                        start_serv = arr

                    serv = service_times[cust] if has_services else 0.0
                    t = start_serv + serv
                    prev = cust

                    # Return to depot from cust
                    ret_travel = dist_matrix[cust, 0]
                    total_dist = route_dist + ret_travel
                    depot_arr = t + ret_travel

                    depot_late = 0.0
                    if has_tw and depot_arr > depot_due_time:
                        depot_late = depot_arr - depot_due_time
                        if hard_time_windows:
                            continue

                    route_dur = depot_arr - t_start
                    duty_viol = 0.0
                    if max_duty > 0.0 and route_dur > max_duty:
                        duty_viol = route_dur - max_duty
                        if hard_duty_limit:
                            continue

                    arc_cost = total_dist + beta * (tw_viol + depot_late) + gamma * duty_viol
                    cand = V[i] + arc_cost
                    if cand < V[j]:
                        V[j] = cand

            return V[n]

        # Stationary CVRPTW with vehicle limit
        K = max_vehicles
        V_mat = np.full((n + 1, K + 1), 1e12, dtype=np.float64)
        V_mat[0, 0] = 0.0

        for v in range(1, K + 1):
            for i in range(n):
                if V_mat[i, v - 1] >= 1e12:
                    continue
                load = 0.0
                t = t_start
                prev = 0
                route_dist = 0.0
                tw_viol = 0.0

                for j in range(i + 1, n + 1):
                    cust = tour[j - 1]
                    load += demands[cust]
                    if load > capacity:
                        break

                    travel = dist_matrix[prev, cust]
                    route_dist += travel
                    arr = t + travel

                    if has_tw and ready_times is not None and due_times is not None:
                        if arr < ready_times[cust]:
                            start_serv = ready_times[cust]
                        else:
                            start_serv = arr
                            if arr > due_times[cust]:
                                lateness = arr - due_times[cust]
                                if hard_time_windows:
                                    break
                                p_fac = priority_factors[cust] if has_priorities else 1.0
                                tw_viol += lateness * p_fac
                    else:
                        start_serv = arr

                    serv = service_times[cust] if has_services else 0.0
                    t = start_serv + serv
                    prev = cust

                    ret_travel = dist_matrix[cust, 0]
                    total_dist = route_dist + ret_travel
                    depot_arr = t + ret_travel

                    depot_late = 0.0
                    if has_tw and depot_arr > depot_due_time:
                        depot_late = depot_arr - depot_due_time
                        if hard_time_windows:
                            continue

                    route_dur = depot_arr - t_start
                    duty_viol = 0.0
                    if max_duty > 0.0 and route_dur > max_duty:
                        duty_viol = route_dur - max_duty
                        if hard_duty_limit:
                            continue

                    arc_cost = total_dist + beta * (tw_viol + depot_late) + gamma * duty_viol
                    cand = V_mat[i, v - 1] + arc_cost
                    if cand < V_mat[j, v]:
                        V_mat[j, v] = cand

        min_cost = 1e12
        for v in range(1, K + 1):
            if V_mat[n, v] < min_cost:
                min_cost = V_mat[n, v]

        if min_cost < 1e12:
            return min_cost

        unc_cost = _split_cost_numba(
            tour, cost_data, demands, capacity, max_vehicles=-1,
            overflow_penalty=overflow_penalty, t_start=t_start,
            bucket_size=bucket_size, service_times=service_times,
            ready_times=ready_times, due_times=due_times,
            depot_due_time=depot_due_time, priority_factors=priority_factors,
            max_duty=max_duty, alpha=alpha, beta=beta, gamma=gamma,
            hard_time_windows=hard_time_windows, hard_duty_limit=hard_duty_limit,
        )
        return unc_cost + overflow_penalty

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # 2. Time-Dependent Tensor Path (B > 1, e.g. Traffic / Valhalla)
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    B = cost_data.shape[0]

    if max_vehicles <= 0:
        V = np.full(n + 1, 1e12, dtype=np.float64)
        V[0] = 0.0

        for i in range(n):
            if V[i] >= 1e12:
                continue
            load = 0.0
            t = t_start
            prev = 0
            tw_viol = 0.0

            for j in range(i + 1, n + 1):
                cust = tour[j - 1]
                load += demands[cust]
                if load > capacity:
                    break

                # Hop from prev to cust
                b = int((t - t_start) / bucket_size)
                if b < 0:
                    b = 0
                elif b >= B:
                    b = B - 1

                travel = cost_data[b, prev, cust]
                arr = t + travel

                if has_tw and ready_times is not None and due_times is not None:
                    if arr < ready_times[cust]:
                        start_serv = ready_times[cust]
                    else:
                        start_serv = arr
                        if arr > due_times[cust]:
                            lateness = arr - due_times[cust]
                            if hard_time_windows:
                                break
                            p_fac = priority_factors[cust] if has_priorities else 1.0
                            tw_viol += lateness * p_fac
                else:
                    start_serv = arr

                serv = service_times[cust] if has_services else 0.0
                t = start_serv + serv
                prev = cust

                # Return to depot from cust
                b_dep = int((t - t_start) / bucket_size)
                if b_dep < 0:
                    b_dep = 0
                elif b_dep >= B:
                    b_dep = B - 1

                depot_arr = t + cost_data[b_dep, cust, 0]
                route_dur = depot_arr - t_start

                depot_late = 0.0
                if has_tw and depot_arr > depot_due_time:
                    depot_late = depot_arr - depot_due_time
                    if hard_time_windows:
                        continue

                duty_viol = 0.0
                if max_duty > 0.0 and route_dur > max_duty:
                    duty_viol = route_dur - max_duty
                    if hard_duty_limit:
                        continue

                arc_cost = route_dur + beta * (tw_viol + depot_late) + gamma * duty_viol
                cand = V[i] + arc_cost
                if cand < V[j]:
                    V[j] = cand

        return V[n]

    # Time-dependent constrained vehicle count with B > 1
    K = max_vehicles
    V_mat = np.full((n + 1, K + 1), 1e12, dtype=np.float64)
    V_mat[0, 0] = 0.0

    for v in range(1, K + 1):
        for i in range(n):
            if V_mat[i, v - 1] >= 1e12:
                continue
            load = 0.0
            t = t_start
            prev = 0
            tw_viol = 0.0

            for j in range(i + 1, n + 1):
                cust = tour[j - 1]
                load += demands[cust]
                if load > capacity:
                    break

                b = int((t - t_start) / bucket_size)
                if b < 0:
                    b = 0
                elif b >= B:
                    b = B - 1

                travel = cost_data[b, prev, cust]
                arr = t + travel

                if has_tw and ready_times is not None and due_times is not None:
                    if arr < ready_times[cust]:
                        start_serv = ready_times[cust]
                    else:
                        start_serv = arr
                        if arr > due_times[cust]:
                            lateness = arr - due_times[cust]
                            if hard_time_windows:
                                break
                            p_fac = priority_factors[cust] if has_priorities else 1.0
                            tw_viol += lateness * p_fac
                else:
                    start_serv = arr

                serv = service_times[cust] if has_services else 0.0
                t = start_serv + serv
                prev = cust

                b_dep = int((t - t_start) / bucket_size)
                if b_dep < 0:
                    b_dep = 0
                elif b_dep >= B:
                    b_dep = B - 1

                depot_arr = t + cost_data[b_dep, cust, 0]
                route_dur = depot_arr - t_start

                depot_late = 0.0
                if has_tw and depot_arr > depot_due_time:
                    depot_late = depot_arr - depot_due_time
                    if hard_time_windows:
                        continue

                duty_viol = 0.0
                if max_duty > 0.0 and route_dur > max_duty:
                    duty_viol = route_dur - max_duty
                    if hard_duty_limit:
                        continue

                arc_cost = route_dur + beta * (tw_viol + depot_late) + gamma * duty_viol
                cand = V_mat[i, v - 1] + arc_cost
                if cand < V_mat[j, v]:
                    V_mat[j, v] = cand

    min_cost = 1e12
    for v in range(1, K + 1):
        if V_mat[n, v] < min_cost:
            min_cost = V_mat[n, v]

    if min_cost < 1e12:
        return min_cost

    unc_cost = _split_cost_numba(
        tour, cost_data, demands, capacity, max_vehicles=-1,
        overflow_penalty=overflow_penalty, t_start=t_start,
        bucket_size=bucket_size, service_times=service_times,
        ready_times=ready_times, due_times=due_times,
        depot_due_time=depot_due_time, priority_factors=priority_factors,
        max_duty=max_duty, alpha=alpha, beta=beta, gamma=gamma,
        hard_time_windows=hard_time_windows, hard_duty_limit=hard_duty_limit,
    )
    return unc_cost + overflow_penalty


@njit
def _split_dag_with_pred(
    tour: np.ndarray,
    cost_data: np.ndarray,
    demands: np.ndarray,
    capacity: float,
    max_vehicles: int = -1,
    t_start: float = 0.0,
    bucket_size: float = 900.0,
    service_times: np.ndarray | None = None,
    ready_times: np.ndarray | None = None,
    due_times: np.ndarray | None = None,
    depot_due_time: float = 1e12,
    priority_factors: np.ndarray | None = None,
    max_duty: float = -1.0,
    alpha: float = 100.0,
    beta: float = 100.0,
    gamma: float = 100.0,
    hard_time_windows: bool = False,
    hard_duty_limit: bool = False,
) -> tuple[float, np.ndarray, int]:
    """Execute Prins Split returning shortest path cost, predecessor array, and best vehicle count."""
    n = len(tour)
    if n == 0:
        return 0.0, np.full(1, -1, dtype=np.int64), 0

    is_stationary = (cost_data.ndim == 2) or (cost_data.shape[0] == 1)
    has_services = service_times is not None
    has_priorities = priority_factors is not None

    has_tw = False
    if due_times is not None:
        for idx in range(len(due_times)):
            if due_times[idx] < 1e11:
                has_tw = True
                break

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # 1. Stationary Path
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    if is_stationary:
        dist_matrix = cost_data if cost_data.ndim == 2 else cost_data[0]

        # Case A: Pure CVRP without time windows & duty limits
        if not has_tw and max_duty <= 0.0:
            if max_vehicles <= 0:
                V = np.full(n + 1, 1e12, dtype=np.float64)
                pred = np.full(n + 1, -1, dtype=np.int64)
                V[0] = 0.0

                for i in range(n):
                    if V[i] >= 1e12:
                        continue
                    load = 0.0
                    cost = 0.0

                    for j in range(i + 1, n + 1):
                        cust = tour[j - 1]
                        load += demands[cust]
                        if load > capacity:
                            break

                        if j == i + 1:
                            cost = dist_matrix[0, cust] + dist_matrix[cust, 0]
                        else:
                            prev = tour[j - 2]
                            cost = cost - dist_matrix[prev, 0] + dist_matrix[prev, cust] + dist_matrix[cust, 0]

                        cand = V[i] + cost
                        if cand < V[j]:
                            V[j] = cand
                            pred[j] = i

                return V[n], pred, 0

            # Constrained vehicle count
            K = max_vehicles
            V_mat = np.full((n + 1, K + 1), 1e12, dtype=np.float64)
            pred_mat = np.full((n + 1, K + 1), -1, dtype=np.int64)
            V_mat[0, 0] = 0.0

            for v in range(1, K + 1):
                for i in range(n):
                    if V_mat[i, v - 1] >= 1e12:
                        continue
                    load = 0.0
                    cost = 0.0

                    for j in range(i + 1, n + 1):
                        cust = tour[j - 1]
                        load += demands[cust]
                        if load > capacity:
                            break

                        if j == i + 1:
                            cost = dist_matrix[0, cust] + dist_matrix[cust, 0]
                        else:
                            prev = tour[j - 2]
                            cost = cost - dist_matrix[prev, 0] + dist_matrix[prev, cust] + dist_matrix[cust, 0]

                        cand = V_mat[i, v - 1] + cost
                        if cand < V_mat[j, v]:
                            V_mat[j, v] = cand
                            pred_mat[j, v] = i

            best_v = 1
            best_cost = 1e12
            for v in range(1, K + 1):
                if V_mat[n, v] < best_cost:
                    best_cost = V_mat[n, v]
                    best_v = v

            if best_cost < 1e12:
                pred_1d = np.full(n + 1, -1, dtype=np.int64)
                curr_node = n
                curr_layer = best_v
                while curr_node > 0 and curr_layer > 0:
                    p = int(pred_mat[curr_node, curr_layer])
                    pred_1d[curr_node] = p
                    curr_node = p
                    curr_layer -= 1
                return best_cost, pred_1d, best_v

            cost_unc, pred_unc, _ = _split_dag_with_pred(
