                tour, cost_data, demands, capacity, max_vehicles=-1
            )
            return cost_unc, pred_unc, -1

        # Case B: Stationary CVRPTW
        if max_vehicles <= 0:
            V = np.full(n + 1, 1e12, dtype=np.float64)
            pred = np.full(n + 1, -1, dtype=np.int64)
            V[0] = 0.0

            for i in range(n):
                if V[i] >= 1e12:
                    continue
                load = 0.0
                t = t_start
                prev = 0
                route_dist = 0.0
                tw_viol = 0.0

                for j in range(i + 1, n + 1):
                    cust = tour[j - 1]
                    load += demands[cust]
                    if load > capacity:
                        break

                    travel = dist_matrix[prev, cust]
                    route_dist += travel
                    arr = t + travel

                    if has_tw and ready_times is not None and due_times is not None:
                        if arr < ready_times[cust]:
                            start_serv = ready_times[cust]
                        else:
                            start_serv = arr
                            if arr > due_times[cust]:
                                lateness = arr - due_times[cust]
                                if hard_time_windows:
                                    break
                                p_fac = priority_factors[cust] if has_priorities else 1.0
                                tw_viol += lateness * p_fac
                    else:
                        start_serv = arr

                    serv = service_times[cust] if has_services else 0.0
                    t = start_serv + serv
                    prev = cust

                    ret_travel = dist_matrix[cust, 0]
                    total_dist = route_dist + ret_travel
                    depot_arr = t + ret_travel

                    depot_late = 0.0
                    if has_tw and depot_arr > depot_due_time:
                        depot_late = depot_arr - depot_due_time
                        if hard_time_windows:
                            continue

                    route_dur = depot_arr - t_start
                    duty_viol = 0.0
                    if max_duty > 0.0 and route_dur > max_duty:
                        duty_viol = route_dur - max_duty
                        if hard_duty_limit:
                            continue

                    arc_cost = total_dist + beta * (tw_viol + depot_late) + gamma * duty_viol
                    cand = V[i] + arc_cost
                    if cand < V[j]:
                        V[j] = cand
                        pred[j] = i

            return V[n], pred, 0

        # Stationary CVRPTW with vehicle limit
        K = max_vehicles
        V_mat = np.full((n + 1, K + 1), 1e12, dtype=np.float64)
        pred_mat = np.full((n + 1, K + 1), -1, dtype=np.int64)
        V_mat[0, 0] = 0.0

        for v in range(1, K + 1):
            for i in range(n):
                if V_mat[i, v - 1] >= 1e12:
                    continue
                load = 0.0
                t = t_start
                prev = 0
                route_dist = 0.0
                tw_viol = 0.0

                for j in range(i + 1, n + 1):
                    cust = tour[j - 1]
                    load += demands[cust]
                    if load > capacity:
                        break

                    travel = dist_matrix[prev, cust]
                    route_dist += travel
                    arr = t + travel

                    if has_tw and ready_times is not None and due_times is not None:
                        if arr < ready_times[cust]:
                            start_serv = ready_times[cust]
                        else:
                            start_serv = arr
                            if arr > due_times[cust]:
                                lateness = arr - due_times[cust]
                                if hard_time_windows:
                                    break
                                p_fac = priority_factors[cust] if has_priorities else 1.0
                                tw_viol += lateness * p_fac
                    else:
                        start_serv = arr

                    serv = service_times[cust] if has_services else 0.0
                    t = start_serv + serv
                    prev = cust

                    ret_travel = dist_matrix[cust, 0]
                    total_dist = route_dist + ret_travel
                    depot_arr = t + ret_travel

                    depot_late = 0.0
                    if has_tw and depot_arr > depot_due_time:
                        depot_late = depot_arr - depot_due_time
                        if hard_time_windows:
                            continue

                    route_dur = depot_arr - t_start
                    duty_viol = 0.0
                    if max_duty > 0.0 and route_dur > max_duty:
                        duty_viol = route_dur - max_duty
                        if hard_duty_limit:
                            continue

                    arc_cost = total_dist + beta * (tw_viol + depot_late) + gamma * duty_viol
                    cand = V_mat[i, v - 1] + arc_cost
                    if cand < V_mat[j, v]:
                        V_mat[j, v] = cand
                        pred_mat[j, v] = i

        best_v = 1
        best_cost = 1e12
        for v in range(1, K + 1):
            if V_mat[n, v] < best_cost:
                best_cost = V_mat[n, v]
                best_v = v

        if best_cost < 1e12:
            pred_1d = np.full(n + 1, -1, dtype=np.int64)
            curr_node = n
            curr_layer = best_v
            while curr_node > 0 and curr_layer > 0:
                p = int(pred_mat[curr_node, curr_layer])
                pred_1d[curr_node] = p
                curr_node = p
                curr_layer -= 1
            return best_cost, pred_1d, best_v

        cost_unc, pred_unc, _ = _split_dag_with_pred(
            tour, cost_data, demands, capacity, max_vehicles=-1,
            t_start=t_start, bucket_size=bucket_size, service_times=service_times,
            ready_times=ready_times, due_times=due_times,
            depot_due_time=depot_due_time, priority_factors=priority_factors,
            max_duty=max_duty, alpha=alpha, beta=beta, gamma=gamma,
            hard_time_windows=hard_time_windows, hard_duty_limit=hard_duty_limit,
        )
        return cost_unc, pred_unc, -1

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # 2. Time-Dependent Tensor Path
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    B = cost_data.shape[0]

    if max_vehicles <= 0:
        V = np.full(n + 1, 1e12, dtype=np.float64)
        pred = np.full(n + 1, -1, dtype=np.int64)
        V[0] = 0.0

        for i in range(n):
            if V[i] >= 1e12:
                continue
            load = 0.0
            t = t_start
            prev = 0
            tw_viol = 0.0

            for j in range(i + 1, n + 1):
                cust = tour[j - 1]
                load += demands[cust]
                if load > capacity:
                    break

                b = int((t - t_start) / bucket_size)
                if b < 0:
                    b = 0
                elif b >= B:
                    b = B - 1

                travel = cost_data[b, prev, cust]
                arr = t + travel

                if has_tw and ready_times is not None and due_times is not None:
                    if arr < ready_times[cust]:
                        start_serv = ready_times[cust]
                    else:
                        start_serv = arr
                        if arr > due_times[cust]:
                            lateness = arr - due_times[cust]
                            if hard_time_windows:
                                break
                            p_fac = priority_factors[cust] if has_priorities else 1.0
                            tw_viol += lateness * p_fac
                else:
                    start_serv = arr

                serv = service_times[cust] if has_services else 0.0
                t = start_serv + serv
                prev = cust

                b_dep = int((t - t_start) / bucket_size)
                if b_dep < 0:
                    b_dep = 0
                elif b_dep >= B:
                    b_dep = B - 1

                depot_arr = t + cost_data[b_dep, cust, 0]
                route_dur = depot_arr - t_start

                depot_late = 0.0
                if has_tw and depot_arr > depot_due_time:
                    depot_late = depot_arr - depot_due_time
                    if hard_time_windows:
                        continue

                duty_viol = 0.0
                if max_duty > 0.0 and route_dur > max_duty:
                    duty_viol = route_dur - max_duty
                    if hard_duty_limit:
                        continue

                arc_cost = route_dur + beta * (tw_viol + depot_late) + gamma * duty_viol
                cand = V[i] + arc_cost
                if cand < V[j]:
                    V[j] = cand
                    pred[j] = i

        return V[n], pred, 0

    # Constrained vehicle count with B > 1
    K = max_vehicles
    V_mat = np.full((n + 1, K + 1), 1e12, dtype=np.float64)
    pred_mat = np.full((n + 1, K + 1), -1, dtype=np.int64)
    V_mat[0, 0] = 0.0

    for v in range(1, K + 1):
        for i in range(n):
            if V_mat[i, v - 1] >= 1e12:
                continue
            load = 0.0
            t = t_start
            prev = 0
            tw_viol = 0.0

            for j in range(i + 1, n + 1):
                cust = tour[j - 1]
                load += demands[cust]
                if load > capacity:
                    break

                b = int((t - t_start) / bucket_size)
                if b < 0:
                    b = 0
                elif b >= B:
                    b = B - 1

                travel = cost_data[b, prev, cust]
                arr = t + travel

                if has_tw and ready_times is not None and due_times is not None:
                    if arr < ready_times[cust]:
                        start_serv = ready_times[cust]
                    else:
                        start_serv = arr
                        if arr > due_times[cust]:
                            lateness = arr - due_times[cust]
                            if hard_time_windows:
                                break
                            p_fac = priority_factors[cust] if has_priorities else 1.0
                            tw_viol += lateness * p_fac
                else:
                    start_serv = arr

                serv = service_times[cust] if has_services else 0.0
                t = start_serv + serv
                prev = cust

                b_dep = int((t - t_start) / bucket_size)
                if b_dep < 0:
                    b_dep = 0
                elif b_dep >= B:
                    b_dep = B - 1

                depot_arr = t + cost_data[b_dep, cust, 0]
                route_dur = depot_arr - t_start

                depot_late = 0.0
                if has_tw and depot_arr > depot_due_time:
                    depot_late = depot_arr - depot_due_time
                    if hard_time_windows:
                        continue

                duty_viol = 0.0
                if max_duty > 0.0 and route_dur > max_duty:
                    duty_viol = route_dur - max_duty
                    if hard_duty_limit:
                        continue

                arc_cost = route_dur + beta * (tw_viol + depot_late) + gamma * duty_viol
                cand = V_mat[i, v - 1] + arc_cost
                if cand < V_mat[j, v]:
                    V_mat[j, v] = cand
                    pred_mat[j, v] = i

    best_v = 1
    best_cost = 1e12
    for v in range(1, K + 1):
        if V_mat[n, v] < best_cost:
            best_cost = V_mat[n, v]
            best_v = v

    if best_cost < 1e12:
        pred_1d = np.full(n + 1, -1, dtype=np.int64)
        curr_node = n
        curr_layer = best_v
        while curr_node > 0 and curr_layer > 0:
            p = int(pred_mat[curr_node, curr_layer])
            pred_1d[curr_node] = p
            curr_node = p
            curr_layer -= 1
        return best_cost, pred_1d, best_v

    cost_unc, pred_unc, _ = _split_dag_with_pred(
        tour, cost_data, demands, capacity, max_vehicles=-1,
        t_start=t_start, bucket_size=bucket_size, service_times=service_times,
        ready_times=ready_times, due_times=due_times,
        depot_due_time=depot_due_time, priority_factors=priority_factors,
        max_duty=max_duty, alpha=alpha, beta=beta, gamma=gamma,
        hard_time_windows=hard_time_windows, hard_duty_limit=hard_duty_limit,
    )
    return cost_unc, pred_unc, -1


def prins_split(
    problem: Problem,
    tour: Sequence[int] | np.ndarray,
    max_vehicles: int | None = None,
    allow_extra_vehicles: bool = False,
    cost_oracle: Any | None = None,
    cost_tensor: CostTensor | None = None,
    max_duty_duration: float | None = None,
    hard_time_windows: bool = False,
    hard_duty_limit: bool = False,
    enforce_priority_early_service: bool = False,
    priority_early_ratio: float = 0.5,
    alpha: float = 100.0,
    beta: float = 100.0,
    gamma: float = 100.0,
) -> tuple[float, list[Route], bool, dict[str, list[str]]]:
    """Partition a giant tour into feasible vehicle routes adhering to capacity, time windows, and duty limits.

    Args:
        problem: Problem instance with depot, customers, and precomputed matrices.
        tour: Sequence of 1-based customer indices (e.g. 1 to n).
        max_vehicles: Optional limit on the number of vehicle routes.
        cost_oracle: Optional CostOracle for road network or traffic evaluation.
        cost_tensor: Optional precomputed CostTensor. If not provided, computed automatically.
        max_duty_duration: Maximum allowed driver duty duration per route.
        hard_time_windows: If True, time-window violations are strictly disallowed in Split.
        hard_duty_limit: If True, duty-limit violations are strictly disallowed in Split.
        enforce_priority_early_service: Tighten time window for priority customers.
        priority_early_ratio: Fraction of window for priority early service.
        alpha: Penalty weight for capacity violations.
        beta: Penalty weight for time window violations.
        gamma: Penalty weight for duty limit violations.

    Returns:
        (total_cost, routes, is_feasible, violations_dict)
    """
    tour_arr = np.asarray(tour, dtype=np.int64)
    n = len(tour_arr)
    if n == 0:
        return 0.0, [], True, {}

    if cost_tensor is None:
        cost_tensor = precompute_cost_tensor(problem, cost_oracle=cost_oracle)

    cost_data = cost_tensor.travel_time_tensor
    dist_mat = cost_tensor.distance_matrix

    num_nodes = problem.num_customers + 1
    demands = np.zeros(num_nodes, dtype=np.float64)
    services = np.zeros(num_nodes, dtype=np.float64)
    ready_times = np.zeros(num_nodes, dtype=np.float64)
    due_times = np.full(num_nodes, 1e12, dtype=np.float64)
    priority_factors = np.ones(num_nodes, dtype=np.float64)

    services[0] = problem.depot.service_time
    ready_times[0] = problem.depot.ready_time
    due_times[0] = problem.depot.due_time

    for idx, c in enumerate(problem.customers, start=1):
        demands[idx] = c.demand
        services[idx] = c.service_time
        ready_times[idx] = c.ready_time
        due_times[idx] = c.due_time
        priority_factors[idx] = 1.0 + (c.priority - 1) * 2.0

        if enforce_priority_early_service and c.priority > 1:
            early_due = c.ready_time + priority_early_ratio * (c.due_time - c.ready_time)
            due_times[idx] = early_due

    capacity = problem.vehicles[0].capacity if problem.vehicles else 100.0
    if max_vehicles is not None:
        limit_v = max_vehicles
    elif problem.vehicles and not allow_extra_vehicles:
        limit_v = len(problem.vehicles)
    else:
        limit_v = -1

    max_duty = -1.0
    if max_duty_duration is not None:
        max_duty = max_duty_duration
    elif problem.vehicles:
        v0 = problem.vehicles[0]
        if math.isfinite(v0.shift_end) and math.isfinite(v0.shift_start):
            diff = v0.shift_end - v0.shift_start
            if diff > 0:
                max_duty = diff

    cost, pred, best_v = _split_dag_with_pred(
        tour_arr,
        cost_data,
        demands,
        capacity,
        limit_v,
        t_start=cost_tensor.t_start,
        bucket_size=cost_tensor.bucket_size,
        service_times=services,
        ready_times=ready_times,
        due_times=due_times,
        depot_due_time=problem.depot.due_time,
        priority_factors=priority_factors,
        max_duty=max_duty,
        alpha=alpha,
        beta=beta,
        gamma=gamma,
        hard_time_windows=hard_time_windows,
        hard_duty_limit=hard_duty_limit,
    )

    # Backtrack route segments from pred array
    segments: list[list[int]] = []
    curr = n
    while curr > 0:
        p = int(pred[curr])
        if p < 0 or p >= curr:
            break
        seg = [int(tour_arr[k]) for k in range(p, curr)]
        segments.append(seg)
        curr = p

    segments.reverse()

    routes: list[Route] = []
    violations: dict[str, list[str]] = {}
    is_feasible = True

    if curr > 0:
        # Hard constraints pruned all valid DAG paths from 0 to n!
        # Fall back to soft/unconstrained split to reconstruct attempted routes and flag violations
        is_feasible = False
        violations.setdefault("feasibility", []).append(
            "Hard constraints violated; no feasible DAG path exists from depot"
        )
        cost_unc, pred_unc, _ = _split_dag_with_pred(
            tour_arr,
            cost_data,
            demands,
            capacity,
            -1,
            t_start=cost_tensor.t_start,
            bucket_size=cost_tensor.bucket_size,
            service_times=services,
            ready_times=ready_times,
            due_times=due_times,
            depot_due_time=problem.depot.due_time,
            priority_factors=priority_factors,
            max_duty=max_duty,
            alpha=alpha,
            beta=beta,
            gamma=gamma,
            hard_time_windows=False,
            hard_duty_limit=False,
        )
        curr = n
        segments = []
        while curr > 0:
            p = int(pred_unc[curr])
            if p < 0 or p >= curr:
                break
            segments.append([int(tour_arr[k]) for k in range(p, curr)])
            curr = p
        segments.reverse()

    eff_max_v = max_vehicles if max_vehicles is not None else (len(problem.vehicles) if (problem.vehicles and not allow_extra_vehicles) else None)
    if eff_max_v is not None and len(segments) > eff_max_v:
        is_feasible = False
        violations.setdefault("fleet_size", []).append(
            f"Required {len(segments)} routes, exceeding available fleet size of {eff_max_v}"
        )
        violations.setdefault("max_vehicles", []).append(
            f"Required {len(segments)} routes, exceeding max limit of {eff_max_v}"
        )

    # Build Route objects with time-dependent simulation
    vehicle_pool = problem.vehicles
    from routing.cost_oracle import Location

    stops_loc = [
        Location(id=problem.depot.id, lat=problem.depot.lat, lon=problem.depot.lon)
    ] + [Location(id=c.id, lat=c.lat, lon=c.lon) for c in problem.customers]

    total_dist = 0.0

    for r_idx, seg in enumerate(segments):
        if r_idx < len(vehicle_pool):
            veh_id = vehicle_pool[r_idx].id
        else:
            veh_id = f"V_{r_idx + 1}"
            if not allow_extra_vehicles:
                is_feasible = False
                violations.setdefault("fleet_size", []).append(
                    f"Route {r_idx + 1} assigned synthetic vehicle '{veh_id}' exceeding fleet size {len(vehicle_pool)}"
                )
        stop_ids: list[str] = []
        arrival_times: list[float] = []
        route_load = 0.0
        route_dist = 0.0

        current_time = problem.depot.ready_time
        prev_node = 0

        for cust_idx in seg:
            cust = problem.customers[cust_idx - 1]
            stop_ids.append(cust.id)
            route_load += cust.demand

            leg_dist = dist_mat[prev_node, cust_idx]
            if cost_oracle is not None:
                leg_time = cost_oracle.travel_time(
                    stops_loc[prev_node], stops_loc[cust_idx], depart_time=current_time
                )
            else:
                b = int((current_time - cost_tensor.t_start) / cost_tensor.bucket_size)
                b = max(0, min(cost_tensor.num_buckets - 1, b))
                leg_time = cost_data[b, prev_node, cust_idx]

            route_dist += leg_dist
            arrival = current_time + leg_time
            arrival_times.append(arrival)

            # Time window verification
            effective_due = cust.due_time
            if enforce_priority_early_service and cust.priority > 1:
                effective_due = cust.ready_time + priority_early_ratio * (cust.due_time - cust.ready_time)

            if arrival > effective_due:
                is_feasible = False
                violations.setdefault("time_window", []).append(
                    f"Stop {cust.id} reached at {arrival:.2f} > due time {effective_due:.2f}"
                )

            start_service = max(arrival, cust.ready_time)
            current_time = start_service + cust.service_time
            prev_node = cust_idx

        # Return to depot
        leg_dist = dist_mat[prev_node, 0]
        if cost_oracle is not None:
            leg_time = cost_oracle.travel_time(
                stops_loc[prev_node], stops_loc[0], depart_time=current_time
            )
        else:
            b = int((current_time - cost_tensor.t_start) / cost_tensor.bucket_size)
            b = max(0, min(cost_tensor.num_buckets - 1, b))
            leg_time = cost_data[b, prev_node, 0]

        route_dist += leg_dist
        final_depot_arrival = current_time + leg_time

        if final_depot_arrival > problem.depot.due_time:
            is_feasible = False
            violations.setdefault("depot_due_time", []).append(
                f"Vehicle {veh_id} returned at {final_depot_arrival:.2f} > {problem.depot.due_time:.2f}"
            )

        if route_load > capacity:
            is_feasible = False
            violations.setdefault("capacity", []).append(
                f"Vehicle {veh_id} load {route_load:.1f} > capacity {capacity:.1f}"
            )

        duration = final_depot_arrival - problem.depot.ready_time
        if max_duty > 0.0 and duration > max_duty:
            if hard_duty_limit:
                is_feasible = False
            violations.setdefault("driver_duty_limit", []).append(
                f"Vehicle {veh_id} duration {duration:.2f}s > max duty {max_duty:.2f}s"
            )

        total_dist += route_dist
        routes.append(
            Route(
                vehicle_id=veh_id,
                stop_ids=stop_ids,
                arrival_times=arrival_times,
                load=route_load,
                distance=route_dist,
                duration=duration,
            )
        )

    # For feasible solution: true distance. For infeasible: penalized cost
    solution_cost = total_dist if is_feasible else max(cost, total_dist + 10000.0)
    return solution_cost, routes, is_feasible, violations


=== FILE: solver/tensor.py ===
"""Time-bucketed travel-time tensor precomputation for Route.it CVRPTW solver.

Bridges the high-level CostOracle (ValhallaOracle, EuclideanOracle, TrafficAdapter)
to high-performance Numba-accelerated split routines by precomputing a 3D
travel-time tensor across discrete time periods.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass
import math
from typing import TYPE_CHECKING
import numpy as np

if TYPE_CHECKING:
    from routing.cost_oracle import CostOracle
    from solver.models import Problem


@dataclass
class CostTensor:
    """Precomputed time-bucketed travel-time tensor and distance matrix for a Problem."""

    travel_time_tensor: np.ndarray  # shape (B, N+1, N+1) float64
    distance_matrix: np.ndarray      # shape (N+1, N+1) float64
    t_start: float = 0.0
    bucket_size: float = 900.0
    num_buckets: int = 1

    @property
    def is_time_dependent(self) -> bool:
        """Whether tensor has multiple time buckets."""
        return self.num_buckets > 1


def precompute_cost_tensor(
    problem: Problem,
    cost_oracle: CostOracle | None = None,
    bucket_size_seconds: float = 900.0,
    num_buckets: int | None = None,
) -> CostTensor:
    """Precompute travel-time tensor and distance matrix for all nodes in a Problem.

    Node 0 is the depot, nodes 1..N are the customers.

    Args:
        problem: CVRPTW Problem instance.
        cost_oracle: Optional CostOracle (e.g. ValhallaOracle, EuclideanOracle).
                     If None, builds from problem.distance_matrix or Euclidean coordinates.
        bucket_size_seconds: Time interval per bucket in seconds (default: 15 min = 900s).
        num_buckets: Optional explicit number of buckets B.

    Returns:
        CostTensor with 3D travel_time_tensor and 2D distance_matrix.
    """
    from routing.cost_oracle import EuclideanOracle, Location

    # Construct ordered stop list: 0 is depot, 1..N are customers
    depot_loc = Location(id=problem.depot.id, lat=problem.depot.lat, lon=problem.depot.lon)
    cust_locs = [Location(id=c.id, lat=c.lat, lon=c.lon) for c in problem.customers]
    all_stops = [depot_loc] + cust_locs
    s = len(all_stops)

    # Fast path 1: No cost_oracle given, but problem already has precomputed Euclidean matrices
    if cost_oracle is None:
        if problem.distance_matrix is not None:
            dist_mat = np.asarray(problem.distance_matrix, dtype=np.float64)
            time_mat = (
                np.asarray(problem.travel_time_matrix, dtype=np.float64)
                if problem.travel_time_matrix is not None
                else dist_mat
            )
            tensor = time_mat.reshape((1, s, s))
            return CostTensor(
                travel_time_tensor=tensor,
                distance_matrix=dist_mat,
                t_start=problem.depot.ready_time,
                bucket_size=bucket_size_seconds,
                num_buckets=1,
            )

        # Build default oracle respecting problem.coordinate_system
        cost_oracle = EuclideanOracle(
            coordinate_system=problem.coordinate_system,
            base_speed_mps=8.333 if problem.coordinate_system == "geographic" else 1.0,
            planar_scale=1.0,
        )

    # Fast path 2: CostOracle is stationary (no dynamic traffic adapter)
    if cost_oracle.traffic_adapter is None:
        res = cost_oracle.matrix(all_stops, depart_time=problem.depot.ready_time)
        durs = np.asarray(res.durations, dtype=np.float64)
        dists = np.asarray(res.distances, dtype=np.float64)
        tensor = durs.reshape((1, s, s))

        # Synchronize problem distance matrix if absent
        if problem.distance_matrix is None:
            problem.distance_matrix = dists.tolist()
            problem.travel_time_matrix = durs.tolist()

        return CostTensor(
            travel_time_tensor=tensor,
            distance_matrix=dists,
            t_start=problem.depot.ready_time,
            bucket_size=bucket_size_seconds,
            num_buckets=1,
        )

    # Path 3: Time-dependent CostOracle (with TrafficAdapter)
    t_min = float(problem.depot.ready_time)
    t_max = (
        float(problem.depot.due_time)
        if math.isfinite(problem.depot.due_time) and problem.depot.due_time > t_min
        else t_min + 86400.0  # 24h default horizon
    )

    if num_buckets is not None:
        b_count = max(1, num_buckets)
    else:
        b_count = max(1, min(96, int(math.ceil((t_max - t_min) / bucket_size_seconds))))

    tensor = np.zeros((b_count, s, s), dtype=np.float64)
    dists = np.zeros((s, s), dtype=np.float64)

    for b in range(b_count):
        t_b = t_min + (b + 0.5) * bucket_size_seconds
        res = cost_oracle.matrix(all_stops, depart_time=t_b)
        tensor[b, :, :] = np.asarray(res.durations, dtype=np.float64)
        if b == 0:
            dists[:, :] = np.asarray(res.distances, dtype=np.float64)

    if problem.distance_matrix is None:
        problem.distance_matrix = dists.tolist()
        problem.travel_time_matrix = tensor[0].tolist()

    return CostTensor(
        travel_time_tensor=tensor,
        distance_matrix=dists,
        t_start=t_min,
        bucket_size=bucket_size_seconds,
        num_buckets=b_count,
    )


=== FILE: solver/validate.py ===
"""Independent solution validator for Route.it CVRPTW solver.

Performs a clean-room verification of all constraints and recomputes exact route costs:
1. Customer coverage (every customer visited exactly once, no missing, no duplicates).
2. Vehicle capacity limits (route total demand <= vehicle capacity).
3. Stop time windows (arrival time <= customer due time, accounting for travel, wait, and service).
4. Depot time windows (vehicle returns before depot due time).
5. Driver duty limits (total shift duration <= maximum shift limit).
6. Fleet size limit (number of used routes <= available vehicles).
7. Independent cost recomputation (distance and duration computed from scratch).
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import math
from typing import Any, Sequence
from pydantic import BaseModel, Field

from solver.models import Customer, Depot, Problem, Route, Solution


class ValidationResult(BaseModel):
    """Structured report of independent solution validation."""

    is_valid: bool = Field(..., description="True if and only if ALL constraints are strictly satisfied")
    violations: dict[str, list[str]] = Field(
        default_factory=dict,
        description="Detailed violation messages grouped by category",
    )
    recomputed_cost: float = Field(..., description="Recomputed total routing cost (distance)")
    recomputed_distance: float = Field(..., description="Total cumulative distance across all routes")
    recomputed_duration: float = Field(..., description="Total operational duration across all routes")
    total_waiting_time: float = Field(0.0, description="Cumulative waiting time before time window openings")
    total_service_time: float = Field(0.0, description="Cumulative service time across all customer visits")
    max_route_duration: float = Field(0.0, description="Longest duration among all routes")
    num_routes_used: int = Field(..., description="Number of routes in the solution")
    customer_count: int = Field(..., description="Total customers serviced")


def _get_travel_cost(
    p1: tuple[float, float],
    p2: tuple[float, float],
    matrix: list[list[float]] | None,
    idx1: int,
    idx2: int,
    cost_oracle: Any | None,
    loc1: Any | None,
    loc2: Any | None,
    depart_time: float,
) -> tuple[float, float]:
    """Helper to obtain (travel_time, distance) between two stops."""
    if cost_oracle is not None and loc1 is not None and loc2 is not None:
        tt = float(cost_oracle.travel_time(loc1, loc2, depart_time=depart_time))
        dist = float(cost_oracle.distance(loc1, loc2))
        return tt, dist

    if matrix is not None:
        tt = float(matrix[idx1][idx2])
        dist = float(matrix[idx1][idx2])
        return tt, dist

    # Planar Euclidean fallback
    dist = math.hypot(p1[0] - p2[0], p1[1] - p2[1])
    return dist, dist


def validate_solution(
    problem: Problem,
    solution: Solution,
    cost_oracle: Any | None = None,
    tolerance: float = 1e-4,
    max_vehicles: int | None = None,
    max_duty_duration: float | None = None,
    hard_duty_limit: bool = True,
    enforce_priority_early_service: bool = False,
    priority_early_ratio: float = 0.5,
) -> ValidationResult:
    """Independently validate a Solution against a Problem instance from scratch.

    Args:
        problem: The Problem definition.
        solution: The Solution to validate.
        cost_oracle: Optional CostOracle for road network or dynamic traffic times.
        tolerance: Numerical tolerance for floating-point comparisons.
        max_vehicles: Optional limit on the number of vehicle routes.
        max_duty_duration: Maximum allowed duration for any single route.
        hard_duty_limit: Whether exceeding max_duty_duration invalidates the solution.
        enforce_priority_early_service: Whether priority customers must be served in earlier window.
        priority_early_ratio: Fraction of window for priority early service (e.g. 0.5 = first 50%).

    Returns:
        ValidationResult with complete status, violations dictionary, and recomputed metrics.
    """
    violations: dict[str, list[str]] = {}

    expected_customer_ids = {c.id for c in problem.customers}
    customer_map: dict[str, Customer] = {c.id: c for c in problem.customers}
    customer_idx_map: dict[str, int] = {c.id: idx for idx, c in enumerate(problem.customers, start=1)}

    # Prepare locations for CostOracle if present
    loc_depot = None
    loc_custs: dict[str, Any] = {}
    if cost_oracle is not None:
        from routing.cost_oracle import Location
        loc_depot = Location(id=problem.depot.id, lat=problem.depot.lat, lon=problem.depot.lon)
        for c in problem.customers:
            loc_custs[c.id] = Location(id=c.id, lat=c.lat, lon=c.lon)

    # 1. Customer Coverage Checks
    visited_ids: list[str] = []
    for r_idx, route in enumerate(solution.routes):
        for cid in route.stop_ids:
            visited_ids.append(cid)

    visited_set = set(visited_ids)
    missing_ids = expected_customer_ids - visited_set
    if missing_ids:
        violations.setdefault("customer_coverage", []).append(
            f"Missing {len(missing_ids)} customer(s): {sorted(list(missing_ids))[:10]}"
        )

    if len(visited_ids) != len(visited_set):
        from collections import Counter
        counts = Counter(visited_ids)
        dups = [cid for cid, count in counts.items() if count > 1]
        violations.setdefault("customer_coverage", []).append(
            f"Duplicate visits to customer(s): {dups[:10]}"
        )

    unknown_ids = visited_set - expected_customer_ids
    if unknown_ids:
        violations.setdefault("customer_coverage", []).append(
            f"Unknown customer ID(s) visited: {sorted(list(unknown_ids))[:10]}"
        )

    # 2. Fleet Size Limit & Registered Vehicle ID Check
    if problem.vehicles:
        known_veh_ids = {v.id for v in problem.vehicles}
        for r_idx, route in enumerate(solution.routes):
            if route.vehicle_id not in known_veh_ids:
                violations.setdefault("fleet_size", []).append(
                    f"Route {r_idx + 1} assigned vehicle '{route.vehicle_id}' which does not exist in problem.vehicles"
                )
        eff_max = max_vehicles if max_vehicles is not None else len(problem.vehicles)
        if len(solution.routes) > eff_max:
            violations.setdefault("fleet_size", []).append(
                f"Solution uses {len(solution.routes)} routes, exceeding available fleet size of {eff_max}"
            )
    elif max_vehicles is not None and len(solution.routes) > max_vehicles:
        violations.setdefault("fleet_size", []).append(
            f"Solution uses {len(solution.routes)} routes, exceeding max limit of {max_vehicles}"
        )

    # 3. Route-by-Route Simulation & Checks
    total_dist = 0.0
    total_duration = 0.0
    total_waiting = 0.0
    total_service = 0.0
    max_route_dur = 0.0

    depot_pt = (problem.depot.lat, problem.depot.lon)

    for r_idx, route in enumerate(solution.routes):
        veh_id = route.vehicle_id
        # Determine capacity for assigned vehicle
        matched_veh = next((v for v in problem.vehicles if v.id == veh_id), None)
        veh_cap = matched_veh.capacity if matched_veh else (problem.vehicles[0].capacity if problem.vehicles else 100.0)

        # Max duty limit check
        route_max_duty = max_duty_duration
        if route_max_duty is None and matched_veh and math.isfinite(matched_veh.shift_end) and math.isfinite(matched_veh.shift_start):
            shift_diff = matched_veh.shift_end - matched_veh.shift_start
            if shift_diff > 0:
                route_max_duty = shift_diff

        # Capacity check
        recomputed_load = 0.0
        for cid in route.stop_ids:
            if cid in customer_map:
                recomputed_load += customer_map[cid].demand

        if recomputed_load > veh_cap + tolerance:
            violations.setdefault("capacity", []).append(
                f"Route {r_idx+1} (vehicle {veh_id}): total load {recomputed_load:.2f} exceeds capacity {veh_cap:.2f}"
            )

        # Timeline simulation
        curr_time = problem.depot.ready_time
        prev_pt = depot_pt
        prev_idx = 0
        prev_loc = loc_depot
        route_dist = 0.0

        for stop_seq, cid in enumerate(route.stop_ids):
            if cid not in customer_map:
                continue
            cust = customer_map[cid]
            cust_idx = customer_idx_map[cid]
            cust_pt = (cust.lat, cust.lon)
            cust_loc = loc_custs.get(cid)

            # Travel to customer
            tt, d = _get_travel_cost(
                prev_pt, cust_pt, problem.travel_time_matrix, prev_idx, cust_idx,
                cost_oracle, prev_loc, cust_loc, curr_time
            )
            route_dist += d
            arrival_time = curr_time + tt

            # Time window verification
            if arrival_time > cust.due_time + tolerance:
                lateness = arrival_time - cust.due_time
                violations.setdefault("time_window", []).append(
                    f"Route {r_idx+1} stop {stop_seq+1} ({cid}): arrived at {arrival_time:.2f} > due {cust.due_time:.2f} (late by {lateness:.2f}s)"
                )

            # Priority early service check
            if enforce_priority_early_service and cust.priority > 1:
                early_due = cust.ready_time + priority_early_ratio * (cust.due_time - cust.ready_time)
                if arrival_time > early_due + tolerance:
                    violations.setdefault("priority_early_service", []).append(
                        f"Priority customer {cid} arrived at {arrival_time:.2f} > early deadline {early_due:.2f}"
                    )

            # Waiting & service
            wait = max(0.0, cust.ready_time - arrival_time)
            total_waiting += wait
            service_start = max(arrival_time, cust.ready_time)
            total_service += cust.service_time
            curr_time = service_start + cust.service_time

            prev_pt = cust_pt
            prev_idx = cust_idx
            prev_loc = cust_loc

        # Return to depot
        tt, d = _get_travel_cost(
            prev_pt, depot_pt, problem.travel_time_matrix, prev_idx, 0,
            cost_oracle, prev_loc, loc_depot, curr_time
        )
        route_dist += d
        depot_arrival = curr_time + tt
        total_dist += route_dist

        # Depot operational window check
        if depot_arrival > problem.depot.due_time + tolerance:
            lateness = depot_arrival - problem.depot.due_time
            violations.setdefault("depot_due_time", []).append(
                f"Route {r_idx+1} (vehicle {veh_id}): returned to depot at {depot_arrival:.2f} > depot due {problem.depot.due_time:.2f} (late by {lateness:.2f}s)"
            )

        route_duration = depot_arrival - problem.depot.ready_time
        total_duration += route_duration
        if route_duration > max_route_dur:
            max_route_dur = route_duration

        # Driver duty limit check
        if route_max_duty is not None and route_duration > route_max_duty + tolerance:
            msg = f"Route {r_idx+1} (vehicle {veh_id}): duration {route_duration:.2f}s exceeds max duty limit {route_max_duty:.2f}s"
            if hard_duty_limit:
                violations.setdefault("driver_duty_limit", []).append(msg)

    is_valid = len(violations) == 0

    return ValidationResult(
        is_valid=is_valid,
        violations=violations,
        recomputed_cost=round(total_dist, 4),
        recomputed_distance=round(total_dist, 4),
        recomputed_duration=round(total_duration, 4),
        total_waiting_time=round(total_waiting, 4),
        total_service_time=round(total_service, 4),
        max_route_duration=round(max_route_dur, 4),
        num_routes_used=len(solution.routes),
        customer_count=len(visited_ids),
    )


=== FILE: tests/__init__.py ===
"""Route.it Test Suite."""


=== FILE: tests/api/__init__.py ===
"""API package tests."""


=== FILE: tests/api/conftest.py ===
"""Pytest fixtures for Route.it API test suite."""

from __future__ import annotations

import pytest
from fastapi.testclient import TestClient

from collections.abc import Generator

from api.main import app
from api.store import InMemoryProblemStore, set_store
from solver.models import Customer, Depot, Problem, Vehicle
from solver.qpso import QPSOConfig, solve_qpso


@pytest.fixture(scope="session", autouse=True)
def prewarm_numba_jit() -> None:
    """Pre-warm Numba JIT once per test session so optimization runs don't incur first-call compilation delays."""
    depot = Depot(id="depot", lat=0.0, lon=0.0, ready_time=0.0, due_time=1000.0)
    customers = [
        Customer(id="c1", lat=1.0, lon=0.0, demand=1.0, ready_time=0.0, due_time=100.0, service_time=5.0),
        Customer(id="c2", lat=2.0, lon=0.0, demand=1.0, ready_time=0.0, due_time=100.0, service_time=5.0),
    ]
    vehicles = [Vehicle(id="v1", capacity=10.0, shift_start=0.0, shift_end=1000.0)]
    prob = Problem(id="warmup_prob", depot=depot, customers=customers, vehicles=vehicles, coordinate_system="planar")
    prob.compute_euclidean_matrices()
    solve_qpso(prob, QPSOConfig(num_particles=5, max_iters=2, seed=42))

=== FILE: tests/api/test_events.py ===
"""Unit tests for dynamic incident injection and re-optimization endpoints."""

from __future__ import annotations

import time
from fastapi.testclient import TestClient


def _solve_problem_sync(client: TestClient, problem_id: str) -> None:
    """Helper to launch solve and wait for completion."""
    solve_resp = client.post(
        f"/problems/{problem_id}/solve",
        json={"num_particles": 15, "max_iters": 20, "seed": 42},
    )
    job_id = solve_resp.json()["job_id"]
    for _ in range(600):  # Poll up to 30.0s (600 * 0.05s)
        time.sleep(0.05)
        st = client.get(f"/problems/{problem_id}/jobs/{job_id}").json()["status"]
        if st in ("completed", "failed"):
            break


def test_inject_event_without_solution_errors(client: TestClient, sample_problem_payload: dict) -> None:
    """Verify 400 when attempting to inject an event into an unsolved problem."""
    client.post("/problems", json=sample_problem_payload)
    event_payload = {
        "event_type": "edge_congestion",
        "trigger_time": 0.0,
        "duration": 500.0,
        "origin_id": "depot",
        "dest_id": "c1",
        "multiplier": 2.0,
    }
    resp = client.post("/problems/test_city_4/events", json=event_payload)
    assert resp.status_code == 400
    assert "not been solved" in resp.json()["detail"].lower()


def test_inject_traffic_event_reoptimization(client: TestClient, sample_problem_payload: dict) -> None:
    """Verify dynamic incident injection triggers tiered re-optimizer and logs audit record."""
    client.post("/problems", json=sample_problem_payload)
    _solve_problem_sync(client, "test_city_4")

    # Inject edge congestion
    event_payload = {
        "event_id": "api_test_cong",
        "event_type": "edge_congestion",
        "trigger_time": 0.0,
        "duration": 600.0,
        "origin_id": "depot",
        "dest_id": "c1",
        "multiplier": 2.0,
        "description": "API Test Congestion on Depot Exit",
    }
    resp = client.post("/problems/test_city_4/events", json=event_payload)
    assert resp.status_code == 200
    reopt_data = resp.json()

    assert reopt_data["event_id"] == "api_test_cong"
    assert reopt_data["tier_selected"] in (
        "DO_NOTHING",
        "TIER_1_PATH_REPAIR",
        "TIER_2_LOCAL_REPAIR",
        "TIER_3_QPSO_WARM_START",
    )
    assert reopt_data["wall_clock_time_ms"] > 0.0
    assert "solution" in reopt_data
    assert reopt_data["solution"]["is_feasible"] is True

    # Audit history endpoint
    hist_resp = client.get("/problems/test_city_4/events")
    assert hist_resp.status_code == 200
    records = hist_resp.json()
    assert len(records) == 1
    assert records[0]["event_id"] == "api_test_cong"

"""Unit tests for Problem CRUD endpoints."""

from __future__ import annotations

from fastapi.testclient import TestClient


def test_create_and_get_problem(client: TestClient, sample_problem_payload: dict) -> None:
    """Verify problem creation, bounding box calculation, and retrieval."""
    # 1. Create problem
    resp = client.post("/problems", json=sample_problem_payload)
    assert resp.status_code == 201
    created = resp.json()
    assert created["id"] == "test_city_4"
    assert created["num_customers"] == 4
    assert created["num_vehicles"] == 2
    assert created["bbox"] == {
        "min_lat": 22.295,
        "min_lon": 73.17,
        "max_lat": 22.32,
        "max_lon": 73.19,
    }

    # 2. Get problem by ID
    get_resp = client.get("/problems/test_city_4")
    assert get_resp.status_code == 200
    prob_data = get_resp.json()
    assert prob_data["id"] == "test_city_4"
    assert len(prob_data["customers"]) == 4

    # 3. Duplicate creation conflict
    dup_resp = client.post("/problems", json=sample_problem_payload)
    assert dup_resp.status_code == 409


def test_list_and_delete_problem(client: TestClient, sample_problem_payload: dict) -> None:
    """Verify listing problems and deletion."""
    client.post("/problems", json=sample_problem_payload)

    # List
    list_resp = client.get("/problems")
    assert list_resp.status_code == 200
    items = list_resp.json()
    assert len(items) == 1
    assert items[0]["id"] == "test_city_4"

    # Delete
    del_resp = client.delete("/problems/test_city_4")
    assert del_resp.status_code == 204

    # Confirm deletion
    get_resp = client.get("/problems/test_city_4")
    assert get_resp.status_code == 404

    assert resp.status_code == 404
    assert "not found" in resp.json()["detail"].lower()


=== FILE: tests/api/test_solve_and_solution.py ===
"""Unit tests for async optimization solve jobs and GeoJSON solution endpoints."""

from __future__ import annotations

import time
import pytest
from fastapi.testclient import TestClient

from routing.cost_oracle import EuclideanOracle
from solver.models import Customer, Depot, Problem, Vehicle
from solver.qpso import QPSOConfig, solve_qpso


def test_solution_not_found_before_solve(client: TestClient, sample_problem_payload: dict) -> None:
    """Verify 404 when querying solution before problem has been solved."""
    client.post("/problems", json=sample_problem_payload)
    resp = client.get("/problems/test_city_4/solution")
    assert resp.status_code == 404
    assert "no solution available" in resp.json()["detail"].lower()


def test_solve_async_job_and_poll_solution(client: TestClient, sample_problem_payload: dict) -> None:
    """Verify async QPSO solve execution, job polling, and GeoJSON solution extraction."""
    client.post("/problems", json=sample_problem_payload)

    # 1. Trigger async solve job
    solve_req = {
        "num_particles": 15,
        "max_iters": 20,
        "cost_oracle_type": "euclidean",
        "seed": 42,
        "apply_tw_repair": True,
    }
    solve_resp = client.post("/problems/test_city_4/solve", json=solve_req)
    assert solve_resp.status_code == 202
    job_data = solve_resp.json()
    job_id = job_data["job_id"]
    assert job_data["problem_id"] == "test_city_4"
    assert job_data["status"] in ("pending", "running", "completed")

    # 2. Poll job status until completed
    max_wait_seconds = 30.0
    start_poll = time.time()
    final_status = job_data["status"]

    while final_status not in ("completed", "failed") and (time.time() - start_poll) < max_wait_seconds:
        time.sleep(0.05)
        poll_resp = client.get(f"/problems/test_city_4/jobs/{job_id}")
        assert poll_resp.status_code == 200
        final_status = poll_resp.json()["status"]

    assert final_status == "completed"

    # 3. Retrieve solution & GeoJSON
    sol_resp = client.get("/problems/test_city_4/solution?sim_time=0.0")
    assert sol_resp.status_code == 200
    sol_data = sol_resp.json()

    assert sol_data["problem_id"] == "test_city_4"
    assert sol_data["total_cost"] > 0.0
    assert sol_data["is_feasible"] is True
    assert len(sol_data["routes"]) >= 1

    # 4. Verify GeoJSON structure
    geojson = sol_data["geojson"]
    assert geojson["type"] == "FeatureCollection"
    features = geojson["features"]
    assert len(features) > 1

    route_features = [f for f in features if f["properties"].get("feature_type") == "route"]
    assert len(route_features) >= 1
    for rf in route_features:
        assert rf["geometry"]["type"] == "LineString"
        assert len(rf["geometry"]["coordinates"]) >= 3  # Depot -> stop -> Depot
        assert "vehicle_id" in rf["properties"]
        assert "color" in rf["properties"]

    stop_features = [f for f in features if f["properties"].get("feature_type") == "customer"]
    assert len(stop_features) == 4
    for sf in stop_features:
        assert sf["geometry"]["type"] == "Point"
        assert sf["properties"]["id"] in ["c1", "c2", "c3", "c4"]
        assert "arrival_time" in sf["properties"]


def test_planar_coordinates_with_explicit_planar_oracle() -> None:
    """Verify that planar Cartesian coordinates remain fully supported and feasible with use_haversine=False."""
    depot = Depot(id="depot", lat=0.0, lon=0.0, ready_time=0.0, due_time=1000.0)
    customers = [
        Customer(id="c1", lat=10.0, lon=0.0, demand=5.0, ready_time=0.0, due_time=500.0, service_time=10.0),
        Customer(id="c2", lat=20.0, lon=0.0, demand=5.0, ready_time=0.0, due_time=500.0, service_time=10.0),
        Customer(id="c3", lat=0.0, lon=10.0, demand=5.0, ready_time=0.0, due_time=500.0, service_time=10.0),
        Customer(id="c4", lat=0.0, lon=20.0, demand=5.0, ready_time=0.0, due_time=500.0, service_time=10.0),
    ]
    vehicles = [
        Vehicle(id="v1", capacity=15.0, shift_start=0.0, shift_end=1000.0),
        Vehicle(id="v2", capacity=15.0, shift_start=0.0, shift_end=1000.0),
    ]
    problem = Problem(id="planar_toy", depot=depot, customers=customers, vehicles=vehicles, coordinate_system="planar")
    problem.compute_euclidean_matrices(speed=1.0)

    oracle = EuclideanOracle(base_speed_mps=1.0, use_haversine=False)
    solution, _ = solve_qpso(problem, QPSOConfig(num_particles=15, max_iters=20, seed=42), cost_oracle=oracle)

    assert solution.is_feasible is True
    assert solution.violations == {}
    assert solution.total_cost == pytest.approx(80.0, rel=1e-3)



=== FILE: tests/api/test_websocket.py ===
"""Unit and integration tests for real-time WebSocket telemetry streaming."""

from __future__ import annotations

import time
from fastapi.testclient import TestClient


def test_websocket_connection_and_ping(client: TestClient) -> None:
    """Verify WebSocket handshake connection and ping-pong reply."""
    with client.websocket_connect("/ws/problems/test_prob_101") as ws:
        # 1. Handshake packet
        handshake = ws.receive_json()
        assert handshake["type"] == "connection_established"
        assert handshake["problem_id"] == "test_prob_101"

        # 2. Ping-pong
        ws.send_text("ping")
        reply = ws.receive_json()
        assert reply["type"] == "pong"


def test_websocket_streams_solve_progress_and_completion(
    client: TestClient, sample_problem_payload: dict
) -> None:
    """Verify WebSocket streams solve_started, solve_progress, and solve_completed packets."""
    client.post("/problems", json=sample_problem_payload)

    with client.websocket_connect("/ws/problems/test_city_4") as ws:
        # Handshake
        handshake = ws.receive_json()
        assert handshake["type"] == "connection_established"

        # Launch solve
        solve_resp = client.post(
            "/problems/test_city_4/solve",
            json={"num_particles": 15, "max_iters": 10, "seed": 42},
        )
        assert solve_resp.status_code == 202

        # Collect incoming stream packets
        received_types: set[str] = set()
        for _ in range(50):
            msg = ws.receive_json()
            received_types.add(msg["type"])
            if msg["type"] == "solve_completed":
                assert msg["is_feasible"] is True
                assert "solution_cost" in msg
                break

        assert "solve_started" in received_types
        assert "solve_progress" in received_types
        assert "solve_completed" in received_types


def test_websocket_streams_reoptimization_telemetry(
    client: TestClient, sample_problem_payload: dict
) -> None:
    """Verify WebSocket streams reoptimization_update telemetry upon incident injection."""
    client.post("/problems", json=sample_problem_payload)

    # Solve first
    solve_resp = client.post(
        "/problems/test_city_4/solve",
        json={"num_particles": 15, "max_iters": 10, "seed": 42},
    )
    job_id = solve_resp.json()["job_id"]
    for _ in range(600):  # Poll up to 30.0s (600 * 0.05s)
        time.sleep(0.05)
        if client.get(f"/problems/test_city_4/jobs/{job_id}").json()["status"] == "completed":
            break

    with client.websocket_connect("/ws/problems/test_city_4") as ws:
        # Handshake
        ws.receive_json()

        # Inject event
        event_payload = {
            "event_type": "edge_congestion",
            "trigger_time": 0.0,
            "duration": 500.0,
            "origin_id": "depot",
            "dest_id": "c1",
            "multiplier": 2.0,
        }
        client.post("/problems/test_city_4/events", json=event_payload)

        # Receive reoptimization_update
        reopt_msg = ws.receive_json()
        assert reopt_msg["type"] == "reoptimization_update"
        assert reopt_msg["problem_id"] == "test_city_4"
        assert "tier_selected" in reopt_msg
        assert "wall_clock_time_ms" in reopt_msg
        assert "cost_after" in reopt_msg


=== FILE: tests/benchmarks/test_check_instances.py ===
"""Unit tests for benchmark instance verification script in benchmarks/check_instances.py."""

from pathlib import Path
import pytest

from benchmarks.check_instances import (
    RECOMMENDED_INSTANCES,
    check_all_instances,
    main,
    verify_instance,
)


def test_verify_existing_reference_instance() -> None:
    """Test verify_instance on the included A-n32-k5.vrp reference file."""
    ref_path = Path("benchmarks/instances/A-n32-k5.vrp")
    result = verify_instance(ref_path, category="CVRPLIB")

    assert result.exists is True
    assert result.parsed is True
    assert result.num_customers == 31
    assert result.num_vehicles == 5
    assert result.capacity == 100.0
    assert result.error is None


def test_verify_missing_instance() -> None:
    """Test verify_instance on a non-existent file."""
    missing_path = Path("benchmarks/instances/non_existent.vrp")
    result = verify_instance(missing_path, category="Test")

    assert result.exists is False
    assert result.parsed is False
    assert result.error == "File missing"


def test_verify_unsupported_extension(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    """Test verify_instance on an unsupported extension like .csv."""
    unsupported = tmp_path / "dummy.csv"
    unsupported.write_text("a,b,c", encoding="utf-8")

    result = verify_instance(unsupported, category="Other")
    assert result.exists is True
    assert result.parsed is False
    assert "Unsupported extension" in (result.error or "")


def test_check_all_instances_covers_recommended() -> None:
    """Test that check_all_instances includes all recommended benchmark targets."""
    instances_dir = Path("benchmarks/instances")
    results = check_all_instances(instances_dir)

    filenames = [r.filename for r in results]
    for target, _ in RECOMMENDED_INSTANCES:
        assert target in filenames


def test_cli_main_non_strict() -> None:
    """Test main() CLI runs successfully in normal (non-strict) mode."""
    exit_code = main(["--instances-dir", "benchmarks/instances"])
    assert exit_code == 0


def test_cli_main_strict_fails_when_files_missing() -> None:
    """Test main() CLI fails with code 1 in strict mode when files are missing."""
    exit_code = main(["--instances-dir", "benchmarks/instances", "--strict"])
    assert exit_code == 1


=== FILE: tests/benchmarks/test_demo_vadodara.py ===
"""Unit tests for Vadodara 30-stop delivery demo script and GeoJSON generation.

Verifies:
1. Generation and validation of the 30-stop Vadodara CVRPTW problem instance.
2. End-to-end execution of demo_vadodara in mock / simulated mode.
3. GeoJSON FeatureCollection structure, stop points, and route line geometries.
4. Execution through the live Valhalla code path using a mocked HTTP service.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import json
from pathlib import Path
from unittest.mock import MagicMock, patch
import pytest

from benchmarks.demo_vadodara import (
    build_vadodara_problem,
    check_valhalla_alive,
    generate_simulated_road_polyline,
    main as demo_main,
)
from routing.cost_oracle import Location, encode_polyline


def test_build_vadodara_problem_structure() -> None:
    """Verify Vadodara problem instance has correct depot, customer count, and fleet capacity."""
    problem = build_vadodara_problem(seed=42)

    assert problem.id == "vadodara_30"
    assert problem.depot.id == "depot"
    # Vadodara city coordinates
    assert 22.20 <= problem.depot.lat <= 22.40
    assert 73.10 <= problem.depot.lon <= 73.30

    assert len(problem.customers) == 30
    assert len(problem.vehicles) == 6

    # Verify unique IDs
    cust_ids = [c.id for c in problem.customers]
    assert len(cust_ids) == len(set(cust_ids))

    # All customers must lie within Vadodara municipal boundaries
    for c in problem.customers:
        assert 22.24 <= c.lat <= 22.38
        assert 73.11 <= c.lon <= 73.25
        assert c.demand > 0.0
        assert c.ready_time <= c.due_time


def test_generate_simulated_road_polyline() -> None:
    """Verify simulated road polyline generates intermediate waypoints between GPS points."""
    p1 = (22.3108, 73.1811)
    p2 = (22.3245, 73.2050)

    polyline = generate_simulated_road_polyline(p1, p2, steps=6)

    # Should have start point, 5 intermediate points, and end point = 7 points
    assert len(polyline) == 7
    # First point: [lon1, lat1]
    assert polyline[0][0] == pytest.approx(p1[1], abs=1e-5)
    assert polyline[0][1] == pytest.approx(p1[0], abs=1e-5)
    # Last point: [lon2, lat2]
    assert polyline[-1][0] == pytest.approx(p2[1], abs=1e-5)
    assert polyline[-1][1] == pytest.approx(p2[0], abs=1e-5)


def test_demo_main_execution_simulated_mode(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    """Verify demo_vadodara runs end-to-end and outputs valid GeoJSON in simulated mode."""
    geojson_out = tmp_path / "vadodara_test_routes.geojson"
    problem_out = tmp_path / "vadodara_test_problem.json"

    test_args = [
        "demo_vadodara.py",
        "--mock",
        "--particles", "10",
        "--iters", "15",
        "--seed", "42",
        "--output", str(geojson_out),
        "--save-problem", str(problem_out),
    ]

    with patch("sys.argv", test_args):
        ret_code = demo_main()

    assert ret_code == 0
    assert problem_out.is_file()
    assert geojson_out.is_file()

    # Validate output GeoJSON
    with geojson_out.open("r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        data = json.load(f)

    assert data["type"] == "FeatureCollection"
    assert data["metadata"]["city"] == "Vadodara, Gujarat, India"
    assert data["metadata"]["num_customers"] == 30
    assert data["metadata"]["is_feasible"] is True

    # Check features
    features = data["features"]
    points = [f for f in features if f["geometry"]["type"] == "Point"]
    linestrings = [f for f in features if f["geometry"]["type"] == "LineString"]

    # 1 depot + 30 customers = 31 points
    assert len(points) == 31
    assert len(linestrings) >= 1

    # Check depot point
    depot_feat = next(f for f in points if f["properties"]["type"] == "depot")
    assert depot_feat["properties"]["name"] is not None

    # Check a LineString feature
    first_route = linestrings[0]
    assert "route_id" in first_route["properties"]
    assert "load" in first_route["properties"]
    assert "distance_km" in first_route["properties"]
    assert len(first_route["geometry"]["coordinates"]) >= 2


def test_demo_main_execution_mocked_valhalla_live_mode(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    """Verify demo_vadodara routes through live Valhalla codepath when service responds."""
    geojson_out = tmp_path / "vadodara_valhalla_routes.geojson"
    problem_out = tmp_path / "vadodara_valhalla_problem.json"

    # Mock matrix response (31 x 31 stops)
    mock_matrix = []
    for _ in range(31):
        row = []
        for _ in range(31):
            row.append({"time": 300.0, "distance": 3.5})
        mock_matrix.append(row)

    mock_matrix_resp = MagicMock()
    mock_matrix_resp.status = 200
    mock_matrix_resp.read.return_value = json.dumps({"sources_to_targets": mock_matrix}).encode("utf-8")
    mock_matrix_resp.__enter__.return_value = mock_matrix_resp

    # Mock route response
    shape = encode_polyline([(22.3108, 73.1811), (22.3150, 73.1850), (22.3200, 73.1900)])
    mock_route_resp = MagicMock()
    mock_route_resp.status = 200
    mock_route_resp.read.return_value = json.dumps({
        "trip": {
            "summary": {"length": 5.2, "time": 450.0},
            "legs": [{"shape": shape}],
        }
    }).encode("utf-8")
    mock_route_resp.__enter__.return_value = mock_route_resp

    test_args = [
        "demo_vadodara.py",
        "--valhalla-url", "http://localhost:8002",
        "--particles", "10",
        "--iters", "10",
        "--seed", "42",
        "--output", str(geojson_out),
        "--save-problem", str(problem_out),
    ]

    def custom_urlopen(req, timeout=None):
        url = req.full_url if hasattr(req, "full_url") else str(req)
        if "/status" in url:
            resp = MagicMock()
            resp.status = 200
            resp.__enter__.return_value = resp
            return resp
        elif "/sources_to_targets" in url:
            return mock_matrix_resp
        elif "/route" in url:
            return mock_route_resp
        return MagicMock(status=200)

    with patch("benchmarks.demo_vadodara.check_valhalla_alive", return_value=True), \
         patch("urllib.request.urlopen", side_effect=custom_urlopen), \
         patch("sys.argv", test_args):
        ret_code = demo_main()

    assert ret_code == 0
    assert geojson_out.is_file()

    with geojson_out.open("r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        data = json.load(f)

    assert "Valhalla (Live @" in data["metadata"]["routing_engine"]


def test_dashboard_scenario_matches_canonical_vadodara_json() -> None:
    """Verify that the dashboard scenario data and benchmarks/instances/vadodara_30.json share identical customers, coordinates, and demands."""
    json_path = Path("benchmarks/instances/vadodara_30.json")
    with json_path.open("r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        canonical_data = json.load(f)

    # 1. Verify canonical structure
    canonical_custs = canonical_data["customers"]
    assert len(canonical_custs) == 30
    assert sum(c["demand"] for c in canonical_custs) == 235.0
    assert len(canonical_data["vehicles"]) == 6
    assert all(v["capacity"] == 80.0 for v in canonical_data["vehicles"])

    # 2. Verify dashboard vadodara_scenario.ts imports this exact canonical JSON
    ts_path = Path("dashboard/src/data/vadodara_scenario.ts")
    ts_content = ts_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    assert "vadodara_30.json" in ts_content
    assert "rawVadodaraJson.customers.map" in ts_content
    assert "rawVadodaraJson.vehicles.map" in ts_content
