"""Unit tests for dynamic events and scenario models in dynamic/events.py."""

from pathlib import Path
import pytest
from pydantic import ValidationError

from dynamic.events import (
    EdgeCongestion,
    NewOrder,
    RoadBlockage,
    Scenario,
    VehicleDelay,
    generate_random_scenario,
)
from routing.cost_oracle import Location, SimulatedEventAdapter, TimeOfDayProfileAdapter
from solver.models import Customer, Depot, Problem, Vehicle


@pytest.fixture
def sample_problem() -> Problem:
    """Provide a standard toy problem for scenario tests."""
    depot = Depot(id="depot", lat=22.30, lon=73.18, ready_time=0.0, due_time=10000.0)
    customers = [
        Customer(
            id=f"c{i}",
            lat=22.30 + 0.01 * i,
            lon=73.18 + 0.01 * i,
            demand=10.0,
            ready_time=100.0 * i,
            due_time=500.0 + 100.0 * i,
            service_time=50.0,
        )
        for i in range(1, 5)
    ]
    vehicles = [Vehicle(id="v1", capacity=100.0), Vehicle(id="v2", capacity=100.0)]
    return Problem(id="toy_test_prob", depot=depot, customers=customers, vehicles=vehicles, coordinate_system="planar")


def test_edge_congestion_validation_and_incidents():
    """Verify EdgeCongestion model validation and incident conversion."""
    # Must fail if no spatial scope provided
    with pytest.raises(ValidationError):
        EdgeCongestion(event_id="bad_cong", trigger_time=100.0, duration=300.0, multiplier=2.0)

    # Valid with origin_id and dest_id
    cong = EdgeCongestion(
        event_id="cong_1",
        trigger_time=100.0,
        duration=600.0,
        multiplier=2.5,
        origin_id="c1",
        dest_id="c2",
        description="Heavy bottleneck",
    )
    assert cong.end_time == 700.0
    incidents = cong.to_simulated_incidents()
    assert len(incidents) == 1
    assert incidents[0].event_id == "cong_1"
    assert incidents[0].speed_multiplier == pytest.approx(1.0 / 2.5)
    assert incidents[0].start_time == 100.0
    assert incidents[0].end_time == 700.0
    assert incidents[0].origin_id == "c1"
    assert incidents[0].dest_id == "c2"

    # Edge set conversion
    cong_multi = EdgeCongestion(
        event_id="cong_multi",
        trigger_time=200.0,
        duration=400.0,
        multiplier=2.0,
        edge_set=[("depot", "c1"), ("c1", "c2")],
    )
    inc_multi = cong_multi.to_simulated_incidents()
    assert len(inc_multi) == 2
    assert inc_multi[0].origin_id == "depot"
    assert inc_multi[0].dest_id == "c1"
    assert inc_multi[1].origin_id == "c1"
    assert inc_multi[1].dest_id == "c2"


def test_road_blockage_validation_and_incidents():
    """Verify RoadBlockage creates incidents with speed_multiplier=0.0."""
    with pytest.raises(ValidationError):
        RoadBlockage(event_id="bad_block", trigger_time=50.0, duration=100.0)

    block = RoadBlockage(
        event_id="block_sayaji",
        trigger_time=500.0,
        duration=1200.0,
        center_lat=22.31,
        center_lon=73.19,
        radius_meters=500.0,
        description="Sinkhole",
    )
    assert block.end_time == 1700.0
    incs = block.to_simulated_incidents()
    assert len(incs) == 1
    assert incs[0].speed_multiplier == 0.0
    assert incs[0].radius_meters == 500.0


def test_vehicle_delay_model():
    """Verify VehicleDelay attributes and default duration behavior."""
    delay = VehicleDelay(
        event_id="delay_v1",
        trigger_time=350.0,
        vehicle_id="v1",
        delay_seconds=180.0,
        stop_id="c2",
    )
    assert delay.delay_seconds == 180.0
    assert delay.duration == 180.0
    assert delay.end_time == 530.0


def test_new_order_model():
    """Verify NewOrder dynamic customer model."""
    new_cust = Customer(
        id="c_urg",
        lat=22.32,
        lon=73.20,
        demand=5.0,
        ready_time=400.0,
        due_time=900.0,
        service_time=30.0,
        priority=3,
    )
    order = NewOrder(event_id="order_1", trigger_time=300.0, customer=new_cust)
    assert order.customer.id == "c_urg"
    assert order.customer.priority == 3
    assert order.trigger_time == 300.0


def test_scenario_json_roundtrip(tmp_path: Path):
    """Test scenario serialization and file persistence."""
    events = [
        EdgeCongestion(
            event_id="e1",
            trigger_time=100.0,
            duration=300.0,
            multiplier=2.0,
            origin_id="depot",
            dest_id="c1",
        ),
        RoadBlockage(
            event_id="e2",
            trigger_time=400.0,
            duration=600.0,
            origin_id="c1",
            dest_id="c2",
        ),
        VehicleDelay(
            event_id="e3",
            trigger_time=250.0,
            vehicle_id="v1",
            delay_seconds=120.0,
        ),
        NewOrder(
            event_id="e4",
            trigger_time=300.0,
            customer=Customer(
                id="c_dyn",
                lat=22.31,
                lon=73.19,
                demand=8.0,
                ready_time=350.0,
                due_time=1000.0,
                service_time=40.0,
            ),
        ),
    ]

    scenario = Scenario(
        id="test_scen_1",
        name="Validation Scenario",
        start_time=0.0,
        duration=3600.0,
        events=events,
    )

    # Save to disk
    file_path = tmp_path / "scenario_test.json"
    scenario.save(file_path)
    assert file_path.is_file()

    # Load from disk
    loaded = Scenario.load(file_path)
    assert loaded.id == "test_scen_1"
    assert len(loaded.events) == 4
    assert loaded.events[0].event_type == "edge_congestion"
    assert loaded.events[1].event_type == "road_blockage"
    assert loaded.events[2].event_type == "vehicle_delay"
    assert loaded.events[3].event_type == "new_order"
    assert loaded.events[0].multiplier == 2.0


def test_traffic_adapter_incident_modulation():
    """Verify applying EdgeCongestion to SimulatedEventAdapter changes speed multiplier."""
    base_adapter = TimeOfDayProfileAdapter({0: 1.0})  # 1.0 constant
    adapter = SimulatedEventAdapter(base_adapter=base_adapter)

    loc1 = Location(id="c1", lat=22.30, lon=73.18)
    loc2 = Location(id="c2", lat=22.31, lon=73.19)

    # Free flow before incident
    assert adapter.get_speed_multiplier(loc1, loc2, depart_time=50.0) == 1.0

    # Add EdgeCongestion with multiplier 2.0 (speed 0.5) from t=100 to t=500
    cong = EdgeCongestion(
        event_id="cong_test",
        trigger_time=100.0,
        duration=400.0,
        multiplier=2.0,
        origin_id="c1",
        dest_id="c2",
    )
    for inc in cong.to_simulated_incidents():
        adapter.add_incident(inc)

    # During event: speed should be halved (0.5)
    assert adapter.get_speed_multiplier(loc1, loc2, depart_time=250.0) == pytest.approx(0.5)
    # Different unblocked edge should remain free flow (1.0)
    loc3 = Location(id="c3", lat=22.32, lon=73.20)
    assert adapter.get_speed_multiplier(loc1, loc3, depart_time=250.0) == 1.0

    # After removal: returns to free flow
    for inc in cong.to_simulated_incidents():
        adapter.remove_incident(inc.event_id)
    assert adapter.get_speed_multiplier(loc1, loc2, depart_time=250.0) == 1.0


def test_deterministic_scenario_generator(sample_problem: Problem):
    """Verify generate_random_scenario reproducibility and output structure."""
    scen_a = generate_random_scenario(sample_problem, num_events=6, seed=123)
    scen_b = generate_random_scenario(sample_problem, num_events=6, seed=123)
    scen_c = generate_random_scenario(sample_problem, num_events=6, seed=999)

    # Determinism with same seed
    assert len(scen_a.events) == 6
    assert scen_a.to_json() == scen_b.to_json()

    # Different seeds yield different schedules
    assert scen_a.to_json() != scen_c.to_json()

    # Events must be chronologically ordered
    trigger_times = [e.trigger_time for e in scen_a.events]
    assert trigger_times == sorted(trigger_times)


=== FILE: tests/dynamic/test_reoptimizer.py ===
"""Unit tests for Dynamic Route Re-optimizer in dynamic/reoptimizer.py."""

import pytest

from dynamic.events import EdgeCongestion, RoadBlockage, VehicleDelay
from dynamic.reoptimizer import (
    DynamicReoptimizer,
    ReoptimizationTier,
    ReoptimizerConfig,
    calculate_stability_penalty,
    full_restart_baseline,
    partition_stops_by_commitment,
)
from routing.cost_oracle import EuclideanOracle, SimulatedEventAdapter, TimeOfDayProfileAdapter
from solver.models import Customer, Depot, Problem, Route, Solution, Vehicle
from solver.validate import validate_solution


@pytest.fixture
def test_fleet_problem() -> Problem:
    """Problem with 4 customers and 2 vehicles for testing re-optimization tiers."""
    depot = Depot(id="depot", lat=0.0, lon=0.0, ready_time=0.0, due_time=1000.0)
    c1 = Customer(id="c1", lat=10.0, lon=0.0, demand=5.0, ready_time=10.0, due_time=100.0, service_time=10.0)
    c2 = Customer(id="c2", lat=20.0, lon=0.0, demand=5.0, ready_time=20.0, due_time=100.0, service_time=10.0)
    c3 = Customer(id="c3", lat=0.0, lon=10.0, demand=5.0, ready_time=10.0, due_time=100.0, service_time=10.0)
    c4 = Customer(id="c4", lat=0.0, lon=20.0, demand=5.0, ready_time=20.0, due_time=100.0, service_time=10.0)
    vehicles = [Vehicle(id="v1", capacity=30.0), Vehicle(id="v2", capacity=30.0)]
    return Problem(id="reopt_prob", depot=depot, customers=[c1, c2, c3, c4], vehicles=vehicles, coordinate_system="planar")


@pytest.fixture
def initial_fleet_solution() -> Solution:
    """Initial solution: v1 -> [c1, c2], v2 -> [c3, c4]."""
    r1 = Route(
        vehicle_id="v1",
        stop_ids=["c1", "c2"],
        arrival_times=[10.0, 30.0],
        load=10.0,
        distance=40.0,
        duration=60.0,
    )
    r2 = Route(
        vehicle_id="v2",
        stop_ids=["c3", "c4"],
        arrival_times=[10.0, 30.0],
        load=10.0,
        distance=40.0,
        duration=60.0,
    )
    return Solution(
        routes=[r1, r2],
        total_cost=200.0,
        is_feasible=True,
    )


def test_partition_stops_by_commitment(test_fleet_problem: Problem):
    """Verify partition into frozen vs uncommitted based on simulation time and commitment window."""
    r1 = Route(
        vehicle_id="v1",
        stop_ids=["c1", "c2"],
        arrival_times=[10.0, 50.0],
        load=10.0,
        distance=40.0,
        duration=70.0,
    )
    # At t=25: c1 completed (10 + 10 = 20 <= 25) -> frozen
    # c2 arrives at 50, with commitment_horizon=15 (50 - 25 = 25 > 15) -> uncommitted
    frozen, uncommitted = partition_stops_by_commitment(
        r1, current_time=25.0, commitment_horizon=15.0, problem=test_fleet_problem
    )
    assert frozen == ["c1"]
    assert uncommitted == ["c2"]

    # At t=25 with larger commitment_horizon=30 (50 - 25 = 25 <= 30) -> c2 committed/frozen
    frozen2, uncommitted2 = partition_stops_by_commitment(
        r1, current_time=25.0, commitment_horizon=30.0, problem=test_fleet_problem
    )
    assert frozen2 == ["c1", "c2"]
    assert uncommitted2 == []


def test_stability_penalty_computation(test_fleet_problem: Problem, initial_fleet_solution: Solution):
    """Verify stability penalty penalizes reassignment and order inversions."""
    config = ReoptimizerConfig(lambda_veh=50.0, lambda_order=10.0)

    # 1. No changes
    pen0, changed0 = calculate_stability_penalty(
        initial_fleet_solution, initial_fleet_solution, {"c1", "c2", "c3", "c4"}, config
    )
    assert pen0 == 0.0
    assert changed0 == []

    # 2. Move c2 to v2: v1 -> [c1], v2 -> [c3, c4, c2]
    sol_reassigned = Solution(
        routes=[
            Route(vehicle_id="v1", stop_ids=["c1"], arrival_times=[10.0], load=5.0, distance=20.0, duration=30.0),
            Route(vehicle_id="v2", stop_ids=["c3", "c4", "c2"], arrival_times=[10.0, 30.0, 60.0], load=15.0, distance=70.0, duration=80.0),
        ],
        total_cost=90.0,
        is_feasible=True,
    )
    pen1, changed1 = calculate_stability_penalty(
        initial_fleet_solution, sol_reassigned, {"c1", "c2", "c3", "c4"}, config
    )
    assert pen1 == 50.0  # 1 reassigned * 50.0
    assert "c2" in changed1

    # 3. Invert order on v1: v1 -> [c2, c1], v2 -> [c3, c4]
    sol_inverted = Solution(
        routes=[
            Route(vehicle_id="v1", stop_ids=["c2", "c1"], arrival_times=[20.0, 40.0], load=10.0, distance=50.0, duration=60.0),
            Route(vehicle_id="v2", stop_ids=["c3", "c4"], arrival_times=[10.0, 30.0], load=10.0, distance=40.0, duration=50.0),
        ],
        total_cost=90.0,
        is_feasible=True,
    )
    pen2, changed2 = calculate_stability_penalty(
        initial_fleet_solution, sol_inverted, {"c1", "c2"}, config
    )
    assert pen2 == 10.0  # 1 inversion * 10.0
    assert "c1" in changed2 and "c2" in changed2


def test_impact_check_tolerance_and_blockage(test_fleet_problem: Problem, initial_fleet_solution: Solution):
    """Verify impact check triggers only when threshold theta is exceeded or edge is blocked."""
    adapter = SimulatedEventAdapter(base_adapter=TimeOfDayProfileAdapter({h: 1.0 for h in range(24)}))
    oracle = EuclideanOracle(base_speed_mps=1.0, use_haversine=False, traffic_adapter=adapter)
    reoptimizer = DynamicReoptimizer(
        test_fleet_problem,
        cost_oracle=oracle,
        config=ReoptimizerConfig(theta=0.10, theta_absolute=5.0),
    )

    # Free flow -> Should NOT replan
    should_replan, reason, delta_f, is_blocked = reoptimizer.check_impact(initial_fleet_solution, current_time=0.0)
    assert should_replan is False
    assert is_blocked is False

    # Minor slowdown (multiplier 1.05) -> Should NOT replan
    mild_cong = EdgeCongestion(
        event_id="cong_mild",
        trigger_time=0.0,
        duration=100.0,
        origin_id="c1",
        dest_id="c2",
        multiplier=1.05,
    )
    for inc in mild_cong.to_simulated_incidents():
        adapter.add_incident(inc)

    should_replan_mild, _, delta_f_mild, _ = reoptimizer.check_impact(initial_fleet_solution, current_time=0.0)
    assert should_replan_mild is False

    # Complete road blockage on edge (c1, c2) -> MUST replan immediately
    adapter.clear_incidents()
    block = RoadBlockage(
        event_id="block_c1_c2",
        trigger_time=0.0,
        duration=100.0,
        origin_id="c1",
        dest_id="c2",
    )
    for inc in block.to_simulated_incidents():
        adapter.add_incident(inc)

    should_replan_block, reason_block, _, is_blocked = reoptimizer.check_impact(initial_fleet_solution, current_time=0.0)
    assert should_replan_block is True
    assert is_blocked is True
    assert "blocked" in reason_block.lower()


def test_tier_1_path_repair_acceptance(test_fleet_problem: Problem, initial_fleet_solution: Solution):
    """Verify Tier 1 Path Repair updates schedule while keeping customer sequence intact."""
    adapter = SimulatedEventAdapter(base_adapter=TimeOfDayProfileAdapter({h: 1.0 for h in range(24)}))
    oracle = EuclideanOracle(base_speed_mps=1.0, use_haversine=False, traffic_adapter=adapter)

    # Moderate congestion on depot->c1 that doesn't breach due_time
    cong = EdgeCongestion(
        event_id="cong_depot_c1",
        trigger_time=0.0,
        duration=100.0,
        origin_id="depot",
        dest_id="c1",
        multiplier=2.0,  # travel time doubles from 10 to 20
    )
    for inc in cong.to_simulated_incidents():
        adapter.add_incident(inc)

    reoptimizer = DynamicReoptimizer(
        test_fleet_problem,
        cost_oracle=oracle,
        config=ReoptimizerConfig(theta=0.01, theta_absolute=2.0, commitment_horizon_seconds=0.0),
    )

    reopt_sol, record = reoptimizer.reoptimize(initial_fleet_solution, current_time=0.0, event=cong)
    assert record.tier_selected == ReoptimizationTier.TIER_1_PATH_REPAIR
    assert record.is_feasible is True
    # Stop sequence must be unchanged
    assert reopt_sol.routes[0].stop_ids == ["c1", "c2"]
    assert reopt_sol.routes[1].stop_ids == ["c3", "c4"]
    # Arrival at c1 adjusted to 20.0
    assert reopt_sol.routes[0].arrival_times[0] == pytest.approx(20.0)


def test_tier_2_local_repair_relocate(test_fleet_problem: Problem, initial_fleet_solution: Solution):
    """Verify Tier 2 Local Repair relocates a congested customer to an uncongested vehicle."""
    adapter = SimulatedEventAdapter(base_adapter=TimeOfDayProfileAdapter({h: 1.0 for h in range(24)}))
    oracle = EuclideanOracle(base_speed_mps=1.0, use_haversine=False, traffic_adapter=adapter)

    # Block edge (c1, c2) on v1's route so v1 cannot go from c1 to c2
    block = RoadBlockage(
        event_id="block_c1_c2",
        trigger_time=0.0,
        duration=100.0,
        origin_id="c1",
        dest_id="c2",
    )
    for inc in block.to_simulated_incidents():
        adapter.add_incident(inc)

    reoptimizer = DynamicReoptimizer(
        test_fleet_problem,
        cost_oracle=oracle,
        config=ReoptimizerConfig(theta=0.01, theta_absolute=2.0, commitment_horizon_seconds=0.0),
    )

    reopt_sol, record = reoptimizer.reoptimize(initial_fleet_solution, current_time=0.0, event=block)
    # Tier 1 fails because edge is blocked -> Tier 2 or 3 succeeds
    assert record.tier_selected in (ReoptimizationTier.TIER_2_LOCAL_REPAIR, ReoptimizationTier.TIER_3_QPSO_WARM_START)
    assert record.is_feasible is True

    # Customer c2 was moved away from c1
    r1 = next(r for r in reopt_sol.routes if r.vehicle_id == "v1")
    # Route 1 should not contain c1 followed by c2
    if "c1" in r1.stop_ids and "c2" in r1.stop_ids:
        idx1 = r1.stop_ids.index("c1")
        idx2 = r1.stop_ids.index("c2")
        assert idx2 != idx1 + 1


def test_full_restart_baseline_comparison(test_fleet_problem: Problem, initial_fleet_solution: Solution):
    """Verify full restart baseline produces a feasible solution and can be compared with tiered re-optimizer."""
    adapter = SimulatedEventAdapter(base_adapter=TimeOfDayProfileAdapter({h: 1.0 for h in range(24)}))
    oracle = EuclideanOracle(base_speed_mps=1.0, use_haversine=False, traffic_adapter=adapter)

    cong = EdgeCongestion(
        event_id="cong_test",
        trigger_time=0.0,
        duration=100.0,
        origin_id="c1",
        dest_id="c2",
        multiplier=1.8,
    )
    for inc in cong.to_simulated_incidents():
        adapter.add_incident(inc)

    reoptimizer = DynamicReoptimizer(test_fleet_problem, cost_oracle=oracle)
    tiered_sol, tiered_rec = reoptimizer.reoptimize(initial_fleet_solution, current_time=0.0, event=cong)

    restart_sol, restart_ms = full_restart_baseline(
        problem=test_fleet_problem,
        current_solution=initial_fleet_solution,
        cost_oracle=oracle,
        current_time=0.0,
        max_iters=30,
        particles=15,
    )

    val_tiered = validate_solution(test_fleet_problem, tiered_sol, cost_oracle=oracle)
    val_restart = validate_solution(test_fleet_problem, restart_sol, cost_oracle=oracle)

    assert val_tiered.is_valid is True
    assert val_restart.is_valid is True
    # Tiered should have logged real wall-clock execution time > 0
    assert tiered_rec.wall_clock_time_ms > 0.0
    assert restart_ms > 0.0


def test_reoptimizer_route_cost_warns_on_unregistered_vehicle(
    test_fleet_problem: Problem, caplog: pytest.LogCaptureFixture
) -> None:
    """Verify that reoptimizer logs an explicit warning when a route vehicle ID is missing from problem.vehicles."""
    import logging
    reopt = DynamicReoptimizer(test_fleet_problem)
    unregistered_route = Route(
        vehicle_id="unknown_veh_99",
        stop_ids=["c1"],
        arrival_times=[10.0],
        load=5.0,
        distance=20.0,
        duration=25.0,
    )
    with caplog.at_level(logging.WARNING):
        cost = reopt._calc_route_cost(unregistered_route)
    assert "unknown_veh_99' not found in problem.vehicles" in caplog.text
    assert cost == 20.0 * 1.0 + 25.0 * 1.0



=== FILE: tests/dynamic/test_simulator.py ===
"""Unit tests for discrete dynamic fleet simulation in dynamic/simulator.py."""

import pytest

from dynamic.events import (
    EdgeCongestion,
    NewOrder,
    RoadBlockage,
    Scenario,
    VehicleDelay,
    generate_random_scenario,
)
from dynamic.simulator import (
    FleetSimulator,
    VehicleStatus,
)
from routing.cost_oracle import EuclideanOracle, SimulatedEventAdapter, TimeOfDayProfileAdapter
from solver.models import Customer, Depot, Problem, Route, Solution, Vehicle


@pytest.fixture
def line_problem() -> Problem:
    """Problem with collinear stops along X axis for easy coordinate verification.

    Depot at (0, 0)
    c1 at (10, 0): ready=15, due=100, service=10
    c2 at (20, 0): ready=20, due=100, service=10
    """
    depot = Depot(id="depot", lat=0.0, lon=0.0, ready_time=0.0, due_time=1000.0)
    c1 = Customer(
        id="c1",
        lat=10.0,
        lon=0.0,
        demand=5.0,
        ready_time=15.0,
        due_time=100.0,
        service_time=10.0,
    )
    c2 = Customer(
        id="c2",
        lat=20.0,
        lon=0.0,
        demand=5.0,
        ready_time=20.0,
        due_time=100.0,
        service_time=10.0,
    )
    vehicles = [Vehicle(id="v1", capacity=50.0)]
    return Problem(id="line_prob", depot=depot, customers=[c1, c2], vehicles=vehicles, coordinate_system="planar")


@pytest.fixture
def line_solution() -> Solution:
    """Solution with single route visiting [c1, c2]."""
    route = Route(
        vehicle_id="v1",
        stop_ids=["c1", "c2"],
        arrival_times=[10.0, 35.0],
        load=10.0,
        distance=40.0,
        duration=65.0,
    )
    return Solution(
        routes=[route],
        total_cost=40.0,
        is_feasible=True,
    )


def test_simulator_basic_lifecycle(line_problem: Problem, line_solution: Solution):
    """Verify state transitions: EN_ROUTE -> WAITING -> SERVICING -> EN_ROUTE -> COMPLETED."""
    oracle = EuclideanOracle(base_speed_mps=1.0, use_haversine=False)
    sim = FleetSimulator(problem=line_problem, solution=line_solution, cost_oracle=oracle, start_time=0.0)

    # Initial state at t=0
    v = sim.vehicles["v1"]
    assert v.status == VehicleStatus.EN_ROUTE
    assert v.current_leg_dest_id == "c1"
    assert v.leg_departure_time == 0.0
    assert v.leg_arrival_time == pytest.approx(10.0)

    # Step to t=5 (midway to c1)
    snap_5 = sim.step(5.0)
    v5 = snap_5.vehicle_states["v1"]
    assert v5.status == VehicleStatus.EN_ROUTE
    assert v5.current_coords[0] == pytest.approx(5.0)  # half way between 0 and 10

    # Step to t=10 (arrives at c1; ready_time is 15 so enters WAITING)
    snap_10 = sim.step(5.0)
    v10 = snap_10.vehicle_states["v1"]
    assert v10.status == VehicleStatus.WAITING
    assert v10.current_coords[0] == pytest.approx(10.0)

    # Step to t=15 (c1 ready_time reached -> starts SERVICING)
    snap_15 = sim.step(5.0)
    v15 = snap_15.vehicle_states["v1"]
    assert v15.status == VehicleStatus.SERVICING
    assert v15.service_end_time == pytest.approx(25.0)

    # Step to t=25 (service finished -> departs to c2)
    snap_25 = sim.step(10.0)
    v25 = snap_25.vehicle_states["v1"]
    assert "c1" in v25.visited_stops
    assert v25.current_leg_dest_id == "c2"
    assert v25.status == VehicleStatus.EN_ROUTE
    assert v25.leg_arrival_time == pytest.approx(35.0)  # dist from c1(10,0) to c2(20,0) is 10

    # Run to completion
    snaps = sim.run_to_completion(step_size=5.0)
    final_v = snaps[-1].vehicle_states["v1"]
    assert final_v.status == VehicleStatus.COMPLETED
    assert final_v.visited_stops == ["c1", "c2"]
    assert snaps[-1].all_completed is True


def test_simulator_vehicle_delay_shifts_arrival(line_problem: Problem, line_solution: Solution):
    """Verify injecting a VehicleDelay shifts the vehicle's arrival time accurately."""
    oracle = EuclideanOracle(base_speed_mps=1.0, use_haversine=False)
    sim = FleetSimulator(problem=line_problem, solution=line_solution, cost_oracle=oracle, start_time=0.0)

    # En route to c1 with initial arrival at t=10
    assert sim.vehicles["v1"].leg_arrival_time == pytest.approx(10.0)

    # Apply 15s unexpected vehicle delay at t=2
    sim.step(2.0)
    sim.apply_event(
        VehicleDelay(
            event_id="delay_test",
            trigger_time=2.0,
            vehicle_id="v1",
            delay_seconds=15.0,
        )
    )

    # New arrival at c1 should be 10 + 15 = 25
    assert sim.vehicles["v1"].leg_arrival_time == pytest.approx(25.0)
    assert sim.vehicles["v1"].delay_seconds == pytest.approx(15.0)

    # At t=10, vehicle is still EN_ROUTE (has not arrived due to delay)
    snap_10 = sim.step(8.0)
    assert snap_10.vehicle_states["v1"].status == VehicleStatus.EN_ROUTE


def test_simulator_edge_congestion_slowdown(line_problem: Problem, line_solution: Solution):
    """Verify EdgeCongestion modulates traffic adapter and increases leg travel time."""
    adapter = SimulatedEventAdapter(base_adapter=TimeOfDayProfileAdapter({h: 1.0 for h in range(24)}))
    oracle = EuclideanOracle(base_speed_mps=1.0, use_haversine=False, traffic_adapter=adapter)
    sim = FleetSimulator(problem=line_problem, solution=line_solution, cost_oracle=oracle, traffic_adapter=adapter)

    # Advance until c1 service finishes at t=25
    sim.run_until(target_time=24.0, step_size=6.0)

    # Apply EdgeCongestion on (c1, c2) with multiplier 2.0 (travel time doubles from 10 to 20)
    cong = EdgeCongestion(
        event_id="cong_c1_c2",
        trigger_time=24.0,
        duration=100.0,
        origin_id="c1",
        dest_id="c2",
        multiplier=2.0,
    )
    sim.apply_event(cong)

    # Step to t=25: c1 service completes, vehicle departs to c2 under congested conditions
    snap_25 = sim.step(1.0)
    v25 = snap_25.vehicle_states["v1"]
    assert v25.current_leg_dest_id == "c2"
    # Nominal travel time was 10.0; with multiplier 2.0, it takes 20.0 -> arrival at 25 + 20 = 45!
    assert v25.leg_arrival_time == pytest.approx(45.0)


def test_simulator_new_order_collection(line_problem: Problem, line_solution: Solution):
    """Verify NewOrder appears in pending_orders snapshot."""
    oracle = EuclideanOracle(base_speed_mps=1.0, use_haversine=False)
    new_cust = Customer(
        id="c_urgent",
        lat=15.0,
        lon=5.0,
        demand=3.0,
        ready_time=50.0,
        due_time=120.0,
        service_time=10.0,
    )
    scenario = Scenario(
        id="scen_new_order",
        start_time=0.0,
        duration=300.0,
        events=[
            NewOrder(event_id="ord_dyn_1", trigger_time=10.0, customer=new_cust)
        ],
    )
    sim = FleetSimulator(problem=line_problem, solution=line_solution, cost_oracle=oracle, scenario=scenario)

    # Before trigger time: no pending orders
    snap_5 = sim.step(5.0)
    assert len(snap_5.pending_orders) == 0

    # At trigger time: new order is captured
    snap_10 = sim.step(5.0)
    assert len(snap_10.pending_orders) == 1
    assert snap_10.pending_orders[0].id == "c_urgent"


def test_deterministic_scenario_simulation(line_problem: Problem, line_solution: Solution):
    """Verify identical random scenario produces bitwise identical simulation trajectories."""
    scenario = generate_random_scenario(
        line_problem,
        num_events=3,
        seed=777,
        start_time=0.0,
        time_horizon=100.0,
    )

    oracle1 = EuclideanOracle(base_speed_mps=1.0, use_haversine=False)
    sim1 = FleetSimulator(problem=line_problem, solution=line_solution, cost_oracle=oracle1, scenario=scenario)
    snaps1 = sim1.run_to_completion(step_size=5.0)

    oracle2 = EuclideanOracle(base_speed_mps=1.0, use_haversine=False)
    sim2 = FleetSimulator(problem=line_problem, solution=line_solution, cost_oracle=oracle2, scenario=scenario)
    snaps2 = sim2.run_to_completion(step_size=5.0)

    assert len(snaps1) == len(snaps2)
    for s1, s2 in zip(snaps1, snaps2):
        assert s1.timestamp == s2.timestamp
        assert s1.active_events == s2.active_events
        assert s1.all_completed == s2.all_completed
        for v_id in s1.vehicle_states:
            st1 = s1.vehicle_states[v_id]
            st2 = s2.vehicle_states[v_id]
            assert st1.status == st2.status
            assert st1.current_coords == st2.current_coords
            assert st1.visited_stops == st2.visited_stops


def test_simulator_road_blockage_and_clearance(line_problem: Problem, line_solution: Solution):
    """Verify RoadBlockage stalls departure until blockage concludes."""
    adapter = SimulatedEventAdapter(base_adapter=TimeOfDayProfileAdapter({h: 1.0 for h in range(24)}))
    oracle = EuclideanOracle(base_speed_mps=1.0, use_haversine=False, traffic_adapter=adapter)
    sim = FleetSimulator(problem=line_problem, solution=line_solution, cost_oracle=oracle, traffic_adapter=adapter)

    # Reach c1 service finish at t=25
    sim.run_until(target_time=24.0, step_size=6.0)

    # Apply RoadBlockage from t=24 to t=60 on edge (c1, c2)
    blockage = RoadBlockage(
        event_id="block_c1_c2",
        trigger_time=24.0,
        duration=36.0,  # ends at t=60
        origin_id="c1",
        dest_id="c2",
    )
    sim.apply_event(blockage)

    # At t=25: vehicle attempts to depart c1 to c2. Edge is blocked!
    sim.step(1.0)
    v = sim.vehicles["v1"]
    assert v.leg_arrival_time == float("inf")

    # Fast forward to t=59: still blocked
    sim.run_until(target_time=59.0, step_size=10.0)
    assert sim.vehicles["v1"].status == VehicleStatus.EN_ROUTE
    assert sim.vehicles["v1"].leg_arrival_time == float("inf")

    # At t=61: blockage expires! Vehicle arrival is rescheduled
    # Note: simulate clearing by removing event
    sim.remove_event("block_c1_c2")
    # Travel time is evaluated
    c1 = line_problem.get_customer("c1")
    c2 = line_problem.get_customer("c2")
    leg_t = oracle.travel_time(c1, c2, 61.0)
    assert leg_t == pytest.approx(10.0)


def test_simulator_servicing_delay(line_problem: Problem, line_solution: Solution):
    """Verify VehicleDelay injected during SERVICING extends service_end_time."""
    oracle = EuclideanOracle(base_speed_mps=1.0, use_haversine=False)
    sim = FleetSimulator(problem=line_problem, solution=line_solution, cost_oracle=oracle)

    # Advance to t=15 where c1 service begins (ends at t=25)
    sim.run_until(target_time=15.0, step_size=5.0)
    v15 = sim.vehicles["v1"]
    assert v15.status == VehicleStatus.SERVICING
    assert v15.service_end_time == pytest.approx(25.0)

    # Inject a 20s delay during service
    sim.apply_event(
        VehicleDelay(
            event_id="cust_delay_1",
            trigger_time=15.0,
            vehicle_id="v1",
            delay_seconds=20.0,
        )
    )

    # Service end time is extended to 25 + 20 = 45!
    assert sim.vehicles["v1"].service_end_time == pytest.approx(45.0)
    assert sim.vehicles["v1"].delay_seconds == pytest.approx(20.0)

    # Step to t=30: vehicle is still SERVICING
    snap_30 = sim.step(15.0)
    assert snap_30.vehicle_states["v1"].status == VehicleStatus.SERVICING

    # Step to t=45: service now completes
    snap_45 = sim.step(15.0)
    assert "c1" in snap_45.vehicle_states["v1"].visited_stops


def test_simulator_multi_vehicle_fleet():
    """Verify concurrent simulation of multiple vehicles across distinct routes."""
    depot = Depot(id="depot", lat=0.0, lon=0.0, ready_time=0.0, due_time=1000.0)
    c1 = Customer(id="c1", lat=10.0, lon=0.0, demand=5.0, ready_time=0.0, due_time=100.0, service_time=5.0)
    c2 = Customer(id="c2", lat=0.0, lon=10.0, demand=5.0, ready_time=0.0, due_time=100.0, service_time=5.0)
    vehicles = [Vehicle(id="v1", capacity=50.0), Vehicle(id="v2", capacity=50.0)]
    prob = Problem(id="multi_prob", depot=depot, customers=[c1, c2], vehicles=vehicles, coordinate_system="planar")

    sol = Solution(
        routes=[
            Route(vehicle_id="v1", stop_ids=["c1"], arrival_times=[10.0], load=5.0, distance=20.0, duration=25.0),
            Route(vehicle_id="v2", stop_ids=["c2"], arrival_times=[10.0], load=5.0, distance=20.0, duration=25.0),
        ],
        total_cost=40.0,
        is_feasible=True,
    )

    oracle = EuclideanOracle(base_speed_mps=1.0, use_haversine=False)
    sim = FleetSimulator(problem=prob, solution=sol, cost_oracle=oracle)

    assert len(sim.vehicles) == 2
    assert sim.vehicles["v1"].current_leg_dest_id == "c1"
    assert sim.vehicles["v2"].current_leg_dest_id == "c2"

    snaps = sim.run_to_completion(step_size=5.0)
    assert snaps[-1].all_completed is True
    assert snaps[-1].vehicle_states["v1"].status == VehicleStatus.COMPLETED
    assert snaps[-1].vehicle_states["v2"].status == VehicleStatus.COMPLETED
    assert snaps[-1].vehicle_states["v1"].visited_stops == ["c1"]
    assert snaps[-1].vehicle_states["v2"].visited_stops == ["c2"]

"""Unit tests for Route.it Cost Oracle, Traffic Adapters, and Caching Subsystem.

Verifies:
1. Abstract and Euclidean cost oracle logic.
2. InMemoryLRUCache and SQLiteCache behavior, time bucketing, and hit/miss metrics.
3. TrafficAdapter hierarchy (TimeOfDayProfileAdapter, SimulatedEventAdapter, ProviderAdapter).
4. Time-dependent sequential route evaluation where stop arrival depends on previous departure.
5. ValhallaOracle chunking and error handling with a mocked HTTP client (no network in regular unit tests).
6. Optional integration test marked with @pytest.mark.integration.
"""

    CacheStats,
    CostCache,

)

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# 1. Location Coercion Tests
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def test_to_location_from_various_inputs() -> None:
    """Verify to_location parses Location, Depot, Customer, dict, and tuple."""
    loc1 = Location(id="loc1", lat=22.3, lon=73.2)
    assert to_location(loc1) == loc1

    depot = Depot(id="depot_0", lat=22.3108, lon=73.1811)
    loc_depot = to_location(depot)
    assert loc_depot.id == "depot_0"
    assert loc_depot.lat == pytest.approx(22.3108)
    assert loc_depot.lon == pytest.approx(73.1811)

    customer = Customer(
        id="c1",
        lat=22.3129,
        lon=73.1906,
        demand=5.0,
        ready_time=0.0,
        due_time=100.0,
        service_time=10.0,
    )
    loc_cust = to_location(customer)
    assert loc_cust.id == "c1"
    assert loc_cust.lat == pytest.approx(22.3129)

    dict_loc = to_location({"id": "d1", "lat": 22.0, "lon": 73.0})
    assert dict_loc.id == "d1"

    tuple_loc = to_location(("t1", 22.1, 73.1))
    assert tuple_loc.id == "t1"

    with pytest.raises(TypeError):
        to_location(12345)


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# 2. Euclidean Oracle Tests
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def test_euclidean_oracle_planar_and_haversine() -> None:
    """Verify distance and duration calculation under planar and haversine modes."""
    # Haversine in Vadodara (~1 km between station and Sayaji Baug)
    p1 = Location(id="stn", lat=22.3108, lon=73.1811)
    p2 = Location(id="park", lat=22.3129, lon=73.1906)

    oracle = EuclideanOracle(base_speed_mps=10.0, use_haversine=True)
    dist = oracle.distance(p1, p2)
    assert 900.0 < dist < 1200.0  # Approx 1 km

    time_sec = oracle.travel_time(p1, p2, depart_time=0.0)
    assert time_sec == pytest.approx(dist / 10.0)

    # Self distance is 0
    assert oracle.distance(p1, p1) == 0.0
    assert oracle.travel_time(p1, p1) == 0.0


def test_euclidean_oracle_matrix_generation() -> None:
    """Verify EuclideanOracle matrix computes correct NxN matrices and unpacks."""
    points = [
        Location(id="p0", lat=22.310, lon=73.180),
        Location(id="p1", lat=22.320, lon=73.180),
        Location(id="p2", lat=22.310, lon=73.190),
    ]
    oracle = EuclideanOracle(base_speed_mps=10.0)
    result = oracle.matrix(points, depart_time=0.0)

    assert len(result.durations) == 3
    assert len(result.distances) == 3

    # Check zero diagonal
    for i in range(3):
        assert result.durations[i][i] == 0.0
        assert result.distances[i][i] == 0.0

    # Symmetry
    assert result.distances[0][1] == pytest.approx(result.distances[1][0])
    assert result.durations[0][2] == pytest.approx(result.durations[2][0])

    # Unpack support
    durs, dists = oracle.matrix(points)
    assert durs == result.durations
    assert dists == result.distances


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# 3. Caching Subsystem Tests
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def test_in_memory_lru_cache() -> None:
    """Verify LRU cache stores, retrieves, respects max size, and tracks stats."""
    cache = InMemoryLRUCache(max_size=3, bucket_size_seconds=900.0)

    # Time bucket check: 0s -> 0, 899s -> 0, 901s -> 1
    assert cache.get_time_bucket(0.0) == 0
    assert cache.get_time_bucket(899.9) == 0
    assert cache.get_time_bucket(900.0) == 1

    # Insert items
    cache.set("a", "b", 0, 100.0, 1000.0)
    cache.set("b", "c", 0, 200.0, 2000.0)
    cache.set("c", "d", 0, 300.0, 3000.0)

    # Lookup hit
    res = cache.get("a", "b", 0)
    assert res == (100.0, 1000.0)
    assert cache.stats.hits == 1

    # Lookup miss (wrong time bucket or unknown pair)
    assert cache.get("a", "b", 1) is None
    assert cache.get("x", "y", 0) is None
    assert cache.stats.misses == 2
    assert cache.stats.hit_ratio == pytest.approx(1 / 3)

    # Eviction test: Add 4th item, oldest ("b", "c") should be evicted because ("a", "b") was recently accessed
    cache.set("d", "e", 0, 400.0, 4000.0)
    assert cache.get("b", "c", 0) is None  # Evicted
    assert cache.get("a", "b", 0) == (100.0, 1000.0)  # Preserved

    cache.clear()
    assert cache.stats.hits == 0
    assert cache.stats.misses == 0
    assert cache.get("a", "b", 0) is None


def test_sqlite_cache() -> None:
    """Verify SQLiteCache persistence and lookup semantics."""
    cache = SQLiteCache(db_path=":memory:", bucket_size_seconds=600.0)

    cache.set("orig1", "dest1", 5, 45.0, 500.0)
    assert cache.get("orig1", "dest1", 5) == (45.0, 500.0)
    assert cache.stats.hits == 1

    # Miss
    assert cache.get("orig1", "dest1", 6) is None
    assert cache.stats.misses == 1

    # Overwrite / update
    cache.set("orig1", "dest1", 5, 55.0, 500.0)
    assert cache.get("orig1", "dest1", 5) == (55.0, 500.0)

    cache.clear()
    assert cache.get("orig1", "dest1", 5) is None


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# 4. Traffic Adapters Tests
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def test_time_of_day_profile_adapter() -> None:
    """Verify simulated time-of-day speed multipliers (Ichoua et al. 2003 model)."""
    adapter = TimeOfDayProfileAdapter()
    p1 = Location(id="p1", lat=22.3, lon=73.2)
    p2 = Location(id="p2", lat=22.4, lon=73.3)

    # 02:00 AM (night) -> 1.15
    night_time = 2 * 3600.0
    assert adapter.get_speed_multiplier(p1, p2, night_time) == pytest.approx(1.15)

    # 08:30 AM (morning rush) -> 0.65
    rush_am = 8.5 * 3600.0
    assert adapter.get_speed_multiplier(p1, p2, rush_am) == pytest.approx(0.65)

    # 17:00 PM (evening rush) -> 0.60
    rush_pm = 17 * 3600.0
    assert adapter.get_speed_multiplier(p1, p2, rush_pm) == pytest.approx(0.60)

    # Custom profile validation
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        TimeOfDayProfileAdapter({25: 1.0})
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        TimeOfDayProfileAdapter({10: -0.5})


def test_simulated_event_adapter_slowdown_and_blockage() -> None:
    """Verify runtime incident injection with slowdowns and blockages."""
    event_adapter = SimulatedEventAdapter()
    p1 = Location(id="orig", lat=22.310, lon=73.180)
    p2 = Location(id="dest", lat=22.320, lon=73.180)

    # Normal time-of-day multiplier at 12:00 PM is 0.90
    noon = 12 * 3600.0
    assert event_adapter.get_speed_multiplier(p1, p2, noon) == pytest.approx(0.90)

    # Add 80% slowdown incident (multiplier 0.2) between 11:30 and 13:00 on this OD pair
    incident = SimulatedIncident(
        event_id="inc_01",
        speed_multiplier=0.2,
        start_time=11.5 * 3600.0,
        end_time=13.0 * 3600.0,
        origin_id="orig",
        dest_id="dest",
        description="Pothole repair on MG Road",
    )
    event_adapter.add_incident(incident)

    # Within incident window -> multiplier drops to 0.2
    assert event_adapter.get_speed_multiplier(p1, p2, noon) == pytest.approx(0.20)

    # Non-matching pair -> unaffected
    p3 = Location(id="other", lat=22.350, lon=73.200)
    assert event_adapter.get_speed_multiplier(p1, p3, noon) == pytest.approx(0.90)

    # After incident end (14:00 PM) -> returns to normal midday
    assert event_adapter.get_speed_multiplier(p1, p2, 14.0 * 3600.0) == pytest.approx(0.90)

    # Test full blockage (multiplier 0.0)
    blockage = SimulatedIncident(
        event_id="inc_block",
        speed_multiplier=0.0,
        start_time=noon,
        end_time=noon + 1800.0,
        origin_id="orig",
        dest_id="dest",
    )
    event_adapter.add_incident(blockage)
    assert event_adapter.get_speed_multiplier(p1, p2, noon + 60.0) == 0.0

    # Under oracle, blockage yields infinite travel time
    oracle = EuclideanOracle(traffic_adapter=event_adapter)
    assert oracle.travel_time(p1, p2, depart_time=noon + 60.0) == float("inf")

    # Remove incident
    event_adapter.remove_incident("inc_block")
    assert oracle.travel_time(p1, p2, depart_time=noon + 60.0) < float("inf")


def test_provider_adapter_stub() -> None:
    """Verify ProviderAdapter logs notice and raises if unintegrated."""
    stub = ProviderAdapter(provider_name="tomtom")
    p1 = Location(id="p1", lat=22.0, lon=73.0)
    p2 = Location(id="p2", lat=22.1, lon=73.1)

    # Without api key -> returns 1.0 baseline
    assert stub.get_speed_multiplier(p1, p2, 0.0) == 1.0

    # With api key -> raises NotImplementedError documenting live connection requirement
    live_stub = ProviderAdapter(provider_name="google_routes", api_key="secret_key")
    with pytest.raises(NotImplementedError):
        live_stub.get_speed_multiplier(p1, p2, 0.0)


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# 5. Time-Dependent Route Evaluation Tests
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def test_evaluate_route_sequential_arrival_chain() -> None:
    """Verify that arrival at stop k depends on actual departure from stop k-1."""
    # 3 stops: depot (0.0, 0.0), stop 1 (1.0, 0.0), stop 2 (2.0, 0.0)
    stops = [
        Location(id="depot", lat=0.0, lon=0.0),
        Location(id="stop1", lat=1.0, lon=0.0),
        Location(id="stop2", lat=2.0, lon=0.0),
    ]
    # Speed 1.0 unit/s -> 1 unit = 1 sec travel time per leg
    oracle = EuclideanOracle(base_speed_mps=1.0, use_haversine=False)


    service_times = [0.0, 5.0, 10.0]  # depot: 0, stop1: 5s, stop2: 10s
    ready_times = [0.0, 3.0, 0.0]     # stop1 ready at t=3s

    # Start at t=0.0
    timeline = oracle.evaluate_route(
        stops,
        start_time=0.0,
        service_times=service_times,
        ready_times=ready_times,
    )

    assert len(timeline.stops) == 3

    # Depot: departs at 0.0
    st0 = timeline.stops[0]
    assert st0.arrival_time == 0.0
    assert st0.departure_time == 0.0

    # Stop 1: leg time is 1.0s. Arrives at 1.0s.
    # Ready time is 3.0s -> wait time is 2.0s!
    # Service starts at 3.0s, takes 5.0s -> Departs at 8.0s!
    st1 = timeline.stops[1]
    assert st1.travel_time_from_prev == pytest.approx(1.0)
    assert st1.arrival_time == pytest.approx(1.0)
    assert st1.wait_time == pytest.approx(2.0)
    assert st1.service_start_time == pytest.approx(3.0)
    assert st1.departure_time == pytest.approx(8.0)

    # Stop 2: leg time is 1.0s. Departs stop 1 at 8.0s -> Arrives at 9.0s!
    # Service takes 10.0s -> Departs at 19.0s.
    st2 = timeline.stops[2]
    assert st2.arrival_time == pytest.approx(9.0)
    assert st2.departure_time == pytest.approx(19.0)

    assert timeline.total_travel_time == pytest.approx(2.0)
    assert timeline.total_duration == pytest.approx(19.0)
    assert timeline.total_wait_time == pytest.approx(2.0)


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# 6. Valhalla Oracle Tests (Mocked Network & Chunking)
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def test_valhalla_oracle_single_query_mocked() -> None:
    """Verify ValhallaOracle single OD query parses time and distance properly."""
    mock_resp = MagicMock()
    mock_resp.status = 200
    mock_resp.read.return_value = json.dumps({
        "sources_to_targets": [
            [{"time": 150.0, "distance": 2.5}]  # 2.5 km -> 2500 m
        ]
    }).encode("utf-8")
    mock_resp.__enter__.return_value = mock_resp

    p1 = Location(id="s1", lat=22.31, lon=73.18)
    p2 = Location(id="s2", lat=22.32, lon=73.19)

    oracle = ValhallaOracle(base_url="http://localhost:8002")

    with patch("urllib.request.urlopen", return_value=mock_resp):
        time_sec = oracle.travel_time(p1, p2)
        dist_m = oracle.distance(p1, p2)

    assert time_sec == 150.0
    assert dist_m == 2500.0


def test_valhalla_oracle_chunked_matrix() -> None:
    """Verify ValhallaOracle breaks 4x4 matrix into 2x2 blocks when chunk_size=2."""
    points = [
        Location(id=f"loc_{i}", lat=22.30 + i * 0.01, lon=73.18 + i * 0.01)
        for i in range(4)
    ]

    cache = InMemoryLRUCache()
    oracle = ValhallaOracle(
        base_url="http://localhost:8002",
        chunk_size=2,  # Forces 4 submatrix queries: (0..1, 0..1), (0..1, 2..3), (2..3, 0..1), (2..3, 2..3)
        cache=cache,
    )

    def mock_urlopen(req, timeout):
        payload = json.loads(req.data.decode("utf-8"))
        num_src = len(payload["sources"])
        num_tgt = len(payload["targets"])
        assert num_src <= 2
        assert num_tgt <= 2

        # Return mock block matrix
        block = []
        for s in payload["sources"]:
            row = []
            for t in payload["targets"]:
                row.append({"time": 60.0, "distance": 1.0})
            block.append(row)

        resp = MagicMock()
        resp.status = 200
        resp.read.return_value = json.dumps({"sources_to_targets": block}).encode("utf-8")
        resp.__enter__.return_value = resp
        return resp

    with patch("urllib.request.urlopen", side_effect=mock_urlopen) as mock_call:
        res = oracle.matrix(points, depart_time=100.0)
        # Should have made 4 calls (2 chunks for src x 2 chunks for tgt)
        assert mock_call.call_count == 4

    assert len(res.durations) == 4
    assert len(res.distances) == 4

    # Diagonal is zero
    for i in range(4):
        assert res.durations[i][i] == 0.0
        assert res.distances[i][i] == 0.0

    # Off-diagonal
    assert res.durations[0][1] == 60.0
    assert res.distances[0][1] == 1000.0  # 1.0 km -> 1000 m

    # Second call with the same depart_time should be 100% cache hits (no urlopen calls)
    with patch("urllib.request.urlopen") as mock_second_call:
        res2 = oracle.matrix(points, depart_time=100.0)
        assert mock_second_call.call_count == 0
        assert res2.durations == res.durations


def test_valhalla_oracle_connection_error() -> None:
    """Verify ValhallaOracle raises ConnectionError when server is unreachable."""
    oracle = ValhallaOracle(base_url="http://127.0.0.1:59999", timeout=0.1)
    p1 = Location(id="s1", lat=22.3, lon=73.1)
    p2 = Location(id="s2", lat=22.4, lon=73.2)

    with pytest.raises(ConnectionError) as exc_info:
        oracle.travel_time(p1, p2)

    assert "Failed to query Valhalla matrix endpoint" in str(exc_info.value)


def test_polyline_encode_decode_roundtrip() -> None:
    """Verify polyline encoding and decoding round-trip preserves precision."""
    original = [(22.3108, 73.1811), (22.3129, 73.1906), (22.3250, 73.2010)]
    encoded = encode_polyline(original, precision=1e6)
    decoded = decode_polyline(encoded, precision=1e6)
    assert len(decoded) == len(original)
    for (lat1, lon1), (lat2, lon2) in zip(original, decoded):
        assert lat1 == pytest.approx(lat2, abs=1e-5)
        assert lon1 == pytest.approx(lon2, abs=1e-5)


def test_valhalla_oracle_route_mocked() -> None:
    """Verify ValhallaOracle.route parses trip geometry and returns GeoJSON coordinates."""
    coords = [(22.3108, 73.1811), (22.3120, 73.1850), (22.3129, 73.1906)]
    shape = encode_polyline(coords, precision=1e6)
    mock_resp = MagicMock()
    mock_resp.status = 200
    mock_resp.read.return_value = json.dumps({
        "trip": {
            "summary": {"length": 1.2, "time": 180.0},
            "legs": [{"shape": shape}],
        }
    }).encode("utf-8")
    mock_resp.__enter__.return_value = mock_resp

    oracle = ValhallaOracle(base_url="http://localhost:8002")
    with patch("urllib.request.urlopen", return_value=mock_resp):
        res = oracle.route([Location(id="s", lat=coords[0][0], lon=coords[0][1]),
                           Location(id="t", lat=coords[-1][0], lon=coords[-1][1])])

    assert res["distance_km"] == 1.2
    assert res["time_sec"] == 180.0
    assert len(res["coordinates"]) == 3
    # In GeoJSON: [longitude, latitude]
    assert res["coordinates"][0][0] == pytest.approx(73.1811, abs=1e-5)
    assert res["coordinates"][0][1] == pytest.approx(22.3108, abs=1e-5)


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# 7. Optional Integration Test
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

@pytest.mark.integration
def test_valhalla_live_docker_integration() -> None:
    """Optional live integration test against running Valhalla Docker instance."""
    import urllib.request

    status_url = "http://localhost:8002/status"
    try:
        req = urllib.request.Request(status_url)
        with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=1.0) as resp:
            if resp.status != 200:
                pytest.skip("Valhalla Docker instance not responding with 200")
    except Exception:
        pytest.skip("Local Valhalla service is offline (Docker container not running)")

    # If reached, Valhalla is running live: test real matrix
    p1 = Location(id="stn", lat=22.3108, lon=73.1811)
    p2 = Location(id="park", lat=22.3129, lon=73.1906)

    oracle = ValhallaOracle(base_url="http://localhost:8002")
    tt = oracle.travel_time(p1, p2)
    dist = oracle.distance(p1, p2)

    assert 60.0 < tt < 600.0
    assert 500.0 < dist < 3000.0

"""Unit tests for Valhalla health check utility in routing/healthcheck.py."""

from unittest.mock import MagicMock, patch
import pytest

from routing.healthcheck import check_valhalla_health, main


def test_healthcheck_offline_service() -> None:
    """Verify healthcheck handles unreachable server gracefully."""
    # Port 59999 should not have a service listening
    report = check_valhalla_health(base_url="http://127.0.0.1:59999", timeout=0.5)

    assert report["healthy"] is False
    assert report["error"] is not None
    assert "Failed to connect" in report["error"]


def test_healthcheck_mocked_healthy_service() -> None:
    """Verify healthcheck parsing on successful /status and /route responses."""
    mock_status_resp = MagicMock()
    mock_status_resp.status = 200
    mock_status_resp.read.return_value = b'{"version": "3.4.0", "tileset_last_modified": 1700000000}'
    mock_status_resp.__enter__.return_value = mock_status_resp

    mock_route_resp = MagicMock()
    mock_route_resp.status = 200
    mock_route_resp.read.return_value = b'{"trip": {"summary": {"length": 2.45, "time": 320}}}'
    mock_route_resp.__enter__.return_value = mock_route_resp

    with patch("urllib.request.urlopen", side_effect=[mock_status_resp, mock_route_resp]):
        report = check_valhalla_health("http://localhost:8002", timeout=1.0)

    assert report["healthy"] is True
    assert report["version"] == "3.4.0"
    assert report["tileset_last_modified"] == 1700000000
    assert report["sample_route_km"] == 2.45
    assert report["sample_route_sec"] == 320
    assert report["status_latency_ms"] is not None
    assert report["route_latency_ms"] is not None

    """Verify CLI returns code 1 when Valhalla is offline."""
    exit_code = main(["--url", "http://127.0.0.1:59999", "--timeout", "0.5"])
    assert exit_code == 1


def test_healthcheck_cli_exit_code_healthy() -> None:
    """Verify CLI returns code 0 when Valhalla is online."""
    mock_status_resp = MagicMock()
    mock_status_resp.status = 200
    mock_status_resp.read.return_value = b'{"version": "3.4.0", "tileset_last_modified": 1700000000}'
    mock_status_resp.__enter__.return_value = mock_status_resp

    mock_route_resp = MagicMock()
    mock_route_resp.status = 200
    mock_route_resp.read.return_value = b'{"trip": {"summary": {"length": 2.45, "time": 320}}}'
    mock_route_resp.__enter__.return_value = mock_route_resp

    with patch("urllib.request.urlopen", side_effect=[mock_status_resp, mock_route_resp]):
        exit_code = main(["--url", "http://localhost:8002", "--timeout", "1.0"])

    assert exit_code == 0

=== FILE: tests/solver/test_fleet_and_units_audit.py ===
"""Tests to reproduce and verify fixes for:
A) Fleet size enforcement as a hard limit in Split and Validator.
B) Unit contract consistency.
C) Explicit coordinate_system ("geographic" vs "planar") without auto-detect.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import math
import pytest
from solver.models import Customer, Depot, Problem, Route, Solution, Vehicle
from solver.split import prins_split
from solver.validate import validate_solution
from routing.cost_oracle import EuclideanOracle, Location


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Issue A: Fleet Size as a Hard Limit
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def test_split_enforces_fleet_size_hard_limit_when_not_allowed_extra() -> None:
    """Split must respect max vehicles from problem.vehicles by default.
    
    If demands require 3 routes but only 2 vehicles are in problem.vehicles,
    it must report is_feasible=False and include a fleet_size violation.
    """
    depot = Depot(id="depot", lat=0.0, lon=0.0, ready_time=0.0, due_time=1000.0)
    # 3 customers, each with demand 60. Vehicle capacity 100.
    # Therefore, 3 routes are strictly required!
    customers = [
        Customer(id=f"c{i+1}", lat=float(i+1), lon=0.0, demand=60.0, ready_time=0.0, due_time=1000.0, service_time=5.0)
        for i in range(3)
    ]
    # Only 2 vehicles available in the fleet
    vehicles = [
        Vehicle(id="V1", capacity=100.0),
        Vehicle(id="V2", capacity=100.0),
    ]
    problem = Problem(id="fleet_test", depot=depot, customers=customers, vehicles=vehicles, coordinate_system="planar")
    problem.compute_euclidean_matrices(speed=1.0)

    # Tour visiting customers 1, 2, 3
    tour = [1, 2, 3]

    # Default allow_extra_vehicles=False:
    cost, routes, is_feasible, violations = prins_split(
        problem, tour, allow_extra_vehicles=False
    )

    # Must be marked infeasible because 3 routes > 2 vehicles!
    assert is_feasible is False
    assert "fleet_size" in violations or "max_vehicles" in violations
    fleet_viols = violations.get("fleet_size", []) + violations.get("max_vehicles", [])
    assert any("2" in v for v in fleet_viols)


def test_split_allows_extra_vehicles_when_flag_is_true() -> None:
    """When allow_extra_vehicles=True, split can generate extra routes."""
    depot = Depot(id="depot", lat=0.0, lon=0.0, ready_time=0.0, due_time=1000.0)
    customers = [
        Customer(id=f"c{i+1}", lat=float(i+1), lon=0.0, demand=60.0, ready_time=0.0, due_time=1000.0, service_time=5.0)
        for i in range(3)
    ]
    vehicles = [
        Vehicle(id="V1", capacity=100.0),
        Vehicle(id="V2", capacity=100.0),
    ]
    problem = Problem(id="fleet_test", depot=depot, customers=customers, vehicles=vehicles, coordinate_system="planar")
    problem.compute_euclidean_matrices(speed=1.0)

    tour = [1, 2, 3]
    cost, routes, is_feasible, violations = prins_split(
        problem, tour, allow_extra_vehicles=True
    )

    assert len(routes) == 3
    assert is_feasible is True
    assert "fleet_size" not in violations
    assert "max_vehicles" not in violations


def test_validator_flags_unregistered_vehicle_id() -> None:
    """Validator must flag any route whose vehicle_id is not in problem.vehicles."""
    depot = Depot(id="depot", lat=0.0, lon=0.0)
    customers = [Customer(id="c1", lat=1.0, lon=0.0, demand=10.0, ready_time=0.0, due_time=100.0, service_time=1.0)]
    vehicles = [Vehicle(id="V1", capacity=50.0)]
    problem = Problem(id="val_test", depot=depot, customers=customers, vehicles=vehicles, coordinate_system="planar")
    problem.compute_euclidean_matrices()

    # Solution assigns route to synthetic vehicle "V_EXTRA" not in problem.vehicles
    sol = Solution(
        routes=[Route(vehicle_id="V_EXTRA", stop_ids=["c1"], arrival_times=[1.0], load=10.0, distance=2.0, duration=3.0)],
        total_cost=2.0,
        is_feasible=True,
    )

    res = validate_solution(problem, sol)
    assert res.is_valid is False
    assert "fleet_size" in res.violations
    assert any("V_EXTRA" in msg for msg in res.violations["fleet_size"])


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Issue C: Coordinate System ("geographic" vs "planar")
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def test_problem_coordinate_system_field() -> None:
    """Problem must have coordinate_system field defaulting to 'geographic'."""
    depot = Depot(id="depot", lat=22.3, lon=73.18)
    customers = [Customer(id="c1", lat=22.31, lon=73.19, demand=10.0, ready_time=0.0, due_time=100.0, service_time=1.0)]
    vehicles = [Vehicle(id="v1", capacity=100.0)]
    p_default = Problem(id="p1", depot=depot, customers=customers, vehicles=vehicles)
    assert p_default.coordinate_system == "geographic"

    p_planar = Problem(id="p2", depot=depot, customers=customers, vehicles=vehicles, coordinate_system="planar")
    assert p_planar.coordinate_system == "planar"


def test_euclidean_oracle_no_autodetect() -> None:
    """EuclideanOracle must use explicit coordinate_system instead of auto-detecting.
    
    If coordinates are in [-90, 90] but coordinate_system='planar', it must compute
    planar hypot distance, NOT earth-curvature Haversine distance.
    """
    p1 = Location(id="loc1", lat=10.0, lon=20.0)
    p2 = Location(id="loc2", lat=13.0, lon=24.0)

    # 3-4-5 triangle in degrees: hypot(3, 4) = 5.0
    oracle_planar = EuclideanOracle(coordinate_system="planar", base_speed_mps=1.0)
    dist_planar = oracle_planar.distance(p1, p2)
    assert dist_planar == pytest.approx(5.0, abs=1e-5)

    oracle_geo = EuclideanOracle(coordinate_system="geographic", base_speed_mps=1.0)
    dist_geo = oracle_geo.distance(p1, p2)
    # 5 degrees on Earth is over 500,000 metres!
    assert dist_geo > 500_000.0

"""Unit tests for Route.it dataset loaders and serializers in solver/io.py."""

from pathlib import Path

def test_load_solomon_hand_made_instance(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    """Test parsing a minimal synthetic Solomon instance file."""
    content = """C101_TINY
VEHICLE
NUMBER     CAPACITY
   2         50.0

CUSTOMER
CUST NO.  XCOORD.   YCOORD.    DEMAND   READY TIME  DUE DATE   SERVICE TIME
    0        0.0       0.0       0.0          0.0     500.0            0.0
    1       10.0      10.0      15.0         10.0     100.0           10.0
    2       20.0      20.0      20.0         50.0     150.0           15.0
"""
    file_path = tmp_path / "tiny_solomon.txt"
    file_path.write_text(content, encoding="utf-8")

    problem = load_solomon(file_path)

    assert problem.id == "C101_TINY"
    assert problem.depot.lat == 0.0
    assert problem.depot.lon == 0.0
    assert problem.depot.due_time == 500.0
    assert problem.num_customers == 2
    assert problem.num_vehicles == 2
    assert problem.vehicles[0].capacity == 50.0

    cust1 = problem.get_customer("1")
    assert cust1.demand == 15.0
    assert cust1.ready_time == 10.0
    assert cust1.due_time == 100.0
    assert cust1.service_time == 10.0

    # Ensure Euclidean matrices were computed
    assert problem.distance_matrix is not None
    assert problem.travel_time_matrix is not None
    assert len(problem.distance_matrix) == 3

    assert problem.num_vehicles == 5
    assert problem.vehicles[0].capacity == 100.0

    # Verify depot

    assert problem.depot.lon == 82.0

    # Verify customer 2 demand = 19, coords: x=96, y=44
    cust2 = problem.get_customer("2")
    assert cust2.demand == 19.0
    assert cust2.lat == 44.0
    assert cust2.lon == 96.0

    # Check total demand matches Augerat benchmark specs (sum of demands across nodes 2..32 = 415)
    total_demand = sum(c.demand for c in problem.customers)
    assert total_demand == 415.0

    # Ensure distance matrix computed
    assert problem.distance_matrix is not None

    depot = Depot(lat=10.0, lon=20.0)
    c1 = Customer(id="C1", lat=12.0, lon=22.0, demand=5.0, ready_time=0.0, due_time=100.0, service_time=10.0)
    v1 = Vehicle(id="V1", capacity=30.0)
    original_problem = Problem(id="roundtrip_prob", depot=depot, customers=[c1], vehicles=[v1])
    original_problem.compute_euclidean_matrices()

    json_file = tmp_path / "problem.json"

    assert loaded_problem.depot.lat == 10.0
    assert loaded_problem.num_customers == 1
    assert loaded_problem.distance_matrix is not None
    assert loaded_problem.distance_matrix[0][1] == original_problem.distance_matrix[0][1]

        total_cost=25.0,
        is_feasible=True,
        violations={},
        algorithm="QPSO",
        execution_time_sec=0.045,
        seed=1234,
    )

    json_file = tmp_path / "solution.json"
    save_solution_json(original_solution, json_file)

    loaded_solution = load_solution_json(json_file)
    assert loaded_solution.total_cost == 25.0
    assert loaded_solution.is_feasible is True
    assert loaded_solution.seed == 1234
    assert len(loaded_solution.routes) == 1
    assert loaded_solution.routes[0].stop_ids == ["C1"]


=== FILE: tests/solver/test_local_search.py ===
"""Unit and benchmark tests for local search operators and memetic QPSO."""

import json
from pathlib import Path
import numpy as np
import pytest

from solver.io import load_cvrplib
from solver.local_search import (
    build_neighbour_list,
    flatten_routes,
    inter_route_relocate,
    inter_route_swap,
    intra_route_2opt,
    project_tour_to_keys,
    run_local_search,
    total_routes_cost,
)
from solver.models import Customer, Depot, Problem, Vehicle
from solver.qpso import QPSOConfig, solve_qpso
from solver.split import prins_split


def test_2opt_untangles_crossing() -> None:
    """Verify that intra-route 2-opt detects crossing edges and untangles them."""
    # Unit square: depot (0,0), 1:(0,1), 2:(1,1), 3:(1,0)
    # A crossed tour: depot -> 1 -> 3 -> 2 -> depot
    dist_mat = np.array(
        [
            [0.0, 1.0, 1.414, 1.0],
            [1.0, 0.0, 1.0, 1.414],
            [1.414, 1.0, 0.0, 1.0],
            [1.0, 1.414, 1.0, 0.0],
        ]
    )
    # Route with crossing: 1 -> 3 -> 2
    route = [1, 3, 2]
    initial_cost = dist_mat[0, 1] + dist_mat[1, 3] + dist_mat[3, 2] + dist_mat[2, 0]

    improved = intra_route_2opt(route, dist_mat)
    assert improved is True
    assert route == [1, 2, 3]

    new_cost = dist_mat[0, 1] + dist_mat[1, 2] + dist_mat[2, 3] + dist_mat[3, 0]
    assert new_cost < initial_cost


def test_inter_route_relocate_operator() -> None:
    """Verify that inter-route relocate moves a misplaced customer to a closer route."""
    # Dist matrix with 4 customers
    dist_mat = np.zeros((5, 5))
    for i in range(5):
        for j in range(5):
            dist_mat[i, j] = abs(i - j)

    # Route 1 has customers [1, 2, 4], Route 2 has [3]
    # Customer 4 is adjacent to 3 in index space
    routes = [[1, 2, 4], [3]]
    loads = [30.0, 10.0]
    demands = np.array([0.0, 10.0, 10.0, 10.0, 10.0])
    capacity = 50.0

    initial_cost = total_routes_cost(routes, dist_mat)
    improved = inter_route_relocate(routes, loads, dist_mat, demands, capacity)

    assert improved is True
    new_cost = total_routes_cost(routes, dist_mat)
    assert new_cost <= initial_cost


def test_inter_route_swap_operator() -> None:
    """Verify that inter-route swap exchanges customers when beneficial."""
    # Depot 0 at center (0,0)
    # Cluster A on left: (-10, 0), Cluster B on right: (10, 0)
    # Node 1: (-10, 1), Node 2: (10, 1)
    # Node 3: (-10, -1), Node 4: (10, -1)
    coords = [(0, 0), (-10, 1), (10, 1), (-10, -1), (10, -1)]
    dist_mat = np.zeros((5, 5))
    for i in range(5):
        for j in range(5):
            dist_mat[i, j] = np.hypot(coords[i][0] - coords[j][0], coords[i][1] - coords[j][1])

    # Misplaced: Route 1 has Left (1) and Right (4); Route 2 has Right (2) and Left (3)
    routes = [[1, 4], [2, 3]]
    loads = [20.0, 20.0]
    demands = np.array([0.0, 10.0, 10.0, 10.0, 10.0])
    capacity = 30.0

    initial_cost = total_routes_cost(routes, dist_mat)
    improved = inter_route_swap(routes, loads, dist_mat, demands, capacity)

    assert improved is True
    new_cost = total_routes_cost(routes, dist_mat)
    assert new_cost < initial_cost


def test_project_tour_to_keys() -> None:
    """Verify that projecting an improved tour to random keys yields exact argsort reconstruction."""
    old_keys = np.array([0.8, 0.2, 0.9, 0.1, 0.5])
    # Original argsort + 1: [4, 2, 5, 1, 3]
    improved_tour = [2, 1, 4, 3, 5]

    new_keys = project_tour_to_keys(old_keys, improved_tour)

    # Decoded permutation must match improved_tour exactly
    decoded = list(np.argsort(new_keys) + 1)
    assert decoded == improved_tour
    # The sorted values of new_keys must equal sorted old_keys
    np.testing.assert_allclose(np.sort(new_keys), np.sort(old_keys))


def test_local_search_never_worsens_cost_and_preserves_feasibility() -> None:
    """Verify on A-n32-k5 that composite local search never worsens cost and maintains capacity."""
    problem = load_cvrplib(Path("benchmarks/instances/A-n32-k5.vrp"))
    dist_mat = np.asarray(problem.distance_matrix, dtype=np.float64)
    demands = np.zeros(problem.num_customers + 1, dtype=np.float64)
    for idx, c in enumerate(problem.customers, start=1):
        demands[idx] = c.demand

    capacity = problem.vehicles[0].capacity
    nb_mat = build_neighbour_list(dist_mat, k_nearest=15)

    rng = np.random.default_rng(999)
    # Test on 3 diverse random tours
    for _ in range(3):
        tour = rng.permutation(np.arange(1, problem.num_customers + 1))
        cost, routes_obj, is_feas, _ = prins_split(problem, tour, allow_extra_vehicles=True)
        assert is_feas is True

        route_indices = [
            [problem.get_customer_index(sid) for sid in r.stop_ids]
            for r in routes_obj
        ]
        initial_cost = total_routes_cost(route_indices, dist_mat)

        opt_routes, opt_cost = run_local_search(
            route_indices, dist_mat, demands, capacity, neighbour_matrix=nb_mat
        )

        assert opt_cost <= initial_cost + 1e-7, f"Cost worsened: {initial_cost} -> {opt_cost}"

        # Verify all customers still present exactly once
        flat = flatten_routes(opt_routes)
        assert len(flat) == problem.num_customers
        assert set(flat) == set(range(1, problem.num_customers + 1))

        # Verify capacities respected
        for r in opt_routes:
            load = sum(demands[c] for c in r)
            assert load <= capacity + 1e-7


def test_qpso_plus_ls_benchmark_comparison(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    """Benchmark test comparing Pure QPSO vs Memetic QPSO+LS on A-n32-k5.

    Results are genuinely measured and saved to tmp_path during test runs to ensure
    real verified records in benchmarks/results/ are never overwritten by tests.
    """
    problem = load_cvrplib(Path("benchmarks/instances/A-n32-k5.vrp"))
    fixed_seed = 42
    particles = 30
    iters = 100

    # 1. Pure QPSO (local search disabled)
    cfg_pure = QPSOConfig(
        num_particles=particles,
        max_iters=iters,
        seed=fixed_seed,
        local_search_freq=0,
    )
    sol_pure, hist_pure = solve_qpso(problem, cfg_pure)

    # 2. Memetic QPSO+LS (local search enabled every 20 iters for top 3 particles)
    cfg_memetic = QPSOConfig(
        num_particles=particles,
        max_iters=iters,
        seed=fixed_seed,
        local_search_freq=20,
        local_search_top_k=3,
        use_neighbour_list=True,
    )
    sol_memetic, hist_memetic = solve_qpso(problem, cfg_memetic)

    # Assertions
    assert sol_pure.is_feasible is True
    assert sol_memetic.is_feasible is True
    assert sol_memetic.total_cost <= sol_pure.total_cost, (
        f"Memetic QPSO cost ({sol_memetic.total_cost:.2f}) was worse than Pure QPSO ({sol_pure.total_cost:.2f})"
    )

    # Save benchmark results to tmp_path so real benchmarks/results files are never overwritten
    out_file = tmp_path / "a_n32_k5_ls_comparison.json"

    data = {
        "instance": "A-n32-k5",
        "seed": fixed_seed,
        "particles": particles,
        "iterations": iters,
        "pure_qpso": {
            "cost": float(sol_pure.total_cost),
            "feasible": sol_pure.is_feasible,
            "routes": len(sol_pure.routes),
            "time_sec": float(sol_pure.execution_time_sec),
        },
        "memetic_qpso_ls": {
            "cost": float(sol_memetic.total_cost),
            "feasible": sol_memetic.is_feasible,
            "routes": len(sol_memetic.routes),
            "time_sec": float(sol_memetic.execution_time_sec),
        },
        "improvement_pct": float((sol_pure.total_cost - sol_memetic.total_cost) / sol_pure.total_cost * 100.0),
    }

    with out_file.open("w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        json.dump(data, f, indent=2)

    assert out_file.is_file()

"""Unit tests for Route.it domain models in solver/models.py."""

import pytest
from pydantic import ValidationError

from solver.models import Customer, Depot, Problem, Route, Solution, Vehicle


def test_depot_creation() -> None:
    """Test valid depot initialization and default properties."""
    depot = Depot(id="main_depot", lat=12.9716, lon=77.5946, ready_time=0.0, due_time=1000.0)
    assert depot.id == "main_depot"
    assert depot.lat == 12.9716
    assert depot.lon == 77.5946
    assert depot.ready_time == 0.0
    assert depot.due_time == 1000.0
    assert depot.service_time == 0.0


def test_depot_negative_due_time() -> None:
    """Test depot rejects negative due time."""
    with pytest.raises(ValidationError):
        Depot(lat=0.0, lon=0.0, due_time=-10.0)


def test_customer_creation_and_time_window_validation() -> None:
    """Test customer creation and rejection of invalid time windows."""
    cust = Customer(
        id="C1",
        lat=12.95,
        lon=77.60,
        demand=15.0,
        ready_time=10.0,
        due_time=50.0,
        service_time=5.0,
        priority=2,
    )
    assert cust.id == "C1"
    assert cust.priority == 2
    assert cust.demand == 15.0

    # Inverted time window: ready_time > due_time
    with pytest.raises(ValidationError, match="cannot exceed due_time"):
        Customer(
            id="C2",
            lat=12.95,
            lon=77.60,
            demand=10.0,
            ready_time=60.0,
            due_time=50.0,
            service_time=5.0,
        )


def test_vehicle_creation_and_constraints() -> None:
    """Test vehicle creation and capacity / shift constraint validation."""
    v = Vehicle(id="V1", capacity=100.0, shift_start=0.0, shift_end=480.0)
    assert v.capacity == 100.0
    assert v.shift_end == 480.0

    # Non-positive capacity
    with pytest.raises(ValidationError):
        Vehicle(id="V2", capacity=0.0)

    # Inverted shift window
    with pytest.raises(ValidationError, match="cannot exceed shift_end"):
        Vehicle(id="V3", capacity=50.0, shift_start=500.0, shift_end=200.0)


def test_problem_uniqueness_and_lookups() -> None:
    """Test Problem instance validation for unique IDs and lookup helpers."""
    depot = Depot(lat=0.0, lon=0.0)
    c1 = Customer(id="1", lat=3.0, lon=4.0, demand=10.0, ready_time=0.0, due_time=100.0, service_time=5.0)
    c2 = Customer(id="2", lat=6.0, lon=8.0, demand=20.0, ready_time=0.0, due_time=100.0, service_time=5.0)
    v1 = Vehicle(id="V1", capacity=50.0)

    prob = Problem(id="toy_prob", depot=depot, customers=[c1, c2], vehicles=[v1])
    assert prob.num_customers == 2
    assert prob.num_vehicles == 1
    assert prob.get_customer("1").demand == 10.0
    assert prob.get_customer_index("1") == 1
    assert prob.get_customer_index("2") == 2

    with pytest.raises(KeyError):
        prob.get_customer("999")

    # Duplicate customer IDs should be rejected
    with pytest.raises(ValidationError, match="Customer IDs must be unique"):
        Problem(id="dup", depot=depot, customers=[c1, c1], vehicles=[v1])


def test_problem_euclidean_matrices() -> None:
    """Test calculation of Euclidean distance and travel-time matrices."""
    depot = Depot(lat=0.0, lon=0.0)
    # 3-4-5 right triangle from depot (0,0) to customer (3,4)
    c1 = Customer(id="1", lat=3.0, lon=4.0, demand=10.0, ready_time=0.0, due_time=100.0, service_time=0.0)
    v1 = Vehicle(id="V1", capacity=50.0)

    prob = Problem(id="geom_prob", depot=depot, customers=[c1], vehicles=[v1], coordinate_system="planar")
    assert prob.distance_matrix is None

    prob.compute_euclidean_matrices(speed=2.0)
    assert prob.distance_matrix is not None
    assert prob.travel_time_matrix is not None

    # Distance depot (0) to c1 (1) is 5.0
    assert pytest.approx(prob.distance_matrix[0][1], rel=1e-5) == 5.0
    assert pytest.approx(prob.distance_matrix[1][0], rel=1e-5) == 5.0
    # Travel time with speed=2.0 is 5.0 / 2.0 = 2.5
    assert pytest.approx(prob.travel_time_matrix[0][1], rel=1e-5) == 2.5


def test_route_and_solution_creation() -> None:
    """Test Route and Solution model serialization."""
    route1 = Route(
        vehicle_id="V1",
        stop_ids=["1", "2"],
        arrival_times=[10.0, 25.0],
        load=30.0,
        distance=15.0,
        duration=35.0,
    )
    solution = Solution(
        routes=[route1],
        total_cost=50.0,
        is_feasible=True,
        violations={},
        algorithm="QPSO",
        execution_time_sec=0.123,
        seed=42,
    )
    assert solution.is_feasible is True
    assert len(solution.routes) == 1
    assert solution.routes[0].load == 30.0

    # Verify JSON serialization & deserialization
    json_str = solution.model_dump_json()
    reloaded = Solution.model_validate_json(json_str)
    assert reloaded.total_cost == 50.0
    assert reloaded.routes[0].stop_ids == ["1", "2"]

"""Unit tests for Google OR-Tools routing baseline in solver/ortools_baseline.py."""

from pathlib import Path
import pytest

from solver.io import load_cvrplib, load_solution_json
from solver.models import Customer, Depot, Problem, Vehicle
from solver.ortools_baseline import ORToolsConfig, solve_ortools
from solver.run import main


def test_ortools_hand_crafted() -> None:
    """Verify OR-Tools finds the optimal routes on the 4-customer toy instance."""
    depot = Depot(id="depot", lat=0.0, lon=0.0)
    customers = [
        Customer(id="1", lat=0.0, lon=1.0, demand=4.0, ready_time=0.0, due_time=100.0, service_time=0.0),
        Customer(id="2", lat=0.0, lon=2.0, demand=6.0, ready_time=0.0, due_time=100.0, service_time=0.0),
        Customer(id="3", lat=1.0, lon=0.0, demand=5.0, ready_time=0.0, due_time=100.0, service_time=0.0),
        Customer(id="4", lat=2.0, lon=0.0, demand=5.0, ready_time=0.0, due_time=100.0, service_time=0.0),
    ]
    vehicles = [Vehicle(id=f"V_{i+1}", capacity=10.0) for i in range(4)]
    problem = Problem(id="toy_ortools", depot=depot, customers=customers, vehicles=vehicles, coordinate_system="planar")
    problem.compute_euclidean_matrices()

    cfg = ORToolsConfig(time_limit_sec=2.0)
    solution = solve_ortools(problem, cfg)

    assert solution.is_feasible is True
    assert solution.violations == {}
    assert len(solution.routes) == 2
    assert pytest.approx(solution.total_cost, rel=1e-3) == 8.0

    all_visited = [cust_id for r in solution.routes for cust_id in r.stop_ids]
    assert set(all_visited) == {"1", "2", "3", "4"}


def test_ortools_on_cvrplib_instance() -> None:
    """Verify OR-Tools solves A-n32-k5 respecting capacities and customer coverage."""
    problem = load_cvrplib(Path("benchmarks/instances/A-n32-k5.vrp"))
    cfg = ORToolsConfig(time_limit_sec=5.0)

    solution = solve_ortools(problem, cfg)

    assert solution.is_feasible is True
    assert solution.violations == {}
    assert solution.algorithm == "OR-Tools"
    assert solution.total_cost > 0.0

    visited: list[str] = []
    capacity = problem.vehicles[0].capacity
    for route in solution.routes:
        assert route.load <= capacity + 1e-7, f"Route {route.vehicle_id} exceeded capacity"
        visited.extend(route.stop_ids)

    expected_ids = {c.id for c in problem.customers}
    assert set(visited) == expected_ids
    assert len(visited) == problem.num_customers


def test_ortools_cli_runner(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    """Verify CLI runner with --solver ortools flag."""
    out_file = tmp_path / "ortools_sol.json"
    argv = [
        "--instance",
        "benchmarks/instances/A-n32-k5.vrp",
        "--solver",
        "ortools",
        "--time-limit",
        "3.0",
        "--out",
        str(out_file),
        "--quiet",
    ]

    ret = main(argv)
    assert ret == 0
    assert out_file.is_file()

    sol = load_solution_json(out_file)
    assert sol.algorithm == "OR-Tools"
    assert sol.is_feasible is True
    assert sol.total_cost > 0.0
