"""Unit tests for QPSO, PSO baseline, and CLI runner."""

from pathlib import Path
import pytest

from solver.io import load_cvrplib, load_solution_json
from solver.pso import PSOConfig, solve_pso
from solver.qpso import QPSOConfig, solve_qpso
from solver.run import main


@pytest.fixture
def a_n32_instance() -> Path:
    """Fixture providing path to reference instance A-n32-k5.vrp."""
    p = Path("benchmarks/instances/A-n32-k5.vrp")
    assert p.is_file(), f"Benchmark instance {p} must exist"
    return p


def test_qpso_determinism_with_fixed_seed(a_n32_instance: Path) -> None:
    """Verify that QPSO produces identical trajectories and solutions with a fixed random seed."""
    problem = load_cvrplib(a_n32_instance)
    cfg = QPSOConfig(num_particles=15, max_iters=25, seed=42)

    sol1, hist1 = solve_qpso(problem, cfg)
    sol2, hist2 = solve_qpso(problem, cfg)

    assert pytest.approx(sol1.total_cost, rel=1e-5) == sol2.total_cost
    assert hist1 == hist2
    assert len(sol1.routes) == len(sol2.routes)

    for r1, r2 in zip(sol1.routes, sol2.routes, strict=True):
        assert r1.stop_ids == r2.stop_ids
        assert pytest.approx(r1.load, rel=1e-5) == r2.load
        assert pytest.approx(r1.distance, rel=1e-5) == r2.distance


def test_qpso_cost_non_increasing_for_gbest(a_n32_instance: Path) -> None:
    """Verify that global best cost never increases across iterations."""
    problem = load_cvrplib(a_n32_instance)
    cfg = QPSOConfig(num_particles=15, max_iters=40, seed=123)

    solution, cost_history = solve_qpso(problem, cfg)

    assert len(cost_history) == cfg.max_iters + 1
    for t in range(len(cost_history) - 1):
        assert cost_history[t + 1] <= cost_history[t] + 1e-9, (
            f"gbest cost increased from {cost_history[t]} to {cost_history[t+1]} at iter {t}"
        )

    # Cost at end of run should be <= initial cost
    assert cost_history[-1] <= cost_history[0]


def test_qpso_customer_coverage_and_capacity_satisfaction(a_n32_instance: Path) -> None:
    """Verify all customers are visited once and vehicle capacities are respected."""
    problem = load_cvrplib(a_n32_instance)
    cfg = QPSOConfig(num_particles=15, max_iters=30, seed=777)

    solution, _ = solve_qpso(problem, cfg)

    assert solution.is_feasible is True
    assert solution.violations == {}
    assert solution.total_cost > 0.0

    visited_stops: list[str] = []
    capacity = problem.vehicles[0].capacity

    for route in solution.routes:
        assert route.load <= capacity, f"Route {route.vehicle_id} exceeded capacity {capacity}"
        visited_stops.extend(route.stop_ids)

    expected_ids = {c.id for c in problem.customers}
    assert set(visited_stops) == expected_ids
    assert len(visited_stops) == problem.num_customers


def test_pso_baseline_execution_and_determinism(a_n32_instance: Path) -> None:
    """Verify baseline PSO execution, feasibility, and seed determinism."""
    problem = load_cvrplib(a_n32_instance)
    cfg = PSOConfig(num_particles=15, max_iters=25, seed=999)

    sol1, hist1 = solve_pso(problem, cfg)
    sol2, hist2 = solve_pso(problem, cfg)

    assert sol1.is_feasible is True
    assert pytest.approx(sol1.total_cost, rel=1e-5) == sol2.total_cost
    assert hist1 == hist2

    # Check non-increasing cost property
    for t in range(len(hist1) - 1):
        assert hist1[t + 1] <= hist1[t] + 1e-9


def test_cli_runner_execution(a_n32_instance: Path, tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    """Verify the CLI runner execution and JSON export."""

    assert sol.seed == 1
    assert sol.total_cost > 0.0

    from solver.models import Customer, Depot, Problem, Vehicle

        Customer(id="C2", lat=15.0, lon=0.0, demand=15.0, ready_time=15.0, due_time=60.0, service_time=5.0, priority=1),
        Customer(id="C3", lat=25.0, lon=0.0, demand=15.0, ready_time=25.0, due_time=100.0, service_time=5.0, priority=1),
        Customer(id="C4", lat=0.0, lon=10.0, demand=12.0, ready_time=0.0, due_time=50.0, service_time=5.0, priority=1),
        Customer(id="C5", lat=0.0, lon=20.0, demand=18.0, ready_time=10.0, due_time=80.0, service_time=5.0, priority=2),
    ]
    vehicles = [
        Vehicle(id="V1", capacity=50.0, shift_start=0.0, shift_end=150.0),
        Vehicle(id="V2", capacity=50.0, shift_start=0.0, shift_end=150.0),
    ]
    problem = Problem(id="toy_cvrptw", depot=depot, customers=customers, vehicles=vehicles, coordinate_system="planar")
    problem.compute_euclidean_matrices(speed=1.0)

    cfg = QPSOConfig(
        num_particles=20,
        max_iters=35,
        seed=42,
        adaptive_penalty=True,
        enforce_priority_early_service=True,
        priority_early_ratio=0.6,
        apply_tw_repair=True,
    )

    sol, hist = solve_qpso(problem, cfg)

    assert sol.is_feasible is True
    assert len(sol.violations) == 0
    assert sol.total_cost > 0.0

    # Independent validation verification
    val = validate_solution(
        problem,
        sol,
        enforce_priority_early_service=True,
        priority_early_ratio=0.6,
    )
    assert val.is_valid is True
    assert val.customer_count == 5



=== FILE: tests/solver/test_repair.py ===
"""Unit tests for time-window repair operator in solver/repair.py."""

from __future__ import annotations

import pytest

from solver.models import Customer, Depot, Problem, Route, Solution, Vehicle
from solver.repair import repair_solution_time_windows
from solver.validate import validate_solution


@pytest.fixture
def problem_with_tight_window() -> Problem:
    """Problem where visiting order determines time window feasibility."""
    depot = Depot(id="depot", lat=0.0, lon=0.0, ready_time=0.0, due_time=200.0)
    # C1 is close with early deadline: dist 5, ready 0, due 15, serv 5
    # C2 is at dist 15, ready 0, due 50, serv 5
    # C3 is at dist 25, ready 0, due 80, serv 5
    customers = [
        Customer(id="C1", lat=5.0, lon=0.0, demand=10.0, ready_time=0.0, due_time=15.0, service_time=5.0),
        Customer(id="C2", lat=15.0, lon=0.0, demand=10.0, ready_time=0.0, due_time=50.0, service_time=5.0),
        Customer(id="C3", lat=25.0, lon=0.0, demand=10.0, ready_time=0.0, due_time=80.0, service_time=5.0),
    ]
    vehicles = [Vehicle(id="V1", capacity=50.0)]
    problem = Problem(id="repair_toy", depot=depot, customers=customers, vehicles=vehicles, coordinate_system="planar")
    problem.compute_euclidean_matrices(speed=1.0)
    return problem


def test_repair_operator_fixes_inverted_order(problem_with_tight_window: Problem) -> None:
    """Verify that repair operator reorders C3 -> C2 -> C1 into C1 -> C2 -> C3 to eliminate late arrival."""
    # Intentional late visiting order: C3 -> C2 -> C1
    # Dep -> C3 (arr=25, dep=30) -> C2 (arr=40, dep=45) -> C1 (arr=55 > due 15!)
    late_route = Route(
        vehicle_id="V1",
        stop_ids=["C3", "C2", "C1"],
        arrival_times=[25.0, 40.0, 55.0],
        load=30.0,
        distance=60.0,
        duration=60.0,
    )
    unrepaired_sol = Solution(
        routes=[late_route],
        total_cost=60.0,
        is_feasible=False,
        violations={"time_window": ["C1 late"]},
    )

    repaired_sol = repair_solution_time_windows(problem_with_tight_window, unrepaired_sol)

    assert repaired_sol.is_feasible is True
    assert len(repaired_sol.violations) == 0

    # The customer C1 must now be serviced first (before its tight deadline)
    r = repaired_sol.routes[0]
    assert r.stop_ids[0] == "C1"

    val = validate_solution(problem_with_tight_window, repaired_sol)
    assert val.is_valid is True


def test_repair_operator_noop_on_feasible_solution(problem_with_tight_window: Problem) -> None:
    """Verify repair operator returns unchanged solution when already feasible."""
    feasible_route = Route(
        vehicle_id="V1",
        stop_ids=["C1", "C2", "C3"],
        arrival_times=[5.0, 20.0, 35.0],
        load=30.0,
        distance=50.0,
        duration=65.0,
    )
    sol = Solution(routes=[feasible_route], total_cost=50.0, is_feasible=True)

    repaired = repair_solution_time_windows(problem_with_tight_window, sol)
    assert repaired.is_feasible is True
    assert repaired.routes[0].stop_ids == ["C1", "C2", "C3"]


=== FILE: tests/solver/test_split.py ===
"""Unit tests for Prins Split algorithm in solver/split.py."""

import numpy as np
from pathlib import Path
import pytest

from solver.io import load_cvrplib
from solver.models import Customer, Depot, Problem, Vehicle
from solver.split import prins_split


def test_prins_split_hand_crafted() -> None:
    """Test Prins Split on a 4-customer hand-checkable instance.

    Depot at (0, 0).
    Cust 1: (1, 0), demand 4.
    Cust 2: (2, 0), demand 6.  Route [1, 2] load=10, cost = 1 + 1 + 2 = 4.0
    Cust 3: (0, 1), demand 5.
    Cust 4: (0, 2), demand 5.  Route [3, 4] load=10, cost = 1 + 1 + 2 = 4.0
    Capacity Q = 10.0.
    Optimal split of tour [1, 2, 3, 4] must yield exactly 2 routes with total cost 8.0.
    """
    depot = Depot(id="depot", lat=0.0, lon=0.0)
    customers = [
        Customer(id="1", lat=0.0, lon=1.0, demand=4.0, ready_time=0.0, due_time=100.0, service_time=0.0),
        Customer(id="2", lat=0.0, lon=2.0, demand=6.0, ready_time=0.0, due_time=100.0, service_time=0.0),
        Customer(id="3", lat=1.0, lon=0.0, demand=5.0, ready_time=0.0, due_time=100.0, service_time=0.0),
        Customer(id="4", lat=2.0, lon=0.0, demand=5.0, ready_time=0.0, due_time=100.0, service_time=0.0),
    ]
    vehicles = [Vehicle(id=f"V_{i+1}", capacity=10.0) for i in range(4)]
    problem = Problem(id="toy_split", depot=depot, customers=customers, vehicles=vehicles, coordinate_system="planar")
    problem.compute_euclidean_matrices()

    tour = [1, 2, 3, 4]
    cost, routes, is_feasible, violations = prins_split(problem, tour)

    assert is_feasible is True
    assert violations == {}
    assert len(routes) == 2
    assert pytest.approx(cost, rel=1e-5) == 8.0

    assert routes[0].stop_ids == ["1", "2"]
    assert pytest.approx(routes[0].load, rel=1e-5) == 10.0
    assert pytest.approx(routes[0].distance, rel=1e-5) == 4.0

    assert routes[1].stop_ids == ["3", "4"]
    assert pytest.approx(routes[1].load, rel=1e-5) == 10.0
    assert pytest.approx(routes[1].distance, rel=1e-5) == 4.0


def test_prins_split_customer_coverage_and_capacity_on_cvrp() -> None:
    """Test that Prins Split on A-n32-k5 visits every customer exactly once and obeys capacity."""
    problem = load_cvrplib(Path("benchmarks/instances/A-n32-k5.vrp"))
    n = problem.num_customers

    # Generate a random tour
    rng = np.random.default_rng(123)
    tour = rng.permutation(np.arange(1, n + 1))

    cost, routes, is_feasible, violations = prins_split(problem, tour)

    assert is_feasible is True
    assert violations == {}
    assert cost > 0.0

    visited_stops = []
    for route in routes:
        assert route.load <= problem.vehicles[0].capacity, f"Route {route.vehicle_id} exceeded capacity"
        visited_stops.extend(route.stop_ids)

    # Every customer visited exactly once
    expected_ids = {c.id for c in problem.customers}
    assert set(visited_stops) == expected_ids
    assert len(visited_stops) == n


def test_prins_split_max_vehicles_constraint() -> None:
    """Test that Prins Split detects and flags max-route-count violations when unachievable."""
    depot = Depot(id="depot", lat=0.0, lon=0.0)
    customers = [
        Customer(id="1", lat=0.0, lon=1.0, demand=6.0, ready_time=0.0, due_time=100.0, service_time=0.0),
        Customer(id="2", lat=0.0, lon=2.0, demand=6.0, ready_time=0.0, due_time=100.0, service_time=0.0),
    ]
    # Capacity is 10.0, so both customers cannot fit in 1 vehicle (6 + 6 = 12 > 10)
    vehicles = [Vehicle(id="V_1", capacity=10.0)]
    problem = Problem(id="toy_cap", depot=depot, customers=customers, vehicles=vehicles, coordinate_system="planar")
    tour = [1, 2]
    cost, routes, is_feasible, violations = prins_split(problem, tour, max_vehicles=1)

    assert is_feasible is False
    assert "max_vehicles" in violations
    assert len(routes) == 2  # Partitioned into 2 routes because 1 is impossible


def test_prins_split_time_windows_forces_split() -> None:
    """Test that conflicting time windows force 2 vehicles even when capacity allows 1."""
    depot = Depot(id="depot", lat=0.0, lon=0.0, ready_time=0.0, due_time=100.0)
    # Total demand = 5 + 5 = 10 <= capacity 20
    # But C1 is at (10, 0) due at 12 (arr=10), service=5, leaves at 15
    # C2 is at (0, 10) due at 12! From C1 (10, 0) to C2 (0, 10), travel time is hypot(10, 10)=14.14
    # Arrives at C2 at 15 + 14.14 = 29.14 >> due 12!
    customers = [
        Customer(id="C1", lat=10.0, lon=0.0, demand=5.0, ready_time=0.0, due_time=12.0, service_time=5.0),
        Customer(id="C2", lat=0.0, lon=10.0, demand=5.0, ready_time=0.0, due_time=12.0, service_time=5.0),
    ]
    vehicles = [Vehicle(id="V1", capacity=20.0), Vehicle(id="V2", capacity=20.0)]
    problem = Problem(id="tw_split", depot=depot, customers=customers, vehicles=vehicles, coordinate_system="planar")
    problem.compute_euclidean_matrices(speed=1.0)

    from solver.models import Solution
    from solver.validate import validate_solution

    # With hard time windows, single route [C1, C2] is impossible; Split must output 2 routes
    cost, routes, is_feasible, violations = prins_split(
        problem, [1, 2], hard_time_windows=True
    )

    assert is_feasible is True
    assert violations == {}
    assert len(routes) == 2
    assert routes[0].stop_ids == ["C1"]
    assert routes[1].stop_ids == ["C2"]

    val_res = validate_solution(problem, Solution(routes=routes, total_cost=cost, is_feasible=is_feasible))
    assert val_res.is_valid is True


def test_prins_split_driver_duty_limit() -> None:
    """Test that max duty limit flags excessive route duration."""
    depot = Depot(id="depot", lat=0.0, lon=0.0, ready_time=0.0, due_time=200.0)
    customers = [
        Customer(id="C1", lat=20.0, lon=0.0, demand=5.0, ready_time=0.0, due_time=100.0, service_time=10.0),
    ]
    # Route depot -> C1 -> depot takes 20 + 10 + 20 = 50s
    vehicles = [Vehicle(id="V1", capacity=20.0)]
    problem = Problem(id="duty_toy", depot=depot, customers=customers, vehicles=vehicles, coordinate_system="planar")
    problem.compute_euclidean_matrices(speed=1.0)

    # Max duty duration 40s < 50s
    cost, routes, is_feasible, violations = prins_split(
        problem, [1], max_duty_duration=40.0, hard_duty_limit=True
    )
    assert is_feasible is False
    assert "driver_duty_limit" in violations



=== FILE: tests/solver/test_tensor.py ===
"""Unit tests for solver CostTensor and CostOracle integration.

Verifies:
1. Precomputing stationary vs time-dependent travel-time tensors.
2. Exact numerical equivalence between 2D distance matrix and 3D tensor (B=1) in Numba.
3. Multi-bucket time-dependent tensor evaluation with TimeOfDayProfileAdapter.
4. QPSO and PSO execution when provided with an explicit CostOracle.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from pathlib import Path
import numpy as np
import pytest

from routing.cost_oracle import (
    EuclideanOracle,
    Location,
    TimeOfDayProfileAdapter,
)
from solver.io import load_cvrplib
from solver.models import Customer, Depot, Problem, Vehicle
from solver.pso import PSOConfig, solve_pso
from solver.qpso import QPSOConfig, solve_qpso
from solver.split import _split_cost_numba, prins_split
from solver.tensor import CostTensor, precompute_cost_tensor


@pytest.fixture
def small_problem() -> Problem:
    """A small hand-crafted CVRPTW problem with 4 customers in Vadodara coordinates."""
    depot = Depot(id="depot", lat=22.3108, lon=73.1811, ready_time=0.0, due_time=36000.0)
    customers = [
        Customer(
            id="c1", lat=22.3129, lon=73.1906, demand=4.0, ready_time=0.0, due_time=10000.0, service_time=300.0
        ),
        Customer(
            id="c2", lat=22.2936, lon=73.1915, demand=6.0, ready_time=0.0, due_time=10000.0, service_time=300.0
        ),
        Customer(
            id="c3", lat=22.3200, lon=73.1700, demand=5.0, ready_time=0.0, due_time=10000.0, service_time=300.0
        ),
        Customer(
            id="c4", lat=22.3050, lon=73.2000, demand=3.0, ready_time=0.0, due_time=10000.0, service_time=300.0
        ),
    ]
    vehicles = [
        Vehicle(id="v1", capacity=10.0, shift_start=0.0, shift_end=36000.0),
        Vehicle(id="v2", capacity=10.0, shift_start=0.0, shift_end=36000.0),
    ]
    problem = Problem(id="small_vadodara", depot=depot, customers=customers, vehicles=vehicles)
    problem.compute_euclidean_matrices()
    return problem


def test_tensor_numerical_equivalence_with_2d_matrix(small_problem: Problem) -> None:
    """Verify that _split_cost_numba on 2D matrix vs 3D tensor (B=1) yields identical cost."""
    dist_mat = np.asarray(small_problem.distance_matrix, dtype=np.float64)
    demands = np.array([0.0, 4.0, 6.0, 5.0, 3.0], dtype=np.float64)
    tour = np.array([1, 2, 3, 4], dtype=np.int64)
    capacity = 10.0

    cost_2d = _split_cost_numba(tour, dist_mat, demands, capacity, max_vehicles=-1)

    # Convert to 3D tensor with B=1
    tensor_3d = dist_mat.reshape((1, 5, 5))
    cost_3d = _split_cost_numba(tour, tensor_3d, demands, capacity, max_vehicles=-1)

    assert cost_2d == pytest.approx(cost_3d, abs=1e-12)


def test_precompute_cost_tensor_stationary(small_problem: Problem) -> None:
    """Verify stationary CostTensor has num_buckets=1 and matching distance matrix."""
    oracle = EuclideanOracle(use_haversine=True, base_speed_mps=10.0)
    tensor = precompute_cost_tensor(small_problem, cost_oracle=oracle)

    assert tensor.num_buckets == 1
    assert tensor.travel_time_tensor.shape == (1, 5, 5)
    assert tensor.distance_matrix.shape == (5, 5)
    assert not tensor.is_time_dependent


def test_precompute_cost_tensor_time_dependent(small_problem: Problem) -> None:
    """Verify time-dependent CostTensor builds multiple buckets with distinct rush-hour costs."""
    adapter = TimeOfDayProfileAdapter()
    oracle = EuclideanOracle(traffic_adapter=adapter, use_haversine=True, base_speed_mps=10.0)

    # 10 hours horizon (0 to 36000s) with 3600s (1h) buckets -> 10 buckets
    tensor = precompute_cost_tensor(
        small_problem, cost_oracle=oracle, bucket_size_seconds=3600.0, num_buckets=10
    )

    assert tensor.num_buckets == 10
    assert tensor.travel_time_tensor.shape == (10, 5, 5)
    assert tensor.is_time_dependent

    # Compare off-peak (hour 0) vs rush peak (hour 8)
    # Hour 8 speed factor is 0.65 (slower -> higher travel time)
    # Hour 0 speed factor is 1.15 (faster -> lower travel time)
    dur_night = tensor.travel_time_tensor[0, 0, 1]
    dur_rush = tensor.travel_time_tensor[8, 0, 1]
    assert dur_rush > dur_night * 1.5


def test_qpso_with_explicit_cost_oracle(small_problem: Problem) -> None:
    """Verify solve_qpso executes with an explicit CostOracle and returns valid routes."""
    oracle = EuclideanOracle(use_haversine=True, base_speed_mps=10.0)
    cfg = QPSOConfig(num_particles=10, max_iters=15, seed=42)

    solution, history = solve_qpso(small_problem, config=cfg, cost_oracle=oracle)

    assert solution.is_feasible is True
    assert solution.total_cost > 0.0
    assert len(solution.routes) >= 2
    assert len(history) == 16


def test_pso_with_explicit_cost_oracle(small_problem: Problem) -> None:
    """Verify solve_pso executes with an explicit CostOracle."""
    oracle = EuclideanOracle(use_haversine=True, base_speed_mps=10.0)
    cfg = PSOConfig(num_particles=10, max_iters=15, seed=42)

    solution, history = solve_pso(small_problem, config=cfg, cost_oracle=oracle)

    assert solution.is_feasible is True
    assert solution.total_cost > 0.0
    assert len(solution.routes) >= 2


=== FILE: tests/solver/test_validate.py ===
"""Unit tests for independent solution validator in solver/validate.py."""

from __future__ import annotations

import pytest

from solver.models import Customer, Depot, Problem, Route, Solution, Vehicle
from solver.validate import ValidationResult, validate_solution


@pytest.fixture
def sample_problem() -> Problem:
    """Fixture with depot and 3 customers with explicit time windows and demands."""
    depot = Depot(id="depot", lat=0.0, lon=0.0, ready_time=0.0, due_time=100.0, service_time=0.0)
    customers = [
        Customer(id="c1", lat=10.0, lon=0.0, demand=20.0, ready_time=10.0, due_time=30.0, service_time=5.0, priority=1),
        Customer(id="c2", lat=20.0, lon=0.0, demand=30.0, ready_time=25.0, due_time=50.0, service_time=5.0, priority=2),
        Customer(id="c3", lat=30.0, lon=0.0, demand=25.0, ready_time=35.0, due_time=70.0, service_time=5.0, priority=1),
    ]
    vehicles = [
        Vehicle(id="v1", capacity=100.0, shift_start=0.0, shift_end=80.0),
        Vehicle(id="v2", capacity=100.0, shift_start=0.0, shift_end=80.0),
    ]
    problem = Problem(id="sample", depot=depot, customers=customers, vehicles=vehicles, coordinate_system="planar")
    problem.compute_euclidean_matrices(speed=1.0)
    return problem


def test_validator_feasible_solution(sample_problem: Problem) -> None:
    """Verify that a strictly valid solution produces is_valid=True with matching costs."""
    # Route: depot -> c1 -> c2 -> c3 -> depot
    # c1: arr=10, wait=0, start=10, serv=5, dep=15
    # c2: arr=25, wait=0, start=25, serv=5, dep=30
    # c3: arr=40, wait=0, start=40, serv=5, dep=45
    # depot: arr=75 <= 100
    route = Route(
        vehicle_id="v1",
        stop_ids=["c1", "c2", "c3"],
        arrival_times=[10.0, 25.0, 40.0],
        load=75.0,
        distance=60.0,
        duration=75.0,
    )
    solution = Solution(routes=[route], total_cost=60.0, is_feasible=True)

    res = validate_solution(sample_problem, solution)
    assert res.is_valid is True
    assert len(res.violations) == 0
    assert res.recomputed_cost == 60.0
    assert res.recomputed_distance == 60.0
    assert res.recomputed_duration == 75.0
    assert res.total_service_time == 15.0
    assert res.total_waiting_time == 0.0
    assert res.customer_count == 3


def test_validator_catches_missing_customer(sample_problem: Problem) -> None:
    """Verify detection of omitted customer."""
    route = Route(
        vehicle_id="v1",
        stop_ids=["c1", "c2"],  # c3 is missing
        arrival_times=[10.0, 25.0],
        load=50.0,
        distance=40.0,
        duration=50.0,
    )
    solution = Solution(routes=[route], total_cost=40.0, is_feasible=False)

    res = validate_solution(sample_problem, solution)
    assert res.is_valid is False
    assert "customer_coverage" in res.violations
    assert "Missing 1 customer" in res.violations["customer_coverage"][0]


def test_validator_catches_duplicate_customer(sample_problem: Problem) -> None:
    """Verify detection of duplicate customer visit."""
    route1 = Route(vehicle_id="v1", stop_ids=["c1", "c2"], arrival_times=[10.0, 25.0], load=50.0, distance=40.0, duration=50.0)
    route2 = Route(vehicle_id="v2", stop_ids=["c2", "c3"], arrival_times=[20.0, 30.0], load=55.0, distance=60.0, duration=65.0)
    solution = Solution(routes=[route1, route2], total_cost=100.0, is_feasible=False)

    res = validate_solution(sample_problem, solution)
    assert res.is_valid is False
    assert "customer_coverage" in res.violations
    assert "Duplicate" in res.violations["customer_coverage"][0]


def test_validator_catches_capacity_overload(sample_problem: Problem) -> None:
    """Verify detection of vehicle capacity breach."""
    # Shrink vehicle capacity to 50
    sample_problem.vehicles[0].capacity = 50.0
    route = Route(
        vehicle_id="v1",
        stop_ids=["c1", "c2", "c3"],  # total load = 75 > 50
        arrival_times=[10.0, 25.0, 40.0],
        load=75.0,
        distance=60.0,
        duration=75.0,
    )
    solution = Solution(routes=[route], total_cost=60.0, is_feasible=False)

    res = validate_solution(sample_problem, solution)
    assert res.is_valid is False
    assert "capacity" in res.violations
    assert "exceeds capacity" in res.violations["capacity"][0]


def test_validator_catches_time_window_violation(sample_problem: Problem) -> None:
    """Verify detection of late arrival at a customer."""
    # Reverse customer order: c3 -> c2 -> c1
    # c3: arr=30 (ready=35, due=70) -> wait 5s, start 35, dep 40
    # c2: arr=40 + 10 = 50 <= 50 -> dep 55
    # c1: arr=55 + 10 = 65 > due 30! (Late by 35s)
    route = Route(
        vehicle_id="v1",
        stop_ids=["c3", "c2", "c1"],
        arrival_times=[30.0, 50.0, 65.0],
        load=75.0,
        distance=60.0,
        duration=75.0,
    )
    solution = Solution(routes=[route], total_cost=60.0, is_feasible=False)

    res = validate_solution(sample_problem, solution)
    assert res.is_valid is False
    assert "time_window" in res.violations
    assert "c1" in res.violations["time_window"][0]


def test_validator_catches_depot_closing_violation(sample_problem: Problem) -> None:
    """Verify detection of late return to depot."""
    sample_problem.depot.due_time = 70.0  # Return takes 75s
    route = Route(
        vehicle_id="v1",
        stop_ids=["c1", "c2", "c3"],
        arrival_times=[10.0, 25.0, 40.0],
        load=75.0,
        distance=60.0,
        duration=75.0,
    )
    solution = Solution(routes=[route], total_cost=60.0, is_feasible=False)

    res = validate_solution(sample_problem, solution)
    assert res.is_valid is False
    assert "depot_due_time" in res.violations


def test_validator_catches_duty_limit_exceeded(sample_problem: Problem) -> None:
    """Verify detection of driver duty limit breach."""
    route = Route(
        vehicle_id="v1",
        stop_ids=["c1", "c2", "c3"],
        arrival_times=[10.0, 25.0, 40.0],
        load=75.0,
        distance=60.0,
        duration=75.0,
    )
    solution = Solution(routes=[route], total_cost=60.0, is_feasible=True)

    # Max duty limit 60.0s < route duration 75.0s
    res = validate_solution(sample_problem, solution, max_duty_duration=60.0, hard_duty_limit=True)
    assert res.is_valid is False
    assert "driver_duty_limit" in res.violations


def test_validator_catches_priority_early_service_violation(sample_problem: Problem) -> None:
    """Verify detection of priority customer serviced late in window."""
    # c2 has priority=2, ready=25, due=50.
    # Early cutoff (ratio 0.5) is 25 + 0.5 * 25 = 37.5.
    # Arrival at c2 in route below:
    # c1: arr=10, dep=15.
    # c3: arr=15 + 20 = 35 (ready=35, dep=40).
    # c2: arr=40 + 10 = 50. Arrived at 50 > 37.5 early deadline!
    route = Route(
        vehicle_id="v1",
        stop_ids=["c1", "c3", "c2"],
        arrival_times=[10.0, 35.0, 50.0],
        load=75.0,
        distance=80.0,
        duration=75.0,
    )
    solution = Solution(routes=[route], total_cost=80.0, is_feasible=True)

    res = validate_solution(
        sample_problem, solution, enforce_priority_early_service=True, priority_early_ratio=0.5
    )
    assert res.is_valid is False
    assert "priority_early_service" in res.violations
    assert "c2" in res.violations["priority_early_service"][0]

"""Smoke tests for Route.it to ensure CI passes and environments are sane."""

import sys


def test_python_version() -> None:
    """Verify that Python runtime meets the minimum >= 3.11 requirement."""
    assert sys.version_info >= (3, 11), f"Requires Python >= 3.11, running {sys.version}"


def test_package_imports() -> None:
    """Verify that all core Route.it packages can be imported cleanly."""
    import api
    import benchmarks
    import dynamic
    import routing
    import solver

    assert solver.__version__ == "0.1.0"
    assert routing.__version__ == "0.1.0"
    assert dynamic.__version__ == "0.1.0"
    assert api.__version__ == "0.1.0"
    assert benchmarks.__version__ == "0.1.0"


def test_basic_arithmetic_sanity() -> None:
    """Basic sanity check ensuring test runner functions properly."""
    assert 1 + 1 == 2
